local Conns, Cleanup = {}, {}
local function bind(signal, fn)
local c = signal:Connect(fn)
Conns[#Conns + 1] = c
return c
end
local cleanupSeen = {}
local function addCleanup(key, fn)
if cleanupSeen[key] then return end
cleanupSeen[key] = true
Cleanup[#Cleanup + 1] = fn
end
local function pathTo(root, ...)
local node = root
for _, name in ipairs({ ... }) do
if not node then return nil end
node = node:FindFirstChild(name)
end
return node
end
local function make(class, props, parent)
local inst = Instance.new(class)
for k, v in pairs(props) do inst[k] = v end
if parent then inst.Parent = parent end
return inst
end

local function scriptEnvs()
local list, seen = {}, {}
local function add(env)
if type(env) == "table" and env ~= _G and not seen[env] then
seen[env] = true
list[#list + 1] = env
end
end
if type(getfenv) == "function" then
for i = 0, 6 do pcall(function() add(getfenv(i)) end) end
end
if type(getrenv) == "function" then
pcall(function()
local env = getrenv()
add(env)
if type(env) == "table" then
add(rawget(env, "_G"))
add(rawget(env, "shared"))
end
end)
end
return list
end
local function getScriptGlobal(key)
for _, env in ipairs(scriptEnvs()) do
local ok, v = pcall(rawget, env, key)
if ok and v ~= nil then return v, env end
end
return nil
end
local function setScriptGlobal(key, value)
local hit = false
for _, env in ipairs(scriptEnvs()) do
local ok, old = pcall(rawget, env, key)
if ok and old ~= nil then
pcall(function() env[key] = value end)
hit = true
end
end
return hit
end
local function looksLikeTrap(m)
if not m:IsA("Model") then return false end
return m:FindFirstChild("HitBox") ~= nil
and (m:FindFirstChild("Open") ~= nil or m:FindFirstChild("Close") ~= nil)
end
local flareCache, flareCacheAt = nil, 0
local function flarePartUncached()
for _, m in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
if m.Name == "FlareGunPickUp" then
local gun = m:FindFirstChild("FlareGun")
if gun and gun:IsA("BasePart") then return gun end
local any = m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
if any then return any end
end
end
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
if d.Name == "FlareGunPickUp" then
local gun = d:FindFirstChild("FlareGun")
if gun and gun:IsA("BasePart") then return gun end
local any = d:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
if any then return any end
elseif d.Name == "FlareGun" and d:IsA("BasePart")
and not d:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Tool")
and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(d:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")) then
return d
end
end
return nil
end
local function flarePart()
if flareCache ~= nil and os.clock() - flareCacheAt < 1 then
if flareCache == false then return nil end
if flareCache.Parent then return flareCache end
end
local found = flarePartUncached()
flareCache, flareCacheAt = found or false, os.clock()
return found
end
local function trapModels()
local out = {}
local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
local traps = debris and debris:FindFirstChild("Traps")
if traps then
for _, m in ipairs(traps:GetChildren()) do
if looksLikeTrap(m) then out[#out + 1] = m end
end
end
local filter = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Filter")
local spawns = filter and filter:FindFirstChild("ScrapSpawns")
if spawns then
for _, part in ipairs(spawns:GetChildren()) do
for _, m in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
if looksLikeTrap(m) then out[#out + 1] = m end
end
end
end
return out
end
local function realUI()
local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if not pg then return nil end
for _, g in ipairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
if g:IsA("ScreenGui") and g.Name == "UI"
and (g:FindFirstChild("Frame2") or g:FindFirstChild("Heartbeat")) then
return g
end
end
return nil
end
local function cam() return Workspace.CurrentCamera end
local function eyeDist(part)
local c = Workspace.CurrentCamera
if not (c and part and part.Parent) then return math.huge end
local ok, d = pcall(function()
local rel = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(c.CFrame.Position)
local h = part.Size * 0.5
return Vector3.new(
math.max(math.abs(rel.X) - h.X, 0),
math.max(math.abs(rel.Y) - h.Y, 0),
math.max(math.abs(rel.Z) - h.Z, 0)).Magnitude
end)
return ok and d or math.huge
end
local function fadeAtEye(part, base, reach)
if not (part and part.Parent) then return end
reach = reach or 9
local d = eyeDist(part)
local t = base
if d < reach then t = base + (1 - base) * (1 - d / reach) end
if t > 0.995 then t = 1 end
pcall(function() part.Transparency = t end)
return t
end
local function fadeGlow(glow, adorn, base, near)
if not (glow and adorn) then return end
local c = Workspace.CurrentCamera
if not c then return end
local eye = c.CFrame.Position
local d
local ok = pcall(function()
if adorn:IsA("Model") then
local cf, size = adorn:GetBoundingBox()
local rel = cf:PointToObjectSpace(eye)
local h = size * 0.5
d = Vector3.new(
math.max(math.abs(rel.X) - h.X, 0),
math.max(math.abs(rel.Y) - h.Y, 0),
math.max(math.abs(rel.Z) - h.Z, 0)).Magnitude
elseif adorn:IsA("BasePart") then
d = eyeDist(adorn)
else
d = (eye - adorn:GetPivot().Position).Magnitude
end
end)
if not (ok and d) then return end
near = near or 11
local t = base
if d < near then t = base + (1 - base) * (1 - d / near) end
pcall(function() glow.FillTransparency = math.min(t, 1) end)
end

local function glide(from, to, seconds)
local t0 = os.clock()
while true do
local live = getHRP()
if not live then return false end
local alpha = math.clamp((os.clock() - t0) / seconds, 0, 1)
live.CFrame = from:Lerp(to, alpha)
live.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
live.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
if alpha >= 1 then return true end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
end
local Fight
local Free
local Stretch
local function clipboard(text)
if typeof(setclipboard) == "function" then
return (pcall(setclipboard, text))
end
return false
end
local HAS_DRAWING = typeof(Drawing) == "table"
local Device = { name = nil, detected = "PC", remembered = false, FILE = "Rake/device.txt", WAIT = 20 }
local IS_MOBILE = false
function Device.detect()
local touch, keys, mouse = false, false, false
pcall(function()
touch = UserInputService.TouchEnabled
keys = UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
mouse = UserInputService.MouseEnabled
end)
return (touch and not keys and not mouse) and "Mobile" or "PC"
end
function Device.read()
local raw
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(Device.FILE) and typeof(readfile) == "function" then
raw = readfile(Device.FILE)
end
end)
raw = tostring(raw or ""):match("^%s*(%a+)")
return (raw == "PC" or raw == "Mobile") and raw or nil
end
function Device.remember(name)
pcall(function()
if name then
if typeof(isfolder) == "function" and typeof(makefolder) == "function" and not isfolder("Rake") then
makefolder("Rake")
end
if typeof(writefile) == "function" then writefile(Device.FILE, name) end
elseif typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(Device.FILE) and typeof(delfile) == "function" then
delfile(Device.FILE)
end
end)
end
function Device.fitScale()
local x, y = 0, 0
pcall(function()
local vp = Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
x, y = vp.X, vp.Y
end)
if not (type(x) == "number" and x > 0) then x = 844 end
if not (type(y) == "number" and y > 0) then y = 390 end
return math.clamp(math.floor(math.min(x / 1000, y / 560, 1) * 20 + 0.5) / 20, 0.55, 1)
end
function Device.build() return nil end function Device.build_disabled()
local parent
pcall(function() if typeof(gethui) == "function" then parent = gethui() end end)
if not parent then parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") end
if not parent then pcall(function() parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) end
if not parent then return nil end
local TEXT, SUB, FAINT = Color3.fromRGB(15, 17, 24), Color3.fromRGB(62, 68, 82), Color3.fromRGB(150, 158, 172)
local ACCENT, EDGE, WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(36, 40, 52), Color3.fromRGB(206, 211, 221), Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

local BOLD = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold)
local W, H = 440, 268
local ui = { conns = {}, remember = false, tiles = {} }
local function tw(o, t, props, style, dir)
local x = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
x:Play()
return x
end
local function corner(r, p) return make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }, p) end
local function label(p, props)
local d = { BackgroundTransparency = 1, FontFace = FONT, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = TEXT,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 5 }
for k, v in pairs(props) do d[k] = v end
return make("TextLabel", d, p)
end
local function hook(signal, fn) ui.conns[#ui.conns + 1] = signal:Connect(fn) end
local gui = make("ScreenGui", { Name = "Ruv&Rill_Device", IgnoreGuiInset = true, ResetOnSpawn = false,
DisplayOrder = 1000000, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling }, parent)
ui.gui = gui
local scrim = make("TextButton", { Name = "Scrim", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Modal = true,
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(6, 7, 9), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 1 }, gui)
local blur
pcall(function() blur = make("BlurEffect", { Name = "RKDeviceBlur", Size = 0 }, Lighting) end)
local holder = make("Frame", { Name = "Card", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 18), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H), ZIndex = 2 }, gui)
corner(8, holder)
local rim = make("UIStroke", { Color = WHITE, Thickness = 1.2, Transparency = 1 }, holder)
local scale = make("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, holder)
local function fit()
local vp
pcall(function() vp = Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize end)
local x = (vp and type(vp.X) == "number" and vp.X > 0) and vp.X or 1280
local y = (vp and type(vp.Y) == "number" and vp.Y > 0) and vp.Y or 720
return math.min(1, (x - 24) / W, (y - 24) / H)
end
ui.fit = fit()
scale.Scale = ui.fit * 0.94
local shadow = make("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Controls/DropShadow.png",
ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice, SliceCenter = Rect.new(10, 10, 118, 118), ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 12, 24),
ImageTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 44, 1, 44), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-22, -14), ZIndex = 2 }, holder)
local okCg, card = pcall(function() return make("CanvasGroup", { GroupTransparency = 1 }, holder) end)
if not okCg then card = make("Frame", {}, holder) end
ui.canvas = okCg

card.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)

card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04

card.ZIndex = 3

Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 28) })

AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 29), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(120, 16) })

Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 54), Size = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0, 20) })

end
local keys = false
pcall(function() keys = UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled end)
local function tile(name, x, desc, key, icon)
local b = make("TextButton", { Name = name, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 90),
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(187, 126), ZIndex = 4 }, card)
corner(6, b)
local stroke = make("UIStroke", { Color = EDGE, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.1,
ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, b)
local sc = make("UIScale", { Scale = 0.96 }, b)
local well = make("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT, BackgroundTransparency = 0.92, BorderSizePixel = 0,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40), ZIndex = 5 }, b)
corner(6, well)
icon(well)
label(b, { Text = name, FontFace = BOLD, TextSize = 17, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 15),
Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 22) })
label(b, { Text = desc, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(98, 106, 122), TextWrapped = true,
TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 66), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 50) })
if keys then
local cap = label(b, { Text = key, FontFace = BOLD, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = FAINT,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.25,
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 14), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20) })
corner(4, cap)
make("UIStroke", { Color = EDGE, Thickness = 1 }, cap)
end
if name == Device.detected then
local pill = label(b, { Text = "detected", FontFace = BOLD, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(34, 120, 72),
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(112, 214, 150),
BackgroundTransparency = 0.72, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 38), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 17) })
corner(4, pill)
end
local t = { btn = b, stroke = stroke, scale = sc, over = false }
function t.rest()
if ui.picked then return end
tw(b, 0.18, { BackgroundTransparency = t.over and 0.08 or 0.3 })
tw(sc, 0.18, { Scale = t.over and 1.025 or 1 })
tw(stroke, 0.18, { Color = t.over and ACCENT or EDGE, Transparency = t.over and 0.55 or 0.1 })
end
hook(b.MouseEnter, function() t.over = true; ui.touched = true; t.rest() end)
hook(b.MouseLeave, function() t.over = false; t.rest() end)
hook(b.MouseButton1Down, function() if not ui.picked then tw(sc, 0.1, { Scale = 0.975 }) end end)
hook(b.MouseButton1Up, function() t.rest() end)
hook(b.Activated, function() ui.pick(name) end)
ui.tiles[name] = t
end
tile("PC", 26, "Keyboard and mouse. Keybinds, hover to star, and the full-size menu.", "1", iconPC)
tile("Mobile", 227, "Touch screen. On-screen buttons, a menu button, and a menu sized to fit.", "2", iconPhone)
local row = make("TextButton", { Name = "DontAsk", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 228), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 24), ZIndex = 4 }, card)
local box = make("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.2, BorderSizePixel = 0,
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), ZIndex = 5 }, row)
corner(3, box)
local boxLine = make("UIStroke", { Color = BOXLINE, Thickness = 1.2 }, box)
local tick = make("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false, ZIndex = 6 }, box)
make("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -2.5, 0.5, 1.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 2), Rotation = 45, ZIndex = 6 }, tick)
make("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BorderSizePixel = 0, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 1.5, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 2), Rotation = -50, ZIndex = 6 }, tick)
label(row, { Text = "Don't ask again", TextColor3 = SUB, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0) })
hook(row.Activated, function()
if ui.picked then return end
ui.remember = not ui.remember
ui.touched = true
tick.Visible = ui.remember
tw(box, 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = ui.remember and ACCENT or WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = ui.remember and 0 or 0.2 })
tw(boxLine, 0.15, { Color = ui.remember and ACCENT or BOXLINE })
end)
local count = label(card, { Text = "", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = FAINT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 232), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 16) })
function ui.countdown(n)
if n ~= ui.shown then ui.shown = n; count.Text = "continuing as " .. Device.detected .. " in " .. n .. "s" end
end
function ui.pick(name)
if ui.picked or ui.superseded then return end
ui.picked = name
local t, other = ui.tiles[name], ui.tiles[name == "PC" and "Mobile" or "PC"]
if t then
tw(t.btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
tw(t.stroke, 0.15, { Color = ACCENT, Transparency = 0, Thickness = 2 })
t.scale.Scale = 1.05
tw(t.scale, 0.35, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
end
if other then
tw(other.btn, 0.2, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.7 })
tw(other.scale, 0.2, { Scale = 0.97 })
end
count.Text = "setting up for " .. name .. "..."
end
function ui.show()
tw(scrim, 0.4, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.45 })
if blur then tw(blur, 0.4, { Size = 18 }) end
tw(holder, 0.5, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) })
tw(scale, 0.5, { Scale = ui.fit }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
tw(shadow, 0.45, { ImageTransparency = 0.5 })
tw(rim, 0.45, { Transparency = 0.2 })
if ui.canvas then tw(card, 0.35, { GroupTransparency = 0 }) end
for i, n in ipairs({ "PC", "Mobile" }) do
local t = ui.tiles[n]
task.delay(0.06 + i * 0.07, function() if t and not ui.picked then t.rest() end end)
end
end
function ui.destroy()
for _, c in ipairs(ui.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
ui.conns = {}
pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
if blur then pcall(function() blur:Destroy() end) end
end
function ui.close(keep)
if not keep then
tw(scrim, 0.3, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
if blur then tw(blur, 0.3, { Size = 0 }) end
end
tw(holder, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -10) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
tw(scale, 0.3, { Scale = ui.fit * 0.96 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
tw(shadow, 0.25, { ImageTransparency = 1 })
tw(rim, 0.25, { Transparency = 1 })
if ui.canvas then tw(card, 0.28, { GroupTransparency = 1 }) else card.Visible = false end
task.wait(0.32)
if keep then
for _, c in ipairs(ui.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
ui.conns = {}
pcall(function() holder:Destroy() end)
return { gui = gui, scrim = scrim, blur = blur, destroy = function()
pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
if blur then pcall(function() blur:Destroy() end) end
end }
end
ui.destroy()
end
function ui.supersede() ui.superseded = true; ui.destroy() end
hook(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gp)
if gp or ui.picked then return end
local k = input.KeyCode
if k == Enum.KeyCode.One or k == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadOne or k == Enum.KeyCode.P then ui.pick("PC")
elseif k == Enum.KeyCode.Two or k == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadTwo or k == Enum.KeyCode.M then ui.pick("Mobile")
elseif k == Enum.KeyCode.Return or k == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter then ui.pick(Device.detected) end
end)
hook(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default end)
pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true end)
end)
pcall(function()
hook(Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"), function()
ui.fit = fit()
if not ui.picked then scale.Scale = ui.fit end
end)
end)
return ui
end
function Device.ask()
Device.detected = Device.detect()
local preset
pcall(function() preset = getgenv and getgenv().Ruv&Rill_DEVICE end)
if preset == "PC" or preset == "Mobile" then Device.name = preset return end
local saved = Device.read()
if saved then Device.name, Device.remembered = saved, true return end
local ok, ui = pcall(Device.build)
if not (ok and ui) then
if not ok then warn("[Ruv&Rill] the PC / Mobile card failed: " .. tostring(ui)) end
Device.name = Device.detected
return
end
local g
pcall(function() g = getgenv and getgenv() end)
if g then
local old = g.Ruv&Rill_DEVICE_CARD
if type(old) == "table" and old.supersede then pcall(old.supersede) end
g.Ruv&Rill_DEVICE_CARD = ui
end
if typeof(game) ~= "Instance" or not coroutine.isyieldable() then
ui.destroy()
if g and g.Ruv&Rill_DEVICE_CARD == ui then g.Ruv&Rill_DEVICE_CARD = nil end
Device.name = Device.detected
return
end
local okRun, err = pcall(function()
ui.show()
local deadline = os.clock() + Device.WAIT
while not ui.picked and not ui.superseded do
if ui.touched then ui.touched = false; deadline = os.clock() + Device.WAIT end
local left = deadline - os.clock()
if left <= 0 then ui.pick(Device.detected) break end
ui.countdown(math.ceil(left))
task.wait(0.1)
end
end)
if ui.superseded then
error("[Ruv&Rill] stopped: the script was run again while this one was asking PC or Mobile", 0)
end
if not okRun then warn("[Ruv&Rill] the PC / Mobile card failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
Device.name = ui.picked or Device.detected
if ui.remember then Device.remember(Device.name); Device.remembered = true end
pcall(function()
task.wait(0.3)
Device.backdrop = ui.close(true)
end)
if not Device.backdrop then pcall(ui.destroy) end
if g and g.Ruv&Rill_DEVICE_CARD == ui then g.Ruv&Rill_DEVICE_CARD = nil end
end
Device.ask()
IS_MOBILE = Device.name == "Mobile"
print("[Ruv&Rill] set up for " .. tostring(Device.name))
Device.Boot = { live = false, n = 0, TOTAL = 19, fails = {}, FILE = "Rake/loader.txt", WAIT = 6, lastAt = 0,
opt = { config = true, assets = true, scan = true, perf = false, safe = false, fast = false } }
do
local Boot = Device.Boot
Boot.CAPS = {
{ key = "files", label = "Files", what = "saving configs and settings",
test = function() return typeof(writefile) == "function" and typeof(readfile) == "function" end },
{ key = "assets", label = "Assets", what = "drawn icons and sounds",
test = function() return typeof(getcustomasset) == "function" end },
{ key = "drawing", label = "Drawing", what = "ESP text and lines",
test = function() return typeof(Drawing) == "table" end },
{ key = "scan", label = "Memory", what = "Infinite Stamina and Night Vision",
test = function() return typeof(getgc) == "function" end },
{ key = "touch", label = "Touch", what = "auto pickups and Bring Scrap",
test = function() return typeof(firetouchinterest) == "function" end },
{ key = "prompts", label = "Prompts", what = "Instant Prompts and Controls",
test = function() return typeof(fireproximityprompt) == "function" end },
{ key = "hidden", label = "Hidden", what = "Insta Kill reach",
test = function() return typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" end },
{ key = "owner", label = "Net owner", what = "Insta Kill checks",
test = function() return typeof(isnetworkowner) == "function" end },
{ key = "fps", label = "FPS cap", what = "FPS Unlocker",
test = function() return typeof(setfpscap) == "function" end },
{ key = "clip", label = "Clipboard", what = "copying config codes",
test = function() return typeof(setclipboard) == "function" end },
}
Boot.LABEL = { ["config manager"] = "Configs and themes", ["menu keybind"] = "Menu keybind",
background = "Background", ["round watchers"] = "Round watchers", autoload = "Your saved config",
["hit ping"] = "Hit sounds", ["safe mode"] = "Safe Mode" }
function Boot.caps()
local out, have = {}, {}
for _, c in ipairs(Boot.CAPS) do
local ok = false
pcall(function() ok = c.test() and true or false end)
out[#out + 1] = { key = c.key, label = c.label, what = c.what, ok = ok }
have[c.key] = ok
end
return out, have
end
function Boot.executor()
local name, ver
pcall(function() if typeof(identifyexecutor) == "function" then name, ver = identifyexecutor() end end)
name = (type(name) == "string" and name ~= "") and name or "your executor"
return (type(ver) == "string" and ver ~= "") and (name .. " " .. ver) or name
end
function Boot.readOpts()
local raw
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(Boot.FILE) and typeof(readfile) == "function" then raw = readfile(Boot.FILE) end
end)
for k, v in tostring(raw or ""):gmatch("(%a+)%s*=%s*(%d)") do
if Boot.opt[k] ~= nil then Boot.opt[k] = v == "1" end
end
end
function Boot.saveOpts()
local l = {}
for _, k in ipairs({ "config", "assets", "scan", "perf", "safe", "fast" }) do
l[#l + 1] = k .. "=" .. (Boot.opt[k] and "1" or "0")
end
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfolder) == "function" and typeof(makefolder) == "function" and not isfolder("Rake") then makefolder("Rake") end
if typeof(writefile) == "function" then writefile(Boot.FILE, table.concat(l, "\n")) end
end)
end
function Boot.autoloadName()
local raw
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile("Rake/configs/autoload.txt") and typeof(readfile) == "function" then
raw = readfile("Rake/configs/autoload.txt")
end
end)
raw = raw and tostring(raw):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
return raw ~= "" and raw or nil
end
function Boot.build(bd, caps, have)
local parent
pcall(function() if typeof(gethui) == "function" then parent = gethui() end end)
if not parent then parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") end
if not parent then pcall(function() parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) end
if not parent and not (bd and bd.gui) then return nil end
local TEXT, SUB, FAINT = Color3.fromRGB(15, 17, 24), Color3.fromRGB(62, 68, 82), Color3.fromRGB(150, 158, 172)
local ACCENT, EDGE, WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(36, 40, 52), Color3.fromRGB(206, 211, 221), Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
local GOOD, BAD, OFF = Color3.fromRGB(72, 199, 116), Color3.fromRGB(214, 58, 58), Color3.fromRGB(190, 196, 208)
local TRACK = Color3.fromRGB(226, 230, 238)
local FONT = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular)
local BOLD = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold)
local W, H, H2, TOP = 460, 362, 236, 70
local ui = { conns = {}, opts = {}, tiles = {}, logs = {} }
for k, v in pairs(Boot.opt) do ui.opts[k] = v end
local function tw(o, t, props, style, dir)
local x = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
x:Play()
return x
end
local function corner(r, p) return make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }, p) end
local function label(p, props)
local d = { BackgroundTransparency = 1, FontFace = FONT, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = TEXT,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex = 5 }
for k, v in pairs(props) do d[k] = v end
return make("TextLabel", d, p)
end

function ui.countdown(n)
if ui.touched then
if ui.shown ~= "stop" then ui.shown = "stop"; startText.Text = "Start" end
return
end
if n ~= ui.shown then ui.shown = n; startText.Text = "Start  " .. n end
end
function ui.start()
if ui.started then return end
ui.started = true
tw(startScale, 0.1, { Scale = 0.97 })
end
function ui.show()
if not shown then
tw(scrim, 0.4, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.45 })
if blur then tw(blur, 0.4, { Size = 18 }) end
end
tw(holder, 0.5, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) })
tw(scale, 0.5, { Scale = fit }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
tw(shadow, 0.45, { ImageTransparency = 0.5 })
tw(rim, 0.45, { Transparency = 0.2 })
if okCg then tw(card, 0.35, { GroupTransparency = 0 }) end
end
function ui.toProgress()
if p2.Visible and not p1.Visible then return end
ui.sub.Text = "Loading for " .. tostring(Device.name or "PC") .. " on " .. Boot.executor()
.. (ui.opts.safe and " - Safe Mode" or "")
p2.Visible = true
tw(p1, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-W, TOP) })
tw(p2, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, TOP) })
tw(holder, 0.35, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H2) })
task.delay(0.32, function() pcall(function() p1.Visible = false end) end)
end
function ui.setStep(i, total, text)
stepText.Text = text
stepCount.Text = i .. " / " .. total
tw(fill, 0.2, { Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(i / total, 0, 0.97), 0, 1, 0) })
end
function ui.addLog(text, ok)
table.insert(ui.logs, 1, { text = text, ok = ok })
while #ui.logs > 4 do table.remove(ui.logs) end
for i, l in ipairs(lines) do
local e = ui.logs[i]
l.f.Visible = e ~= nil
if e then
l.text.Text = e.text
l.dot.BackgroundColor3 = e.ok and GOOD or BAD
l.text.TextColor3 = e.ok and SUB or BAD
end
end
end
function ui.done(fails)
stepCount.Text = ""
tw(fill, 0.25, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = #fails == 0 and GOOD or BAD })
if #fails == 0 then
stepText.Text = "Ready"
else
stepText.Text = "Ready - with " .. #fails .. " problem" .. (#fails == 1 and "" or "s")
local names = {}
for _, f in ipairs(fails) do names[#names + 1] = f.name end
result.Text = "Failed: " .. table.concat(names, ", ") .. ". The rest works."
result.TextColor3 = BAD
end
end
function ui.destroy()
for _, c in ipairs(ui.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
ui.conns = {}
pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
if blur then pcall(function() blur:Destroy() end) end
end
function ui.close()
tw(scrim, 0.35, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
if blur then tw(blur, 0.35, { Size = 0 }) end
tw(holder, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -10) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
tw(scale, 0.3, { Scale = fit * 0.96 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
tw(shadow, 0.25, { ImageTransparency = 1 })
tw(rim, 0.25, { Transparency = 1 })
if okCg then tw(card, 0.28, { GroupTransparency = 1 }) else card.Visible = false end
task.delay(0.38, ui.destroy)
end
hook(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gp)
if gp or ui.started then return end
local k = input.KeyCode
if k == Enum.KeyCode.Return or k == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter then ui.start() end
end)
hook(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default end)
pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true end)
end)
return ui
end
function Boot.open(bd)
Boot.readOpts()
local caps, have = Boot.caps()
Boot.missing = {}
for _, c in ipairs(caps) do
if not c.ok then Boot.missing[#Boot.missing + 1] = c end
end
if not have.assets then Boot.opt.assets = false end
if not have.scan then Boot.opt.scan = false end
if typeof(game) ~= "Instance" or not coroutine.isyieldable() then
if bd and bd.destroy then pcall(bd.destroy) end
return
end
local ok, ui = pcall(Boot.build, bd, caps, have)
if not (ok and ui) then
if not ok then warn("[Ruv&Rill] the loading screen failed: " .. tostring(ui)) end
if bd and bd.destroy then pcall(bd.destroy) end
return
end
pcall(function()
local g = getgenv and getgenv()
if g then
local old = g.Ruv&Rill_LOADER
if type(old) == "table" and old.destroy and old ~= ui then pcall(old.destroy) end
g.Ruv&Rill_LOADER = ui
end
end)
Boot.ui, Boot.live, Boot.lastAt = ui, true, os.clock()
local okRun, err = pcall(function()
ui.show()
if not Boot.opt.fast then
local deadline = os.clock() + Boot.WAIT
while not ui.started do
if ui.touched then deadline = math.huge end
local left = deadline - os.clock()
if left <= 0 then ui.start() break end
ui.countdown(math.ceil(left))
task.wait(0.1)
end
for k, v in pairs(ui.opts) do Boot.opt[k] = v end
Boot.saveOpts()
ui.toProgress()
task.wait(0.35)
end
end)
if not okRun then warn("[Ruv&Rill] the loading screen failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
Boot.lastAt = os.clock()
task.spawn(function()
while Boot.live do
task.wait(1)
if Boot.live and os.clock() - Boot.lastAt > 20 then Boot.finish("stalled") end
end
end)
end
function Boot.step(name)
if not Boot.live then return end
local ui = Boot.ui
if Boot.cur and ui then pcall(ui.addLog, Boot.cur, true) end
Boot.n = Boot.n + 1
Boot.cur, Boot.lastAt = name, os.clock()
if ui then pcall(ui.setStep, Boot.n, math.max(Boot.TOTAL, Boot.n), name) end
pcall(task.wait)
end
function Boot.fail(name, err)
Boot.fails[#Boot.fails + 1] = { name = name, err = tostring(err) }
if Boot.live and Boot.ui then
pcall(Boot.ui.addLog, name .. " - failed", false)
if Boot.cur == name then Boot.cur = nil end
end
end
function Boot.finish(why)
if not Boot.live then return end
Boot.live = false
local ui = Boot.ui
Boot.ui = nil
if not ui then return end
if Boot.cur then pcall(ui.addLog, Boot.cur, true) end
Boot.cur = nil
if why == "stalled" then
Boot.fails[#Boot.fails + 1] = { name = "loading stopped at step " .. Boot.n, err = "stalled" }
end
pcall(ui.done, Boot.fails)
task.spawn(function()
task.wait(#Boot.fails > 0 and 2.6 or 0.7)
if Boot.reveal then pcall(Boot.reveal) end
pcall(ui.close)
pcall(function()
local g = getgenv and getgenv()
if g and g.Ruv&Rill_LOADER == ui then g.Ruv&Rill_LOADER = nil end
end)
end)
end
Boot.open(Device.backdrop)
Device.backdrop = nil
end
local getgc = Device.Boot.opt.scan and getgc or nil
local getcustomasset = Device.Boot.opt.assets and getcustomasset or nil
local drawings = {}
local function newDraw(class, props)
if not HAS_DRAWING then return nil end
local ok, d = pcall(function() return Drawing.new(class) end)
if not ok or not d then return nil end
for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do pcall(function() d[k] = v end) end
drawings[#drawings + 1] = d
return d
end
addCleanup("drawings", function()
for _, d in ipairs(drawings) do
pcall(function() d.Visible = false end)
pcall(function() d:Remove() end)
end
drawings = {}
end)
local function canWrite()
return typeof(writefile) == "function" and typeof(isfolder) == "function"
and typeof(isfile) == "function"
end
local function ensureFolder(path)
if not canWrite() then return false end
return (pcall(function()
if not isfolder(path) then makefolder(path) end
end))
end
local repo = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/deividcomsono/Obsidian/main/"
local function cachePath(name)
return "Rake/lib/" .. name:gsub("[^%w]", "_") .. ".lua"
end
local function usable(src)
if type(src) ~= "string" or #src < 200 then return false end
return (loadstring(src, "@check")) ~= nil
end
local function cacheRead(path)
if not (canWrite() and typeof(readfile) == "function" and isfile(path)) then
return nil
end
local read, cached = pcall(readfile, path)
return (read and usable(cached)) and cached or nil
end
local function fetch(name, force)
local path = cachePath(name)
if not force then
local cached = cacheRead(path)
if cached then return cached, "cached" end
end
local got, source = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(repo .. name) end)
if got and usable(source) then
if canWrite() then
ensureFolder("Rake")
ensureFolder("Rake/lib")
pcall(writefile, path, source)
end
return source, "fetched"
end
if force then
local cached = cacheRead(path)
if cached then return cached, "cached (refresh failed)" end
end
if canWrite() and typeof(delfile) == "function" and isfile(path) then
pcall(delfile, path)
end
return nil, got and "what downloaded is not Lua - rate limited, most likely"
or tostring(source)
end
local function loadPart(name, force)
local source, how = fetch(name, force)
if not source then return nil, how end
local chunk, err = loadstring(source, "@" .. name)
if not chunk then return nil, "compile: " .. tostring(err) end
local ok, result = pcall(chunk)
if not ok then return nil, "run: " .. tostring(result) end
return result, how
end

local Library = {
Options={}, Toggles={}, Unloaded=false, Toggled=true,
IsMobile = UIS.TouchEnabled and not UIS.MouseEnabled,
DPIScale=1, Connections={}, _unload={}, _registry={}, Font=Enum.Font.Gotham,

Buttons={},
}
local Options, Toggles = Library.Options, Library.Toggles
local function new(class, props, kids)
local i = Instance.new(class)
if props then for k, v in pairs(props) do if k ~= "Parent" then i[k] = v end end end
if kids then for _, c in ipairs(kids) do c.Parent = i end end
if props and props.Parent then i.Parent = props.Parent end
return i
end
local cornerList = {}
Library.CornerMax = 4
local function cornerPx(px) return px >= 999 and px or math.min(px, Library.CornerMax or 4) end
local function corner(px, parent)
local c = new("UICorner", { CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,cornerPx(px)), Parent=parent })
cornerList[#cornerList+1] = { c, px }
if #cornerList % 400 == 0 then
local keep = {}
for _, e in ipairs(cornerList) do if e[1].Parent then keep[#keep+1] = e end end
cornerList = keep
end
return c
end
function Library:SetCornerRadius(v)
self.CornerMax = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 4, 0, 16)
local keep = {}
for _, e in ipairs(cornerList) do
if e[1].Parent then
pcall(function() e[1].CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, cornerPx(e[2])) end)
keep[#keep+1] = e
end
end
cornerList = keep
end
local function stroke(col, tr, parent, thick)
return new("UIStroke", { Color=col, Transparency=tr, Thickness=thick or 1,
ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border, Parent=parent })
end
local function pad(parent, t, b, l, r)
return new("UIPadding", { Parent=parent, PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,t or 0), PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,b or t or 0),
PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,l or t or 0), PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,r or l or t or 0) })
end
local function vlist(parent, gap)
return new("UIListLayout", { Parent=parent, FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
Padding=UDim.new(0,gap or 4), SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder })
end
local function hlist(parent, gap, align)
return new("UIListLayout", { Parent=parent, FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,
Padding=UDim.new(0,gap or 6), SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,
HorizontalAlignment=align or Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left })
end
local function txt(props)
local d = { BackgroundTransparency=1, FontFace=FF.thin, TextColor3=T.Text, TextSize=13,
TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Center }
for k, v in pairs(props) do d[k] = v end
return new("TextLabel", d)
end
local function tween(inst, time, goal, style, dir)
local ok, tw = pcall(function()
return TweenService:Create(inst, TweenInfo.new(time, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), goal)
end)
if ok and tw then tw:Play() end
return tw
end
local function track(conn) Library.Connections[#Library.Connections+1] = conn; return conn end
local function reg(inst, prop, token) Library._registry[#Library._registry+1] = { inst=inst, prop=prop, token=token } end
local function glyphFor(name) return (name and Glyph[name]) or (name and name:sub(1,1):upper()) or "" end
local function safeKey(name)
if not name or name == "None" then return nil end
local ok, k = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[name] end)
return ok and k or nil
end
function Library:OnUnload(fn) self._unload[#self._unload+1] = fn end
local function apply(obj, v, silent)
obj.Value = v
if obj.Display then pcall(obj.Display, obj) end
if not silent and obj.Callback then pcall(obj.Callback, obj.Value) end
end
local function liquid(host, rad, o)
o = o or {}
host.BackgroundTransparency = 1
local shadow = new("ImageLabel", { Parent=host, Name="_shadow", BackgroundTransparency=1, Image=SHADOW_IMG,
ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Slice, SliceCenter=Rect.new(10,10,118,118), ImageColor3=T.Shadow,
ImageTransparency=o.shadow or TR.shadow, Size=UDim2.new(1,36,1,36), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(-18,-12), ZIndex=1 })
local body = new("Frame", { Parent=host, Name="_fill", BackgroundColor3=o.fill or T.Column,
BackgroundTransparency=o.transp or TR.column, Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1), ZIndex=1 })
corner(rad, body)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=body, Rotation=90, Color=ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1), T.Tint),
Transparency=NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.1) }) })
local gloss = new("Frame", { Parent=body, Name="_gloss", BackgroundColor3=T.Film,
Size=o.gloss or UDim2.new(1,0,0.5,0), ZIndex=1 })
corner(rad, gloss)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=gloss, Rotation=90, Transparency=NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,TR.sheenTop), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1) }) })
local rim = stroke(T.Rim, 0, body, o.rim or 1.2)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=rim, Rotation=40, Transparency=NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35,0.72), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7,0.86),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.35) }) })
local inner = new("Frame", { Parent=body, Name="_inner", BackgroundTransparency=1,
Position=UDim2.fromOffset(1,1), Size=UDim2.new(1,-2,1,-2), ZIndex=1 })
corner(math.max(rad-1, 0), inner)
local hair = stroke(T.Rim, 0.5, inner, 1)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=hair, Rotation=90, Transparency=NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.15), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,0.9), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.6) }) })
return body, shadow
end
local function hoverFx(btn, skip, rest, lit, press)
rest, lit, press = rest or TR.rowOff, lit or TR.rowHover, press or TR.rowPress
local function to(tr) if not (skip and skip()) then tween(btn, EASE.snap, { BackgroundTransparency=tr }) end end
local over = false
track(btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() over = true; to(lit) end))
track(btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() over = false; to(rest) end))
track(btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() to(press) end))
track(btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() to(over and lit or rest) end))
end
local NOTE_W, NOTE_MAX, NOTE_CHARS = 200, 4, 110
local notifyHolder
local notifyCards = {}
local function notifyLayout()
local y = 0
for _, n in ipairs(notifyCards) do
local c = n.card
if c and c.Parent then
n.y = y
tween(c, EASE.open, { Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, y) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
y = y + n.h + 6
end
end
end
local function notifyDismiss(n)
if n.gone then return end
n.gone = true
for i, e in ipairs(notifyCards) do if e == n then table.remove(notifyCards, i) break end end
local card = n.card
pcall(function() tween(card, EASE.move, { Position = UDim2.new(1, NOTE_W + 40, 0, n.y or 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In) end)
task.delay(EASE.move + 0.02, function() pcall(function() card:Destroy() end) end)
notifyLayout()
end
function Library:Notify(o)
o = o or {}
if not notifyHolder then return end
local title = tostring(o.Title or "Ruv&Rill")
local desc = tostring(o.Description or ""):gsub("%s+", " ")
if #desc > NOTE_CHARS then desc = desc:sub(1, NOTE_CHARS - 3) .. "..." end
local life = math.clamp(tonumber(o.Time) or 4, 1.5, 8)
local key = title .. "|" .. desc
for _, n in ipairs(notifyCards) do
if n.key == key and not n.gone then
n.gen = n.gen + 1
local gen = n.gen
if n.bar then n.bar.Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,0,2); tween(n.bar, life, { Size=UDim2.new(0,0,0,2) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear) end
task.delay(life, function() if n.gen == gen then notifyDismiss(n) end end)
return
end
end
while #notifyCards >= NOTE_MAX do notifyDismiss(notifyCards[1]) end
local card = new("Frame", { Parent=notifyHolder, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(NOTE_W, 34), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0),
Position=UDim2.new(1, NOTE_W + 40, 0, 0), ZIndex=6001 })
liquid(card, 10, { fill=T.Popup, transp=TR.popup, gloss=UDim2.new(1,0,0,18) })
local content = new("Frame", { Parent=card, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0),
AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex=6003 })
pad(content, 6, 8, 10, 10); vlist(content, 0)
txt({ Parent=content, Text=title, FontFace=FF.med, TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Text, LayoutOrder=1,
TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,14), ZIndex=6004 })
if desc ~= "" then
txt({ Parent=content, Text=desc, FontFace=FF.thin, TextSize=11, TextWrapped=true, LayoutOrder=2,
TextColor3=T.Sub, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0), AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex=6004 })
end
local bar = new("Frame", { Parent=card, BackgroundColor3=T.Accent, BackgroundTransparency=0.8, BorderSizePixel=0,
Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,2), Position=UDim2.new(0,10,1,-4), ZIndex=6004 })
corner(999, bar)
tween(bar, life, { Size=UDim2.new(0,0,0,2) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
local n = { card=card, key=key, gen=0, bar=bar, h=34, y=0 }
local function fit()
local ok, cy = pcall(function() return content.AbsoluteSize.Y end)
if not (ok and type(cy)=="number") then return end
local h = math.clamp(math.floor(cy + 0.5), 28, 96)
if n.h ~= h then n.h = h; card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(NOTE_W, h); notifyLayout() end
end
track(content:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(fit))
table.insert(notifyCards, n)
task.defer(function() fit(); notifyLayout() end)
task.delay(life, function() if n.gen == 0 then notifyDismiss(n) end end)
end
local screenGui, mainFrame, windowFrame, columnsScroll, popupLayer
local currentPopup, currentFeature
local keyPickers = {}
local capturingKey = false
local popupConns = {}
local function trackPopup(c) popupConns[#popupConns+1] = c; return c end
local closePopup
local function guiPoint(input)
return Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
end
local function dragify(grab, onMove, onStart, onEnd, persistent)
local dragging, id = false, nil
local hold = persistent and track or trackPopup
track(grab.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true; id = input
local at = guiPoint(input)
if onStart then onStart(at) end
onMove(at)
end
end))
hold(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
onMove(guiPoint(input))
end
end))
hold(UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
dragging = false
if onEnd then onEnd(guiPoint(input)) end
end
end))
end
local function block(parent, h, order)
local fr = new("Frame", { Parent=parent, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,h),
LayoutOrder=order or 1, ZIndex=5003, AutomaticSize = h==0 and Enum.AutomaticSize.Y or nil })
return fr
end
local renderers = {}
renderers.Slider = function(obj, parent, order, persistent)
local fr = block(parent, 46, order)
txt({ Parent=fr, Text=obj.Text or "", TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Sub,
Size=UDim2.new(0.6,0,0,16), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,0), ZIndex=5004 })
local valLbl = txt({ Parent=fr, Text="", TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Text, TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
Size=UDim2.new(0.4,0,0,16), Position=UDim2.new(0.6,0,0,0), ZIndex=5004 })
local hit = new("Frame", { Parent=fr, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,22),
Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,18), ZIndex=5004 })
local track_ = new("Frame", { Parent=hit, BackgroundColor3=T.Inset, BackgroundTransparency=TR.inset,
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,4), Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,0), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0.5), ZIndex=5004 })
corner(999, track_)
local fill = new("Frame", { Parent=track_, BackgroundColor3=T.Accent, BackgroundTransparency=0.1,
Size=UDim2.new(0,0,1,0), ZIndex=5005 })
corner(999, fill)
local handle = new("Frame", { Parent=track_, BackgroundColor3=T.Knob, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(16,16),
AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5), Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,0), ZIndex=5006 })
corner(999, handle); stroke(T.EdgeDk, 0.35, handle, 1)
local function pct() local d=(obj.Max-obj.Min); return d==0 and 0 or (obj.Value-obj.Min)/d end
obj._widgetDisplay = function()
local p = math.clamp(pct(), 0, 1)
tween(fill, 0.08, { Size = UDim2.new(p,0,1,0) })
tween(handle, 0.08, { Position = UDim2.new(p,0,0.5,0) })
valLbl.Text = tostring(obj.Value)..(obj.Suffix or "")
end
obj._widgetDisplay()
dragify(hit, function(mp)
local abs, sz = track_.AbsolutePosition.X, track_.AbsoluteSize.X
local p = sz>0 and math.clamp((mp.X-abs)/sz, 0, 1) or 0
local raw = obj.Min + p*(obj.Max-obj.Min)
obj:SetValue(raw)
end, function() tween(handle, EASE.snap, { Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20,20) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end,
function() tween(handle, EASE.snap, { Size=UDim2.fromOffset(16,16) }) end, persistent)
return fr
end
local function optionRow(listParent, label, order, onClick)
local b = new("TextButton", { Parent=listParent, BackgroundColor3=T.Row, BackgroundTransparency=TR.rowOff,
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,26), Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, LayoutOrder=order, ZIndex=5006 })
corner(6, b); hoverFx(b)
local lbl = txt({ Parent=b, Text=label, TextSize=12, Size=UDim2.new(1,-28,1,0), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8,0), ZIndex=5007 })
local tick = txt({ Parent=b, Text=utf8.char(0x2713), TextSize=13, TextColor3=T.Accent, Visible=false,
Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20,26), Position=UDim2.new(1,-24,0,0), TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex=5007 })
track(b.Activated:Connect(onClick))
return b, lbl, tick
end
renderers.Dropdown = function(obj, parent, order)
local fr = block(parent, 0, order)
fr.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
vlist(fr, 4)
txt({ Parent=fr, Text=obj.Text or "", TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Sub, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,16), LayoutOrder=1, ZIndex=5004 })
local head = new("TextButton", { Parent=fr, BackgroundColor3=T.Widget, BackgroundTransparency=TR.widget,
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,28), Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, LayoutOrder=2, ZIndex=5004 })
corner(R.widget, head); stroke(T.Rim, 0.3, head, 1)
local headLbl = txt({ Parent=head, Text="", TextSize=12, Size=UDim2.new(1,-26,1,0), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(9,0),
TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex=5005 })
local chev = txt({ Parent=head, Text=utf8.char(0x25BC), TextSize=9, TextColor3=T.Sub, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20,28),
Position=UDim2.new(1,-22,0,0), TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex=5005 })
local listWrap = new("Frame", { Parent=fr, BackgroundColor3=T.Widget, BackgroundTransparency=TR.widget,
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0), LayoutOrder=3, ZIndex=5004, Visible=false, ClipsDescendants=true })
corner(R.widget, listWrap); stroke(T.Rim, 0.3, listWrap, 1)
local list = new("ScrollingFrame", { Parent=listWrap, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),
CanvasSize=UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness=3,
ScrollingDirection=Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ZIndex=5005 })
pad(list, 4,4,4,4); vlist(list, 3)
local open = false
local function headText()
if obj.Multi then
local n=0; for _ in pairs(obj.Value or {}) do n=n+1 end
return n==0 and "None" or (n.." selected")
end
return obj.Value ~= nil and tostring(obj.Value) or "None"
end
local listH = 0
local setOpen
local function rebuild()
for _, c in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("TextButton") then c:Destroy() end end
local i = 0
if obj.AllowNull and not obj.Multi then
i=i+1; optionRow(list, "(none)", i, function() obj:SetValue(nil); setOpen(false) end)
end
for _, v in ipairs(obj.Values or {}) do
i=i+1
local _, lbl, tick = optionRow(list, tostring(v), i, function()
if obj.Multi then
local set = {}; for k,on in pairs(obj.Value or {}) do if on then set[k]=true end end
if set[v] then set[v]=nil else set[v]=true end
obj:SetValue(set)
else
obj:SetValue(v); setOpen(false)
end
end)
local sel = obj.Multi and (obj.Value and obj.Value[v]) or (obj.Value==v)
tick.Visible = sel and true or false
if sel then lbl.FontFace = FF.med end
end
listH = math.min(#(obj.Values or {}) + (obj.AllowNull and 1 or 0), 8) * 29 + 8
if open then listWrap.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,listH) end
end
setOpen = function(v)
open = v and true or false
tween(chev, EASE.move, { Rotation = open and 180 or 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
if open then
listWrap.Visible = true
rebuild()
listWrap.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
tween(listWrap, EASE.move, { Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,listH) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
else
tween(listWrap, EASE.snap, { Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0) })
task.delay(EASE.snap, function() if not open then listWrap.Visible = false end end)
end
end
obj._widgetDisplay = function() headLbl.Text = headText(); if open then rebuild() end end
obj._rebuild = rebuild
obj._widgetDisplay()
track(head.Activated:Connect(function() setOpen(not open) end))
return fr
end
local function hsvArea(obj, parent, order)
local top = obj.Title and 20 or 0
local fr = block(parent, 116 + top, order)
if obj.Title then
txt({ Parent=fr, Text=tostring(obj.Title), TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Sub, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,16), ZIndex=5004 })
end
local hue, sat, val = 0, 0, 1
local function pull() hue, sat, val = obj.Value:ToHSV() end
pull()
local sv = new("Frame", { Parent=fr, BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromHSV(hue,1,1), Size=UDim2.fromOffset(120,110),
Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,top), ZIndex=5004 }); corner(R.sv, sv)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=sv, Color=ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.fromHSV(hue,1,1)) })
local blackWrap = new("Frame", { Parent=sv, BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(0,0,0), Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1), ZIndex=5005 })
corner(R.sv, blackWrap)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=blackWrap, Rotation=90, Transparency=NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0) }) })
local svCur = new("Frame", { Parent=sv, BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1), Size=UDim2.fromOffset(8,8),
AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5), ZIndex=5007 }); corner(999, svCur); stroke(T.EdgeDk,0.2,svCur,1)
local hueBar = new("Frame", { Parent=fr, Size=UDim2.new(0,16,0,110), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(128,top), ZIndex=5004 })
corner(6, hueBar)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=hueBar, Rotation=90, Color=ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromHSV(0,1,1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.17,Color3.fromHSV(0.17,1,1)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33,Color3.fromHSV(0.33,1,1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,Color3.fromHSV(0.5,1,1)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.67,Color3.fromHSV(0.67,1,1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83,Color3.fromHSV(0.83,1,1)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromHSV(1,1,1)) }) })
local hueCur = new("Frame", { Parent=hueBar, BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1), Size=UDim2.new(1,4,0,3),
AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5), Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,0), ZIndex=5007 }); stroke(T.EdgeDk,0.2,hueCur,1)
local sw = new("Frame", { Parent=fr, BackgroundColor3=obj.Value, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(40,24),
Position=UDim2.fromOffset(156,top), ZIndex=5004 }); corner(6, sw); stroke(T.Edge,0.5,sw,1)
sw:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true); sv:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
local function repaint()
sv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hue,1,1)
sv:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient").Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.fromHSV(hue,1,1))
svCur.Position = UDim2.new(sat,0,1-val,0)
hueCur.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,hue,0)
sw.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hue,sat,val)
end
obj._widgetDisplay = function() pull(); repaint() end
obj._repaint = repaint
repaint()
local function commit()
local c = Color3.fromHSV(hue, sat, val)
obj.Value = c
if obj._swatch then obj._swatch.BackgroundColor3 = c end
repaint()
if obj.Callback then pcall(obj.Callback, c) end
end
dragify(sv, function(mp)
local a,s = sv.AbsolutePosition, sv.AbsoluteSize
sat = s.X>0 and math.clamp((mp.X-a.X)/s.X,0,1) or 0
val = s.Y>0 and 1-math.clamp((mp.Y-a.Y)/s.Y,0,1) or 1
commit()
end)
dragify(hueBar, function(mp)
local a,s = hueBar.AbsolutePosition, hueBar.AbsoluteSize
hue = s.Y>0 and math.clamp((mp.Y-a.Y)/s.Y,0,1) or 0
commit()
end)
return fr
end
renderers.ColorPicker = hsvArea
renderers.KeyPicker = function(obj, parent, order)
local fr = block(parent, 54, order)
txt({ Parent=fr, Text=obj.Text or "Keybind", TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Sub,
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,16), ZIndex=5004 })
local bind = new("TextButton", { Parent=fr, BackgroundColor3=T.Widget, BackgroundTransparency=TR.widget,
Size=UDim2.new(0.58,-4,0,26), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,22), Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, ZIndex=5004 })
corner(R.widget, bind); stroke(T.Rim,0.3,bind,1); hoverFx(bind, nil, TR.widget, 0.15, 0.05)
local bl = txt({ Parent=bind, Text=obj.Value or "None", TextSize=12, Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),
TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex=5005 })
local modeBtn = new("TextButton", { Parent=fr, BackgroundColor3=T.Widget, BackgroundTransparency=TR.widget,
Size=UDim2.new(0.42,-4,0,26), Position=UDim2.new(1,0,0,22), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0),
Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, ZIndex=5004 })
corner(R.widget, modeBtn); stroke(T.Rim,0.3,modeBtn,1); hoverFx(modeBtn, nil, TR.widget, 0.15, 0.05)
local ml = txt({ Parent=modeBtn, Text=obj.Mode or "Toggle", TextSize=11, TextColor3=T.Sub, Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),
TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex=5005 })
obj._widgetDisplay = function()
bl.Text = obj.Value or "None"; ml.Text = obj.Mode or "Toggle"
bl.TextColor3 = obj._conflict and T.Bad or T.Text
end
local MODES = { "Toggle", "Hold", "Always", "Press" }
track(modeBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
local i=1; for k,m in ipairs(MODES) do if m==obj.Mode then i=k break end end
obj.Mode = MODES[(i % #MODES)+1]; obj:Display()
end))
track(bind.Activated:Connect(function()
bl.Text = "..."
capturingKey = true
local c; c = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
c:Disconnect()
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
local n = input.KeyCode.Name
if n=="Backspace" or n=="Delete" or n=="Escape" then obj:SetValue("None") else obj:SetValue(n) end
elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then obj:SetValue("None")
else obj:Display() end
task.defer(function() capturingKey = false end)
end)
end))
return fr
end
renderers.Input = function(obj, parent, order)
local fr = block(parent, 0, order)
fr.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
vlist(fr, 4)
txt({ Parent=fr, Text=obj.Text or "", TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Sub, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,16), LayoutOrder=1, ZIndex=5004 })
local box = new("TextBox", { Parent=fr, BackgroundColor3=T.Widget, BackgroundTransparency=TR.widget,
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,28), LayoutOrder=2, FontFace=FF.thin, TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Text,
Text=tostring(obj.Value or ""), PlaceholderText=obj.Placeholder or "", PlaceholderColor3=T.Faint,
ClearTextOnFocus=obj.ClearTextOnFocus and true or false, TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ZIndex=5005 })
corner(R.widget, box); pad(box,0,0,9,9)
local boxEdge = stroke(T.Rim,0.3,box,1)
track(box.Focused:Connect(function() tween(boxEdge, EASE.snap, { Color=T.AccentHi, Transparency=0.2 }) end))
track(box.FocusLost:Connect(function() tween(boxEdge, EASE.snap, { Color=T.Rim, Transparency=0.3 }) end))
obj._widgetDisplay = function() if not box:IsFocused() then box.Text = tostring(obj.Value or "") end end
if obj.Finished then
track(box.FocusLost:Connect(function() obj:SetValue(box.Text) end))
else
track(box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() obj:SetValue(box.Text) end))
end
return fr
end

AutoButtonColor=false, ZIndex=5004 })

Size=UDim2.fromOffset(36,20), Position=UDim2.new(1,0,0.5,0), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0.5), ZIndex=5005 })
corner(999, pill); stroke(T.Rim, 0.4, pill, 1)

Position=UDim2.new(0,2,0.5,0), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0.5), ZIndex=5006 })
corner(999, knob); stroke(T.EdgeDk, 0.45, knob, 1)
obj.Display = function()
local on = obj.Value and true or false

end
obj:Display()
track(b.Activated:Connect(function() obj:SetValue(not obj.Value) end))

renderers.Button = function(obj, parent, order)
local fr = block(parent, 32, order)
local b = new("TextButton", { Parent=fr, BackgroundColor3=T.Widget, BackgroundTransparency=TR.widget,
Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1), Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, ZIndex=5004 })
corner(R.button, b); stroke(T.Rim, 0.25, b, 1); hoverFx(b, nil, TR.widget, 0.15, 0.05)
local rest = obj.Bad and T.Bad or T.Text
local lbl = txt({ Parent=b, Text=obj.Text or "Button", TextSize=12, TextColor3=rest, Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),
TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, FontFace=FF.med, ZIndex=5005 })
local armed = false
track(b.Activated:Connect(function()
if obj.DoubleClick and not armed then
armed = true; lbl.Text = "Confirm?"; lbl.TextColor3 = T.Warn
task.delay(2, function() if armed then armed=false; lbl.Text=obj.Text or "Button"; lbl.TextColor3 = rest end end)
return
end
armed = false; lbl.Text = obj.Text or "Button"; lbl.TextColor3 = rest
if obj.Func then pcall(obj.Func) end
end))
return fr
end
renderers.Label = function(obj, parent, order)
