Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0), AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex=5004 })
obj.Display = function() l.Text = obj.Value or "" end
obj._lbl = l
return fr
end
closePopup = function()
if currentPopup then
local old = currentPopup; currentPopup = nil
local sc = old:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
if sc then tween(sc, EASE.snap, { Scale=0.94 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
task.delay(EASE.snap, function() pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end)
end
for _, c in ipairs(popupConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
popupConns = {}
if currentFeature then for _, c in ipairs(currentFeature.popupControls) do c._widgetDisplay = nil end end
currentFeature = nil
end
local function openPopup(feature)
closePopup()
currentFeature = feature
local w = math.floor(POPUP_W * Library.DPIScale)
local vp = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(1280,720)
local maxH = math.floor(((vp.Y and vp.Y > 0) and vp.Y or 720) * 0.6)
local pop = new("Frame", { Parent=popupLayer, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w, 60), ZIndex=5001, Active=true })
liquid(pop, R.popup, { fill=T.Popup, transp=TR.popup, gloss=UDim2.new(1,0,0,56) })
local uiscale = new("UIScale", { Parent=pop, Scale=0.9 })
currentPopup = pop
local titleBar = new("Frame", { Parent=pop, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,32), ZIndex=5003, Active=true })
local grip = new("Frame", { Parent=titleBar, BackgroundColor3=T.Sub, BackgroundTransparency=0.7,
Size=UDim2.fromOffset(26,3), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0), Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,4), ZIndex=5004 })
corner(999, grip)
txt({ Parent=titleBar, Text=feature.title or feature.name.Text, FontFace=FF.med, TextSize=14, TextColor3=T.Text,
Size=UDim2.new(1,-40,1,0), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(13,0), ZIndex=5004 })
local close = new("TextButton", { Parent=titleBar, BackgroundColor3=T.Widget, BackgroundTransparency=TR.widget,
Text=Glyph.close, FontFace=FF.med, TextSize=15, TextColor3=T.Sub, AutoButtonColor=false,
Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20,20), Position=UDim2.new(1,-10,0.5,0), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0.5), ZIndex=5004 })
corner(999, close); stroke(T.Rim, 0.3, close, 1); hoverFx(close, nil, TR.widget, 0.1, 0)
track(close.Activated:Connect(closePopup))
local body = new("ScrollingFrame", { Parent=pop, BackgroundTransparency=1, Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,32),
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,20), CanvasSize=UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
ScrollBarThickness=2, ScrollBarImageColor3=T.Sub, ScrollBarImageTransparency=0.6,
ScrollingDirection=Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ZIndex=5003 })
pad(body, 2, 10, 11, 11)
local blayout = vlist(body, 8)
for i, c in ipairs(feature.popupControls) do
local r = renderers[c.Type]
if r then pcall(r, c, body, i) end
end
local placed = false
local function resize()
local c = (blayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y or 0) + 14
local hh = math.clamp(c, 20, maxH)
body.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,hh)
pop.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 32 + hh)
if placed then
local lim = screenGui.AbsoluteSize.Y - MARGIN - pop.Size.Y.Offset
if pop.Position.Y.Offset > lim then
pop.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(pop.Position.X.Offset, math.max(MARGIN, lim))
end
end
end
track(blayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(resize))
task.defer(function()
resize()
if not (currentPopup == pop and feature.row and feature.row.Parent) then return end
local screen = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
local rp, rs = feature.row.AbsolutePosition - popupLayer.AbsolutePosition, feature.row.AbsoluteSize
local pw, ph = w, pop.Size.Y.Offset
local x = rp.X + rs.X + 8
if x + pw > screen.X - MARGIN then x = rp.X - pw - 8 end
if x < MARGIN then x = MARGIN end
local y = rp.Y
if y + ph > screen.Y - MARGIN then y = screen.Y - ph - MARGIN end
if y < MARGIN then y = MARGIN end
pop.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
placed = true
end)
tween(uiscale, EASE.open, { Scale=1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
local dragging, dragStart, startOff, dragTo, dragTw
track(titleBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging=true; dragStart=input.Position; startOff=Vector2.new(pop.Position.X.Offset, pop.Position.Y.Offset)
dragTo = nil
tween(grip, EASE.snap, { BackgroundTransparency=0.3, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(34,3) })
end
end))
trackPopup(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and (input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
local dd = input.Position - dragStart
local screen, ps = screenGui.AbsoluteSize, pop.AbsoluteSize
local x = math.clamp(startOff.X + dd.X, MARGIN - ps.X + 60, screen.X - 60)
local y = math.clamp(startOff.Y + dd.Y, MARGIN, math.max(MARGIN, screen.Y - 40))
dragTo = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
dragTw = tween(pop, 0.07, { Position=dragTo })
end
end))
trackPopup(UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and (input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
dragging=false
if dragTw then pcall(function() dragTw:Cancel() end) end
if dragTo then pop.Position = dragTo end
tween(grip, EASE.snap, { BackgroundTransparency=0.7, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(26,3) })
end
end))
end
local function togglePopup(feature)
if currentFeature == feature then closePopup() else openPopup(feature) end
end
local function ensureDots(feature)
if feature.dots then return feature.dots end
local d = new("TextButton", { Parent=feature.right, BackgroundColor3=T.Row, BackgroundTransparency=TR.rowOff,
Size=UDim2.fromOffset(24,18), Text=Glyph.dots, FontFace=FF.med, TextSize=13, TextColor3=T.Sub,
AutoButtonColor=false, Active=true, ZIndex=6, LayoutOrder=3 })
corner(999, d); hoverFx(d, nil, TR.rowOff, 0.45, 0.25)
feature.dots=d
track(d.Activated:Connect(function() togglePopup(feature) end))
return d
end
local function ensureKeyChip(feature, kp)
if feature.keyChip then return feature.keyChip end
local c = new("TextButton", { Parent=feature.right, BackgroundColor3=T.Widget, BackgroundTransparency=0.5,
Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0,20), AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X, Text="", AutoButtonColor=false,
Active=true, ZIndex=6, LayoutOrder=2 })
corner(999,c); stroke(T.Edge,0.85,c,1); pad(c,0,0,8,8)
local l = txt({ Parent=c, Text="", TextSize=11, TextColor3=T.AccentHi, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex=7 })
feature.keyChip=c
local function refresh() l.Text = (kp.Value and kp.Value~="None") and kp.Value or Glyph.dots end
kp._chipRefresh = refresh; refresh()
track(c.Activated:Connect(function()
l.Text="..."
local cc; cc = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
cc:Disconnect()
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
local n=input.KeyCode.Name
kp:SetValue((n=="Backspace" or n=="Delete" or n=="Escape") and "None" or n)
elseif input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then kp:SetValue("None") else refresh() end
end)
end))
return c
end
local function ensureSwatch(feature, cp)
if feature.swatch then return feature.swatch end
local s = new("TextButton", { Parent=feature.right, BackgroundColor3=cp.Value, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(18,18),
Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, Active=true, ZIndex=6, LayoutOrder=1 })
corner(999,s); stroke(T.Edge,0.4,s,1); feature.swatch=s; cp._swatch=s
s:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
track(s.Activated:Connect(function() togglePopup(feature) end))
return s
end
local function refreshDots(feature)
for _, c in ipairs(feature.popupControls) do
if not (c.Type=="KeyPicker" and c.NoUI) then ensureDots(feature); return end
end
end
local Fav = { FILE = "Rake/favourites.txt", GOLD = Color3.fromRGB(255, 186, 38), rows = {} }
function Fav.set()
if Fav.keys then return Fav.keys end
Fav.keys = {}
local raw
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfile)=="function" and isfile(Fav.FILE) and typeof(readfile)=="function" then raw = readfile(Fav.FILE) end
end)
for k in tostring(raw or ""):gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do Fav.keys[k] = true end
return Fav.keys
end
function Fav.save()
local l = {}
for k in pairs(Fav.set()) do l[#l+1] = k end
table.sort(l)
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfolder)=="function" and typeof(makefolder)=="function" and not isfolder("Rake") then makefolder("Rake") end
if typeof(writefile)=="function" then writefile(Fav.FILE, table.concat(l, "\n")) end
end)
end
function Fav.paint(f, hover)
local s = f.star
if not s then return end
local on = Fav.set()[f.favKey] == true
local show = on or (hover and true or false)
f.starShown = show
if show then s.Visible = true end
local img = f.starImg
if img and not Fav.noImage then
s.Text = ""
img.Visible = true
img.Image = (Fav.icon and Fav.icon(on and "star" or "star-o")) or img.Image
img.ImageColor3 = on and Fav.GOLD or T.Faint
tween(img, EASE.snap, { ImageTransparency = show and 0 or 1 })
else
if img then img.Visible = false end
s.Text = Glyph.star
tween(s, EASE.snap, { TextTransparency = on and 0 or (show and 0.6 or 1) })
end
tween(f.name, EASE.snap, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(show and 24 or 10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, show and -50 or -36, 1, 0) })
if not show then task.delay(EASE.snap, function() if not f.starShown then s.Visible = false end end) end
end
function Fav.flip(f)
local keys = Fav.set()
local on = not keys[f.favKey]
keys[f.favKey] = on or nil
Fav.save()
Fav.paint(f, f.hover)
if f.favCol and f.favCol.sortRows then f.favCol.sortRows() end
pcall(function()
Library:Notify({ Title = "Ruv&Rill", Description = (on and "Pinned " or "Unpinned ") .. tostring(f.title or f.favKey), Time = 2 })
end)
end
function Fav.probe(img)
if Fav.probing then return end
Fav.probing = true
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function() game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync({ img }) end)
local ok = false
pcall(function() ok = img.IsLoaded end)
if not ok then task.wait(3); pcall(function() ok = img.IsLoaded end) end
if not ok then
Fav.noImage = true
for _, f in ipairs(Fav.rows) do pcall(Fav.paint, f, false) end
end
end)
end
function Fav.add(f, gb, key)
local col = gb and gb._column
if not (key and col and col.rows) or col.noSort then return end
local entry = col.rows[#col.rows]
if not (entry and rawequal(entry.row, f.row)) then return end
entry.fav = key
f.favKey, f.favCol = key, col
local s = new("TextButton", { Parent=f.row, BackgroundTransparency=1, Text="", FontFace=FF.med, TextSize=12,
TextColor3=Fav.GOLD, TextTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(18, ROW_H), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(5, 0),
AutoButtonColor=false, Active=true, Visible=false, ZIndex=6 })
f.star = s
local id = Fav.icon and Fav.icon("star")
if id then
f.starImg = new("ImageLabel", { Parent=s, BackgroundTransparency=1, Image=id, ImageColor3=T.Faint,
ImageTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit, ZIndex=7 })
Fav.probe(f.starImg)
end
Fav.rows[#Fav.rows+1] = f
track(s.Activated:Connect(function() Fav.flip(f) end))
track(f.row.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function() Fav.flip(f) end))
if not Library.IsMobile then
track(f.row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
f.hover = true
local t0 = os.clock(); f.hoverAt = t0
task.delay(0.35, function() if f.hover and f.hoverAt == t0 then Fav.paint(f, true) end end)
end))
track(f.row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
f.hover = false; f.hoverAt = nil
if f.starShown then Fav.paint(f, false) end
end))
end
track(f.row.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
local t0, p0 = os.clock(), input.Position
f.holdAt = t0
task.delay(0.5, function()
if f.holdAt ~= t0 then return end
local st = input.UserInputState
if st == Enum.UserInputState.End or st == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then return end
if (input.Position - p0).Magnitude > 12 then return end
f.suppressTap = true
Fav.flip(f)
end)
end))
track(f.row.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
f.holdAt = nil
if f.suppressTap then task.delay(0.2, function() f.suppressTap = nil end) end
end))
if Fav.set()[key] then Fav.paint(f, false) end
end
Library._fav = Fav
local function makeRow(gb, kind)
gb._order = (gb._order or 0) + 1
local container = gb.Container
local row = new("TextButton", { Parent=container, BackgroundColor3=T.Row, BackgroundTransparency=TR.rowOff,
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,ROW_H), Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, Active=true,
LayoutOrder=gb._order, ZIndex=3 })
corner(R.row, row)
local accent = new("Frame", { Parent=row, BackgroundColor3=T.Row, BackgroundTransparency=1,
Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1), ZIndex=3 })
corner(R.row, accent)
local accentRim = stroke(T.Rim, 1, accent, 1)
local name = txt({ Parent=row, Text="", Size=UDim2.new(1,-36,1,0), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(10,0),
TextSize=12, TextColor3=T.Sub, TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex=4 })
local right = new("Frame", { Parent=row, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.new(0,30,1,0),
Position=UDim2.new(1,-4,0.5,0), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0.5), ZIndex=4 })
hlist(right, 5, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right)
if gb._column and gb._column.rows then table.insert(gb._column.rows, { row=row, label=name }) end
local feature = { row=row, accent=accent, accentRim=accentRim, name=name, right=right, kind=kind,
popupControls={}, container=container }
hoverFx(row, function() return feature.noHover end)
return feature
end
local function makeColorPicker(idx, o, feature)
local cp = { Type="ColorPicker", Value=o.Default or Color3.new(1,1,1), Title=o.Title,
Transparency=o.Transparency or 0, Callback=o.Callback }
cp.Display = function(self)
if self._swatch then self._swatch.BackgroundColor3 = self.Value end
if self._widgetDisplay then pcall(self._widgetDisplay) end
end
cp.SetValueRGB = function(self, c) self.Value=c; self:Display(); if self.Callback then pcall(self.Callback, self.Value) end end
cp.SetValue = cp.SetValueRGB
cp.OnChanged = function(self, fn) self.Changed=fn end
Options[idx]=cp
feature.popupControls[#feature.popupControls+1]=cp
refreshDots(feature)
return cp
end
local function makeKeyPicker(idx, o, feature, parentObj)
local kp = { Type="KeyPicker", Value=(o.Default or "None"), Mode=o.Mode or "Toggle", Toggled=false,
Text=o.Text, SyncToggleState=o.SyncToggleState, NoUI=o.NoUI, Callback=o.Callback, _parent=parentObj, Idx=idx }
kp.Display = function(self) if self._chipRefresh then self._chipRefresh() end; if self._widgetDisplay then pcall(self._widgetDisplay) end end
kp.SetValue = function(self, n)
self.Value=n
if not Library.Loading then pcall(Library.KeyClash, Library, self) end
self:Display(); if self.Callback then pcall(self.Callback, self.Value) end
end
kp.GetState = function(self)
if self.Mode=="Always" then return true end
if self.Mode=="Hold" then local k=safeKey(self.Value); return (k and UIS:IsKeyDown(k)) or false end
if self.Mode=="Toggle" then
if self.SyncToggleState and self._parent and self._parent.Value ~= nil then
return self._parent.Value and true or false
end
return self.Toggled
end
return false
end
kp.OnChanged = function(self, fn) self.Changed=fn end
Options[idx]=kp
keyPickers[#keyPickers+1]=kp
feature.popupControls[#feature.popupControls+1]=kp
if not o.NoUI then refreshDots(feature) end
return kp
end
local function makeToggle(gb, idx, o)
local feature = makeRow(gb, "toggle")
feature.name.Text = o.Text or idx; feature.title = o.Text or idx
local obj = { Type="Toggle", Value=o.Default and true or false, Text=o.Text, Default=o.Default,
Callback=o.Callback, Disabled=o.Disabled, Tooltip=o.Tooltip, Toggled=false, _feature=feature }
feature.obj = obj
obj.Display = function(self)
local on = self.Value and true or false
tween(feature.accent, EASE.move, { BackgroundTransparency = on and TR.rowOn or 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
tween(feature.accentRim, EASE.move, { Transparency = on and 0.35 or 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
tween(feature.name, EASE.move, { TextColor3 = self.Disabled and T.Faint or (on and T.Text or T.Sub) })
feature.name.FontFace = on and FF.med or FF.thin
if gb._column and gb._column.updateCount then gb._column.updateCount() end
end
obj.SetValue = function(self, v)
if self.Disabled then
if v and self.LockedWhy and not Library.Loading then Library:LockedNote(self) end
return
end
self.Value = v and true or false
self:Display()
for _, c in ipairs(feature.popupControls) do
if c.Type=="KeyPicker" and c.SyncToggleState then c.Toggled = self.Value end
end
if self.Callback then pcall(self.Callback, self.Value) end
end
obj.OnChanged = function(self, fn) self.Changed=fn end
obj.AddColorPicker = function(self, i2, o2) makeColorPicker(i2, o2, feature); return self end
obj.AddKeyPicker = function(self, i2, o2) makeKeyPicker(i2, o2, feature, self); refreshDots(feature); return self end
Toggles[idx] = obj
if gb._column then gb._column.toggles[#gb._column.toggles+1] = obj end
if o.Disabled then feature.noHover = true; feature.row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.95; feature.name.TextColor3 = T.Faint
else Fav.add(feature, gb, idx) end
track(feature.row.Activated:Connect(function() if feature.suppressTap then return end; obj:SetValue(not obj.Value) end))
obj:Display()
return obj
end
local function attachConfig(gb, obj, o, idx)
local owner = (not o.Standalone) and gb._cfgOwner or nil
if owner then
owner.popupControls[#owner.popupControls+1] = obj
refreshDots(owner)
return
end
gb._order = (gb._order or 0) + 1
local row = new("Frame", { Parent=gb.Container, BackgroundColor3=T.Row, BackgroundTransparency=TR.rowOff,
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0), AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder=gb._order, ZIndex=3 })
corner(R.row, row)
local host = new("Frame", { Parent=row, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0),
AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex=4 })
pad(host, 5, 6, 10, 10)
local rd = renderers[obj.Type]
if rd then pcall(rd, obj, host, 1, true) end
if gb._column and gb._column.rows then table.insert(gb._column.rows, { row=row, key=tostring(o.Text or idx), last=o.SortLast }) end
end
local function makeSubToggle(gb, idx, o)
local obj = { Type="SubToggle", Value=o.Default and true or false, Text=o.Text, Default=o.Default,
Callback=o.Callback, Idx=idx }
obj.Display = function(self) end
obj.SetValue = function(self, v)
self.Value = v and true or false
pcall(self.Display, self)
if self.Callback then pcall(self.Callback, self.Value) end
end
obj.OnChanged = function(self, fn) self.Changed=fn end
Toggles[idx] = obj
local owner = gb._cfgOwner
if owner then
owner.popupControls[#owner.popupControls+1] = obj
refreshDots(owner)
end
return obj
end
local function makeSlider(gb, idx, o)
local obj = { Type="Slider", Value=o.Default or o.Min or 0, Min=o.Min or 0, Max=o.Max or 100,
Rounding=o.Rounding or 0, Suffix=o.Suffix, Text=o.Text, Callback=o.Callback }
local function rnd(v) local m=10^(obj.Rounding or 0); return math.floor(v*m+0.5)/m end
obj.Display = function(self)
if self._rowVal then self._rowVal.Text = tostring(self.Value)..(self.Suffix or "") end
if self._widgetDisplay then pcall(self._widgetDisplay) end
end
obj.SetValue = function(self, v) apply(self, math.clamp(rnd(v), self.Min, self.Max), false) end
obj.OnChanged = function(self, fn) self.Changed=fn end
Options[idx] = obj
attachConfig(gb, obj, o, idx)
obj:Display()
return obj
end
local function makeDropdown(gb, idx, o)
local obj = { Type="Dropdown", Value=o.Default, Values=o.Values or {}, Multi=o.Multi and true or false,
AllowNull=o.AllowNull and true or false, Text=o.Text, Callback=o.Callback, SpecialType=o.SpecialType }
if obj.Multi and type(obj.Value) ~= "table" then obj.Value = {} end
local function headText()
if obj.Multi then local n=0; for _,on in pairs(obj.Value or {}) do if on then n=n+1 end end; return n==0 and "None" or (n.." selected") end
return obj.Value ~= nil and tostring(obj.Value) or "None"
end
obj.Display = function(self)
if self._rowVal then self._rowVal.Text = headText() end
if self._widgetDisplay then pcall(self._widgetDisplay) end
end
obj.SetValue = function(self, v) apply(self, v, false) end
obj.GetValues = function(self) return self.Values end
obj.SetValues = function(self, arr)
self.Values = arr or {}
if self.Multi then
local set={}; for k,on in pairs(self.Value or {}) do local keep=false; for _,v in ipairs(self.Values) do if v==k then keep=true break end end; if keep and on then set[k]=true end end
self.Value = set
else
local ok=false; for _,v in ipairs(self.Values) do if v==self.Value then ok=true break end end
if not ok then self.Value = (not self.AllowNull) and self.Values[1] or nil end
end
self:Display()
end
obj.OnChanged = function(self, fn) self.Changed=fn end
Options[idx] = obj
attachConfig(gb, obj, o, idx)
obj:Display()
return obj
end
local function makeInput(gb, idx, o)
local obj = { Type="Input", Value=o.Default or "", Default=o.Default, Finished=o.Finished,
ClearTextOnFocus=o.ClearTextOnFocus, Placeholder=o.Placeholder, Text=o.Text, Numeric=o.Numeric, Callback=o.Callback }
obj.Display = function(self)
if self._rowVal then self._rowVal.Text = tostring(self.Value or "") end
if self._widgetDisplay then pcall(self._widgetDisplay) end
end
obj.SetValue = function(self, s) apply(self, tostring(s or ""), false) end
obj.OnChanged = function(self, fn) self.Changed=fn end
Options[idx] = obj
attachConfig(gb, obj, o, idx)
obj:Display()
return obj
end
local function makeButton(gb, o)
local feature = makeRow(gb, "button")
feature.name.Text = o.Text or "Button"; feature.title = o.Text
feature.name.TextColor3 = o.Text and (o.Text:find("Unload") or o.Text:find("Panic")) and T.Bad or T.Text
local obj = { Type="Button", Text=o.Text, Func=o.Func, DoubleClick=o.DoubleClick, _feature=feature }
obj.Key = tostring(gb._column and gb._column.name or "") .. "/" .. tostring(o.Text or "")
Library.Buttons[#Library.Buttons+1] = obj
local function idleColor() return (o.Text and (o.Text:find("Unload") or o.Text:find("Panic"))) and T.Bad or T.Text end
local armed=false
obj.SetValue=function() end
obj.Display=function(self) feature.name.TextColor3 = self.Disabled and T.Faint or idleColor() end
obj.AddButton = function(self, o2) return makeButton(gb, o2) end
obj.AddKeyPicker = function(self, i2, o2)
local kp = makeKeyPicker(i2, o2, feature, self)
kp._press = function()
if obj.Disabled then if obj.LockedWhy then Library:LockedNote(obj) end return end
if obj.Func then obj.Func() end
end
return self
end
if o.Text and not o.DoubleClick then Fav.add(feature, gb, "@" .. tostring(gb._column and gb._column.name or "") .. "/" .. o.Text) end
track(feature.row.Activated:Connect(function()
if feature.suppressTap then return end
if obj.Disabled then if obj.LockedWhy then Library:LockedNote(obj) end return end
if obj.DoubleClick and not armed then
armed=true; feature.name.Text="Confirm?"; feature.name.TextColor3=T.Warn
task.delay(2, function() if armed then armed=false; feature.name.Text=o.Text or "Button"; obj:Display() end end)
return
end
armed=false; feature.name.Text=o.Text or "Button"; obj:Display()
if obj.Func then pcall(obj.Func) end
end))
return obj
end
local function makeLabel(gb, text, wrap)
local feature = makeRow(gb, "label")
feature.accent.Visible = false
feature.noHover = true
feature.right.Size = UDim2.new(0, 84, 0, ROW_H)
feature.right.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
feature.right.Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 4)
feature.name.TextColor3 = T.Sub
feature.name.Text = text or ""
if wrap then
feature.row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
feature.row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
feature.name.TextWrapped = true
feature.name.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
feature.name.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,0)
end
local obj = { Type="Label", Value=text, _feature=feature }
obj.SetText = function(self, s) feature.name.Text = tostring(s or ""); self.Value=s end
obj.SetVisible = function(self, b) feature.row.Visible = b and true or false end
obj.Display = function() end
obj.AddColorPicker = function(self, i2, o2) makeColorPicker(i2, o2, feature); return self end
obj.AddKeyPicker = function(self, i2, o2) makeKeyPicker(i2, o2, feature, self); return self end
return obj
end
local function makeGroupbox(column, title, side, boxIndex)
local gb = { Container=column.body, _owner=nil, _cfgOwner=nil, _order=0, _column=column, _wrapper=column.body }
gb.AddToggle = function(self, idx, o) local t=makeToggle(self, idx, o or {}); self._owner=t._feature; self._cfgOwner=t._feature; return t end
gb.AddSlider = function(self, idx, o) return makeSlider(self, idx, o or {}) end
gb.AddSubToggle = function(self, idx, o) return makeSubToggle(self, idx, o or {}) end
gb.AddDropdown = function(self, idx, o) return makeDropdown(self, idx, o or {}) end
gb.AddInput = function(self, idx, o) return makeInput(self, idx, o or {}) end
gb.AddButton = function(self, o) local b=makeButton(self, o or {}); self._owner=b._feature; self._cfgOwner=b._feature; return b end
gb.AddLabel = function(self, text, wrap) local l=makeLabel(self, text, wrap); self._owner=l._feature; return l end
gb.AddColorPicker = function(self, idx, o) if self._owner then return makeColorPicker(idx, o or {}, self._owner) end end
gb.AddKeyPicker = function(self, idx, o) if self._owner then local kp=makeKeyPicker(idx, o or {}, self._owner, self._owner.obj); refreshDots(self._owner); return kp end end
gb.AddDivider = function(self) end
gb.AddItem = function(self, item) if typeof(item)=="Instance" then item.Parent=column.body end end
gb.Resize = function(self) if column.resize then column.resize() end end
return gb
end

local B64 = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local function b64decode(str)
local map = {}
for i = 1, 64 do map[B64:byte(i)] = i - 1 end
local out, acc, bits = {}, 0, 0
for i = 1, #str do
local v = map[str:byte(i)]
if v then
acc = acc * 64 + v; bits = bits + 6
if bits >= 8 then
bits = bits - 8
out[#out+1] = string.char(math.floor(acc / 2^bits))
acc = acc % 2^bits
end
end
end
return table.concat(out)
end
local iconIds = {}
local function iconAsset(name)
if iconIds[name] ~= nil then return iconIds[name] or nil end
iconIds[name] = false
local data = ICON_PNG[name]
if not (data and typeof(getcustomasset)=="function" and typeof(writefile)=="function") then return nil end
local ok, id = pcall(function()
if typeof(isfolder)=="function" and typeof(makefolder)=="function" then
if not isfolder("Rake") then makefolder("Rake") end
if not isfolder("Rake/icons") then makefolder("Rake/icons") end
end
local path = "Rake/icons/" .. ICON_VER .. "-" .. name .. ".png"
if not (typeof(isfile)=="function" and isfile(path)) then writefile(path, b64decode(data)) end
return getcustomasset(path)
end)
if ok and type(id)=="string" and id ~= "" then iconIds[name] = id; return id end
return nil
end
Fav.icon = iconAsset
local ICON = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
local function ipart(parent, w, h, ox, oy, rad, rot, outline)
local f = new("Frame", { Parent=parent, AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
Position=UDim2.new(0.5,ox,0.5,oy), Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w,h), Rotation=rot or 0,
BackgroundColor3=ICON, BackgroundTransparency = outline and 1 or 0, ZIndex=8 })
if rad then corner(rad, f) end
if outline then stroke(ICON, 0, f, outline) end
return f
end
local function drawGlyph(box, name)
local function d(...) return ipart(box, ...) end
if name=="person-standing" then
d(6,6,0,-5,999); d(12,9,0,5,5)
elseif name=="eye" then
d(20,12,0,0,999,0,1.4); d(6.5,6.5,0,0,999)
elseif name=="zap" then
d(16,16,0,0,999,0,1.4); d(3,3,0,0,999)
d(3,1.4,-9,0,1); d(3,1.4,9,0,1); d(1.4,3,0,-9,1); d(1.4,3,0,9,1)
elseif name=="bug" then
d(9,11,0,2,5,0,1.4); d(6,6,0,-5,999)
d(4,1.4,-6,0,1,30); d(4,1.4,6,0,1,-30); d(4,1.4,-6,4,1,-30); d(4,1.4,6,4,1,30)
elseif name=="map-pin" then
d(13,13,0,-3,999,0,1.4); d(4,4,0,-3,999); d(6,6,0,5,2,45)
elseif name=="palette" then
d(19,18,0,1,999,0,1.4); d(2.6,2.6,-5,-3,999); d(2.6,2.6,0,-5,999); d(2.6,2.6,5,-2,999)
elseif name=="toggle-left" then
d(20,12,0,0,6,0,1.4); d(7,7,-5,0,999)
elseif name=="sliders-horizontal" then
d(18,1.6,0,-6,1); d(6,6,4,-6,999); d(18,1.6,0,0,1); d(6,6,-4,0,999); d(18,1.6,0,6,1); d(6,6,3,6,999)
elseif name=="package" then
d(16,16,0,1,3,0,1.4); d(16,1.6,0,-6,1); d(1.6,16,0,1,1)
elseif name=="terminal" then
d(20,15,0,0,3,0,1.4); d(3,1.5,-5,-2,1,40); d(3,1.5,-5,1.5,1,-40); d(6,1.5,3,4,1)
elseif name=="settings" then
d(12,12,0,0,999,0,1.5); d(4.5,4.5,0,0,999)
d(3,3,0,-7.5,1); d(3,3,0,7.5,1); d(3,3,-7.5,0,1); d(3,3,7.5,0,1)
d(3,3,-5.5,-5.5,1,45); d(3,3,5.5,5.5,1,45); d(3,3,-5.5,5.5,1,45); d(3,3,5.5,-5.5,1,45)
else
txt({ Parent=box, Text=(name or "?"):sub(1,1):upper(), FontFace=FF.bold, TextSize=13, TextColor3=ICON,
Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1), TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex=8 })
end
end
local ICON_TILE = 22
local function makeIcon(parent, name)
local box = new("Frame", { Parent=parent, BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1), Size=UDim2.fromOffset(ICON_TILE,ICON_TILE),
Position=UDim2.fromOffset(9,(COL_HEADER-ICON_TILE)/2), ZIndex=6 })
corner(7, box)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=box, Rotation=90,
Color=ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(74,80,96), Color3.fromRGB(28,32,42)) })
stroke(T.Rim, 0.6, box, 1)
local shine = new("Frame", { Parent=box, BackgroundColor3=T.Film, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0.5,0), ZIndex=7 })
corner(7, shine)
new("UIGradient", { Parent=shine, Rotation=90, Transparency=NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.78), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1) }) })
local glyph = new("Frame", { Parent=box, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20,20),
AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5), Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5), ZIndex=8 })
local aid = iconAsset(name)
if aid then
local img = new("ImageLabel", { Parent=glyph, BackgroundTransparency=1, Image=aid, ImageColor3=ICON,
Size=UDim2.fromOffset(15,15), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5), Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5),
ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit, ZIndex=9 })
task.delay(4, function()
local loaded = false
pcall(function() loaded = img.IsLoaded end)
if img.Parent and not loaded then
pcall(function() img:Destroy() end)
new("UIScale", { Parent=glyph, Scale=0.7 }); drawGlyph(glyph, name)
end
end)
else
new("UIScale", { Parent=glyph, Scale=0.7 }); drawGlyph(glyph, name)
end
return box
end
local columns = {}
local colPos = {}
local function saveColPos()
local lines={}
for _,c in ipairs(columns) do
local pp = c.frame.Position
lines[#lines+1] = c.name.."|"..math.floor(pp.X.Offset)..","..math.floor(pp.Y.Offset)
end
pcall(function() if typeof(writefile)=="function" then writefile("Rake/colpos_v3.txt", table.concat(lines,"\n")) end end)
end
function Library:ResetLayout()
local vp = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(1280,720)
local vpx = (vp.X and vp.X > 0) and vp.X or 1280
local vpy = (vp.Y and vp.Y > 0) and vp.Y or 720
local dps = (Library.DPIScale and Library.DPIScale > 0) and Library.DPIScale or 1
vpx, vpy = vpx / dps, vpy / dps
local colw = math.clamp(math.floor((vpx - 24 - 10*COL_GAP) / 11), 150, 225)
local perRow = math.max(1, math.floor((vpx - 24) / (colw + COL_GAP)))
for idx, c in ipairs(columns) do
local i = idx - 1
local rr, cc = math.floor(i / perRow), i % perRow
colPos[c.name] = nil
if c.frame then tween(c.frame, 0.25, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12 + cc*(colw + COL_GAP), 12 + rr*math.floor(vpy * 0.5)) }) end
end
pcall(function() if typeof(delfile)=="function" and typeof(isfile)=="function" and isfile("Rake/colpos_v3.txt") then delfile("Rake/colpos_v3.txt") end end)
end
local blurEffect
Library.BlurBackground, Library.BlurSize, Library._blurWhy = true, 14, {}
function Library:Blur(why, on)
self._blurWhy[why] = on and true or nil
local want = (self.BlurBackground and next(self._blurWhy) ~= nil) and self.BlurSize or 0
pcall(function()
if want > 0 and not (blurEffect and blurEffect.Parent) then
blurEffect = new("BlurEffect", { Name = "RuvRillLibBlur", Size = 0, Parent = game:GetService("Lighting") })
end
if blurEffect then tween(blurEffect, EASE.open, { Size = want }) end
end)
end
local function setBlur(on)
Library:Blur("menu", on)
end
local function makeColumn(name, icon)
local idx = #columns
local vp = (workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize) or Vector2.new(1280,720)
local vpx = (vp.X and vp.X > 0) and vp.X or 1280
local vpy = (vp.Y and vp.Y > 0) and vp.Y or 720
local dps = (Library.DPIScale and Library.DPIScale > 0) and Library.DPIScale or 1
vpx, vpy = vpx / dps, vpy / dps
local colw = math.clamp(math.floor((vpx - 24 - 10*COL_GAP) / 11), 150, 225)
local maxBodyH = math.clamp(math.floor(vpy - 118), 300, 780)
local perRow = math.max(1, math.floor((vpx - 24) / (colw + COL_GAP)))
local rr, cc = math.floor(idx / perRow), idx % perRow
local defX = 12 + cc*(colw + COL_GAP)
local defY = 12 + rr*math.floor(vpy * 0.5)
local sp = colPos[name]
local col = new("Frame", { Parent=mainFrame, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(colw, COL_HEADER+40),
Position=UDim2.fromOffset(sp and sp.x or defX, sp and sp.y or defY), ZIndex=3 })
local glassBody, glassShadow = liquid(col, R.column, { gloss=UDim2.new(1,0,0,64) })
reg(glassBody, "BackgroundColor3", "Column")
local uiscale = new("UIScale", { Parent=col, Scale=1 })
local header = new("TextButton", { Parent=col, BackgroundTransparency=1, Text="", AutoButtonColor=false,
Active=true, Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,COL_HEADER), Position=UDim2.new(), ZIndex=5 })
makeIcon(header, icon)
txt({ Parent=header, Text=name, FontFace=FF.med, TextSize=14, TextColor3=T.Text,
Size=UDim2.new(1,-46,1,0), Position=UDim2.fromOffset(9+ICON_TILE+8,0), ZIndex=6 })
local body = new("ScrollingFrame", { Parent=col, BackgroundTransparency=1, Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,COL_HEADER),
Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,40), CanvasSize=UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
ScrollBarThickness=2, ScrollBarImageColor3=T.Sub, ScrollBarImageTransparency=0.6,
ScrollingDirection=Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ZIndex=4 })
pad(body, 2,8,7,7)
local blayout = vlist(body, 2)
local function resize()
local c = (blayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y or 0) + 12
local hh = math.clamp(c, 12, maxBodyH)
body.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,hh)
col.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(colw, COL_HEADER + hh)
end
track(blayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(resize))
task.defer(resize)
local dragging, dragStart, startOff, dragTarget, dragTween
track(header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging=true; dragStart=input.Position; startOff=Vector2.new(col.Position.X.Offset, col.Position.Y.Offset)
dragTarget = nil
col.ZIndex = 50
tween(uiscale, EASE.snap, { Scale=0.975 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
tween(glassBody, EASE.snap, { BackgroundTransparency = math.max(0, TR.column-0.1) })
tween(glassShadow, EASE.snap, { ImageTransparency = math.max(0, TR.shadow-0.2) })
end
end))
track(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and (input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
local dd = (input.Position - dragStart) / ((Library.DPIScale and Library.DPIScale > 0) and Library.DPIScale or 1)
dragTarget = UDim2.fromOffset(startOff.X + dd.X, startOff.Y + dd.Y)
dragTween = tween(col, 0.07, { Position = dragTarget })
end
end))
track(UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and (input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
dragging=false; col.ZIndex = 3
if dragTween then pcall(function() dragTween:Cancel() end); dragTween = nil end
if dragTarget then col.Position = dragTarget end
saveColPos()
tween(uiscale, EASE.move, { Scale=1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
tween(glassBody, EASE.move, { BackgroundTransparency = TR.column })
tween(glassShadow, EASE.move, { ImageTransparency = TR.shadow })
end
end))
local column = { frame=col, body=body, toggles={}, boxIndex=0, name=name, rows={}, resize=resize, scale=uiscale }
column.noSort = (name == "Settings")
column.updateCount = function() end
column.sortRows = function()
local function rk(r) return ((r.key or (r.label and r.label.Text)) or ""):lower() end
if not column.noSort then
local favs = Fav.set()
local function grp(r) return r.last and 2 or ((r.fav and favs[r.fav]) and 0 or 1) end
table.sort(column.rows, function(a,b)
local ga, gb = grp(a), grp(b)
if ga ~= gb then return ga < gb end
return rk(a) < rk(b)
end)
end
for i,r in ipairs(column.rows) do r.row.LayoutOrder = i end
resize()
end
task.defer(column.sortRows)
task.delay(0.4, column.sortRows)
columns[#columns+1] = column
return column
end
local function makeTab(name, icon)
local column = makeColumn(name, icon)
local tab = {}
tab.AddLeftGroupbox = function(self, title) column.boxIndex = column.boxIndex + 1; return makeGroupbox(column, title, "L", column.boxIndex) end
tab.AddRightGroupbox = function(self, title) column.boxIndex = column.boxIndex + 1; return makeGroupbox(column, title, "R", column.boxIndex) end
tab.AddLeftTabbox = tab.AddLeftGroupbox
tab.AddRightTabbox = tab.AddRightGroupbox
tab._column = column
return tab
end
local toggleSeq = 0
local searchScale
local function animateMenu(show)
toggleSeq = toggleSeq + 1
local seq = toggleSeq
if not mainFrame then return end
if show then
mainFrame.Visible = true
for i, c in ipairs(columns) do
if c.scale then
c.scale.Scale = 0.92
task.delay((i-1)*0.02, function()
if toggleSeq == seq then tween(c.scale, EASE.open, { Scale=1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint) end
end)
end
end
if searchScale then searchScale.Scale = 0.9; tween(searchScale, EASE.open, { Scale=1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint) end
else
for _, c in ipairs(columns) do
if c.scale then tween(c.scale, EASE.snap, { Scale=0.95 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
end
if searchScale then tween(searchScale, EASE.snap, { Scale=0.9 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
task.delay(EASE.snap, function()
if toggleSeq == seq and mainFrame and not Library.Toggled then mainFrame.Visible = false end
end)
end
end
function Library:Toggle(force)
local show = force; if show==nil then show = not self.Toggled end
self.Toggled = show
if not show then closePopup() end
animateMenu(show)
setBlur(show)
if self._orbPaint then pcall(self._orbPaint, show) end
if self._onToggle then pcall(self._onToggle, show) end
end
function Library:SetMenuOrb(on)
if self.MenuOrb then self.MenuOrb.Visible = on and true or false end
end
function Library:CreateWindow(opts) opts = opts or {}; opts.AutoShow = false;
opts = opts or {}
self.ToggleKeybind = opts.ToggleKeybind or Enum.KeyCode.RightShift
local parent = (typeof(gethui)=="function" and gethui()) or LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or game:GetService("CoreGui")
screenGui = new("ScreenGui", { Enabled = false, ResetOnSpawn = false }, { Name="RuvRillLib", IgnoreGuiInset=true, ResetOnSpawn=false,
ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, DisplayOrder=999999, Parent=parent })
self.ScreenGui = screenGui
track(RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if Library.Toggled then
pcall(function() UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default end)
pcall(function() if not UIS.MouseIconEnabled then UIS.MouseIconEnabled = true end end)
end
end))
local ds = (self.DPIScale and self.DPIScale > 0) and self.DPIScale or 1
mainFrame = new("Frame", { Parent=screenGui, Name="Main", BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.fromScale(1/ds,1/ds), ZIndex=2 })
new("UIScale", { Parent=mainFrame, Scale=self.DPIScale })
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfile)=="function" and isfile("Rake/colpos_v3.txt") and typeof(readfile)=="function" then
for line in tostring(readfile("Rake/colpos_v3.txt")):gmatch("[^\n]+") do
local nm,x,y = line:match("^(.-)|(-?%d+),(-?%d+)$")
if nm then colPos[nm] = { x=tonumber(x), y=tonumber(y) } end
end
end
end)
popupLayer = new("Frame", { Parent=screenGui, Name="PopupLayer", BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1), ZIndex=5000 })
track(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if not currentPopup then return end
local t = input.UserInputType
if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
local p = guiPoint(input)
local slack = 2
pcall(function() slack = 2 + math.abs(game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset().Y) end)
local function inside(g)
if not (g and g.Parent) then return false end
local a, sz = g.AbsolutePosition, g.AbsoluteSize
return p.X >= a.X - 2 and p.X <= a.X + sz.X + 2 and p.Y >= a.Y - slack and p.Y <= a.Y + sz.Y + slack
end
if inside(currentPopup) then return end
if currentFeature and inside(currentFeature.dots) then return end
closePopup()
end))
notifyHolder = new("Frame", { Parent=screenGui, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.new(0,NOTE_W+10,1,-24),
Position=UDim2.new(1,-14,0,14), AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0), ZIndex=6000 })
if opts.ShowMobileButtons then
local ORB = 54
local orb = new("TextButton", { Parent=screenGui, Name="MenuOrb", Size=UDim2.fromOffset(ORB,ORB),
Position=UDim2.new(1,-(ORB+14),0.4,0), Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, Active=true,
ZIndex=7000, Visible=self.IsMobile and true or false })
local body = liquid(orb, 999, { fill=T.Popup, transp=TR.popup, gloss=UDim2.new(1,0,0.55,0) })
local oscale = new("UIScale", { Parent=orb, Scale=1 })
local bars = {}
for i = 1, 3 do
local b = new("Frame", { Parent=orb, BackgroundColor3=T.Accent, AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),
Size=UDim2.fromOffset(20,2.6), Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,(i-2)*6.5), ZIndex=7003 })
corner(999, b)
bars[i] = b
end
local function paint(open)
local col = open and Color3.new(1,1,1) or T.Accent
tween(body, EASE.move, { BackgroundColor3 = open and T.Accent or T.Popup,
BackgroundTransparency = open and 0.1 or TR.popup })
tween(bars[1], EASE.move, { Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,open and 0 or -6.5),
Rotation=open and 45 or 0, BackgroundColor3=col }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
tween(bars[2], EASE.move, { Size=UDim2.fromOffset(open and 2 or 20, 2.6),
BackgroundTransparency=open and 1 or 0, BackgroundColor3=col }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
tween(bars[3], EASE.move, { Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,open and 0 or 6.5),
Rotation=open and -45 or 0, BackgroundColor3=col }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
end
self._orbPaint = paint
self.MenuOrb = orb
paint(self.Toggled)
task.delay(0.2, function()
pcall(function()
if not (typeof(isfile)=="function" and typeof(readfile)=="function" and isfile("Rake/menuorb.txt")) then return end
local x, y = tostring(readfile("Rake/menuorb.txt")):match("^%s*(%-?[%d%.]+)%s+(%-?[%d%.]+)")
x, y = tonumber(x), tonumber(y)
local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
if x and y and vp.X > 0 then
orb.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(x, 4, math.max(4, vp.X-ORB-4)),
math.clamp(y, 4, math.max(4, vp.Y-ORB-4)))
end
end)
end)
local press
track(orb.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
local t = input.UserInputType
if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
press = { input=input, touch=(t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch), start=input.Position,
base=orb.AbsolutePosition - screenGui.AbsolutePosition, moved=false }
tween(oscale, EASE.snap, { Scale=0.9 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
end))
track(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if not press then return end
local mine = (press.touch and input == press.input)
or (not press.touch and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement)
if not mine then return end
local d = input.Position - press.start
if not press.moved and math.abs(d.X) + math.abs(d.Y) > 8 then press.moved = true end
if press.moved then
local vp = screenGui.AbsoluteSize
orb.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(press.base.X + d.X, 4, math.max(4, vp.X-ORB-4)),
math.clamp(press.base.Y + d.Y, 4, math.max(4, vp.Y-ORB-4)))
end
end))
track(UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if not press then return end
local mine = (press.touch and input == press.input)
or (not press.touch and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
if not mine then return end
local p = press; press = nil
tween(oscale, EASE.move, { Scale=1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
if p.moved then
pcall(function()
if typeof(writefile)=="function" then
writefile("Rake/menuorb.txt", string.format("%d %d", orb.Position.X.Offset, orb.Position.Y.Offset))
end
end)
else
Library:Toggle()
end
end))
end

corner(999, magRing); stroke(T.Sub, 0.1, magRing, 1.6)

if magImg then tween(magImg, EASE.move, { ImageColor3=T.Sub, ImageTransparency=0.05 }) end
end))
local fold, colShown = {}, {}
local function setRowShown(r, show)
local row = r.row
local st = fold[row]
if not st then st = { open = true, seq = 0 }; fold[row] = st end
if st.open == show then return end
st.open = show
st.seq = st.seq + 1
local seq = st.seq
if not show then
local sc = (Library.DPIScale and Library.DPIScale > 0) and Library.DPIScale or 1
if row.AutomaticSize == Enum.AutomaticSize.Y then st.auto = true end
if st.rest ~= false then
local h = st.auto and math.floor(row.AbsoluteSize.Y / sc + 0.5) or row.Size.Y.Offset
if h and h > 0 then st.h = h end
end
st.rest = false
row.ClipsDescendants = true
row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, st.h or ROW_H)
tween(row, EASE.move, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
if r.label then tween(r.label, EASE.snap, { TextTransparency = 1 }) end
task.delay(EASE.move, function() if st.seq == seq then row.Visible = false end end)
else
row.Visible = true
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
tween(row, EASE.move, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, st.h or ROW_H) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
if r.label then tween(r.label, EASE.move, { TextTransparency = 0 }) end
task.delay(EASE.move, function()
if st.seq ~= seq then return end
st.rest = true
if st.auto then
row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
end
end)
end
end
local function setColShown(col, show)
if (colShown[col] ~= false) == show then return end
colShown[col] = show
if show then
col.frame.Visible = true
if col.scale then col.scale.Scale = 0.94; tween(col.scale, EASE.move, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint) end
else
if col.scale then tween(col.scale, EASE.move, { Scale = 0.94 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
task.delay(EASE.move, function() if colShown[col] == false then col.frame.Visible = false end end)
end
end
local function runSearch(q)
q = tostring(q or ""):lower()
for _, col in ipairs(columns) do
local colVis = false
for _, r in ipairs(col.rows or {}) do
local nm = (r.key or (r.label and r.label.Text)) or ""
local m = (q=="") or (string.find(nm:lower(), q, 1, true) ~= nil)
setRowShown(r, m and true or false)
if m then colVis = true end
end
setColShown(col, (q=="") or colVis)
end
end
track(sbox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() runSearch(sbox.Text) end))
track(sbox.Focused:Connect(function()
tween(searchBar, EASE.move, { Size=UDim2.fromOffset(380,36) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
tween(searchBody, EASE.move, { BackgroundTransparency=math.max(0, TR.popup-0.1) })
end))
track(sbox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
tween(searchBar, EASE.move, { Size=UDim2.fromOffset(340,36) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
tween(searchBody, EASE.move, { BackgroundTransparency=TR.popup })
end))
local Window = {}
function Window:AddTab(name, icon) return makeTab(name, icon) end
if opts.AutoShow ~= false then
Library.Toggled = true; mainFrame.Visible = true; setBlur(true)
task.defer(function() if Library.Toggled then animateMenu(true) end end)
end
return Window
end
local function resolveKey()
local tk = Library.ToggleKeybind
if typeof(tk)=="EnumItem" then return tk end
if type(tk)=="table" and tk.Value then local k = safeKey(tk.Value); if k then return k end end
return Enum.KeyCode.RightShift
end
track(UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
if gpe then return end
if capturingKey then return end
if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
if input.KeyCode == resolveKey() then Library:Toggle() end
for _, kp in ipairs(keyPickers) do
local kc = safeKey(kp.Value)
if kc and kc == input.KeyCode then
if kp.Mode=="Toggle" then
kp.Toggled = not kp.Toggled
if kp.SyncToggleState and kp._parent and kp._parent.SetValue then kp._parent:SetValue(not kp._parent.Value) end
elseif kp.Mode=="Press" then
if kp._press then pcall(kp._press) elseif kp._parent and kp._parent.SetValue then kp._parent:SetValue(not kp._parent.Value) end
elseif kp.Mode=="Hold" then
kp.Toggled = true
if kp.SyncToggleState and kp._parent and kp._parent.SetValue then kp._parent:SetValue(true) end
end
end
end
end
end))
track(UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
for _, kp in ipairs(keyPickers) do
if kp.Mode=="Hold" and safeKey(kp.Value)==input.KeyCode then
kp.Toggled = false
if kp.SyncToggleState and kp._parent and kp._parent.SetValue then kp._parent:SetValue(false) end
end
end
end
end))
function Library:KeyConflicts()
local byKey, out = {}, {}
for _, kp in ipairs(keyPickers) do
kp._conflict = nil
if kp.Mode ~= "Always" and safeKey(kp.Value) then
byKey[kp.Value] = byKey[kp.Value] or {}
table.insert(byKey[kp.Value], kp)
end
end
for key, l in pairs(byKey) do
if #l > 1 then
for _, kp in ipairs(l) do kp._conflict = true end
out[#out+1] = { key = key, binds = l }
end
end
table.sort(out, function(a, b) return a.key < b.key end)
for _, kp in ipairs(keyPickers) do if kp._widgetDisplay then pcall(kp._widgetDisplay) end end
return out
end
function Library:KeyName(kp)
return tostring(kp.Text or (kp._parent and kp._parent.Text) or kp.Idx or "a bind")
end
function Library:KeyClash(kp)
self:KeyConflicts()
if not kp._conflict then return end
local others = {}
for _, o in ipairs(keyPickers) do
if o ~= kp and o._conflict and o.Value == kp.Value then others[#others+1] = self:KeyName(o) end
end
self:Notify({ Title = "Keybind clash", Description = tostring(kp.Value) .. " is also " .. table.concat(others, ", "), Time = 5 })
end
function Library:CheckKeys(quiet)
local list = self:KeyConflicts()
if #list == 0 then
if not quiet then self:Notify({ Title = "Keybinds", Description = "no clashes", Time = 3 }) end
return list
end
local bits = {}
for _, c in ipairs(list) do
local names = {}
for _, o in ipairs(c.binds) do names[#names+1] = self:KeyName(o) end
bits[#bits+1] = c.key .. ": " .. table.concat(names, " + ")
end
self:Notify({ Title = #list .. " keybind clash" .. (#list == 1 and "" or "es"), Description = table.concat(bits, "  |  "), Time = 8 })
return list
end
function Library:SetDPIScale(pct)
self.DPIScale = (pct or 100)/100
if mainFrame then
local s=mainFrame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale"); if s then s.Scale = self.DPIScale end
mainFrame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1/self.DPIScale, 1/self.DPIScale)
end
end
Library.FONTS = {
Nunito = "Nunito", Montserrat = "Montserrat", ["Source Sans"] = "SourceSansPro", Roboto = "Roboto",
Ubuntu = "Ubuntu", ["Builder Sans"] = "BuilderSans", Arial = "Arimo", Oswald = "Oswald",
Titillium = "TitilliumWeb", Michroma = "Michroma", Fredoka = "FredokaOne", Jura = "Jura",
["Roboto Mono"] = "RobotoMono", Zekton = "Zekton",
}
Library.FONT_NAMES = { "Nunito", "Montserrat", "Source Sans", "Roboto", "Ubuntu", "Builder Sans", "Arial",
"Oswald", "Titillium", "Michroma", "Fredoka", "Jura", "Roboto Mono", "Zekton" }
function Library:SetFont(name)
local fam = type(name) == "string" and Library.FONTS[name]
if not fam then self.Font = name return end
local path = "rbxasset://fonts/families/" .. fam .. ".json"
self.FontName = name
FF.thin = Font.new(path, Enum.FontWeight.Light)
FF.med  = Font.new(path, Enum.FontWeight.Regular)
FF.bold = Font.new(path, Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold)
if not screenGui then return end
for _, d in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
pcall(function()
local old = d.FontFace
d.FontFace = Font.new(path, old.Weight, old.Style)
end)
end
end
end
function Library:UpdateColorsUsingRegistry()
local map = { AccentColor="Accent", MainColor="Column", BackgroundColor="Base", OutlineColor="Edge", FontColor="Text" }
for k, tok in pairs(map) do if self.Scheme[k] then T[tok] = self.Scheme[k] end end
for _, e in ipairs(self._registry) do
local col = T[e.token]
if col and e.inst and e.inst.Parent then pcall(function() e.inst[e.prop] = col end) end
end
end
function Library:Unload()
self.Unloaded = true
pcall(closePopup)
for _, fn in ipairs(self._unload) do pcall(fn) end
for _, c in ipairs(self.Connections) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
pcall(function() if blurEffect then blurEffect:Destroy() end end)
pcall(function() if screenGui then screenGui:Destroy() end end)
for k in pairs(self.Options) do self.Options[k]=nil end
for k in pairs(self.Toggles) do self.Toggles[k]=nil end
end
Library.SetWatermarkVisibility = function() end
Library.SetWatermark = function() end
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local SaveManager = { Folder="Rake", SubFolder="", Ignore={} }
local THEME_IDX = { BackgroundColor=true, MainColor=true, AccentColor=true, OutlineColor=true, FontColor=true, FontFace=true }
local SELF_IDX  = { SaveManager_ConfigName=true, SaveManager_ConfigList=true, SaveManager_ShareCode=true }
function SaveManager:_skip(idx) return self.Ignore[idx] or SELF_IDX[idx] or (self.IgnoreTheme and THEME_IDX[idx]) end
function SaveManager:SetLibrary(l) self.Library=l end
function SaveManager:SetFolder(f) self.Folder=f; pcall(function() if typeof(makefolder)=="function" and typeof(isfolder)=="function" and not isfolder(f) then makefolder(f) end end) end
function SaveManager:SetSubFolder(f) self.SubFolder=f end
function SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings() self.IgnoreTheme=true end
function SaveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes(list) for _,i in ipairs(list or {}) do self.Ignore[i]=true end end
function SaveManager:_dir() return self.Folder end
function SaveManager:_configs() return self:_dir() end
local function fileWrite(p, s) pcall(function() if typeof(writefile)=="function" then writefile(p, s) end end) end
local function fileRead(p) local ok, r = pcall(function() if typeof(isfile)=="function" and isfile(p) and typeof(readfile)=="function" then return readfile(p) end end); return ok and r or nil end
function SaveManager:Save(name)
if not name or name=="" then return false, "no name" end
local data = { objects = {} }
for idx, t in pairs(Toggles) do if not self:_skip(idx) then data.objects[idx] = { type="Toggle", value=t.Value } end end
for idx, o in pairs(Options) do
if not self:_skip(idx) then
if o.Type=="ColorPicker" then data.objects[idx] = { type="ColorPicker", r=o.Value.R, g=o.Value.G, b=o.Value.B }
elseif o.Type=="KeyPicker" then data.objects[idx] = { type="KeyPicker", value=o.Value, mode=o.Mode }
elseif o.Type then data.objects[idx] = { type=o.Type, value=o.Value } end
end
end
local ok, enc = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(data) end)
if ok then fileWrite(self:_configs().."/"..name..".json", enc) end
return true
end
function SaveManager:Load(name)
local raw = fileRead(self:_configs().."/"..name..".json")
if not raw then return false, "not found" end
local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
if not (ok and data and data.objects) then return false, "corrupt" end
Library.Loading = true
for idx, rec in pairs(data.objects) do
if not self:_skip(idx) then
local o = Options[idx] or Toggles[idx]
if o then pcall(function()
if rec.type=="ColorPicker" and o.SetValueRGB then o:SetValueRGB(Color3.new(rec.r, rec.g, rec.b))
elseif rec.type=="KeyPicker" then if rec.mode then o.Mode=rec.mode end; if o.SetValue then o:SetValue(rec.value) end
elseif o.SetValue then o:SetValue(rec.value) end
end) end
end
end
Library.Loading = false
pcall(function() Library:KeyConflicts() end)
return true
end
function SaveManager:RefreshConfigList()
local out = {}
pcall(function()
if typeof(listfiles)=="function" then
for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(self:_configs())) do
local nm = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
if nm then out[#out+1] = nm end
end
end
end)
return out
end
function SaveManager:LoadAutoloadConfig()
local raw = fileRead(self:_configs().."/autoload.txt")
if raw and raw~="" then self:Load(raw) end
end
function SaveManager:SaveAutoloadConfig(name) fileWrite(self:_configs().."/autoload.txt", name or "") end
local Code = { B62 = "0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ",
MODES = { "Toggle", "Hold", "Always", "Press" },
TAG = { Slider = "#", Dropdown = "=", ColorPicker = "$", KeyPicker = "@", Input = "'" },
KIND = { ["#"] = true, ["="] = true, ["*"] = true, ["$"] = true, ["@"] = true, ["'"] = true } }
SaveManager.NoShare = {}
function SaveManager:SetNoShare(list) for _, i in ipairs(list or {}) do self.NoShare[i] = true end end
function Code.enc(n)
n = math.max(0, math.floor((tonumber(n) or 0) + 0.5))
local s = ""
repeat
local d = n % 62
s = Code.B62:sub(d + 1, d + 1) .. s
n = (n - d) / 62
until n == 0
return s
end
function Code.dec(s)
if not s or s == "" then return nil end
local n = 0
for i = 1, #s do
local d = Code.B62:find(s:sub(i, i), 1, true)
if not d then return nil end
n = n * 62 + d - 1
end
return n
end
function Code.id(idx, salt)
local h = 2166136261
local s = Code.B62:sub(salt + 1, salt + 1) .. idx
for i = 1, #s do
h = bit32.bxor(h, s:byte(i))
h = (bit32.lshift(h, 24) + h * 403) % 4294967296
end
local e = Code.enc(h % 238328)
return string.rep("0", 3 - #e) .. e
end
function Code.keyName(n)
if n == 0 then return "None" end
if not Code.keys then
Code.keys = {}
pcall(function() for _, k in ipairs(Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems()) do Code.keys[k.Value] = k.Name end end)
end
return Code.keys[n] or "None"
end
function Code.pay(o)
local t = o.Type
if t == "Toggle" or t == "SubToggle" then return o.Value and "1" or "0"
elseif t == "Slider" then return Code.enc(((tonumber(o.Value) or o.Min) - o.Min) * 10 ^ (o.Rounding or 0))
elseif t == "Dropdown" then
local vals = o.Values or {}
if o.Multi then
if #vals > 40 then return nil end
local m = 0
for i, v in ipairs(vals) do if type(o.Value) == "table" and o.Value[v] then m = m + 2 ^ (i - 1) end end
return Code.enc(m)
end
if o.Value == nil then return "0" end
for i, v in ipairs(vals) do if v == o.Value then return Code.enc(i) end end
return nil
elseif t == "ColorPicker" then
local c = o.Value
if not c then return nil end
return Code.enc(math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5) * 65536 + math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5) * 256 + math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
elseif t == "KeyPicker" then
local k, m = safeKey(o.Value), 1
for i, name in ipairs(Code.MODES) do if name == o.Mode then m = i end end
return Code.enc((k and k.Value or 0) * 4 + m - 1)
elseif t == "Input" then
local v = tostring(o.Value or "")
local pre = v:sub(1, 13) == "rbxassetid://" and "_r" or ""
if pre ~= "" then v = v:sub(14) end
return pre .. (v:gsub("[^%w]", function(ch) return string.format("_%02X", ch:byte()) end))
end
return nil
end
function Code.apply(o, p)
local t = o.Type
if t == "Toggle" or t == "SubToggle" then o:SetValue(p == "1") return end
if t == "Input" then
local pre = p:sub(1, 2) == "_r" and "rbxassetid://" or ""
if pre ~= "" then p = p:sub(3) end
o:SetValue(pre .. (p:gsub("_(%x%x)", function(h) return string.char(tonumber(h, 16)) end)))
return
end
local k = Code.dec(p)
if not k then return end
if t == "Slider" then o:SetValue(o.Min + k / 10 ^ (o.Rounding or 0))
elseif t == "Dropdown" then
local vals = o.Values or {}
if o.Multi then
local set = {}
for i, v in ipairs(vals) do if math.floor(k / 2 ^ (i - 1)) % 2 == 1 then set[v] = true end end
o:SetValue(set)
elseif k == 0 then
if o.AllowNull then o:SetValue(nil) end
elseif vals[k] ~= nil then
o:SetValue(vals[k])
end
elseif t == "ColorPicker" then
local c = Color3.fromRGB(math.floor(k / 65536) % 256, math.floor(k / 256) % 256, k % 256)
if o.SetValueRGB then o:SetValueRGB(c) else o:SetValue(c) end
elseif t == "KeyPicker" then
o.Mode = Code.MODES[k % 4 + 1]
o:SetValue(Code.keyName(math.floor(k / 4)))
end
end
function SaveManager:_shareable()
local out = {}
for idx, t in pairs(Toggles) do
if (t.Type == "Toggle" or t.Type == "SubToggle") and not (self:_skip(idx) or self.NoShare[idx]) then out[#out+1] = idx end
end
for idx, o in pairs(Options) do
if Code.TAG[o.Type] and not o.SpecialType and not (self:_skip(idx) or self.NoShare[idx]) then out[#out+1] = idx end
end
table.sort(out)
return out
end
function SaveManager:SnapDefaults()
self._def = {}
for _, idx in ipairs(self:_shareable()) do
local o = Toggles[idx] or Options[idx]
local ok, p = pcall(Code.pay, o)
if ok then self._def[idx] = p end
end
end
function SaveManager:ExportCode()
local list = self:_shareable()
local ids, salt
for sl = 0, 61 do
local seen, clash = {}, false
ids = {}
for _, idx in ipairs(list) do
local id = Code.id(idx, sl)
if seen[id] then clash = true break end
seen[id] = true; ids[idx] = id
end
if not clash then salt = sl break end
end
if not salt then return nil, "id clash" end
local def = self._def or {}
local on, off, rest = {}, {}, {}
for _, idx in ipairs(list) do
local o = Toggles[idx] or Options[idx]
local ok, p = pcall(Code.pay, o)
if ok and p and p ~= def[idx] then
if o.Type == "Toggle" or o.Type == "SubToggle" then
if p == "1" then on[#on+1] = ids[idx] else off[#off+1] = ids[idx] end
else
rest[#rest+1] = ((o.Type == "Dropdown" and o.Multi) and "*" or Code.TAG[o.Type]) .. ids[idx] .. p
end
end
end
return "NW" .. (salt > 0 and Code.B62:sub(salt + 1, salt + 1) or "")
.. (#on > 0 and ("+" .. table.concat(on)) or "") .. (#off > 0 and ("-" .. table.concat(off)) or "")
.. table.concat(rest)
end
function SaveManager:ImportCode(code)
code = tostring(code or ""):gsub("%s", "")
if code:sub(1, 2) ~= "NW" then return false, "not a config code" end
local i, salt = 3, 0
local c3 = code:sub(3, 3)
if c3:match("^%w$") then salt = Code.dec(c3); i = 4 end
local list = self:_shareable()
local byId = {}
for _, idx in ipairs(list) do byId[Code.id(idx, salt)] = idx end
local want, unknown = {}, 0
while i <= #code do
local tag = code:sub(i, i)
i = i + 1
if tag == "+" or tag == "-" then
while code:sub(i, i + 2):match("^%w%w%w$") do
local idx = byId[code:sub(i, i + 2)]
if idx and Toggles[idx] then want[idx] = (tag == "+") and "1" or "0" else unknown = unknown + 1 end
i = i + 3
end
elseif Code.KIND[tag] then
local id = code:sub(i, i + 2)
if not id:match("^%w%w%w$") then return false, "cut short" end
i = i + 3
local p = code:match("^[%w_]*", i) or ""
i = i + #p
local idx = byId[id]
if idx and Options[idx] then want[idx] = p else unknown = unknown + 1 end
else
return false, "unreadable at character " .. (i - 1)
end
end
local def, n = self._def or {}, 0
local function put(idx)
local o = Toggles[idx] or Options[idx]
local p = want[idx] or def[idx]
if not (o and p) then return end
local okc, cur = pcall(Code.pay, o)
if okc and cur == p then return end
if pcall(Code.apply, o, p) then n = n + 1 end
end
Library.Loading = true
pcall(function()
for _, idx in ipairs(list) do if not Toggles[idx] then put(idx) end end
for _, idx in ipairs(list) do if Toggles[idx] then put(idx) end end
end)
Library.Loading = false
pcall(function() Library:CheckKeys(true) end)
return true, n, unknown
end
function SaveManager:BuildConfigSection(tab)
local gb = tab:AddLeftGroupbox("Configuration")
gb:AddInput("SaveManager_ConfigName", { Text="Config name", Default="", Finished=true, Placeholder="my config" })
gb:AddDropdown("SaveManager_ConfigList", { Text="Configs", Values=self:RefreshConfigList(), AllowNull=true, Default=nil })
local function typed() return (Options.SaveManager_ConfigName and Options.SaveManager_ConfigName.Value) or "" end
local function picked() return (Options.SaveManager_ConfigList and Options.SaveManager_ConfigList.Value) or "" end
local function chosen() local n = picked(); if n == "" then n = typed() end; return n end
gb:AddButton({ Text="Create / Save", Func=function() local n=typed(); if n=="" then n=picked() end; if n~="" then self:Save(n); if Options.SaveManager_ConfigList then Options.SaveManager_ConfigList:SetValues(self:RefreshConfigList()) end; self.Library:Notify({Title="Ruv&Rill", Description="saved "..n, Time=3}) end end })
gb:AddButton({ Text="Load", Func=function() local n=chosen(); if n~="" then local ok, why = self:Load(n); self.Library:Notify({Title="Ruv&Rill", Description=ok and ("loaded "..n) or ("could not load "..n..": "..tostring(why)), Time=3}) end end })
gb:AddButton({ Text="Refresh List", Func=function() if Options.SaveManager_ConfigList then Options.SaveManager_ConfigList:SetValues(self:RefreshConfigList()) end end })
gb:AddButton({ Text="Set As Autoload", Func=function() local n=chosen(); if n~="" then self:SaveAutoloadConfig(n); self.Library:Notify({Title="Ruv&Rill", Description="autoload "..n, Time=3}) end end })
gb:AddInput("SaveManager_ShareCode", { Text="Config code", Default="", Finished=false, ClearTextOnFocus=false,
Placeholder="paste a code, then Load Config Code", Standalone=true })
gb:AddButton({ Text="Copy Config Code", Func=function()
local code, why = self:ExportCode()
if not code then self.Library:Notify({Title="Ruv&Rill", Description="no code: "..tostring(why), Time=3}) return end
if Options.SaveManager_ShareCode then Options.SaveManager_ShareCode:SetValue(code) end
local copied = false
pcall(function() if typeof(setclipboard)=="function" then setclipboard(code); copied = true end end)
self.Library:Notify({Title="Ruv&Rill", Description=(copied and "copied a " or "made a ")..#code.."-character code", Time=3})
end })
gb:AddButton({ Text="Load Config Code", Func=function()
local ok, n, unknown = self:ImportCode(Options.SaveManager_ShareCode and Options.SaveManager_ShareCode.Value or "")
self.Library:Notify({Title="Ruv&Rill", Description=ok and ("code loaded - "..n.." changed"..((unknown or 0) > 0 and (", "..unknown.." not in this build") or "")) or ("could not load: "..tostring(n)), Time=4})
end })
end
function Library:SetLocked(obj, why)
local f = obj._feature
if why then
if obj.Type == "Toggle" and obj.Value and not obj.Disabled then obj:SetValue(false) end
if obj.Disabled and not obj._locked then return end
obj.Disabled, obj.LockedWhy, obj._locked = true, why, true
if f then f.noHover = true end
elseif obj._locked then
obj.Disabled, obj.LockedWhy, obj._locked = false, nil, nil
if f then f.noHover = false end
else
return
end
pcall(obj.Display, obj)
end
function Library:LockedNote(obj)
local now = os.clock()
if now - (obj._lockSaid or 0) < 1.5 then return end
obj._lockSaid = now
pcall(function() self:Notify({ Title="Ruv&Rill", Description=obj.LockedWhy, Time=3 }) end)
end
local TH = {}
TH.T0 = {}
for k, v in pairs(T) do TH.T0[k] = v end
TH.TOKENS = { "Column", "Popup", "Base", "Tint", "Row", "Widget", "Inset", "Text", "Sub", "Faint",
"Accent", "AccentHi", "AccentDeep", "OnAccent", "Edge", "EdgeDk" }
function TH.key(c) return math.floor(c.R*255+0.5)*65536 + math.floor(c.G*255+0.5)*256 + math.floor(c.B*255+0.5) end
TH.RESERVED = { [TH.key(Color3.new(1,1,1))]=true, [TH.key(Color3.new(0,0,0))]=true,
[TH.key(Color3.fromRGB(74,80,96))]=true, [TH.key(Color3.fromRGB(28,32,42))]=true }
function TH.lum(c) return 0.2126*c.R + 0.7152*c.G + 0.0722*c.B end
Library.THEMES = {
{ name="White",    a=Color3.fromRGB(250,251,255), b=Color3.fromRGB(222,230,250), accent=Color3.fromRGB(36,40,52),    text=Color3.fromRGB(15,17,24) },
{ name="Midnight", a=Color3.fromRGB(46,50,64),    b=Color3.fromRGB(14,16,24),    accent=Color3.fromRGB(150,170,255), text=Color3.fromRGB(236,239,246) },
{ name="Green",    a=Color3.fromRGB(58,196,122),  b=Color3.fromRGB(16,92,54),    accent=Color3.fromRGB(205,255,225), text=Color3.fromRGB(246,255,249) },
{ name="Blue",     a=Color3.fromRGB(72,142,255),  b=Color3.fromRGB(22,52,136),   accent=Color3.fromRGB(210,228,255), text=Color3.fromRGB(245,249,255) },
{ name="Purple",   a=Color3.fromRGB(150,106,255), b=Color3.fromRGB(62,34,136),   accent=Color3.fromRGB(228,214,255), text=Color3.fromRGB(250,246,255) },
{ name="Red",      a=Color3.fromRGB(238,82,86),   b=Color3.fromRGB(116,20,32),   accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,214,214), text=Color3.fromRGB(255,247,247) },
{ name="Pink",     a=Color3.fromRGB(255,124,192), b=Color3.fromRGB(146,40,108),  accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,222,242), text=Color3.fromRGB(255,247,252) },
{ name="Orange",   a=Color3.fromRGB(255,158,66),  b=Color3.fromRGB(156,70,14),   accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,234,204), text=Color3.fromRGB(255,250,244) },
{ name="Teal",     a=Color3.fromRGB(42,198,196),  b=Color3.fromRGB(12,92,104),   accent=Color3.fromRGB(204,255,250), text=Color3.fromRGB(244,255,255) },
{ name="Gold",     a=Color3.fromRGB(236,194,82),  b=Color3.fromRGB(134,94,20),   accent=Color3.fromRGB(255,244,204), text=Color3.fromRGB(255,252,240) },
}
TH.BACK = Color3.fromRGB(40, 42, 48)
TH.INK, TH.ROW_INK = 5.0, 4.5
function TH.lin(v) return v <= 0.04045 and v / 12.92 or ((v + 0.055) / 1.055) ^ 2.4 end
function TH.rl(c) return 0.2126 * TH.lin(c.R) + 0.7152 * TH.lin(c.G) + 0.0722 * TH.lin(c.B) end
function TH.cr(x, y)
local a, b = TH.rl(x), TH.rl(y)
if a < b then a, b = b, a end
return (a + 0.05) / (b + 0.05)
end
function TH.shown(c, opacity) return TH.BACK:Lerp(c, opacity) end
function Library:DeriveTheme(sp)
if sp.name == "White" and not sp.custom then
local t = {}
for _, tok in ipairs(TH.TOKENS) do t[tok] = TH.T0[tok] end
return t
end
local W, K = Color3.new(1,1,1), Color3.new(0,0,0)
local a, b, acc, tx = sp.a, sp.b, sp.accent, sp.text
local dark = TH.lum(tx) > 0.5
local glassOp, popOp = 1 - TR.column, 1 - TR.popup
local top, pop, row, widget = a, nil, nil, nil
if dark then
local s = 0
while TH.cr(tx, TH.shown(top, glassOp)) < TH.INK and s < 1 do
s = math.min(s + 0.02, 1)
top = a:Lerp(b, s)
end
local from, k = top, 0
while TH.cr(tx, TH.shown(top, glassOp)) < TH.INK and k < 0.6 do
k = k + 0.02
top = from:Lerp(K, k)
end
pop = top:Lerp(W, 0.08)
local popFrom, kp = pop, 0
while TH.cr(tx, TH.shown(pop, popOp)) < TH.INK and kp < 0.6 do
kp = kp + 0.02
pop = popFrom:Lerp(K, kp)
end
local glass = TH.shown(top, glassOp)
local function shade(opacity, most, deep)
local r = most
while r >= 0.06 do
local c = top:Lerp(W, r)
local onIt = glass:Lerp(c, opacity)
if TH.cr(tx, onIt) >= TH.ROW_INK and TH.cr(onIt, glass) >= 1.12 then return c end
r = r - 0.02
end
return top:Lerp(K, deep)
end
row = shade(1 - TR.rowOn, 0.35, 0.35)
widget = shade(1 - TR.widget, 0.2, 0.3)
else
pop = a:Lerp(W, 0.4)
row = a:Lerp(W, 0.92)
widget = a:Lerp(W, 0.96)
end
local t = {}
t.Column = top
t.Popup  = pop
t.Base   = dark and top:Lerp(K, 0.06) or top:Lerp(K, 0.02)
t.Tint   = Color3.new(math.clamp(b.R / math.max(top.R, 0.004), 0, 1),
math.clamp(b.G / math.max(top.G, 0.004), 0, 1), math.clamp(b.B / math.max(top.B, 0.004), 0, 1))
t.Row    = row
t.Widget = widget
t.Inset  = dark and b:Lerp(K, 0.25) or top:Lerp(tx, 0.12)
t.Text   = tx
t.Sub    = tx:Lerp(top, dark and 0.22 or 0.28)
t.Faint  = tx:Lerp(top, dark and 0.45 or 0.5)
t.Accent = acc
t.AccentHi   = dark and acc:Lerp(tx, 0.3) or acc:Lerp(W, 0.3)
t.AccentDeep = acc:Lerp(K, 0.2)
t.OnAccent   = TH.lum(acc) > 0.55 and Color3.fromRGB(8,10,16) or Color3.fromRGB(250,250,252)
t.Edge   = sp.outline or (dark and top:Lerp(W, 0.3) or top:Lerp(tx, 0.18))
t.EdgeDk = t.Edge:Lerp(dark and W or K, 0.12)
return t
end
function Library:UniqueTheme(t)
local used = {}
for k in pairs(TH.RESERVED) do used[k] = true end
for tok, col in pairs(T) do
if typeof(col) == "Color3" and t[tok] == nil then used[TH.key(col)] = true end
end
for _, tok in ipairs(TH.TOKENS) do
local c = t[tok]
if c then
local r, g, bl = math.floor(c.R*255+0.5), math.floor(c.G*255+0.5), math.floor(c.B*255+0.5)
local n = 0
while used[r*65536 + g*256 + bl] and n < 96 do
n = n + 1
local d = (n % 2 == 1) and 1 or -1
local ch = n % 3
if ch == 0 then bl = math.clamp(bl + d, 0, 255)
elseif ch == 1 then g = math.clamp(g + d, 0, 255)
else r = math.clamp(r + d, 0, 255) end
end
used[r*65536 + g*256 + bl] = true
t[tok] = Color3.fromRGB(r, g, bl)
end
end
return t
end
Library.FADE = 0.45
function Library:ApplyTokens(newT, fade)
self:FinishFade()
local byColor = {}
for tok, col in pairs(T) do if typeof(col) == "Color3" then byColor[TH.key(col)] = tok end end
local changed = {}
for tok, col in pairs(newT) do
if typeof(T[tok]) == "Color3" and TH.key(T[tok]) ~= TH.key(col) then changed[tok] = col end
end
for tok, col in pairs(newT) do T[tok] = col end
self.Scheme.MainColor, self.Scheme.BackgroundColor = T.Column, T.Base
self.Scheme.AccentColor, self.Scheme.OutlineColor, self.Scheme.FontColor = T.Accent, T.Edge, T.Text
if not next(changed) or not self.ScreenGui then return 0 end
local function swap(c)
local tok = byColor[TH.key(c)]
return tok and changed[tok] or nil
end
local jobs = fade and {} or nil
local function put(inst, prop, v)
if not jobs then inst[prop] = v return end
local cur = inst[prop]
if TH.key(cur) == TH.key(v) then return end
jobs[#jobs + 1] = { inst = inst, prop = prop, from = cur, to = v }
end
local n = 0
for _, d in ipairs(self.ScreenGui:GetDescendants()) do
if not d:GetAttribute("NoTheme") then
pcall(function()
if d:IsA("GuiObject") then
local v = swap(d.BackgroundColor3)
if v then put(d, "BackgroundColor3", v) n = n + 1 end
if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
v = swap(d.TextColor3)
if v then put(d, "TextColor3", v) n = n + 1 end
if d:IsA("TextBox") then
v = swap(d.PlaceholderColor3)
if v then put(d, "PlaceholderColor3", v) end
end
elseif d:IsA("ImageLabel") or d:IsA("ImageButton") then
v = swap(d.ImageColor3)
if v then put(d, "ImageColor3", v) n = n + 1 end
elseif d:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
v = swap(d.ScrollBarImageColor3)
if v then put(d, "ScrollBarImageColor3", v) end
end
elseif d:IsA("UIStroke") then
local v = swap(d.Color)
if v then put(d, "Color", v) n = n + 1 end
elseif d:IsA("UIGradient") then
local times, from, to, any = {}, {}, {}, false
for i, kp in ipairs(d.Color.Keypoints) do
local v = swap(kp.Value)
if v then any = true end
times[i], from[i], to[i] = kp.Time, kp.Value, v or kp.Value
end
if any then
if jobs then
jobs[#jobs + 1] = { inst = d, grad = true, times = times, from = from, to = to }
else
local out = {}
for i, t in ipairs(times) do out[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(t, to[i]) end
d.Color = ColorSequence.new(out)
end
n = n + 1
end
end
end)
end
end
for _, e in ipairs(self._registry) do
local col = T[e.token]
if col and e.inst and e.inst.Parent then pcall(put, e.inst, e.prop, col) end
end
if jobs and #jobs > 0 then self:RunFade(jobs) end
return n
end
function TH.land(j, e)
if j.dropped then return end
local inst = j.inst
if not inst.Parent then j.dropped = true return end
if j.grad then
local out = {}
for i, t in ipairs(j.times) do
out[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(t, e >= 1 and j.to[i] or j.from[i]:Lerp(j.to[i], e))
end
inst.Color = ColorSequence.new(out)
return
end
if j.last and TH.key(inst[j.prop]) ~= TH.key(j.last) then j.dropped = true return end
local v = e >= 1 and j.to or j.from:Lerp(j.to, e)
inst[j.prop] = v
j.last = v
end
function Library:RunFade(jobs)
if #jobs > 6000 then
for _, j in ipairs(jobs) do pcall(TH.land, j, 1) end
return
end
local f = { jobs = jobs, t = 0 }
self._fade = f
f.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
f.t = f.t + dt
local p = math.clamp(f.t / Library.FADE, 0, 1)
local e = p < 0.5 and 2 * p * p or 1 - ((-2 * p + 2) ^ 2) / 2
for _, j in ipairs(f.jobs) do pcall(TH.land, j, p >= 1 and 1 or e) end
if p >= 1 then
f.conn:Disconnect()
if self._fade == f then self._fade = nil end
end
end)
end
function Library:FinishFade()
local f = self._fade
if not f then return end
self._fade = nil
if f.conn then f.conn:Disconnect() end
for _, j in ipairs(f.jobs) do pcall(TH.land, j, 1) end
end
function Library:ApplyTheme(sp, fade)
local t = self:UniqueTheme(self:DeriveTheme(sp))
self.Theme = sp
return self:ApplyTokens(t, fade)
end
local ThemeManager = { Folder="Rake" }
function ThemeManager:SetLibrary(l) self.Library=l end
function ThemeManager:SetFolder(f) self.Folder=f end
function ThemeManager:ApplyToTab(tab)
local gb = tab:AddRightGroupbox("Theme")
local mgr = self
local feat = makeRow(gb, "label")
feat.accent.Visible = false
feat.noHover = true
feat.right.Visible = false
feat.name.Text = "Presets"
feat.name.TextColor3 = T.Sub
feat.name.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,ROW_H)
feat.row.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
feat.row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
new("UIPadding", { Parent=feat.row, PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,7) })
local grid = new("Frame", { Parent=feat.row, BackgroundTransparency=1, Position=UDim2.fromOffset(9, ROW_H-3),
Size=UDim2.new(1,-18,0,0), AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ZIndex=4 })
new("UIGridLayout", { Parent=grid, CellSize=UDim2.fromOffset(30,18), CellPadding=UDim2.fromOffset(6,6),
SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder })
mgr.presetBtns = {}
for i, th in ipairs(Library.THEMES) do
local btn = new("TextButton", { Parent=grid, Text="", AutoButtonColor=false, BackgroundTransparency=1,
LayoutOrder=i, ZIndex=5 })
local ring = new("Frame", { Parent=btn, BackgroundTransparency=1, Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1), ZIndex=5 })
corner(999, ring)
local ringStroke = stroke(T.Accent, 1, ring, 1.5)
local function dot(col, x, z)
local d = new("Frame", { Parent=btn, BackgroundColor3=col, Size=UDim2.fromOffset(14,14),
AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0.5), Position=UDim2.new(0,x,0.5,0), ZIndex=z })
d:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
corner(999, d)
stroke(Color3.new(1,1,1), 0.3, d, 1)
end
dot(th.a, 2, 6)
dot(th.b, 14, 7)
local sc = new("UIScale", { Parent=btn, Scale=1 })
track(btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
feat.name.Text = "Presets - " .. th.name
tween(sc, EASE.snap, { Scale=1.12 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
end))
track(btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
feat.name.Text = "Presets"
tween(sc, EASE.move, { Scale=1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
end))
track(btn.Activated:Connect(function()
local sp = {}
for k, v in pairs(th) do sp[k] = v end
mgr:SetTheme(sp, false, true)
end))
mgr.presetBtns[i] = { name=th.name, stroke=ringStroke }
end
local function addColor(idx, title, field)
local holder = gb:AddLabel(title)
holder:AddColorPicker(idx, { Title=title, Default=Library.THEMES[1][field] or T.Edge, Callback=function(c)
if mgr.syncing then return end
local sp = {}
for k, v in pairs(Library.Theme or Library.THEMES[1]) do sp[k] = v end
sp[field] = c
sp.name, sp.custom = "Custom", true
mgr:SetTheme(sp)
end })
end
addColor("MainColor", "Glass Top", "a")
addColor("BackgroundColor", "Glass Bottom", "b")
addColor("AccentColor", "Accent", "accent")
addColor("OutlineColor", "Outline", "outline")
addColor("FontColor", "Text", "text")
local fontDrop = gb:AddDropdown("FontFace", { Text="Menu Font", Values=Library.FONT_NAMES, Default="Nunito",
Callback=function(f)
pcall(function() Library:SetFont(f) end)
fileWrite(self.Folder .. "/menufont.txt", tostring(f or "Nunito"))
end })
local saved = fileRead(self.Folder .. "/menufont.txt")
if saved and Library.FONTS[saved] and saved ~= "Nunito" then
pcall(function() fontDrop:SetValue(saved) end)
end
end
function ThemeManager:SetTheme(sp, quiet, fade)
Library:ApplyTheme(sp, fade and Library.Toggled and not quiet)
self:SyncPickers()
self:PaintPresets()
if not quiet then self:SaveTheme() end
end
function ThemeManager:SyncPickers()
local sp = Library.Theme
if not sp then return end
self.syncing = true
pcall(function()
local map = { MainColor=sp.a, BackgroundColor=sp.b, AccentColor=sp.accent, OutlineColor=sp.outline or T.Edge, FontColor=sp.text }
for idx, c in pairs(map) do
local o = Options[idx]
if o and c then o.Value = c; o:Display() end
end
end)
self.syncing = false
end
function ThemeManager:PaintPresets()
local name = Library.Theme and Library.Theme.name
for _, p in ipairs(self.presetBtns or {}) do
pcall(function() p.stroke.Transparency = (p.name == name) and 0 or 1 end)
end
end
function ThemeManager:SaveTheme()
local sp = Library.Theme
if not sp then return end
local function c3(c) return c and { math.floor(c.R*255+0.5), math.floor(c.G*255+0.5), math.floor(c.B*255+0.5) } or nil end
local ok, enc = pcall(function()
return HttpService:JSONEncode({ name=sp.name, custom=sp.custom, a=c3(sp.a), b=c3(sp.b),
accent=c3(sp.accent), text=c3(sp.text), outline=c3(sp.outline) })
end)
if ok then fileWrite(self.Folder .. "/theme.json", enc) end
end
function ThemeManager:LoadTheme()
local sp
local raw = fileRead(self.Folder .. "/theme.json")
if raw then
pcall(function()
local d = HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
local function c3(t) return type(t) == "table" and Color3.fromRGB(t[1], t[2], t[3]) or nil end
sp = { name=d.name, custom=d.custom, a=c3(d.a), b=c3(d.b), accent=c3(d.accent), text=c3(d.text), outline=c3(d.outline) }
