if not (sp.a and sp.b and sp.accent and sp.text) then sp = nil end
end)
end
if sp and not sp.custom then
for _, th in ipairs(Library.THEMES) do
if th.name == sp.name then
sp = {}
for k, v in pairs(th) do sp[k] = v end
break
end
end
end
if not sp then
sp = {}
for k, v in pairs(Library.THEMES[1]) do sp[k] = v end
end
self:SetTheme(sp, true)
end
Library.SaveManager = SaveManager
Library.ThemeManager = ThemeManager
return Library
end)()
local ThemeManager = Library.ThemeManager
local SaveManager  = Library.SaveManager
local Options = Library.Options
local Toggles = Library.Toggles
Library.IsMobile = IS_MOBILE
if IS_MOBILE then Library.DPIScale = Device.fitScale() end
Device.Boot.reveal = function()
if Library.ScreenGui then Library.ScreenGui.Enabled = true end
Library:Toggle(true)
end
Device.Boot.step("Menu library")
local function notify(text, seconds)
pcall(function()
Library:Notify({ Title = "Ruv&Rill", Description = tostring(text), Time = seconds or 4 })
end)
end
local function saveDump(name, text)
local path = "Rake/dumps/" .. name
if canWrite() then
ensureFolder("Rake")
ensureFolder("Rake/dumps")
if pcall(writefile, path, text) then
clipboard(text)
notify("wrote " .. path, 6)
return true, "wrote " .. path
end
end
if clipboard(text) then
notify("no writefile - " .. name .. " copied to the clipboard", 7)
return true, "copied to the clipboard"
end
notify("no writefile and no setclipboard on this executor", 7)
return false, "nowhere to put it"
end
local Stam = {
on = false, nvg = false, status = "off",
tbl = nil, nvgTbl = nil, last = 0, found = false,
mode = "Default", stockTake = nil, stockJump = nil, stockRegen = nil, stockCooldown = nil,
refillAt = 0.35, boosting = false,
}
function Stam.vitaminTool()
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local tool = (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Vitamins"))
or (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Vitamins"))
return tool
end
function Stam.keepStock(t)
pcall(function()
if Stam.stockTake == nil and type(rawget(t, "STAMINA_TAKE")) == "number" then
Stam.stockTake = t.STAMINA_TAKE
end
if Stam.stockJump == nil and type(rawget(t, "JUMP_STAMINA")) == "number" then
Stam.stockJump = t.JUMP_STAMINA
end
if Stam.stockRegen == nil and type(rawget(t, "STAMINA_REGEN")) == "number" then
Stam.stockRegen = t.STAMINA_REGEN
end
if Stam.stockCooldown == nil and type(rawget(t, "JUMP_COOLDOWN")) == "number" then
Stam.stockCooldown = t.JUMP_COOLDOWN
end
end)
end
function Stam.restoreStock()
local t = Stam.tbl
if not t then return end
pcall(function()
if Stam.stockTake ~= nil and rawget(t, "STAMINA_TAKE") ~= nil then
t.STAMINA_TAKE = Stam.stockTake
end
if Stam.stockJump ~= nil and rawget(t, "JUMP_STAMINA") ~= nil then
t.JUMP_STAMINA = Stam.stockJump
end
if Stam.stockRegen ~= nil and rawget(t, "STAMINA_REGEN") ~= nil then
t.STAMINA_REGEN = Stam.stockRegen
end
if Stam.stockCooldown ~= nil and rawget(t, "JUMP_COOLDOWN") ~= nil then
t.JUMP_COOLDOWN = Stam.stockCooldown
end
end)
Stam.stockTake, Stam.stockJump, Stam.stockRegen, Stam.stockCooldown = nil, nil, nil, nil
end
addCleanup("stamina", function()
Stam.restoreStock()
local tool = Stam.vitaminTool()
local active = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Active")
if active then pcall(function() active.Value = false end) end
end)
function Stam.setMode(mode)
Stam.mode = mode
Stam.boosting = false
Stam.restoreStock()
if Stam.on then Stam.apply() end
end
function Stam.find(marker)
if typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then return nil end
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok then return nil end
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" then
local got, has = pcall(function() return rawget(v, marker) ~= nil end)
if got and has then return v end
end
end
return nil
end
function Stam.findLive()
local t = Stam.live
if t then
local ok, isNum = pcall(function() return type(rawget(t, "stamina")) == "number" end)
if ok and isNum then return t end
end
Stam.live = nil
if typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then return nil end
if os.clock() - (Stam.liveHunt or -math.huge) < 3 then return nil end
Stam.liveHunt = os.clock()
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok then return nil end
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" and v ~= Stam then
local fine, hit = pcall(function()
return type(rawget(v, "stamina")) == "number" and rawget(v, "STAMINA_REGEN") == nil
end)
if fine and hit then
Stam.live = v
return v
end
end
end
return nil
end
function Stam.maxStamina()
local t = Stam.tbl
local m = t and tonumber(rawget(t, "MAX_STAMINA"))
return (m and m > 0) and m or 100
end
function Stam.spoofStep(dt)
local live = Stam.live
if not live then return end
pcall(function()
local cur = rawget(live, "stamina")
if type(cur) ~= "number" then return end
local max = Stam.maxStamina()
if Stam.boosting then
cur = math.min(max, cur + max * (dt or 1 / 60) * 1.8)
live.stamina = cur
if cur >= max * 0.97 then Stam.boosting = false end
elseif cur < max * Stam.refillAt then
Stam.boosting = true
end
end)
end
function Stam.apply()
local t = Stam.tbl
if not (t and pcall(function() return rawget(t, "stamina") end)) then
t = Stam.find("STAMINA_REGEN") or Stam.find("stamina")
Stam.tbl = t
end
if not t then
Stam.found = false
Stam.status = typeof(getgc) == "function"
and "cannot find the stamina table yet - move around once"
or "this executor has no getgc, so infinite stamina cannot work"
return false
end
Stam.found = true
Stam.keepStock(t)
if Stam.mode == "Vitamins Spoof" then
local tool = Stam.vitaminTool()
local active = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Active")
local liveT = Stam.findLive()
if liveT then
Stam.restoreStock()
local cur = 0
pcall(function() cur = rawget(liveT, "stamina") or 0 end)
Stam.status = string.format("vitamins spoof - stamina %d, refills below %d%%%s",
math.floor(cur), math.floor(Stam.refillAt * 100), active and ", Active held" or "")
if active then pcall(function() active.Value = true end) end
return true
end
local live = 0
pcall(function()
if rawget(t, "STAMINA_REGEN") ~= nil then t.STAMINA_REGEN = 100 end
if rawget(t, "JUMP_COOLDOWN") ~= nil then t.JUMP_COOLDOWN = 0 end
if Stam.stockTake and rawget(t, "STAMINA_TAKE") ~= nil then
t.STAMINA_TAKE = Stam.stockTake * 0.15
end
if Stam.stockJump and rawget(t, "JUMP_STAMINA") ~= nil then
t.JUMP_STAMINA = Stam.stockJump * 0.15
end
if type(rawget(t, "stamina")) == "number" then
live = t.stamina
if live < 25 then t.stamina = 25 end
end
end)
if active then pcall(function() active.Value = true end) end
Stam.status = string.format("vitamins spoof (fallback, no live bar found) - stamina %d, drain at 15%%, regen pinned%s",
math.floor(live), active and ", Active held" or "")
return true
end
pcall(function()
if rawget(t, "STAMINA_REGEN") ~= nil then t.STAMINA_REGEN = 100 end
if rawget(t, "STAMINA_TAKE") ~= nil then t.STAMINA_TAKE = 0 end
if rawget(t, "JUMP_STAMINA") ~= nil then t.JUMP_STAMINA = 0 end
if rawget(t, "JUMP_COOLDOWN") ~= nil then t.JUMP_COOLDOWN = 0 end
if rawget(t, "stamina") ~= nil then t.stamina = 100 end
end)
local liveT = Stam.findLive()
if liveT then pcall(function() liveT.stamina = Stam.maxStamina() end) end
Stam.status = "holding stamina - STAMINA_TAKE is zero"
return true
end
Stam.NVG_DRAIN = {
"NVG_TAKE", "NV_TAKE", "NIGHTVISION_TAKE", "NIGHT_VISION_TAKE",
"NVG_DRAIN", "NV_DRAIN", "NVGTAKE", "GOGGLE_TAKE", "GOGGLES_TAKE",
}
Stam.NVG_REGEN = {
"NVG_REGEN", "NV_REGEN", "NIGHTVISION_REGEN", "NIGHT_VISION_REGEN",
"NVG_GAIN", "NV_GAIN",
}
function Stam.nvgModule()
local t = Stam.nvgMod
local ok = t and pcall(function() return type(rawget(t, "power")) == "number" end)
if ok and t and type(rawget(t, "power")) == "number" then return t end
Stam.nvgMod = nil
if typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then return nil end
if os.clock() - (Stam.nvgHunt or -math.huge) < 5 then return nil end
Stam.nvgHunt = os.clock()
local got, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not got then return nil end
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" then
local fine, hit = pcall(function()
return rawget(v, "NVHandler") ~= nil
and rawget(v, "isSetup") ~= nil
and type(rawget(v, "power")) == "number"
end)
if fine and hit then
Stam.nvgMod = v
return v
end
end
end
return nil
end
function Stam.applyNvg()
local t = Stam.nvgTbl
if not t and os.clock() - (Stam.nvgDrainHunt or 0) >= 4 then
Stam.nvgDrainHunt = os.clock()
for _, key in ipairs(Stam.NVG_DRAIN) do
t = Stam.find(key)
if t then
Stam.nvgKey = key
break
end
end
Stam.nvgTbl = t
end
local top = 100
if t then
if Stam.nvgSavedFor ~= t then Stam.nvgSaved, Stam.nvgSavedFor = {}, t end
local saved = Stam.nvgSaved
pcall(function()
for _, key in ipairs(Stam.NVG_DRAIN) do
if rawget(t, key) ~= nil then
if saved[key] == nil then saved[key] = t[key] end
t[key] = 0
end
end
for _, key in ipairs(Stam.NVG_REGEN) do
if rawget(t, key) ~= nil then
if saved[key] == nil then saved[key] = t[key] end
t[key] = 100
end
end
if type(rawget(t, "MAX_NVG_P")) == "number" then top = t.MAX_NVG_P end
end)
end
Stam.nvgTop = top
local mod = Stam.nvgModule()
if mod then
pcall(function() mod.power = top end)
Stam.nvgStatus = "holding night vision - battery pinned at " .. top
.. (t and (" (" .. tostring(Stam.nvgKey) .. " zeroed)") or "")
return true
end
Stam.nvgStatus = "equip the goggles once so the module latches, then it holds"
return false
end
function Stam.enforce()
if Stam.conn then return end
Stam.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
if not Stam.on and not Stam.nvg then return end
if Stam.on and Stam.mode == "Vitamins Spoof" then Stam.spoofStep(dt) end
if os.clock() - Stam.last < 0.5 then
local t = Stam.tbl
if Stam.on and Stam.mode ~= "Vitamins Spoof" then
pcall(function()
if t and rawget(t, "stamina") ~= nil then t.stamina = 100 end
if Stam.live and type(rawget(Stam.live, "stamina")) == "number" then
Stam.live.stamina = Stam.maxStamina()
end
end)
end
if Stam.nvg and Stam.nvgMod then
pcall(function() Stam.nvgMod.power = Stam.nvgTop or 100 end)
end
return
end
Stam.last = os.clock()
if Stam.on then Stam.apply() end
if Stam.nvg then Stam.applyNvg() end
end)
addCleanup("stamloop", function()
if Stam.conn then Stam.conn:Disconnect() Stam.conn = nil end
end)
end
function Stam.set(on)
Stam.on = on
if on then
Stam.enforce()
if not Stam.apply() then notify(Stam.status, 6) end
else
Stam.restoreStock()
local tool = Stam.vitaminTool()
local active = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Active")
if active then pcall(function() active.Value = false end) end
Stam.status = "off"
end
end
function Stam.setNvg(on)
Stam.nvg = on
if on then
Stam.enforce()
if not Stam.applyNvg() then
notify("no night vision table found under any of the "
.. #Stam.NVG_DRAIN .. " names it knows - press Dev > Find Night "
.. "Vision with the goggles ON and send me the file", 9)
else
notify(Stam.nvgStatus, 5)
end
else
Stam.nvgStatus = "off"
Stam.restoreNvg()
end
end
function Stam.restoreNvg()
local t, saved = Stam.nvgSavedFor, Stam.nvgSaved
Stam.nvgSaved, Stam.nvgSavedFor = nil, nil
if not (t and saved) then return end
pcall(function()
for key, v in pairs(saved) do t[key] = v end
end)
end
addCleanup("nvg", function() Stam.restoreNvg() end)
local Move = {
speedOn = false, walkSpeed = 16, baseWalk = 16,
speedMode = "Hold WalkSpeed",
fovOn = false, fov = 70, baseFov = 70,
noFall = false,
boundHum = nil, animOn = false, animSaved = nil, animLast = 0,
}
function Move.enforce()
if Move.loop then return end
local function fix()
local hum = getHum()
if Move.speedOn and hum and not Move.ADDITIVE[Move.speedMode]
and hum.WalkSpeed ~= Move.walkSpeed then
hum.WalkSpeed = Move.walkSpeed
end
local c = cam()
if Move.fovOn and c and math.abs(c.FieldOfView - Move.fov) > 0.01 then
c.FieldOfView = Move.fov
end
end
Move.fix = fix
Move.loop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
fix()
if os.clock() - (Move.animLast or 0) >= 0.25 then
Move.animLast = os.clock()
local hum = getHum()
local fast = (Move.speedOn and hum and Move.walkSpeed > Move.baseWalk + 2) or false
if fast ~= Move.animOn then
Move.animOn = fast
Move.setRunAnim(fast)
end
end
if Move.animOn then Move.holdAnimSpeed() end
end)
Move.rebind()
addCleanup("moveloop", function()
if Move.loop then Move.loop:Disconnect() Move.loop = nil end
if Move.humConn then Move.humConn:Disconnect() Move.humConn = nil end
if Move.camConn then Move.camConn:Disconnect() Move.camConn = nil end
Move.boundHum, Move.animOn = nil, false
Move.setRunAnim(false)
local hum, c = getHum(), cam()
if hum then pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = Move.baseWalk end) end
if c then pcall(function() c.FieldOfView = Move.baseFov end) end
end)
end
function Move.rebind()
if not Move.fix then return end
if Move.humConn then Move.humConn:Disconnect() Move.humConn = nil end
if Move.camConn then Move.camConn:Disconnect() Move.camConn = nil end
local hum, c = getHum(), cam()
if hum ~= Move.boundHum then
Move.animSaved, Move.animOn = nil, false
Move.boundHum = hum
end
if hum then Move.humConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(Move.fix) end
if c then Move.camConn = c:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FieldOfView"):Connect(Move.fix) end
end
function Move.setAntiRagdoll(on)
Move.antiRagdoll = on
if not on then
if Move.ragConn then Move.ragConn:Disconnect() Move.ragConn = nil end
Move.ragStatus = "off"
local hum = getHum()
if hum then
pcall(function()
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, true)
end)
end
return
end
if Move.ragConn then return end
Move.ragCaught = 0
Move.ragConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if not Move.antiRagdoll then return end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local hum = getHum()
if not char or not hum then return end
local caught = false
if hum.PlatformStand then
hum.PlatformStand = false
caught = true
end
if hum.Sit then
hum.Sit = false
caught = true
end
if not hum.AutoRotate and os.clock() - (Move.autoRotateHeld or 0) > 0.5 then
hum.AutoRotate = true
end
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Motor6D") and not d.Enabled then
d.Enabled = true
caught = true
end
end
local timer = char:FindFirstChild("RagdollTime")
if timer then
local ok, value = pcall(function() return timer.Value end)
if ok and type(value) == "number" and value > 0 then
pcall(function() timer.Value = 0 end)
caught = true
end
local switch = timer:FindFirstChild("RagdollSwitch")
if switch and switch.Value == true then
pcall(function() switch.Value = false end)
caught = true
end
end
local state = hum:GetState()
if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
caught = true
end
pcall(function()
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, false)
end)
if caught then Move.ragCaught = Move.ragCaught + 1 end
Move.ragStatus = Move.ragCaught > 0
and ("caught " .. Move.ragCaught)
or "watching"
end)
addCleanup("antiragdoll", function()
if Move.ragConn then Move.ragConn:Disconnect() Move.ragConn = nil end
local hum = getHum()
if hum then
pcall(function()
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, true)
end)
end
end)
end
Move.flySpeed = 70
Move.blinkDist = 26
Move.jumpPower = 50
Move.baseGravity = Workspace.Gravity
function Move.setFly(on)
Move.fly = on
if not on then
if Move.flyConn then Move.flyConn:Disconnect() Move.flyConn = nil end
if Move.flyForce then pcall(function() Move.flyForce:Destroy() end) Move.flyForce = nil end
if Move.flyGyro then pcall(function() Move.flyGyro:Destroy() end) Move.flyGyro = nil end
return
end
if Move.flyConn or Move.flyForce then Move.setFly(false) end
Move.fly = true
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then
notify("no character to fly", 4)
Move.fly = false
pcall(function()
if Toggles.RK_Fly then Toggles.RK_Fly:SetValue(false) end
end)
return
end
Move.flyForce = make("BodyVelocity", {
Name = "RKFly", MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5),
Velocity = Vector3.zero, P = 1500,
}, hrp)
Move.flyGyro = make("BodyGyro", {
Name = "RKFlyGyro", MaxTorque = Vector3.new(4e5, 4e5, 4e5),
P = 3000, CFrame = hrp.CFrame,
}, hrp)
Move.flyConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local root, c = getHRP(), cam()
if not root or not c or not Move.flyForce then return end
if not Move.flyForce.Parent and Move.flyForce ~= nil then
local stillOn = Move.fly
Move.setFly(false)
if stillOn then Move.setFly(true) end
return
end
if Free and Free.on then
Move.flyForce.Velocity = Vector3.zero
return
end
if Move.flyForce.Parent ~= root then
Move.flyForce.Parent = root
if Move.flyGyro then Move.flyGyro.Parent = root end
end
local dir = Vector3.zero
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir += c.CFrame.LookVector end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir -= c.CFrame.LookVector end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir += c.CFrame.RightVector end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir -= c.CFrame.RightVector end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dir += Vector3.yAxis end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then dir -= Vector3.yAxis end
if dir.Magnitude == 0 then
local hum = getHum()
if hum and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then dir = hum.MoveDirection end
end
if Move.flyGyro then Move.flyGyro.CFrame = c.CFrame end
Move.flyForce.Velocity = (dir.Magnitude > 0 and dir.Unit or Vector3.zero) * Move.flySpeed
end)
addCleanup("fly", function() Move.setFly(false) end)
end
function Move.setInfJump(on)
Move.infJump = on
if on and not Move.jumpConn then
Move.jumpConn = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
local hum = getHum()
if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
end)
addCleanup("infjump", function() Move.setInfJump(false) end)
elseif not on and Move.jumpConn then
Move.jumpConn:Disconnect()
Move.jumpConn = nil
end
end
function Move.blink()
local hrp, c = getHRP(), cam()
if not hrp or not c then return end
local flat = Vector3.new(c.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, c.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
if flat.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char then
pcall(function() char:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame + flat.Unit * Move.blinkDist) end)
end
end
function Move.setJumpPower(v)
Move.jumpPower = v
local hum = getHum()
if not hum then return end
if Move.baseJump == nil then
Move.baseJump = hum.JumpPower
Move.baseUseJump = hum.UseJumpPower
end
pcall(function()
hum.UseJumpPower = true
hum.JumpPower = v
end)
end
addCleanup("jumppower", function()
local hum = getHum()
if hum and Move.baseJump then
pcall(function()
hum.JumpPower = Move.baseJump
hum.UseJumpPower = Move.baseUseJump
end)
end
end)
function Move.setGravity(v)
Move.gravity = v
pcall(function() Workspace.Gravity = v end)
end
addCleanup("gravity", function()
pcall(function() Workspace.Gravity = Move.baseGravity end)
end)
Move.flingLimit = 200
function Move.flingHit(v)
local flat = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
local cap = Move.flingLimit * 1.5
return flat.Magnitude > cap or v.Y > cap
end
function Move.setAntiFling(on)
Move.antiFling = on
if not on then
if Move.flingConn then Move.flingConn:Disconnect() Move.flingConn = nil end
if Move.flingBeat then Move.flingBeat:Disconnect() Move.flingBeat = nil end
for part, was in pairs(Move.flingParts or {}) do
if part.Parent then pcall(function() part.CanCollide = was end) end
end
Move.flingParts, Move.flingSafe = {}, nil
return
end
Move.flingParts = Move.flingParts or {}
if Move.flingConn then return end
Move.flingConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if not Move.antiFling then return end
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
local char = plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character
if char then
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.CanCollide then
if Move.flingParts[d] == nil then Move.flingParts[d] = true end
d.CanCollide = false
end
end
end
end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
if v.Magnitude > Move.flingLimit and not Move.fly then
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v.Unit * Move.flingLimit
end
local a = hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity
if a.Magnitude > 30 then hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = a.Unit * 30 end
end)
Move.flingBeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Move.antiFling then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp or hrp.Anchored or Move.fly or (Free and Free.on)
or (Move.flingPaused and Move.flingPaused()) then
Move.flingSafe = nil
return
end
local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
if Move.walkFling and Move.wfReal then
local shove = Move.wfReal * Move.FLING_POWER + Vector3.new(0, Move.FLING_POWER, 0)
if (v - shove).Magnitude < 1 then v = Move.wfRaw or Move.wfReal end
end
if Move.flingHit(v) then
pcall(function()
if Move.flingSafe then hrp.CFrame = Move.flingSafe end
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end)
if Move.walkFling then Move.wfReal = Vector3.zero end
else
Move.flingSafe = hrp.CFrame
end
end)
addCleanup("antifling", function() Move.setAntiFling(false) end)
end
Move.FLING_POWER = 10000
Move.FLING_SELF = 100
function Move.walkFlingCalm(v, hum)
local limit = math.max(Move.FLING_SELF, (hum and hum.WalkSpeed or 16) * 3)
local flat = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
local x, z, y = v.X, v.Z, v.Y
if flat.Magnitude > limit then x, z = 0, 0 end
if y > limit then y = 0 end
return Vector3.new(x, y, z)
end
function Move.setWalkFling(on)
Move.walkFling = on
if not on then
for _, k in ipairs({ "wfBeat", "wfRender", "wfStep" }) do
if Move[k] then Move[k]:Disconnect() Move[k] = nil end
end
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp and Move.wfReal then pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Move.wfReal end) end
Move.wfReal, Move.wfRaw = nil, nil
return
end
if Move.wfBeat then return end
Move.wfFlip = 0.1
Move.wfBeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Move.walkFling then return end
local hrp, hum = getHRP(), getHum()
if not (hrp and hum) or hum.Health <= 0 or hrp.Anchored then Move.wfReal = nil return end
local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
Move.wfRaw = v
if not Move.fly then
v = Move.walkFlingCalm(v, hum)
local a = hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity
if a.Magnitude > 30 then pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end) end
end
Move.wfReal = v
pcall(function()
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = v * Move.FLING_POWER + Vector3.new(0, Move.FLING_POWER, 0)
end)
end)
Move.wfRender = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp and Move.wfReal then pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Move.wfReal end) end
end)
Move.wfStep = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
local hrp = getHRP()
if not (hrp and Move.wfReal) then return end
Move.wfFlip = -Move.wfFlip
pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Move.wfReal + Vector3.new(0, Move.wfFlip, 0) end)
end)
addCleanup("walkfling", function() Move.setWalkFling(false) end)
end
function Move.setNoclip(on)
Move.noclip = on
if not on then
if Move.clipConn then Move.clipConn:Disconnect() Move.clipConn = nil end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char then
for part, was in pairs(Move.clipped or {}) do
if part and part.Parent then pcall(function() part.CanCollide = was end) end
end
end
Move.clipped = {}
return
end
Move.clipped = Move.clipped or {}
if Move.clipConn then return end
Move.clipConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if not Move.noclip then return end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if not char then return end
for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
if Move.clipped[part] == nil then Move.clipped[part] = true end
part.CanCollide = false
end
end
end)
addCleanup("noclip", function() Move.setNoclip(false) end)
end
function Move.animate()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
return char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
end
function Move.runAnimId()
local a = Move.animate()
if not a then return nil end
for _, v in ipairs(a:GetChildren()) do
if v:IsA("StringValue") and v.Name:sub(1, 3) == "run" then
local anim = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Animation")
if anim and anim.AnimationId ~= "" then return anim.AnimationId end
end
end
return nil
end
function Move.reloadAnims()
local hum = getHum()
local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
if not animator then return end
pcall(function()
for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
local n = track.Name
if n == "WalkAnim" or n == "RunAnim" or n == "walk" or n == "run" then
track:Stop(0.1)
end
end
end)
end
function Move.setRunAnim(on)
local a = Move.animate()
if not a then return end
if on then
local runId = Move.runAnimId()
if not runId then return end
local changed = false
Move.animSaved = Move.animSaved or {}
for _, v in ipairs(a:GetChildren()) do
if v:IsA("StringValue") and v.Name:sub(1, 4) == "walk" then
local anim = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Animation")
if anim and anim.AnimationId ~= runId then
if Move.animSaved[anim] == nil then Move.animSaved[anim] = anim.AnimationId end
pcall(function() anim.AnimationId = runId end)
changed = true
end
end
end
if changed then Move.reloadAnims() end
elseif Move.animSaved then
local changed = false
for anim, was in pairs(Move.animSaved) do
if anim and anim.Parent then
pcall(function() anim.AnimationId = was end)
changed = true
end
end
Move.animSaved = nil
if changed then Move.reloadAnims() end
end
end
addCleanup("runanim", function() Move.setRunAnim(false) end)
Move.animCap = 1.35
function Move.holdAnimSpeed()
local hum = getHum()
local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
if not animator then return end
pcall(function()
for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
if track.Name == "WalkAnim" or track.Name == "RunAnim" then
if track.Speed > Move.animCap then track:AdjustSpeed(Move.animCap) end
end
end
end)
end
Move.SPEED_MODES = {
"Hold WalkSpeed", "Game Config", "Velocity", "Displace",
"Linear Velocity", "Impulse", "Step", "Everything",
}
Move.LOOP_MODES = {
Velocity = true, Displace = true, ["Linear Velocity"] = true,
Impulse = true, Step = true, Everything = true,
}
function Move.clearSpeedMode()
if Move.cfgTbl and Move.cfgSaved then
pcall(function()
for k, v in pairs(Move.cfgSaved) do Move.cfgTbl[k] = v end
end)
end
Move.cfgTbl, Move.cfgSaved = nil, nil
if Move.pushConn then Move.pushConn:Disconnect() Move.pushConn = nil end
if Move.lv then pcall(function() Move.lv:Destroy() end) Move.lv = nil end
if Move.lvAtt then pcall(function() Move.lvAtt:Destroy() end) Move.lvAtt = nil end
end
function Move.applyConfig()
local t = Move.cfgTbl
if not t then
t = Stam.find("STAMINA_REGEN")
if not t then return 0 end
Move.cfgTbl = t
end
Move.cfgSaved = Move.cfgSaved or {}
local n = 0
pcall(function()
for _, key in ipairs({ "WS", "RS" }) do
if type(rawget(t, key)) == "number" then
if Move.cfgSaved[key] == nil then Move.cfgSaved[key] = t[key] end
t[key] = Move.walkSpeed
n = n + 1
end
end
end)
return n
end
Move.ADDITIVE = { Displace = true, Impulse = true, Step = true }
function Move.setSpeedMode(mode)
Move.clearSpeedMode()
Move.speedMode = mode
if Move.speedOn and Move.ADDITIVE[mode] then
local hum = getHum()
if hum then pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = Move.baseWalk end) end
end
if not Move.speedOn then return end
Move.applySpeedMode()
end
function Move.applySpeedMode()
local mode = Move.speedMode
if mode == "Game Config" or mode == "Everything" then
local n = Move.applyConfig()
notify(n > 0
and ("walk speed through the game's own WS/RS - " .. n .. " set")
or "the config table is not on the heap yet - move around once", n > 0 and 4 or 6)
end
if Move.LOOP_MODES[mode] then Move.pushLoop() end
end
function Move.setSpeed(on)
Move.speedOn = on
if not on then Move.clearSpeedMode() end
if on then
local hum = getHum()
if hum and hum.WalkSpeed > 0 and hum.WalkSpeed <= 30 and not Move.boundHum then
Move.baseWalk = hum.WalkSpeed
end
Move.enforce()
Move.applySpeedMode()
else
Move.animOn = false
Move.setRunAnim(false)
local hum = getHum()
if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = Move.baseWalk end
end
end
function Move.setFovOn(on)
Move.fovOn = on
if on then
Move.enforce()
else
local c = cam()
if c then c.FieldOfView = Move.baseFov end
end
end
Move.fallClamp = 45
Move.fallKeys = { "FALL_DAMAGE", "FALL_TAKE", "FALL_HEIGHT", "FALL_MULT",
"FallDamage", "fallDamage", "fallHeight", "FALL_MIN" }
function Move.patchFallTable()
if typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then return false end
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok then return false end
if Move.fallSaved then return (Move.fallPatched or 0) > 0 end
Move.fallSaved = {}
local hits = 0
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" then
for _, key in ipairs(Move.fallKeys) do
local got, has = pcall(function() return rawget(v, key) ~= nil end)
if got and has then
Move.fallSaved[#Move.fallSaved + 1] = { t = v, k = key, was = v[key] }
pcall(function() v[key] = 0 end)
hits = hits + 1
end
end
end
end
Move.fallPatched = hits
return hits > 0
end
function Move.setNoFall(on)
Move.noFall = on
if not on then
if Move.fallConn then Move.fallConn:Disconnect() Move.fallConn = nil end
for _, rec in ipairs(Move.fallSaved or {}) do
if rec.t then pcall(function() rec.t[rec.k] = rec.was end) end
end
Move.fallSaved = nil
Move.fallStatus = "off"
return
end
Move.patchFallTable()
Move.fallStatus = (Move.fallPatched or 0) > 0
and ("holding state, and zeroed " .. Move.fallPatched .. " fall values")
or "holding state (no fall table found - that is normal)"
if Move.fallConn then return end
Move.fallConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if not Move.noFall then return end
local hum, hrp = getHum(), getHRP()
if not hum or not hrp then return end
local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
if v.Y < -6 then
local state = hum:GetState()
if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
end
if v.Y < -Move.fallClamp then
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, -Move.fallClamp, v.Z)
end
if Move.flyForce and Move.flyForce.Parent then
local want = Move.flyForce.Velocity
if want.Y < -Move.fallClamp then
Move.flyForce.Velocity = Vector3.new(want.X, -Move.fallClamp, want.Z)
end
end
end
end)
addCleanup("nofall", function() Move.setNoFall(false) end)
end
local Teleport = { busy = false, arrived = nil }
Teleport.BEAT = 0.1
function Teleport.go(cf)
if Teleport.busy then return false end
if Free and Free.on then
notify("turn FreeCam off first", 4)
return false
end
if Teleport.hiddenTo and Teleport.hiddenTo(cf) then return true end
local char, hrp = LocalPlayer.Character, getHRP()
if not (char and hrp) then return false end
Teleport.busy = true
local ok = pcall(function()
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(cf.Position) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation
end)
task.delay(Teleport.BEAT, function() Teleport.busy = false end)
if ok then Teleport.watch(cf) end
return ok
end
Teleport.WATCH = 4
Teleport.SAMPLE = 0.15
Teleport.SNAP = 14
function Teleport.watch(cf)
local id = {}
Teleport.watching = id
Teleport.arrived = true
task.spawn(function()
local until_ = os.clock() + Teleport.WATCH
local last = cf.Position
while os.clock() < until_ and Teleport.watching == id do
task.wait(Teleport.SAMPLE)
if Teleport.busy then return end
local live = getHRP()
local hum = getHum()
if not live then return end
local here = live.Position
local jumped = (here - last).Magnitude
last = here
local walking = hum and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1
if walking then return end
if jumped > Teleport.SNAP then
Teleport.arrived = false
notify(string.format("the server pulled you back %d studs", math.floor(jumped)), 6)
return
end
end
end)
end
function Teleport.toFlare()
local gun = flarePart()
if not gun then
notify("the flare gun has not spawned yet", 4)
return
end
if Teleport.go(CFrame.new(gun.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0))) and typeof(firetouchinterest) == "function" then
task.spawn(function()
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp and gun.Parent then
pcall(function()
firetouchinterest(hrp, gun, 0)
task.wait()
firetouchinterest(hrp, gun, 1)
end)
end
end)
end
end
function Teleport.toMouse()
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
local mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
if not mouse.Target then
notify("point at the ground, not the sky", 3)
return
end
Teleport.go(CFrame.new(mouse.Hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation)
end
Move.voidY = -80
function Move.setAntiVoid(on)
Move.antiVoid = on
if not on then
if Move.voidConn then Move.voidConn:Disconnect() Move.voidConn = nil end
return
end
if Move.voidConn then return end
Move.voidConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() pcall(Move.voidStep) end)
addCleanup("antivoid", function() Move.setAntiVoid(false) end)
end
function Move.voidStep()
if not Move.antiVoid then return end
local hrp, hum = getHRP(), getHum()
if not (hrp and hum) or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
if Move.fly or (Free and Free.on) or Teleport.busy or hrp.Anchored then return end
if Teleport.hidden and Teleport.hidden() then return end
local p = hrp.Position
if p.Y < Move.voidY then
if os.clock() - (Move.voidAt or 0) < 0.6 then return end
Move.voidAt = os.clock()
local back = Move.voidSafe
if not (back and back.Y > Move.voidY + 10) then back = LOCATIONS[1].pos end
if Teleport.go(CFrame.new(back + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))) then
notify("Anti Void caught you - back on the ground", 3)
end
return
end
if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and os.clock() - (Move.voidNoteAt or 0) >= 0.25 then
Move.voidNoteAt = os.clock()
Move.voidSafe = p
end
end
Free = {
on = false, keepFx = true, speed = 1.2, sens = 0.25, smooth = 0.12, turnSmooth = 0.045,
yaw = 0, pitch = 0, pos = nil, vel = Vector3.zero,
wantYaw = 0, wantPitch = 0,
anchored = nil,
anchorAt = nil,
}
local FREE_KEYS = {
[Enum.KeyCode.W] = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
[Enum.KeyCode.S] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1),
[Enum.KeyCode.A] = Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),
[Enum.KeyCode.D] = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),
[Enum.KeyCode.E] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
[Enum.KeyCode.Q] = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
}
function Free.set(on)
Free.on = on
local c = cam()
if not c then return end
if not on then
if Free.conn then Free.conn:Disconnect() Free.conn = nil end
pcall(function()
UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
c.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
local hum = getHum()
if hum then c.CameraSubject = hum end
end)
pcall(function()
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp and hrp == Free.anchored then
if Free.anchorAt then hrp.CFrame = Free.anchorAt end
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.Anchored = false
elseif Free.anchored then
pcall(function() Free.anchored.Anchored = false end)
end
end)
Free.anchored, Free.anchorAt = nil, nil
Free.pos = nil
return
end
local start = c.CFrame
Free.pos = start.Position
Free.vel = Vector3.zero
local look = start.LookVector
Free.yaw = math.atan2(-look.X, -look.Z)
Free.pitch = math.asin(math.clamp(look.Y, -1, 1))
Free.wantYaw, Free.wantPitch = Free.yaw, Free.pitch
pcall(function()
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp then
Free.anchorAt = hrp.CFrame
Free.anchored = hrp
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.Anchored = true
end
end)
c.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
if Free.conn then return end
Free.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt) pcall(Free.step, dt) end)
addCleanup("freecam", function() Free.set(false) end)
end
function Free.step(dt)
local live = cam()
if not Free.on or not live or not Free.pos then return end
local menuOpen = Library and Library.Toggled
local typing = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil
if menuOpen or typing then
UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
else
UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition
local delta = UserInputService:GetMouseDelta()
Free.wantYaw = Free.wantYaw - delta.X * Free.sens * 0.01
Free.wantPitch = math.clamp(
Free.wantPitch - delta.Y * Free.sens * 0.01, -1.4, 1.4)
end
local turn = 1 - math.exp(-dt / math.max(Free.turnSmooth, 0.001))
Free.yaw = Free.yaw + (Free.wantYaw - Free.yaw) * turn
Free.pitch = Free.pitch + (Free.wantPitch - Free.pitch) * turn
local rot = CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(Free.pitch, Free.yaw, 0)
local wanted = Vector3.zero
if not typing then
for key, dir in pairs(FREE_KEYS) do
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(key) then wanted += dir end
end
end
if wanted.Magnitude > 0 then
local boost = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 3 or 1
local slow = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) and 0.25 or 1
wanted = rot:VectorToWorldSpace(wanted.Unit) * Free.speed * 16 * boost * slow
end
local blend = 1 - math.exp(-dt / math.max(Free.smooth, 0.001))
Free.vel = Free.vel:Lerp(wanted, blend)
Free.pos = Free.pos + Free.vel * dt
local out = CFrame.new(Free.pos) * rot
if Free.keepFx and Stretch and Stretch.on then out = out * Stretch.matrix() end
live.CFrame = out
end
local Fog = { on = false, saved = nil }
function Fog.value()
local props = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("CurrentLightingProperties")
return props and props:FindFirstChild("FogEnd") or nil
end
function Fog.set(on)
Fog.on = on
local v = Fog.value()
if not on then
if Fog.conn then Fog.conn:Disconnect() Fog.conn = nil end
if v and Fog.saved ~= nil then v.Value = Fog.saved end
Fog.saved = nil
for a, was in pairs(Fog.atmos or {}) do
if a and a.Parent then pcall(function() a.Density = was.d a.Haze = was.h end) end
end
Fog.atmos = nil
return
end
if v and Fog.saved == nil then Fog.saved = v.Value end
Fog.atmos = Fog.atmos or {}
if not Fog.conn then
Fog.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Fog.on then return end
local live = Fog.value()
if live and live.Value < 9e8 then live.Value = 9e9 end
pcall(function() if Lighting.FogEnd < 9e8 then Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9 end end)
for _, a in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
if a:IsA("Atmosphere") and a.Name ~= "RKAtmos" and a.Name ~= "RKSkyAtmos" then
if not Fog.atmos[a] then Fog.atmos[a] = { d = a.Density, h = a.Haze } end
if a.Density > 0 then a.Density = 0 end
if a.Haze > 0 then a.Haze = 0 end
end
end
end)
addCleanup("fogloop", function() Fog.set(false) end)
end
if v then v.Value = 9e9 end
end
local Esp = {
rake = false, players = false, flare = false, supply = false,
location = false, scrap = false, showDistance = false,
traps = false, rusty = false,
rakeTracerThick = 1, playerTracerThick = 1,
rakeShow = { Name = true, Health = true, Chams = true, Outline = true },
playerShow = { Name = true, Chams = true, Outline = true },
fovCircle = false, fovSize = 100,
arrowRake = false, arrowPlayers = false, arrowLen = 15, arrowGap = 3,
textSize = 12,
chams = {},
chamWant = {}, chamLit = 0, chamBoxed = 0, chamHeld = 0, reserved = 0,
gathered = false, live = {},
skeleton = false, health = false,
forceLabels = nil,
pool = { text = {}, line = {}, tri = {}, label = {}, lineF = {}, rect = {} },
used = { text = 0, line = 0, tri = 0, label = 0, lineF = 0, rect = 0 },
gui = nil,
drawn = 0, running = false,
}
Esp.colors = {
traps    = Color3.fromRGB(150, 120, 255),
rusty    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60),
rake     = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
players  = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 34),
flare    = Color3.fromRGB(0, 225, 255),
supply   = Color3.fromRGB(251, 255, 0),
location = Color3.fromRGB(255, 136, 0),
scrap    = Color3.fromRGB(210, 120, 40),
}
Esp.fovColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
Esp.chamStyle = "Highlight"
Esp.chamFill = 0.35
Esp.chamOutline = 0
local CHAM_STYLES = { "Highlight", "Box" }
function Esp.canDraw()
if not HAS_DRAWING then return false end
if Esp.forceLabels ~= nil then return not Esp.forceLabels end
return not IS_MOBILE
end
function Esp.setLabels(mode)
if mode == "Labels" then
Esp.forceLabels = true
elseif mode == "Drawing" then
Esp.forceLabels = false
else
Esp.forceLabels = nil
end
for _, kind in ipairs({ "text", "line", "tri", "label", "lineF" }) do
for _, d in ipairs(Esp.pool[kind] or {}) do
pcall(function() d.Visible = false end)
end
Esp.used[kind] = 0
end
end
function Esp.board()
if Esp.gui and Esp.gui.Parent then
if Esp.boardAt and not Esp.boardMoved and os.clock() - Esp.boardAt > 1 then
Esp.boardMoved = true
local ok, size = pcall(function() return Esp.gui.AbsoluteSize end)
if ok and size and size.X < 1 then
local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if pg and Esp.gui.Parent ~= pg then
pcall(function() Esp.gui.Parent = pg end)
notify("ESP text was in a container this client does not "
.. "draw - moved it to PlayerGui", 6)
end
end
end
return Esp.gui
end
local parent = (typeof(gethui) == "function" and gethui())
or LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if not parent then return nil end
Esp.pool.label, Esp.pool.lineF, Esp.pool.rect = {}, {}, {}
Esp.circleF, Esp.silentRingF = nil, nil
Esp.gui = make("ScreenGui", {
Name = "RKEspText", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
DisplayOrder = 9998, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
}, parent)
Esp.boardAt, Esp.boardMoved = os.clock(), false
return Esp.gui
end
addCleanup("esptext", function()
if Esp.gui then pcall(function() Esp.gui:Destroy() end) Esp.gui = nil end
Esp.pool.label, Esp.pool.lineF, Esp.pool.rect = {}, {}, {}
Esp.circleF, Esp.silentRingF = nil, nil
end)
function Esp.ring(name)
local gui = Esp.board()
if not gui then return nil end
local f = make("Frame", {
Name = name, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
}, gui)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, f)
make("UIStroke", { Thickness = 1, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) }, f)
return f
end
function Esp.ringAt(f, centre, radius, colour)
if not f then return end
f.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(radius * 2, radius * 2)
local stroke = f:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
if stroke then stroke.Color = colour end
f.Visible = true
end
function Esp.text(str, pos, color)
local n = Esp.used.text + 1
Esp.used.text = n
if Esp.canDraw() and not Esp.drawTextDead then
local d = Esp.pool.text[n]
if not d then
d = newDraw("Text", { Center = true, Outline = false, Font = 2 })
if d then
Esp.pool.text[n] = d
elseif n == 1 then
Esp.drawTextDead = true
notify("Drawing text is not available - using labels instead", 5)
end
end
if d then
d.Size = Esp.textSize
d.Text = str
d.Color = color
d.Position = pos
d.Visible = true
return
end
end
local gui = Esp.board()
if not gui then return end
local l = Esp.pool.label[n]
if not l then
l = make("TextLabel", {
Name = "RKText", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4,
TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 20),
}, gui)
Esp.pool.label[n] = l
end
l.TextSize = Esp.textSize + 1
l.Text = str
l.TextColor3 = color
l.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(pos.X, pos.Y)
l.Visible = true
end
function Esp.line(from, to, color, thick, z, alpha)
if thick and thick < 1 then
alpha = (alpha or 1) * math.max(0.15, thick)
thick = 1
end
local n = Esp.used.line + 1
Esp.used.line = n
if Esp.canDraw() and not Esp.drawLineDead then
local d = Esp.pool.line[n]
if not d then
d = newDraw("Line", { Thickness = 1 })
if d then
Esp.pool.line[n] = d
elseif n == 1 then
Esp.drawLineDead = true
end
end
if d then
d.From, d.To, d.Color = from, to, color
d.Thickness = thick or 1
d.ZIndex = z or 1
d.Transparency = alpha or 1
d.Visible = true
return
end
end
local gui = Esp.board()
if not gui then return end
local f = Esp.pool.lineF[n]
if not f then
f = make("Frame", {
Name = "RKLine", BorderSizePixel = 0,
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
}, gui)
Esp.pool.lineF[n] = f
end
local d2 = to - from
local len = d2.Magnitude
if len < 1 then f.Visible = false return end
local mid = (from + to) / 2
f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(mid.X, mid.Y)
f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len, thick and math.max(1, thick) or 2)
f.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d2.Y, d2.X))
f.ZIndex = z or 1
f.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (alpha or 1)
f.BackgroundColor3 = color
f.Visible = true
end
function Esp.rect(x, y, w, h, colour, alpha)
local gui = Esp.board()
if not gui then return end
local n = Esp.used.rect + 1
Esp.used.rect = n
local f = Esp.pool.rect[n]
if not f then
f = make("Frame", { Name = "RKRect", BorderSizePixel = 0 }, gui)
Esp.pool.rect[n] = f
end
f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(x), math.floor(y))
f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, math.floor(w)), math.max(1, math.floor(h)))
f.BackgroundColor3 = colour
f.BackgroundTransparency = alpha or 0
f.Visible = true
end
function Esp.triangle(a, b, c, color)
local n = Esp.used.tri + 1
Esp.used.tri = n
if Esp.canDraw() and not Esp.drawTriDead then
local d = Esp.pool.tri[n]
if not d then
d = newDraw("Triangle", { Filled = true })
if d then
Esp.pool.tri[n] = d
elseif n == 1 then
Esp.drawTriDead = true
end
end
if d then
d.PointA, d.PointB, d.PointC, d.Color = a, b, c, color
d.Visible = true
return
end
end
Esp.line(a, b, color)
Esp.line(b, c, color)
Esp.line(c, a, color)
end
function Esp.hideRest()
Esp.used.label = Esp.used.text
Esp.used.lineF = Esp.used.line
for kind, list in pairs(Esp.pool) do
for i = Esp.used[kind] + 1, #list do
local d = list[i]
if d then pcall(function() d.Visible = false end) end
end
end
end
local function anchorPart(inst)
if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst end
if inst:IsA("Model") and inst.PrimaryPart then return inst.PrimaryPart end
return inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
Esp.CHAM_RAKE, Esp.CHAM_PLAYER, Esp.CHAM_PRIZE = 0, 1, 2
Esp.CHAM_TRAP, Esp.CHAM_SCRAP = 3, 4
Esp.CHAM_FLOOR = 6
Esp.CHAM_GRACE = 0.75
function Esp.chamBudget()
return math.max(31 - 3 - (Esp.reserved or 0) - 2, Esp.CHAM_FLOOR)
end
function Esp.crateAnchor(box, hit)
if box:IsA("BasePart") then return box end
local best, bestV, loose, looseV
for _, d in ipairs(box:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") and d ~= hit and d.Transparency < 1 then
local sz = d.Size
local v = sz.X * sz.Y * sz.Z
if not d.Anchored and (not loose or v > looseV) then loose, looseV = d, v end
if not best or v > bestV then best, bestV = d, v end
end
end
return loose or best or hit
end
function Esp.cham(inst, color, only, outlineOnly, prio, noOutline)
if not inst or not inst.Parent then return end
Esp.chamWant[#Esp.chamWant + 1] = {
inst = inst, color = color, only = only,
outlineOnly = outlineOnly, prio = prio or Esp.CHAM_SCRAP, noOutline = noOutline,
}
end
function Esp.chamAt(inst)
local ok, at = pcall(function()
if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst.Position end
return inst:GetPivot().Position
end)
return ok and at or nil
end
function Esp.applyChams(me)
local want = Esp.chamWant
local boxOnly = Esp.chamStyle ~= "Highlight"
local budget = Esp.chamBudget()
if not boxOnly and #want > budget then
for i, req in ipairs(want) do
req.seq = i
local at = Esp.chamAt(req.inst)
req.dist = (at and me) and (at - me).Magnitude or math.huge
local row = Esp.chams[req.inst]
if row and row.kind == "Highlight" then req.dist = req.dist - 40 end
end
table.sort(want, function(a, b)
if a.prio ~= b.prio then return a.prio < b.prio end
if a.dist ~= b.dist then return a.dist < b.dist end
return a.seq < b.seq
end)
end
Esp.chamLit, Esp.chamBoxed = 0, 0
for i, req in ipairs(want) do
local kind = (not boxOnly and i <= budget) and "Highlight" or "Box"
if kind == "Highlight" then Esp.chamLit = Esp.chamLit + 1
else Esp.chamBoxed = Esp.chamBoxed + 1 end
Esp.buildCham(req.inst, req.color, req.only, req.outlineOnly, kind, req.noOutline)
end
end
function Esp.buildCham(inst, color, only, outlineOnly, want, noOutline)
if not inst or not inst.Parent then return end
local row = Esp.chams[inst]
if row and (row.kind ~= want or not row.obj or not row.obj.Parent) then
pcall(function() row.obj:Destroy() end)
row, Esp.chams[inst] = nil, nil
end
if want == "Highlight" then
if not row then
local hl = make("Highlight", {
Name = "RKCham", Adornee = inst, Enabled = true,
DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop,
}, inst)
row = { kind = "Highlight", obj = hl }
Esp.chams[inst] = row
end
if row.obj.Adornee ~= inst then row.obj.Adornee = inst end
row.obj.FillColor, row.obj.OutlineColor = color, color
row.obj.FillTransparency = outlineOnly and 1 or Esp.chamFill
row.obj.OutlineTransparency = noOutline and 1 or Esp.chamOutline
row.obj.Enabled = true
return
end
local part = anchorPart(inst)
local size, cf
if only and only:IsA("BasePart") then
part = only
size = only.Size
elseif inst:IsA("Model") then
local ok, bb, bs = pcall(function()
local c, sz = inst:GetBoundingBox()
return c, sz
end)
if ok and bb then cf, size = bb, bs end
end
if not part then
Esp.chams[inst] = nil
return
end
if not row then
local box = make("BoxHandleAdornment", {
Name = "RKCham", Adornee = part, AlwaysOnTop = true, ZIndex = 1,
Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), Transparency = Esp.chamFill,
}, part)
row = { kind = "Box", obj = box }
Esp.chams[inst] = row
end
if row.obj.Adornee ~= part then
row.obj.Adornee = part
row.obj.Parent = part
end
row.obj.Size = size or (part.Size * 1.02)
row.obj.CFrame = cf and part.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(cf) or CFrame.new()
row.obj.Color3 = color
row.obj.Transparency = Esp.chamFill
row.obj.Visible = true
end
function Esp.setFarRender(on)
Esp.farRender = on
if not on then
if Esp.qualityConn then Esp.qualityConn:Disconnect() Esp.qualityConn = nil end
if Esp.qualityWas ~= nil then
pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Esp.qualityWas end)
Esp.qualityWas = nil
end
if Esp.savedQualityWas ~= nil then
pcall(function()
UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").SavedQualityLevel =
Esp.savedQualityWas
end)
Esp.savedQualityWas = nil
end
Esp.qualityHow = "off"
return
end
local function pin()
local ok = pcall(function()
local r = settings().Rendering
if Esp.qualityWas == nil then Esp.qualityWas = r.QualityLevel end
if r.QualityLevel ~= Enum.QualityLevel.Level21 then
r.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level21
end
end)
if ok then
Esp.qualityHow = "Level21 (settings)"
return
end
local ok2 = pcall(function()
local ugs = UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings")
if Esp.savedQualityWas == nil then
Esp.savedQualityWas = ugs.SavedQualityLevel
end
ugs.SavedQualityLevel = Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel10
end)
Esp.qualityHow = ok2 and "QualityLevel10 (user setting)"
or "REFUSED - this executor cannot set it, use Esc > Settings"
end
pin()
if Esp.qualityConn then return end
Esp.qualityConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Esp.farRender then return end
if os.clock() - (Esp.qualityAt or 0) < 1 then return end
Esp.qualityAt = os.clock()
pin()
end)
addCleanup("farrender", function() Esp.setFarRender(false) end)
end
function Esp.chamReadout()
local q = "unknown"
pcall(function() q = tostring(settings().Rendering.QualityLevel):gsub("Enum.QualityLevel.", "") end)
return string.format(
"chams: %d highlighted, %d boxed, %d standing | budget %d of the engine's 31"
.. (((Esp.reserved or 0) > 0) and (", %d on your weapon"):format(Esp.reserved) or "")
.. "\nrender quality: %s%s",
Esp.chamLit or 0, Esp.chamBoxed or 0, Esp.chamHeld or 0, Esp.chamBudget(), q,
Esp.farRender and (" | pinned: " .. tostring(Esp.qualityHow)) or "")
.. (((Esp.errors or 0) > 0) and string.format(
"\n%d ESP errors, last: %s", Esp.errors, tostring(Esp.lastError)) or "")
end
function Esp.clearChams(pred)
for inst, row in pairs(Esp.chams) do
if not pred or pred(inst) then
pcall(function() row.obj:Destroy() end)
Esp.chams[inst] = nil
end
end
if not pred then Esp.chamLit, Esp.chamBoxed = 0, 0 end
end
addCleanup("chams", function() Esp.clearChams() end)
local function project(pos)
local c = cam()
if not c then return nil end
local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
return Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y), on, sp.Z
end
function Esp.charBox(head, hrp)
local topWorld = (head or hrp).Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.1, 0)
local botWorld = hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
local ts, _, tz = project(topWorld)
local bs, _, bz = project(botWorld)
if not (ts and bs and tz and bz and tz > 0 and bz > 0) then return nil end
local top, bottom = math.min(ts.Y, bs.Y), math.max(ts.Y, bs.Y)
local h = bottom - top
if h < 3 then return nil end
local w = h * 0.42
local cx = (ts.X + bs.X) / 2
return { top = top, bottom = bottom, left = cx - w / 2, right = cx + w / 2, h = h, w = w }
end
Esp.BONES_R6 = {
{ "Head", "Torso" },
{ "Torso", "Left Arm" }, { "Torso", "Right Arm" },
{ "Torso", "Left Leg" }, { "Torso", "Right Leg" },
}
Esp.BONES_R15 = {
{ "Head", "UpperTorso" }, { "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso" },
{ "UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm" }, { "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm" },
{ "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand" },
{ "UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm" }, { "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm" },
{ "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" },
{ "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg" }, { "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg" },
{ "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot" },
{ "LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg" }, { "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg" },
{ "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot" },
}
Esp.skel = { style = "Outlined", thick = 1.5, colourMode = "Player", colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), head = true }
Esp.SKEL_STYLES = { "Outlined", "Plain", "Glow" }
Esp.SKEL_COLOURS = { "Player", "Custom", "Health" }
function Esp.partAt(part, y) return (part.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, y, 0)).Position end
function Esp.skeletonBones(char)
local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not (head and head:IsA("BasePart")) then head = nil end
if torso and torso:IsA("BasePart") then
local la, ra = char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm"), char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
local ll, rl = char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg"), char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
local neck, pelvis = Esp.partAt(torso, 0.9), Esp.partAt(torso, -0.9)
local out = { { neck, pelvis } }
if head then out[#out + 1] = { head.Position - head.CFrame.UpVector * 0.5, neck } end
for _, limb in ipairs({ la, ra }) do
if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") then
local sh, el, ha = Esp.partAt(limb, 0.8), Esp.partAt(limb, 0), Esp.partAt(limb, -0.95)
out[#out + 1] = { neck, sh }; out[#out + 1] = { sh, el }; out[#out + 1] = { el, ha }
end
end
for _, limb in ipairs({ ll, rl }) do
if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") then
local hip, kn, ft = Esp.partAt(limb, 0.85), Esp.partAt(limb, 0), Esp.partAt(limb, -0.95)
out[#out + 1] = { pelvis, hip }; out[#out + 1] = { hip, kn }; out[#out + 1] = { kn, ft }
end
end
return out, head
end
local out = {}
for _, bone in ipairs(Esp.BONES_R15) do
local a, b = char:FindFirstChild(bone[1]), char:FindFirstChild(bone[2])
if a and b and a:IsA("BasePart") and b:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = { a.Position, b.Position } end
end
return out, head
end
function Esp.styledLine(a, b, col, thick, style)
if style == "Outlined" then
Esp.line(a, b, Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12), thick + 2, 1, 0.85)
elseif style == "Glow" then
Esp.line(a, b, col, thick + 5, 1, 0.22)
Esp.line(a, b, col, thick + 2.5, 1, 0.35)
end
Esp.line(a, b, col, thick, 2, 1)
end
function Esp.drawSkeleton(char, colour)
local k = Esp.skel
local col = colour
if k.colourMode == "Custom" then
col = k.colour
elseif k.colourMode == "Health" then
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum and hum.MaxHealth > 0 then col = Esp.hpColour(math.clamp(hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth, 0, 1)) end
end
local bones, head = Esp.skeletonBones(char)
for _, bone in ipairs(bones) do
local sa, _, az = project(bone[1])
local sb, _, bz = project(bone[2])
if sa and sb and az and bz and az > 0 and bz > 0 then Esp.styledLine(sa, sb, col, k.thick, k.style) end
end
if k.head and head then
local c = cam()
local sc, _, hz = project(head.Position)
local se = c and project(head.Position + c.CFrame.RightVector * 0.62)
if sc and hz and hz > 0 and se then
local rad = (se - sc).Magnitude
if rad > 2 then
local prev
for i = 0, 12 do
local ang = i / 12 * math.pi * 2
local pt = sc + Vector2.new(math.cos(ang), math.sin(ang)) * rad
if prev then Esp.styledLine(prev, pt, col, k.thick, k.style) end
prev = pt
end
end
end
end
end
Esp.BOX_STYLES = { "Corners", "Full", "Filled" }
Esp.box = { player = { style = "Corners", thick = 1.5 }, rake = { style = "Full", thick = 2 } }
function Esp.modelBox(model)
local ok, cf, size = pcall(function() return model:GetBoundingBox() end)
if not (ok and cf and size) then return nil end
local hx, hy, hz = size.X / 2, size.Y / 2, size.Z / 2
local minX, minY, maxX, maxY = math.huge, math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
for _, sy in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
for _, sz in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
local sp, _, z = project((cf * CFrame.new(sx * hx, sy * hy, sz * hz)).Position)
if not (sp and z and z > 0) then return nil end
minX, maxX = math.min(minX, sp.X), math.max(maxX, sp.X)
minY, maxY = math.min(minY, sp.Y), math.max(maxY, sp.Y)
end
end
end
if maxY - minY < 3 then return nil end
return { top = minY, bottom = maxY, left = minX, right = maxX, h = maxY - minY, w = maxX - minX }
end
function Esp.drawBox(box, col, cfg)
if not box then return end
local l, t, r, b = box.left, box.top, box.right, box.bottom
local thick = cfg.thick
local function seg(x1, y1, x2, y2)
local p1, p2 = Vector2.new(x1, y1), Vector2.new(x2, y2)
Esp.line(p1, p2, Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12), thick + 2, 1, 0.85)
Esp.line(p1, p2, col, thick, 2, 1)
end
if cfg.style == "Corners" then
local cw, ch = (r - l) * 0.28, (b - t) * 0.2
seg(l, t, l + cw, t); seg(l, t, l, t + ch)
seg(r, t, r - cw, t); seg(r, t, r, t + ch)
seg(l, b, l + cw, b); seg(l, b, l, b - ch)
seg(r, b, r - cw, b); seg(r, b, r, b - ch)
else
if cfg.style == "Filled" then Esp.rect(l, t, r - l, b - t, col, 0.84) end
seg(l, t, r, t); seg(r, t, r, b); seg(r, b, l, b); seg(l, b, l, t)
end
end
function Esp.hpColour(frac)
return Color3.fromRGB(
math.floor(255 * math.min(1, 2 - frac * 2)),
math.floor(255 * math.min(1, frac * 2)),
38)
end
function Esp.drawHealth(char, box)
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not (hum and box) then return end
local maxHp = hum.MaxHealth
if type(maxHp) ~= "number" or maxHp <= 0 then return end
local frac = math.clamp(hum.Health / maxHp, 0, 1)
local x = box.right + 4
Esp.rect(x - 1, box.top - 1, 5, box.h + 2, Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 10), 0.25)
local fill = math.max(1, box.h * frac)
Esp.rect(x, box.bottom - fill, 3, fill, Esp.hpColour(frac), 0)
end
local function arrowPoints(from, dir, length)
local tip = from + dir * length
local perp = Vector2.new(-dir.Y, dir.X)
return tip, from + perp * (length / 2), from - perp * (length / 2)
end
function Esp.step()
Esp.used.text, Esp.used.line, Esp.used.tri, Esp.used.rect = 0, 0, 0, 0
Esp.live, Esp.chamWant, Esp.gathered = {}, {}, false
local c = cam()
if not c then Esp.hideRest() return end
local view = c.ViewportSize
local centre = Vector2.new(view.X / 2, view.Y / 2)
local foot = Vector2.new(view.X / 2, view.Y)
local me = myPos()
local live = Esp.live
if Fight.silentRing then
if not Esp.silentRingF then Esp.silentRingF = Esp.ring("RKSilentRing") end
local live = Fight.silent and Fight.silentOff
and Fight.silentOff <= (Fight.silentFov or 360) / 2
Esp.ringAt(Esp.silentRingF, centre, Fight.fovRadius(c),
live and Color3.fromRGB(140, 255, 170) or Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255))
elseif Esp.silentRingF then
Esp.silentRingF.Visible = false
end
if Esp.fovCircle then
if not Esp.circleF then Esp.circleF = Esp.ring("RKFovRing") end
Esp.ringAt(Esp.circleF, centre, Esp.fovSize, Esp.fovColor)
elseif Esp.circleF then
Esp.circleF.Visible = false
end
local arrowOffset = Esp.fovSize + Esp.arrowGap
local r = rake()
local rakeHrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if rakeHrp and not rakeHrp:IsA("BasePart") then rakeHrp = nil end
if r and rakeHrp then
local hum = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local show = Esp.rakeShow
if Esp.rake then
if show.Chams or show.Outline then
Esp.cham(r, Esp.colors.rake, nil, not show.Chams, Esp.CHAM_RAKE, not show.Outline)
live[r] = true
end
if show.Name or show.Health or show.Distance then
local head = r:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not (head and head:IsA("BasePart")) then head = rakeHrp end
local sp, on = project(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
if sp and on then
local bits = {}
if show.Name then bits[#bits + 1] = "Rake" end
if show.Health then
bits[#bits + 1] = hum and string.format("HP %d / %d", math.floor(Fight.rakeHp(hum)), math.floor(hum.MaxHealth)) or "HP ?"
end
if show.Distance and me then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("%dm", math.floor((rakeHrp.Position - me).Magnitude))
end
Esp.text(table.concat(bits, " | "), sp, Esp.colors.rake)
end
end
if show.Box then Esp.drawBox(Esp.modelBox(r), Esp.colors.rake, Esp.box.rake) end
if show.Tracer then
local sp, on = project(rakeHrp.Position)
if sp and on then Esp.line(foot, sp, Esp.colors.rake, Esp.rakeTracerThick) end
end
end
if Esp.arrowRake then
local sp, on, z = project(rakeHrp.Position)
if sp and not on then
local d = sp - centre
if z and z < 0 then d = -d end
if d.Magnitude > 0.01 then
local dir = d.Unit
local a, b, cc = arrowPoints(centre + dir * arrowOffset, dir, Esp.arrowLen)
Esp.triangle(a, b, cc, Esp.colors.rake)
end
end
end
end
local pshow = Esp.playerShow
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local head = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
if hrp and not hrp:IsA("BasePart") then hrp = nil end
if head and not head:IsA("BasePart") then head = nil end
if hrp then
if Esp.players then
if pshow.Chams or pshow.Outline then
Esp.cham(plr.Character, Esp.colors.players, nil, not pshow.Chams, Esp.CHAM_PLAYER, not pshow.Outline)
live[plr.Character] = true
end
if pshow.Name or pshow.Distance then
local sp, on = project((head or hrp).Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.6, 0))
if sp and on then
local bits = {}
if pshow.Name then bits[#bits + 1] = plr.Name end
if pshow.Distance and me then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("%dm", math.floor((hrp.Position - me).Magnitude))
end
Esp.text(table.concat(bits, " | "), sp, Esp.colors.players)
end
end
if pshow.Skeleton then Esp.drawSkeleton(plr.Character, Esp.colors.players) end
if pshow.Box or pshow["Health Bar"] then
local box = Esp.charBox(head, hrp)
if pshow.Box then Esp.drawBox(box, Esp.colors.players, Esp.box.player) end
if pshow["Health Bar"] then Esp.drawHealth(plr.Character, box) end
end
if pshow.Tracer then
local sp, on = project(hrp.Position)
if sp and on then Esp.line(foot, sp, Esp.colors.players, Esp.playerTracerThick) end
end
end
if Esp.arrowPlayers then
local sp, on, z = project(hrp.Position)
if sp and not on then
local d = sp - centre
if z and z < 0 then d = -d end
if d.Magnitude > 0.01 then
local dir = d.Unit
local a, b, cc = arrowPoints(centre + dir * arrowOffset, dir, Esp.arrowLen)
Esp.triangle(a, b, cc, Esp.colors.players)
end
end
end
end
end
end
if Esp.flare then
for _, m in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
if m.Name == "FlareGunPickUp" then
local gun = m:FindFirstChild("FlareGun")
if not (gun and gun:IsA("BasePart")) then
gun = m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
if gun then
Esp.cham(m, Esp.colors.flare, nil, false, Esp.CHAM_PRIZE)
live[m] = true
local sp, on = project(gun.Position)
if sp and on then
local label = "Flare Gun"
if Esp.showDistance and me then
label = label .. string.format(" | %dm", math.floor((gun.Position - me).Magnitude))
end
Esp.text(label, sp, Esp.colors.flare)
end
end
end
end
end
if Esp.supply then
local crates = pathTo(Workspace, "Debris", "SupplyCrates")
if crates then
for _, box in ipairs(crates:GetChildren()) do
local hit = box:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if not (hit and hit:IsA("BasePart")) then
hit = (box:IsA("BasePart") and box)
or box:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
if hit then
Esp.cham(box, Esp.colors.supply, hit, true, Esp.CHAM_PRIZE)
live[box] = true
local at = Esp.crateAnchor(box, hit)
local sp, on = project(at.Position)
if sp and on then
local label = "Supply Drop"
if Esp.showDistance and me then
label = label .. string.format(" | %dm", math.floor((at.Position - me).Magnitude))
end
Esp.text(label, sp, Esp.colors.supply)
end
end
end
end
end
if Esp.scrap then
local spawns = pathTo(Workspace, "Filter", "ScrapSpawns")
if spawns then
for _, d in ipairs(spawns:GetDescendants()) do
if d.Name == "Scrap" and d:IsA("BasePart") then
Esp.cham(d, Esp.colors.scrap, nil, false, Esp.CHAM_SCRAP)
live[d] = true
local sp, on = project(d.Position)
if sp and on then
local parent = d.Parent
local pts = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("PointsVal")
local lvl = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("LevelVal")
local label = "Scrap"
if pts and pts:IsA("ValueBase") then
label = label .. ", Points " .. tostring(pts.Value)
end
if lvl and lvl:IsA("ValueBase") then
label = label .. ", Level " .. tostring(lvl.Value)
end
if Esp.showDistance and me then
label = label .. string.format(" | %dm", math.floor((d.Position - me).Magnitude))
end
Esp.text(label, sp, Esp.colors.scrap)
end
end
end
end
end
if Esp.traps or Esp.rusty then
for _, m in ipairs(trapModels()) do
local isRusty = m:GetAttribute("ServerSpawned") == true
local wanted = if isRusty then Esp.rusty else Esp.traps
if wanted then
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if not (box and box:IsA("BasePart")) then
box = m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
if box then
local key = isRusty and "rusty" or "traps"
Esp.cham(m, Esp.colors[key], nil, false, Esp.CHAM_TRAP)
live[m] = true
local sp, on = project(box.Position)
if sp and on then
local label = isRusty and "RUSTY TRAP" or "Rake Trap"
if Esp.showDistance and me then
label = label .. string.format(" | %dm", math.floor((box.Position - me).Magnitude))
end
Esp.text(label, sp, Esp.colors[key])
end
end
end
end
end
if Esp.location then
for _, loc in ipairs(LOCATIONS) do
local sp, on = project(loc.pos)
if sp and on then
local label = "[LOCATION] " .. loc.name
if Esp.showDistance and me then
label = label .. string.format(" | %dm", math.floor((loc.pos - me).Magnitude))
end
Esp.text(label, sp, Esp.colors.location)
end
end
end
Esp.gathered = true
end
function Esp.finish()
local c = cam()
Esp.applyChams(myPos() or (c and c.CFrame.Position) or nil)
local live = Esp.live or {}
local now = os.clock()
local held = 0
for inst, row in pairs(Esp.chams) do
local gone = not inst.Parent
if not gone and Esp.gathered and not live[inst] then
row.missing = row.missing or now
gone = (now - row.missing) >= Esp.CHAM_GRACE
elseif live[inst] then
row.missing = nil
end
if gone then
pcall(function() row.obj:Destroy() end)
Esp.chams[inst] = nil
else
held = held + 1
end
end
Esp.chamHeld = held
Esp.drawn = Esp.used.text + Esp.used.line + Esp.used.tri + Esp.used.rect
Esp.hideRest()
end
function Esp.refreshRunning()
local want = Fight.silentRing
or Esp.rake or Esp.players or Esp.flare or Esp.supply or Esp.location
or Esp.scrap or Esp.traps or Esp.rusty
or Esp.fovCircle or Esp.arrowRake or Esp.arrowPlayers
if want and not Esp.conn then
Esp.running = true
Esp.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local ok, err = pcall(Esp.step)
if not ok then
Esp.lastError = tostring(err)
Esp.errors = (Esp.errors or 0) + 1
end
pcall(Esp.finish)
end)
addCleanup("esploop", function()
if Esp.conn then Esp.conn:Disconnect() Esp.conn = nil end
end)
elseif not want and Esp.conn then
Esp.conn:Disconnect()
Esp.conn = nil
Esp.running = false
Esp.used.text, Esp.used.line, Esp.used.tri, Esp.used.rect = 0, 0, 0, 0
Esp.hideRest()
if Esp.circleF then Esp.circleF.Visible = false end
if Esp.silentRingF then Esp.silentRingF.Visible = false end
Esp.clearChams()
end
end
function Esp.set(field, value)
Esp[field] = value
Esp.refreshRunning()
if not value then
Esp.clearChams()
end
end
local HpBar = { on = false }
function HpBar.build()
if HpBar.back or not HAS_DRAWING then return end
HpBar.back = newDraw("Square", { Filled = true, Thickness = 1,
Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50) })
HpBar.fill = newDraw("Square", { Filled = true, Thickness = 1,
Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0) })
HpBar.text = newDraw("Text", { Size = 14, Center = true, Outline = true,
Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) })
HpBar.info = newDraw("Text", { Size = 14, Center = true, Outline = true,
Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) })
end
function HpBar.hide()
for _, k in ipairs({ "back", "fill", "text", "info" }) do
local d = HpBar[k]
if d then pcall(function() d.Visible = false end) end
end
end
function HpBar.set(on)
HpBar.on = on
HpBar.build()
if not on then
HpBar.hide()
if HpBar.conn then HpBar.conn:Disconnect() HpBar.conn = nil end
return
end
if HpBar.conn then return end
HpBar.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not HpBar.on or not HpBar.back then return end
local r, c = rake(), cam()
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not (r and hrp and hum and c) then HpBar.hide() return end
local view = c.ViewportSize
local w, h, margin = 150, 15, 20
local origin = Vector2.new(view.X - w - margin, view.Y - h - margin)
local perc = math.clamp(Fight.rakeHp(hum) / math.max(hum.MaxHealth, 1), 0, 1)
HpBar.back.Size, HpBar.back.Position = Vector2.new(w, h), origin
HpBar.fill.Size, HpBar.fill.Position = Vector2.new(w * perc, h), origin
HpBar.fill.Color = perc > 0.5
and Color3.new(1 - (perc - 0.5) * 2, 1, 0)
or Color3.new(1, perc * 2, 0)
HpBar.text.Text = string.format("%d / %d", math.floor(Fight.rakeHp(hum)), math.floor(hum.MaxHealth))
HpBar.text.Position = origin + Vector2.new(w / 2, h / 2 - 7)
local me = myPos()
local dist = me and math.floor((hrp.Position - me).Magnitude) or 0
local target = r:FindFirstChild("TargetVal")
local who = target and target.Value and target.Value.Parent
HpBar.info.Text = string.format("%d studs   %s", dist,
who and ("after " .. tostring(who)) or "no target")
HpBar.info.Position = origin + Vector2.new(w / 2, -18)
HpBar.back.Visible, HpBar.fill.Visible = true, true
HpBar.text.Visible, HpBar.info.Visible = true, true
end)
addCleanup("hpbar", function()
if HpBar.conn then HpBar.conn:Disconnect() HpBar.conn = nil end
HpBar.hide()
end)
end
Fight = {
aura = false, silent = false, silentHits = 0,
toolName = "StunStick", equipRange = 100,
mode = "Default", burst = 3, healBack = 10, healWait = 12,
struck = 0, waitingFrom = nil, waitingSince = nil,
range = 200, gap = 0.1, last = 0, swings = 0, status = "off",
hitbox = false, hitboxSize = 12,
noCooldown = false, noBreak = false, antiBlock = false, patchStatus = "off",
noAnim = false,
fastGap = 0.05,
reach = false,
trigger = false, triggerGap = 0.5, TRIGGER_REACH = 16, TRIGGER_FOV = 100,
triggerLast = 0, triggerSwings = 0, triggerStatus = "off",
}
function Fight.setAntiBlock(on)
Fight.antiBlock = on
if on then Fight.holdHitPart() end
end
Fight.BACK_DOT = -0.65
function Fight.onBack(rh, pos)
local to = pos - rh.Position
to = Vector3.new(to.X, 0, to.Z)
local lv = rh.CFrame.LookVector
lv = Vector3.new(lv.X, 0, lv.Z)
if to.Magnitude < 0.1 or lv.Magnitude < 0.1 then return false end
return lv.Unit:Dot(to.Unit) < Fight.BACK_DOT
end
function Fight.behindCF(rh, from)
local back = -rh.CFrame.LookVector
back = Vector3.new(back.X, 0, back.Z)
back = back.Magnitude > 0.05 and back.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
local right = Vector3.new(-back.Z, 0, back.X)
local side = (from and (from - rh.Position):Dot(right) >= 0) and 1 or -1
local params = RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { rake(), LocalPlayer.Character }
local first
for _, deg in ipairs({ 0, 35 * side, -35 * side }) do
local a = math.rad(deg)
local spot = rh.Position + (back * math.cos(a) + right * math.sin(a)) * 6 - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
local cf = CFrame.new(spot, rh.Position)
first = first or cf
local ok, wall = pcall(function() return Workspace:Raycast(rh.Position, spot - rh.Position, params) end)
if ok and not wall then return cf end
end
return first
end
function Fight.hitSpot(target)
if Fight.antiBlock then
local r = rake()
local rh = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if rh and rh:IsA("BasePart") then
return rh.Position - rh.CFrame.LookVector * 3 - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
end
end
return target and target.Position or Vector3.zero
end
function Fight.rakeHp(hum)
if Fight.instaWriting and Fight.instaHp then return Fight.instaHp end
return hum.Health
end
function Fight.aimHitPart(target)
Fight.lastTarget = target
local tool = Fight.stick()
local hitPart = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("HitPart")
if hitPart and target then
pcall(function() hitPart.Position = Fight.hitSpot(target) end)
end
end
function Fight.lookAngle(c, at)
local to = at - c.CFrame.Position
if to.Magnitude < 0.01 then return 0 end
local dot = math.clamp(c.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(to.Unit), -1, 1)
return math.deg(math.acos(dot))
end
function Fight.fovRadius(c)
local half = (Fight.silentFov or 360) / 2
local view = c.ViewportSize
local corner = math.sqrt(view.X * view.X + view.Y * view.Y) / 2
if half <= 0 then return 0 end
if half >= 89 then return corner end
local vhalf = math.rad(c.FieldOfView) / 2
local r = (view.Y / 2) * math.tan(math.rad(half)) / math.tan(vhalf)
return math.min(r, corner)
end
function Fight.silentTarget()
local r = rake()
local me = myPos()
if not (r and me) then return nil end
local hum = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum and Fight.rakeHp(hum) <= 0 then return nil end
local c = cam()
local half = (Fight.silentFov or 360) / 2
local best, bestOff, bestDist
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
local dist = (d.Position - me).Magnitude
if dist <= Fight.range then
local off = c and Fight.lookAngle(c, d.Position) or 0
if off <= half and (not bestOff or off < bestOff) then
best, bestOff, bestDist = d, off, dist
end
end
end
end
Fight.silentOff = bestOff
if not best then return nil end
return Fight.aimTarget(r) or best
end
function Fight.setSilent(on)
Fight.silent = on
Fight.silentHits = 0
if on then Fight.trackMouse() Fight.trackActivated() Fight.holdHitPart() end
if not on then
if Fight.silentConn then Fight.silentConn:Disconnect() Fight.silentConn = nil end
return
end
if Fight.silentConn then return end
Fight.silentConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Fight.silent then return end
if not Fight.wantSwing() then return end
if os.clock() - math.max(Fight.silentLast or 0, Fight.spamLast or 0) < Fight.gap then return end
if not Fight.stick() then return end
local target = Fight.silentTarget()
if not target then return end
Fight.silentLast = os.clock()
Fight.aimHitPart(target)
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
if Fight.swing(target) then
Fight.silentHits = (Fight.silentHits or 0) + 1
end
end)
addCleanup("silentaim", function()
if Fight.silentConn then Fight.silentConn:Disconnect() Fight.silentConn = nil end
end)
end
Fight.AIM_MODES = { "Head", "Body" }
Fight.aimAt = "Head"
Fight.HEAD_PARTS = { "Head", "HeadBase", "HMesh" }
Fight.BODY_PARTS = { "HumanoidRootPart", "Torso", "TorsoM" }
function Fight.aimTarget(r)
local want = (Fight.aimAt == "Body") and Fight.BODY_PARTS or Fight.HEAD_PARTS
for _, name in ipairs(want) do
local p = r:FindFirstChild(name)
if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then return p end
end
local other = (Fight.aimAt == "Body") and Fight.HEAD_PARTS or Fight.BODY_PARTS
for _, name in ipairs(other) do
local p = r:FindFirstChild(name)
if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then return p end
end
return nil
end
Fight.silentFov = 120
Fight.silentRing = false
Fight.silentOff = nil
Fight.coolKeys = { "COOLDOWN", "ATTACK_COOLDOWN", "SWING_COOLDOWN", "HIT_COOLDOWN",
"AttackCooldown", "attackCooldown", "cooldown", "DEBOUNCE", "Debounce" }
Fight.breakKeys = { "MALFUNCTION", "MALFUNCTION_CHANCE", "BREAK_CHANCE",
"MalfunctionChance", "malfunction", "FAIL_CHANCE", "JAM_CHANCE" }
local DEBOUNCE_NAMES = { db = true, DB = true, Debounce = true, debounce = true,
Cooldown = true, cooldown = true, CD = true }
local BREAK_NAMES = { Broken = true, broken = true, Jammed = true, jammed = true,
Malfunction = true, malfunction = true, Disabled = true }
function Fight.patchTool()
local tool = Fight.stick()
if not tool then return 0, 0 end
local cool, broke = 0, 0
if (Fight.noCooldown or Fight.noBreak) and tool:IsA("Tool") and not tool.Enabled then
pcall(function() tool.Enabled = true end)
if Fight.noBreak then
broke = broke + 1
Fight.unjammed = (Fight.unjammed or 0) + 1
else
cool = cool + 1
end
end
for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BoolValue") then
if Fight.noCooldown and DEBOUNCE_NAMES[d.Name] and d.Value == true then
pcall(function() d.Value = false end)
cool = cool + 1
elseif Fight.noBreak and BREAK_NAMES[d.Name] and d.Value == true then
pcall(function() d.Value = false end)
broke = broke + 1
end
elseif d:IsA("NumberValue") or d:IsA("IntValue") then
local lower = string.lower(d.Name)
if Fight.noCooldown and d.Value ~= 0
and (lower:find("cool", 1, true) or lower:find("time", 1, true)
or lower:find("delay", 1, true) or lower:find("rate", 1, true)) then
pcall(function() d.Value = 0 end)
cool = cool + 1
elseif Fight.noBreak and d.Value ~= 0
and (lower:find("chance", 1, true) or lower:find("malf", 1, true)) then
pcall(function() d.Value = 0 end)
broke = broke + 1
end
end
end
local ok, attrs = pcall(function() return tool:GetAttributes() end)
if ok and attrs then
for key, value in pairs(attrs) do
local lower = string.lower(key)
if Fight.noCooldown and type(value) == "number" and value ~= 0
and (lower:find("cool", 1, true) or lower:find("time", 1, true)) then
pcall(function() tool:SetAttribute(key, 0) end)
cool = cool + 1
elseif Fight.noCooldown and value == true and DEBOUNCE_NAMES[key] then
pcall(function() tool:SetAttribute(key, false) end)
cool = cool + 1
elseif Fight.noBreak and BREAK_NAMES[key] and value == true then
pcall(function() tool:SetAttribute(key, false) end)
broke = broke + 1
end
end
end
return cool, broke
end
function Fight.patchWeapon()
local toolCool, toolBreak = Fight.patchTool()
local gcCool, gcBreak = 0, 0
if typeof(getgc) == "function" then
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if ok then
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" then
if Fight.noCooldown then
for _, key in ipairs(Fight.coolKeys) do
if type(rawget(v, key)) == "number" then
pcall(rawset, v, key, 0)
gcCool = gcCool + 1
end
end
end
if Fight.noBreak then
for _, key in ipairs(Fight.breakKeys) do
if type(rawget(v, key)) == "number" then
pcall(rawset, v, key, 0)
gcBreak = gcBreak + 1
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
local bits = {}
if Fight.noCooldown then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("cooldown: %d on the tool, %d on the heap", toolCool, gcCool)
end
if Fight.noBreak then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("un-jammed %d times, %d heap writes",
Fight.unjammed or 0, gcBreak)
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("held back %d of our swings", Fight.paced or 0)
bits[#bits + 1] = "(paces the menu's swings, not your own clicks)"
end
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("swinging every %.2fs", Fight.gap or 0.1)
Fight.patchStatus = #bits > 0 and table.concat(bits, "\n") or "off"
return (toolCool + toolBreak + gcCool + gcBreak) > 0
end
function Fight.setAutoEquip(on)
Fight.autoEquip = on
if not on then
if Fight.equipConn then Fight.equipConn:Disconnect() Fight.equipConn = nil end
return
end
if Fight.equipConn then return end
Fight.equipConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Fight.autoEquip then return end
if os.clock() - (Fight.equipLast or 0) < 0.4 then return end
Fight.equipLast = os.clock()
local char, hum = LocalPlayer.Character, getHum()
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if not (char and hum and bp) then return end
local r = rake()
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local me = myPos()
local close = hrp and me and (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude <= Fight.equipRange
local held = char:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName)
local stowed = bp:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName)
if close and not held and stowed and not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(stowed) end)
elseif not close and held then
pcall(function() hum:UnequipTools() end)
end
end)
addCleanup("autoequip", function() Fight.setAutoEquip(false) end)
end
function Fight.setNoCooldown(on)
Fight.noCooldown = on
if not on then
if Fight.spamConn then Fight.spamConn:Disconnect() Fight.spamConn = nil end
if not Fight.noBreak then Fight.setPatch("noBreak", false) end
return
end
Fight.setPatch("noCooldown", true)
