Fight.trackMouse()
Fight.trackActivated()
if Fight.spamConn then return end
Fight.spamConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Fight.noCooldown then return end
if not Fight.wantSwing() then return end
if os.clock() - math.max(Fight.spamLast or 0, Fight.silentLast or 0) < (Fight.gap or 0.1) then return end
local tool, ev = Fight.stick()
if not tool then return end
local target = Fight.silentTarget()
if not target then
Fight.spamLast = os.clock()
if Fight.onSwing then pcall(Fight.onSwing) end
if ev then pcall(function() ev:FireServer("S") end) end
return
end
Fight.spamLast = os.clock()
Fight.aimHitPart(target)
Fight.swing(target)
end)
Fight.holdHitPart()
addCleanup("nocooldown", function() Fight.setNoCooldown(false) end)
end
Fight.SWING_ANIMS = { SwingAnim = true, Swing = true, AttackAnim = true }
function Fight.cutSwingAnim()
local hum = getHum()
if not hum then return end
local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
local ok, tracks = pcall(function()
if animator then return animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() end
return hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
end)
if not (ok and tracks) then return end
for _, track in ipairs(tracks) do
local name = tostring(track.Name or "")
if Fight.SWING_ANIMS[name] then
pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
end
end
end
function Fight.setNoAnim(on)
Fight.noAnim = on
if not on then
if Fight.noAnimConn then Fight.noAnimConn:Disconnect() Fight.noAnimConn = nil end
return
end
if Fight.noAnimConn then return end
Fight.noAnimConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Fight.noAnim then return end
Fight.cutSwingAnim()
end)
addCleanup("noswinganim", function()
if Fight.noAnimConn then Fight.noAnimConn:Disconnect() Fight.noAnimConn = nil end
end)
end
local SwingFx = { on = false, style = "Soft", speed = 1, size = 1, t0 = nil }
SwingFx.PRESETS = {
Soft     = { dur = 0.42, keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.32, 26, 6, 6, 0.1 }, { 0.66, -10, -4, 0, 0 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
Short    = { dur = 0.24, keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.38, 20, -6, 0, 0.15 }, { 0.72, -6, 0, 0, 0 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
Slash    = { dur = 0.46, keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.22, 14, -38, 12, 0 }, { 0.52, -6, 55, -6, 0.3 }, { 0.78, -4, 22, 0, 0.1 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
Overhead = { dur = 0.55, keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.34, 72, 0, 8, -0.1 }, { 0.58, -55, 4, 0, 0.35 }, { 0.8, -22, 0, 0, 0.1 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
Jab      = { dur = 0.3,  keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.18, 4, 0, 0, -0.35 }, { 0.42, 6, -4, 0, 1.3 }, { 0.62, 4, -2, 0, 1.0 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
Heavy    = { dur = 0.8,  keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.45, 85, -22, 12, -0.2 }, { 0.6, -68, 26, -4, 0.45 }, { 0.78, -52, 18, 0, 0.2 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
Flick    = { dur = 0.18, keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.4, -18, 12, -8, 0.15 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
Uppercut = { dur = 0.48, keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.3, -42, 6, 4, 0 }, { 0.6, 68, -8, 0, 0.3 }, { 0.82, 24, -2, 0, 0.1 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
Windmill = { dur = 0.6,  keys = { { 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 }, { 0.25, 40, 0, 30, 0 }, { 0.5, 0, 0, 55, 0.2 }, { 0.75, -40, 0, 28, 0 }, { 1, 0, 0, 0, 0 } } },
}
SwingFx.NAMES = { "Soft", "Short", "Slash", "Overhead", "Jab", "Heavy", "Flick", "Uppercut", "Windmill" }
function SwingFx.joint()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if not char then return nil end
local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
local j = torso and torso:FindFirstChild("Right Shoulder")
if not j then
local ua = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm")
j = ua and ua:FindFirstChild("RightShoulder")
end
if j and j:IsA("Motor6D") then return j end
return nil
end
function SwingFx.sample(keys, t, c)
local n = #keys
if t <= keys[1][1] then return keys[1][c] end
if t >= keys[n][1] then return keys[n][c] end
local i = 1
while i < n - 1 and t > keys[i + 1][1] do i = i + 1 end
local k0, k1 = keys[i], keys[i + 1]
local km, kp = keys[i - 1] or k0, keys[i + 2] or k1
local h = k1[1] - k0[1]
local u = (t - k0[1]) / h
local m0 = (i == 1) and 0 or (k1[c] - km[c]) / (k1[1] - km[1]) * h
local m1 = (i + 1 == n) and 0 or (kp[c] - k0[c]) / (kp[1] - k0[1]) * h
local u2, u3 = u * u, u * u * u
return (2 * u3 - 3 * u2 + 1) * k0[c] + (u3 - 2 * u2 + u) * m0
+ (-2 * u3 + 3 * u2) * k1[c] + (u3 - u2) * m1
end
local Shoulder = { j = nil, orig = nil, conn = nil }
function Shoulder.release()
if Shoulder.j and Shoulder.orig then
local j, o = Shoulder.j, Shoulder.orig
pcall(function() j.C0 = o end)
end
Shoulder.j, Shoulder.orig, Shoulder.wrote = nil, nil, nil
end
function Shoulder.step()
local swinging = SwingFx.t0 ~= nil
if not swinging then Shoulder.release() return end
local j = SwingFx.joint()
if not j then return end
if j ~= Shoulder.j then
Shoulder.release()
Shoulder.j, Shoulder.orig = j, j.C0
elseif Shoulder.wrote and j.C0 ~= Shoulder.wrote then
Shoulder.orig = j.C0
end
local c0 = SwingFx.pose(Shoulder.orig) or Shoulder.orig
if j.C0 ~= c0 then pcall(function() j.C0 = c0 end) end
Shoulder.wrote = c0
end
function Shoulder.refresh()
local want = SwingFx.on
if want and not Shoulder.conn then
local ok = pcall(function()
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RKShoulder", Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, function() pcall(Shoulder.step) end)
end)
Shoulder.conn = ok and "bound" or RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function() pcall(Shoulder.step) end)
addCleanup("shoulder", function()
Shoulder.unbind()
Shoulder.release()
end)
elseif not want and Shoulder.conn then
Shoulder.unbind()
SwingFx.t0 = nil
Shoulder.release()
end
end
function Shoulder.unbind()
if Shoulder.conn == "bound" then
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RKShoulder") end)
elseif Shoulder.conn then
pcall(function() Shoulder.conn:Disconnect() end)
end
Shoulder.conn = nil
end
function SwingFx.restore()
SwingFx.t0 = nil
end
function SwingFx.trigger()
if not SwingFx.on then return end
local p = SwingFx.PRESETS[SwingFx.style]
if not p then return end
local now = os.clock()
if SwingFx.t0 and now - SwingFx.t0 < (p.dur / SwingFx.speed) * 0.55 then return end
if not SwingFx.joint() then return end
SwingFx.t0 = now
pcall(Fight.cutSwingAnim)
end
function SwingFx.pose(base)
local p = SwingFx.PRESETS[SwingFx.style]
if not (p and SwingFx.t0) then SwingFx.t0 = nil return nil end
local t = (os.clock() - SwingFx.t0) / (p.dur / SwingFx.speed)
if t >= 1 then SwingFx.t0 = nil return nil end
local k, sz = p.keys, SwingFx.size
local pitch = math.rad(SwingFx.sample(k, t, 2) * sz)
local yaw = math.rad(SwingFx.sample(k, t, 3) * sz)
local roll = math.rad(SwingFx.sample(k, t, 4) * sz)
local push = SwingFx.sample(k, t, 5) * sz
return CFrame.new(base.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, -push)) * CFrame.Angles(pitch, yaw, roll) * (base - base.Position)
end
local HandView = { on = false, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0, rx = 0, ry = 0, rz = 0, showArm = true,
grip = nil, gripOrig = nil, findAt = 0 }
function HandView.findGrip()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
if not tool then return nil end
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if (d:IsA("Motor6D") or d:IsA("Weld")) and d.Part0 and d.Part1 then
if d.Part1:IsDescendantOf(tool) and not d.Part0:IsDescendantOf(tool) then return d end
end
end
return nil
end
function HandView.restoreGrip()
if HandView.grip and HandView.gripOrig then
local g, o = HandView.grip, HandView.gripOrig
pcall(function() g.C0 = o end)
end
HandView.grip, HandView.gripOrig = nil, nil
end
function HandView.step()
if not HandView.on then return end
local grip = HandView.grip
if os.clock() - HandView.findAt > 0.25 or not (grip and grip.Parent) then
HandView.findAt = os.clock()
grip = HandView.findGrip()
end
if grip ~= HandView.grip then
HandView.restoreGrip()
if grip then HandView.grip, HandView.gripOrig = grip, grip.C0 end
end
local c = cam()
if not (grip and c and grip.Part0) then return end
local o = HandView.gripOrig
local camRot = c.CFrame - c.CFrame.Position
local armRot = grip.Part0.CFrame - grip.Part0.CFrame.Position
local move = armRot:VectorToObjectSpace(camRot:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(HandView.x, HandView.y, -HandView.z)))
local turn = armRot:Inverse() * camRot
* CFrame.Angles(math.rad(HandView.rx), math.rad(HandView.ry), math.rad(HandView.rz))
* camRot:Inverse() * armRot
pcall(function() grip.C0 = CFrame.new(o.Position + move) * turn * (o - o.Position) end)
end
local NoArms = { on = false }
NoArms.ARMS = { "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand",
"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" }
function NoArms.body()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local out = {}
if not char then return out end
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if (d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Decal")) and not d:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool") then
out[#out + 1] = d
end
end
return out
end
function NoArms.parts(which)
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local out = {}
if not char then return out end
local set = {}
for _, n in ipairs(which or NoArms.ARMS) do
local p = char:FindFirstChild(n)
if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = p; set[p] = true end
end
for _, acc in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
if acc:IsA("Accessory") then
local h = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
local w = h and h:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld")
if w and (set[w.Part0] or set[w.Part1]) then out[#out + 1] = h end
end
end
return out
end
function HandView.armStep()
if NoArms.on then
for _, p in ipairs(NoArms.body()) do
if p.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= 1 then p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end
end
elseif HandView.on and HandView.showArm then
for _, p in ipairs(NoArms.parts({ "Right Arm", "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand" })) do
if p.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= 0 then p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end
end
end
end
function HandView.refreshArms()
local want = NoArms.on or (HandView.on and HandView.showArm)
if want and not HandView.armBound then
local ok = pcall(function()
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RKArms", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function() pcall(HandView.armStep) end)
end)
HandView.armBound = ok and "bound" or RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function() pcall(HandView.armStep) end)
addCleanup("arms", function()
NoArms.on, HandView.on = false, false
HandView.refreshArms()
end)
elseif not want and HandView.armBound then
if HandView.armBound == "bound" then
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RKArms") end)
else
pcall(function() HandView.armBound:Disconnect() end)
end
HandView.armBound = nil
for _, p in ipairs(NoArms.body()) do pcall(function() p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end) end
end
end
function HandView.set(on)
HandView.on = on
if on and not HandView.bound then
local ok = pcall(function()
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RKHandView", Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, HandView.step)
end)
HandView.bound = ok and "bound" or RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(HandView.step)
addCleanup("handview", function() HandView.set(false) end)
elseif not on and HandView.bound then
if HandView.bound == "bound" then
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RKHandView") end)
else
pcall(function() HandView.bound:Disconnect() end)
end
HandView.bound = nil
HandView.restoreGrip()
end
HandView.refreshArms()
end
function NoArms.set(on)
local was = NoArms.on
NoArms.on = on
HandView.refreshArms()
if was and not on and HandView.armBound then
for _, part in ipairs(NoArms.body()) do pcall(function() part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end) end
end
end
function SwingFx.watch()
local hum = getHum()
local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
if animator and animator ~= SwingFx.animator then
if SwingFx.animConn then SwingFx.animConn:Disconnect() end
SwingFx.animator = animator
SwingFx.animConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
if not SwingFx.on then return end
if Fight.SWING_ANIMS[tostring(track.Name or "")] then
pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
SwingFx.trigger()
end
end)
end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local tool = char and char:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName)
if tool and tool ~= SwingFx.tool then
if SwingFx.toolConn then SwingFx.toolConn:Disconnect() end
SwingFx.tool = tool
SwingFx.toolConn = tool.Activated:Connect(SwingFx.trigger)
end
end
function SwingFx.set(on)
SwingFx.on = on
Fight.onSwing = on and SwingFx.trigger or nil
if not on then
SwingFx.restore()
for _, k in ipairs({ "conn", "animConn", "toolConn" }) do
if SwingFx[k] then SwingFx[k]:Disconnect() SwingFx[k] = nil end
end
SwingFx.animator, SwingFx.tool = nil, nil
Shoulder.refresh()
return
end
Shoulder.refresh()
if SwingFx.conn then return end
SwingFx.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not SwingFx.on then return end
if os.clock() - (SwingFx.watchAt or 0) > 0.5 then SwingFx.watchAt = os.clock(); pcall(SwingFx.watch) end
end)
addCleanup("swingfx", function() SwingFx.set(false) end)
end
function SwingFx.preview()
local was = SwingFx.on
if not was then SwingFx.set(true) end
SwingFx.t0 = nil
SwingFx.trigger()
if not was then
local p = SwingFx.PRESETS[SwingFx.style]
task.delay((p and p.dur or 0.5) / SwingFx.speed + 0.1, function()
if not Toggles.RK_SwingFx or not Toggles.RK_SwingFx.Value then SwingFx.set(false) end
end)
end
end
function Fight.repace()
if Fight.noBreak then
Fight.gap = math.max(Fight.safeGap, 0.1)
elseif Fight.noCooldown then
Fight.gap = Fight.fastGap or 0.05
else
Fight.gap = 0.1
end
end
function Fight.setPatch(field, value)
Fight[field] = value
if field == "noBreak" and value then Fight.unjammed, Fight.paced = 0, 0 end
if Fight.noCooldown or Fight.noBreak then
if not Fight.patchConn then
Fight.patchConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not (Fight.noCooldown or Fight.noBreak) then return end
Fight.patchTool()
if os.clock() - (Fight.patchLast or 0) < 3 then return end
Fight.patchLast = os.clock()
Fight.patchWeapon()
end)
addCleanup("weaponpatch", function()
if Fight.patchConn then Fight.patchConn:Disconnect() Fight.patchConn = nil end
end)
end
Fight.patchWeapon()
else
if Fight.patchConn then Fight.patchConn:Disconnect() Fight.patchConn = nil end
Fight.patchStatus = "off"
end
Fight.repace()
end
function Fight.trackActivated()
if Fight.actBound then return end
Fight.actBound = true
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
local tool = Fight.stick()
if tool and Fight.actTool ~= tool then
Fight.actTool = tool
pcall(function() tool.Activated:Connect(function() Fight.heldAt = os.clock() end) end)
end
end)
end
function Fight.wantSwing()
return Fight.mb1 or (os.clock() - (Fight.heldAt or -1e9) < 0.3)
end
function Fight.trackMouse()
if Fight.mouseBound then return end
Fight.mouseBound = true
Fight.touchN = 0
local function isClick(t)
return t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end
bind(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gp)
local t = input.UserInputType
if t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then Fight.touchN = Fight.touchN + 1 end
if t == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and not gp then Fight.mb1 = true end
end)
bind(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
local t = input.UserInputType
if t == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then Fight.touchN = math.max(Fight.touchN - 1, 0) end
if isClick(t) then Fight.mb1 = false end
end)
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
if not Fight.wantSwing() then return end
local mouseDown = false
pcall(function()
mouseDown = UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
end)
if not mouseDown and (Fight.touchN or 0) <= 0 then Fight.mb1 = false end
end)
end
function Fight.stick()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local tool = char and char:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName)
return tool, tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Event") or nil
end
function Fight.landHit(target)
local live = select(2, Fight.stick())
if not (live and target) then return false end
if Fight.antiBlock then
local r = rake()
local rh = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local myHrp = getHRP()
local function fire()
local liveH = select(2, Fight.stick())
if liveH then pcall(function() liveH:FireServer("H", target) end) end
end
local spot = (rh and myHrp) and Fight.behindCF(rh, myHrp.Position)
local hop = spot and myHrp and (spot.Position - myHrp.Position).Magnitude
if not (rh and myHrp and spot) or Fight.flanking
or hop > 25 or (hop < 2.5 and Fight.onBack(rh, myHrp.Position)) then
fire()
else
Fight.flanking = true
local home = myHrp.CFrame
pcall(function()
myHrp.CFrame = spot
myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
end)
task.wait()
if not Fight.onBack(rh, myHrp.Position) then
pcall(function()
myHrp.CFrame = Fight.behindCF(rh, myHrp.Position)
myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
end)
task.wait()
end
fire()
task.wait()
local back = getHRP()
if back then pcall(function() back.CFrame = home back.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end) end
Fight.flanking = false
end
else
pcall(function() live:FireServer("H", target) end)
end
return true
end
Fight.safeGap = 0.55
function Fight.swing(target, keepAnim)
local tool, event = Fight.stick()
if not (tool and event and target) then return false, "notool" end
if Fight.noBreak then
local now = os.clock()
if now - (Fight.swungAt or 0) < Fight.safeGap - 0.01 then
Fight.paced = (Fight.paced or 0) + 1
return false, "paced"
end
Fight.swungAt = now
end
if Fight.flanking then return false, "flanking" end
if Fight.onSwing then pcall(Fight.onSwing) end
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function() event:FireServer("S") end)
task.wait()
Fight.landHit(target)
end)
Fight.stickAt = os.clock()
if Fight.noAnim and not keepAnim then
task.defer(Fight.cutSwingAnim)
end
return true
end
function Fight.holdHitPart()
if Fight.holdConn then return end
Fight.holdConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local tool = Fight.stick()
local hitPart = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("HitPart")
if not hitPart then return end
local function rakeHRP()
local r = rake()
return r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local target
if Fight.aura then
target = Fight.lastTarget
if not (target and target.Parent) then target = rakeHRP() end
elseif Fight.silent then
target = Fight.silentTarget()
elseif Fight.trigger then
local me = myPos()
local r2 = rake()
local thum = r2 and r2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hrp = r2 and r2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if thum and Fight.rakeHp(thum) > 0 and me and hrp
and (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude <= Fight.TRIGGER_REACH then
target = hrp
end
elseif Fight.hitbox then
target = Fight.lastTarget
if not (target and target.Parent) then target = rakeHRP() end
local me = myPos()
if not (me and target and (target.Position - me).Magnitude <= Fight.hitboxSize) then
target = nil
end
elseif Fight.antiBlock then
target = rakeHRP()
end
if not (target and target.Parent) then return end
pcall(function() hitPart.Position = Fight.hitSpot(target) end)
end)
addCleanup("hitpart", function()
if Fight.holdConn then Fight.holdConn:Disconnect() Fight.holdConn = nil end
end)
end
function Fight.expanderTarget()
if not Fight.hitbox then return nil end
local r = rake()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local me = myPos()
if not (hum and hrp and me) or Fight.rakeHp(hum) <= 0 then return nil end
if (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude > Fight.hitboxSize then return nil end
return hrp
end
function Fight.onGameSwing()
if Fight.aura or Fight.silent then return end
if os.clock() - (Fight.expAt or 0) < 0.3 then return end
if not Fight.expanderTarget() then return end
Fight.expAt = os.clock()
Fight.expSwings = (Fight.expSwings or 0) + 1
task.spawn(function()
task.wait()
local target = Fight.expanderTarget()
if target then Fight.landHit(target) end
end)
end
function Fight.watchSwings(on)
if not on then
if Fight.swingWatch then Fight.swingWatch:Disconnect() Fight.swingWatch = nil end
if Fight.swingAnimConn then Fight.swingAnimConn:Disconnect() Fight.swingAnimConn = nil end
Fight.swingAnimator = nil
return
end
if Fight.swingWatch then return end
Fight.swingWatch = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if os.clock() - (Fight.swingLook or 0) < 0.5 then return end
Fight.swingLook = os.clock()
local hum = getHum()
local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
if not animator or animator == Fight.swingAnimator then return end
if Fight.swingAnimConn then Fight.swingAnimConn:Disconnect() end
Fight.swingAnimator = animator
Fight.swingAnimConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
if Fight.SWING_ANIMS[tostring(track.Name or "")] and Fight.stick() then
Fight.onGameSwing()
end
end)
end)
addCleanup("swingwatch", function() Fight.watchSwings(false) end)
end
function Fight.enforce()
if Fight.conn then return end
Fight.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local r = rake()
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if Fight.aura and hrp and os.clock() - Fight.last >= Fight.gap then
Fight.last = os.clock()
local me = myPos()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if me and (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude < Fight.range then
local allowed = true
if Fight.mode == "Smart" and hum then
if Fight.waitingFrom then
local healed = Fight.rakeHp(hum) - Fight.waitingFrom
local waited = os.clock() - (Fight.waitingSince or 0)
if healed >= Fight.healBack or waited >= Fight.healWait then
Fight.waitingFrom, Fight.waitingSince = nil, nil
Fight.struck = 0
else
allowed = false
Fight.status = string.format("letting it heal  %d / %d",
math.max(math.floor(healed), 0), Fight.healBack)
end
elseif Fight.struck >= Fight.burst then
Fight.waitingFrom = Fight.rakeHp(hum)
Fight.waitingSince = os.clock()
allowed = false
end
end
if allowed then
Fight.aimHitPart(hrp)
local hit, why = Fight.swing(hrp)
if hit then
Fight.swings = Fight.swings + 1
Fight.struck = Fight.struck + 1
Fight.status = Fight.mode == "Smart"
and string.format("hit %d of %d", Fight.struck, Fight.burst)
or string.format("swinging  (%d)", Fight.swings)
elseif why == "paced" then
Fight.status = string.format("holding back - %.2fs between swings",
Fight.gap or 0.1)
else
Fight.status = "no StunStick equipped"
end
end
else
Fight.status = "out of range"
end
elseif Fight.aura and not hrp then
Fight.status = "no Rake in the world"
Fight.struck, Fight.waitingFrom = 0, nil
end
if Fight.trigger and hrp then
local me = myPos()
local thum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not (thum and Fight.rakeHp(thum) > 0) then
Fight.triggerStatus = "off"
elseif me and (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude <= Fight.TRIGGER_REACH then
local look = cam()
if look and Fight.lookAngle(look, hrp.Position) > Fight.TRIGGER_FOV then
Fight.triggerStatus = "off"
elseif os.clock() - (Fight.triggerLast or 0) >= Fight.triggerGap then
Fight.triggerLast = os.clock()
local tool = Fight.stick()
if not tool then
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char and not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local stowed = bp and bp:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName)
local h = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if h and stowed then pcall(function() h:EquipTool(stowed) end) end
tool = Fight.stick()
end
end
if tool then
Fight.aimHitPart(hrp)
pcall(function() tool.Enabled = true tool:Activate() end)
Fight.triggerSwings = (Fight.triggerSwings or 0) + 1
Fight.triggerStatus = string.format("swinging  (%d)", Fight.triggerSwings)
else
Fight.triggerStatus = "no StunStick"
end
end
else
Fight.triggerStatus = "off"
end
elseif Fight.trigger and not hrp then
Fight.triggerStatus = "off"
end
end)
addCleanup("fightloop", function()
if Fight.conn then Fight.conn:Disconnect() Fight.conn = nil end
local r = rake()
local box = r and r:FindFirstChild("RKHitbox")
if box then pcall(function() box:Destroy() end) end
end)
end
function Fight.setTrigger(on)
Fight.trigger = on
Fight.triggerSwings = 0
if on then
Fight.holdHitPart()
Fight.enforce()
Fight.triggerStatus = "on"
else
Fight.triggerStatus = "off"
if not Fight.aura and Fight.conn then
Fight.conn:Disconnect() Fight.conn = nil
end
end
end
function Fight.set(field, value)
Fight[field] = value
if (field == "aura" or field == "hitbox") and value then Fight.holdHitPart() end
if field == "hitbox" then Fight.watchSwings(value) end
if Fight.aura then
Fight.enforce()
elseif Fight.conn and not Fight.trigger then
Fight.conn:Disconnect()
Fight.conn = nil
local r = rake()
local box = r and r:FindFirstChild("RKHitbox")
if box then pcall(function() box:Destroy() end) end
end
if field == "aura" and not value then Fight.status = "off" end
end
function Fight.setReach(on)
Fight.reach = on
if on then
if not Fight.circle then
Fight.circle = make("Part", {
Name = "RKReach", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
Transparency = 0.6,
Size = Vector3.new(Fight.hitboxSize * 2, 0.2, Fight.hitboxSize * 2),
}, Workspace)
make("CylinderMesh", {}, Fight.circle)
end
Fight.reachConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp and Fight.circle then
Fight.circle.Size = Vector3.new(Fight.hitboxSize * 2, 0.2, Fight.hitboxSize * 2)
Fight.circle.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
end
end)
addCleanup("reach", function() Fight.setReach(false) end)
else
if Fight.reachConn then Fight.reachConn:Disconnect() Fight.reachConn = nil end
if Fight.circle then pcall(function() Fight.circle:Destroy() end) Fight.circle = nil end
end
end
local Ex = { hidden = false, hidePart = nil, instaCrate = false, killRake = false, status = "",
flareFrom = nil, flareStatus = "not fired yet",
flingCrate = false, flingStatus = "off",
flingRake = false, flingRakeStatus = "off",
trapArea = false, trapAreaStatus = "off" }
function Ex.safeDoor()
local remote = pathTo(Workspace, "Map", "SafeHouse", "Door", "RemoteEvent")
if not remote then
notify("the safe house door remote is not there", 4)
return
end
pcall(function() remote:FireServer("Door") end)
end
function Ex.safeDoorParts()
local door = pathTo(Workspace, "Map", "SafeHouse", "Door")
local out = {}
if door then
if door:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = door end
for _, d in ipairs(door:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = d end
end
end
return out
end
function Ex.setDoorNoclip(on)
Ex.doorNoclip = on
if not on then
if Ex.doorNoclipConn then Ex.doorNoclipConn:Disconnect() Ex.doorNoclipConn = nil end
for p, was in pairs(Ex.doorNoclipSaved or {}) do
if p and p.Parent then pcall(function() p.CanCollide = was end) end
end
Ex.doorNoclipSaved = nil
notify("safe house door noclip off", 3)
return
end
local parts = Ex.safeDoorParts()
if #parts == 0 then
notify("no safe house door in this map", 4)
Ex.doorNoclip = false
return
end
Ex.doorNoclipSaved = Ex.doorNoclipSaved or {}
for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
if Ex.doorNoclipSaved[p] == nil then Ex.doorNoclipSaved[p] = p.CanCollide end
pcall(function() p.CanCollide = false end)
end
if Ex.doorNoclipConn then return end
Ex.doorNoclipConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.doorNoclip then return end
for _, p in ipairs(Ex.safeDoorParts()) do
if Ex.doorNoclipSaved[p] == nil then Ex.doorNoclipSaved[p] = p.CanCollide end
if p.CanCollide then pcall(function() p.CanCollide = false end) end
end
end)
notify("safe house door noclip on - walk straight through it", 4)
addCleanup("doornoclip", function() Ex.setDoorNoclip(false) end)
end
function Ex.openHouseDoor()
task.spawn(function()
local hrp = getHRP()
local door = pathTo(Workspace, "Map", "SafeHouse", "Door", "Door")
local remote = pathTo(Workspace, "Map", "SafeHouse", "Door", "RemoteEvent")
if not (hrp and door and remote) then
notify("cannot reach the safe house door from here", 4)
return
end
Ex.rootHeld = (Ex.rootHeld or 0) + 1
local back = hrp.CFrame
pcall(function()
hrp.CFrame = door.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, -7, 0)
task.wait()
hrp.Anchored = true
task.wait(0.4)
pcall(function() remote:FireServer("Door") end)
task.wait(0.4)
local live = getHRP()
if live then
live.CFrame = back
task.wait()
live.Anchored = false
end
end)
Ex.rootHeld = math.max(0, (Ex.rootHeld or 1) - 1)
end)
end
function Ex.restorePower()
task.spawn(function()
local hrp = getHRP()
local remote = pathTo(Workspace, "Map", "PowerStation", "StationFolder", "RemoteEvent")
if not (hrp and remote) then
notify("the power station remote is not there", 4)
return
end
notify("holding position at the station for 20 seconds", 6)
for _ = 1, 30 do
local live = getHRP()
if not live then break end
live.CFrame = POWER_STATION_CF
task.wait()
end
Ex.rootHeld = (Ex.rootHeld or 0) + 1
local live = getHRP()
if live then live.Anchored = true end
pcall(function() remote:FireServer("StationStart") end)
task.wait(20)
local after = getHRP()
if after then after.Anchored = false end
Ex.rootHeld = math.max(0, (Ex.rootHeld or 1) - 1)
notify("power sequence finished", 4)
end)
end
function Ex.openCrate(box)
if typeof(box) == "Instance" then
local unlock = box:FindFirstChild("UnlockValue")
if unlock then pcall(function() unlock.Value = 100 end) end
local prompt = pathTo(box, "GUIPart", "ProximityPrompt")
if prompt then
pcall(function()
for key in pairs(prompt:GetAttributes()) do
prompt:SetAttribute(key, false)
end
end)
Ex.firePrompt(prompt)
end
end
local remote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SupplyClientEvent")
if not remote then
notify("SupplyClientEvent is not there", 4)
return
end
pcall(function() remote:FireServer("Open", true) end)
end
function Ex.setInstaCrate(on)
Ex.instaCrate = on
if not on then
if Ex.crateConn then Ex.crateConn:Disconnect() Ex.crateConn = nil end
return
end
if Ex.crateConn then return end
Ex.crateConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.instaCrate then return end
local crates = pathTo(Workspace, "Debris", "SupplyCrates")
if not crates then return end
for _, box in ipairs(crates:GetChildren()) do
local prompt = pathTo(box, "GUIPart", "ProximityPrompt")
if prompt then
pcall(function()
for key in pairs(prompt:GetAttributes()) do
prompt:SetAttribute(key, false)
end
prompt.HoldDuration = 0
end)
end
local unlock = box:FindFirstChild("UnlockValue")
if unlock then pcall(function() unlock.Value = 100 end) end
end
end)
addCleanup("crateloop", function()
if Ex.crateConn then Ex.crateConn:Disconnect() Ex.crateConn = nil end
end)
end
Ex.autoGet = { Vest = false, UV_Lamp = false, StunStick = false }
Ex.autoGetRange = 20
Ex.autoGetConn = nil
function Ex.autoGetAny()
return Ex.autoGet.Vest or Ex.autoGet.UV_Lamp or Ex.autoGet.StunStick
end
function Ex.alreadyHave(name)
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
return (char and char:FindFirstChild(name) ~= nil)
or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name) ~= nil)
end
function Ex.grabWanted()
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not (hrp and Ex.autoGetAny()) then return end
local look = {}
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do look[#look + 1] = d end
local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
if debris then
for _, d in ipairs(debris:GetDescendants()) do look[#look + 1] = d end
end
for _, d in ipairs(look) do
local name = d.Name
if Ex.autoGet[name] and not Ex.alreadyHave(name) then
local part = d:IsA("BasePart") and d
or (d:IsA("Model") and (d.PrimaryPart or d:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")))
or (d:IsA("Tool") and d:FindFirstChild("Handle"))
local owner = d:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
owner = owner and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(owner) ~= nil
if part and not owner
and (part.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= Ex.autoGetRange then
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function()
firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 0)
task.wait()
firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 1)
end)
end)
end
end
end
end
function Ex.setAutoGet(name, on)
Ex.autoGet[name] = on and true or false
if not Ex.autoGetAny() then
if Ex.autoGetConn then Ex.autoGetConn:Disconnect() Ex.autoGetConn = nil end
return
end
if Ex.autoGetConn then return end
Ex.autoGetConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.autoGetAny() then return end
if os.clock() - (Ex.autoGetAt or 0) < 0.15 then return end
Ex.autoGetAt = os.clock()
pcall(Ex.grabWanted)
end)
addCleanup("autoget", function()
if Ex.autoGetConn then Ex.autoGetConn:Disconnect() Ex.autoGetConn = nil end
end)
end
function Ex.cutTouches(part)
local cut = {}
if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then return cut end
for _, signal in ipairs({ "Touched", "TouchEnded" }) do
local ok, conns = pcall(function() return getconnections(part[signal]) end)
if ok and type(conns) == "table" then
for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
pcall(function()
if c.Disable then c:Disable() cut[#cut + 1] = c end
end)
end
end
end
Ex.trapCut = Ex.trapCut or {}
Ex.trapCut[part] = cut
return cut
end
function Ex.reTouches(part)
local cut = Ex.trapCut and Ex.trapCut[part]
if not cut then return end
for _, c in ipairs(cut) do
pcall(function() if c.Enable then c:Enable() end end)
end
Ex.trapCut[part] = nil
end
Ex.trapRadius = 16
function Ex.setAntiTrap(on)
Ex.antiTrap = on
if not on then
if Ex.trapConn then Ex.trapConn:Disconnect() Ex.trapConn = nil end
for part, was in pairs(Ex.trapParts or {}) do
if part and part.Parent then pcall(function() part.CanTouch = was end) end
Ex.reTouches(part)
end
Ex.trapParts, Ex.trapCut = {}, {}
Ex.antiTrapStatus = "off"
return
end
Ex.trapParts = Ex.trapParts or {}
Ex.trapCut = Ex.trapCut or {}
if Ex.trapConn then return end
Ex.trapConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.antiTrap then return end
if os.clock() - (Ex.trapLast or 0) < 0.25 then return end
Ex.trapLast = os.clock()
local me = myPos()
if not me then return end
local near = 0
local rk = Ex.instaArmed and rake()
local rkRoot = rk and rk:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local rakeAt = rkRoot and rkRoot.Position
for _, m in ipairs(trapModels()) do
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if m == Ex.killTrap or (Ex.trapPlayers and (m == Ex.trapWantModel
or (Ex.trapBusy and (Ex.trapBusy[m] or 0) > os.clock())))
or (rakeAt and box and box:IsA("BasePart") and Ex.instaArmed
and (Ex.instaAny or m:GetAttribute("Player") == LocalPlayer.Name)
and (box.Position - rakeAt).Magnitude <= Ex.killCatch) then
for _, part in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") and Ex.trapParts[part] ~= nil then
local was = Ex.trapParts[part]
pcall(function() part.CanTouch = was end)
Ex.reTouches(part)
Ex.trapParts[part] = nil
end
end
elseif box and box:IsA("BasePart")
and (box.Position - me).Magnitude <= Ex.trapRadius then
near = near + 1
for _, part in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
if Ex.trapParts[part] == nil then
Ex.trapParts[part] = part.CanTouch
Ex.cutTouches(part)
end
if part.CanTouch then part.CanTouch = false end
end
end
end
end
Ex.antiTrapStatus = near > 0 and (near .. " disarmed near you") or "clear"
end)
addCleanup("antitrap", function() Ex.setAntiTrap(false) end)
end
Ex.floatHeight = 20
Ex.floatSize = 14
function Ex.floatPad()
if Ex.floatPart and Ex.floatPart.Parent then return Ex.floatPart end
Ex.floatPart = make("Part", {
Name = "RKFloat", Anchored = true, CanCollide = true, CanQuery = false,
Transparency = 0.65, Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 170, 255),
Size = Vector3.new(Ex.floatSize, 1, Ex.floatSize),
Position = Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0),
}, Workspace)
return Ex.floatPart
end
function Ex.groundY(from)
local params = RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
local skip = { Ex.floatPart }
if LocalPlayer.Character then skip[#skip + 1] = LocalPlayer.Character end
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = skip
local hit
pcall(function()
hit = Workspace:Raycast(from, Vector3.new(0, -300, 0), params)
end)
return hit and hit.Position.Y or (from.Y - 5)
end
function Ex.setFloat(on)
Ex.float = on
if not on then
if Ex.floatConn then Ex.floatConn:Disconnect() Ex.floatConn = nil end
if Ex.floatPart then Ex.floatPart.Position = Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0) end
return
end
Ex.floatPad()
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then
notify("no character to float", 4)
Ex.float = false
return
end
local groundY = Ex.groundY(hrp.Position)
local top = groundY + Ex.floatHeight
Ex.floatPart.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, top, hrp.Position.Z)
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char then
pcall(function()
char:PivotTo(CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, top + 3.5, hrp.Position.Z))
end)
end
if Ex.floatConn then return end
Ex.floatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.float then return end
local live = getHRP()
local pad = Ex.floatPart
if not (live and pad and pad.Parent) then return end
if Move.fly or Free.on or Ex.hidden or live.Anchored then return end
local d = live.Position - pad.Position
if math.abs(d.Y - 3.5) > 6 then return end
if math.abs(d.X) > Ex.floatSize or math.abs(d.Z) > Ex.floatSize then return end
pad.Position = Vector3.new(live.Position.X, pad.Position.Y, live.Position.Z)
end)
addCleanup("float", function() Ex.setFloat(false) end)
end
addCleanup("floatpart", function()
if Ex.floatPart then pcall(function() Ex.floatPart:Destroy() end) Ex.floatPart = nil end
end)
function Ex.supplyBox()
local crates = pathTo(Workspace, "Debris", "SupplyCrates")
if not crates then return nil, nil end
local me = myPos()
local bestBox, bestPart, bestD
for _, box in ipairs(crates:GetChildren()) do
local part
if box:IsA("BasePart") then
part = box
elseif box:IsA("Model") and box.PrimaryPart then
part = box.PrimaryPart
else
local any
for _, d in ipairs(box:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
if not d.Anchored then part = d break end
any = any or d
end
end
part = part or any
end
if part then
local dst = me and (part.Position - me).Magnitude or 0
if not bestD or dst < bestD then bestBox, bestPart, bestD = box, part, dst end
end
end
return bestBox, bestPart
end
Ex.FLING_SPIN = 90
Ex.FLING_UP   = 160
Ex.FLING_OUT  = 750
Ex.flingRange = 25
function Ex.setFlingCrate(on)
Ex.flingCrate = on
if not on then
if Ex.flingConn then Ex.flingConn:Disconnect() Ex.flingConn = nil end
for p in pairs(Ex.flingCollided or {}) do
if p and p.Parent then pcall(function() p.CanCollide = true end) end
end
Ex.flingCollided = {}
Ex.flingStatus, Ex.flingPart, Ex.flingFrom = "off", nil, nil
return
end
if Ex.flingConn then return end
Ex.flingFrom, Ex.flingPart = nil, nil
Ex.flingAt, Ex.flingStatus = 0, "looking for a crate"
Ex.flingCollided = Ex.flingCollided or {}
Ex.flingConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.flingCrate then return end
local model, part = Ex.supplyBox()
if not part then
Ex.flingStatus = "no supply drop in the world"
Ex.flingFrom, Ex.flingPart = nil, nil
return
end
if Ex.flingPart ~= part then Ex.flingPart, Ex.flingFrom = part, nil end
local me = myPos()
local away = me and (part.Position - me).Magnitude or math.huge
if away > Ex.flingRange then
Ex.flingStatus = string.format("get closer - %.0fm away (within %dm flings it)",
away, Ex.flingRange)
return
end
pcall(function()
local dir = me and (part.Position - me) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
dir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
dir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
local vel = dir * Ex.FLING_OUT + Vector3.new(0, Ex.FLING_UP, 0)
local spin = Vector3.new(Ex.FLING_SPIN, Ex.FLING_SPIN, Ex.FLING_SPIN)
local parts = (model and model:IsA("Model")) and model:GetDescendants() or { part }
for _, d in ipairs(parts) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
if d.CanCollide and Ex.flingCollided[d] == nil then Ex.flingCollided[d] = true end
d.CanCollide = false
if d.Anchored then d.Anchored = false end
d.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel
d.AssemblyAngularVelocity = spin
end
end
if model and model.PivotTo then model:PivotTo(model:GetPivot() + dir * 6 + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)) end
end)
local now = os.clock()
if not Ex.flingFrom then
Ex.flingFrom, Ex.flingAt = part.Position, now
return
end
local since = now - Ex.flingAt
if since >= 0.4 then
local moved = (part.Position - Ex.flingFrom).Magnitude
Ex.flingFrom, Ex.flingAt = part.Position, now
Ex.flingStatus = string.format("%s - %.0fm/s (%s)",
moved > 3 and "FLINGING it away" or "not moving (too far to own it?)",
moved / since, model and model.Name or "crate")
end
end)
addCleanup("flingcrate", function() Ex.setFlingCrate(false) end)
end
Ex.FLING_RAKE_UP, Ex.FLING_RAKE_OUT, Ex.FLING_RAKE_SPIN, Ex.FLING_RAKE_GAP = 220, 170, 40, 0.35
function Ex.flingRakeTick()
if typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then
pcall(sethiddenproperty, LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", 1000)
end
local r = rake()
local root = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not (root and root:IsA("BasePart")) then
Ex.flingRakeStatus = "no Rake in the world"
return
end
local ours = false
pcall(function() ours = (not root.Anchored) and root.ReceiveAge <= 0.005 end)
if not ours then
Ex.flingRakeStatus = "the server is running his physics - get close to him (or trap him near you)"
if not Ex.flingRakeTold then
Ex.flingRakeTold = true
notify("Fling Rake: get close to the Rake - he is launched the moment your game runs his physics", 6)
end
return
end
local now = os.clock()
if now - (Ex.flingRakeAt or 0) < Ex.FLING_RAKE_GAP then return end
Ex.flingRakeAt = now
local me = myPos() or root.Position
local away = root.Position - me
away = Vector3.new(away.X, 0, away.Z)
away = away.Magnitude > 0.1 and away.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
local hum = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
pcall(function()
if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown) end
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = away * Ex.FLING_RAKE_OUT + Vector3.new(0, Ex.FLING_RAKE_UP, 0)
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(Ex.FLING_RAKE_SPIN, Ex.FLING_RAKE_SPIN * 0.6, 0)
end)
Ex.flingRakeCount = (Ex.flingRakeCount or 0) + 1
Ex.flingRakeStatus = "launched him (" .. Ex.flingRakeCount .. ")"
if now - (Ex.flingRakeSaidAt or 0) > 3 then
Ex.flingRakeSaidAt = now
notify("Flung the Rake", 3)
end
end
function Ex.setFlingRake(on)
Ex.flingRake = on
if not on then
if Ex.flingRakeConn then Ex.flingRakeConn:Disconnect() Ex.flingRakeConn = nil end
Ex.flingRakeStatus = "off"
return
end
if Ex.flingRakeConn then return end
Ex.flingRakeAt, Ex.flingRakeCount, Ex.flingRakeTold = 0, 0, false
if typeof(sethiddenproperty) ~= "function" then
notify("Fling Rake: this executor has no sethiddenproperty - you have to be right next to him", 6)
end
Ex.flingRakeConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if Ex.flingRake then pcall(Ex.flingRakeTick) end
end)
addCleanup("flingrake", function() Ex.setFlingRake(false) end)
end
function Ex.haveFlare()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
return (char and char:FindFirstChild("FlareGun") ~= nil)
or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("FlareGun") ~= nil)
end
function Ex.flareTool()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local held = char and char:FindFirstChild("FlareGun")
if held and held:IsA("Tool") then return held, true end
local stowed = bp and bp:FindFirstChild("FlareGun")
if stowed and stowed:IsA("Tool") then return stowed, false end
return nil, false
end
Ex.FLARE_ORIGINS = { "FlarePos", "MuzzlePart" }
function Ex.flareMuzzle(tool)
if not tool then return nil, "no flare gun" end
for _, name in ipairs(Ex.FLARE_ORIGINS) do
local part = tool:FindFirstChild(name, true)
if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
return part, name
end
end
return nil, "no FlarePos or MuzzlePart inside the tool"
end
function Ex.holdPart(part, where, seconds)
local until_ = os.clock() + seconds
local conn
conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if os.clock() > until_ or not (part and part.Parent) then
if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
return
end
pcall(function() part.Position = where end)
end)
return conn
end
function Ex.flareOrigin()
local name = Ex.flareFrom
if not name or name == "" then return nil, "no player picked" end
local plr = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
local char = plr and plr.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return nil, name .. " is not in the world right now" end
return hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), name
end
function Ex.flareBlocked()
local drop = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SupplyDB")
if drop then
local ok, v = pcall(function() return drop.Value end)
if not ok or v ~= false then
return "a supply drop is already on its way - the gun will not "
.. "signal twice"
end
end
local blood = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("InitiateBloodHour")
if blood then
local ok, v = pcall(function() return blood.Value end)
if ok and v == true then return "Blood Hour - the gun will not signal" end
end
return nil
end
function Ex.fireFlare()
local tool, equipped = Ex.flareTool()
if not tool then
notify("no flare gun on you - Auto Flare collects one", 5)
return
end
local spent = tool:FindFirstChild("FiredValue")
if spent and spent:IsA("BoolValue") and spent.Value == true then
Ex.flareStatus = "this flare gun has already been fired"
notify(Ex.flareStatus, 5)
return
end
local blocked = Ex.flareBlocked()
if blocked then
Ex.flareStatus = blocked
notify(blocked, 6)
return
end
if not equipped then
local hum = getHum()
if not hum then return end
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
task.wait(0.2)
tool = select(1, Ex.flareTool())
if not tool then return end
end
local where, who = Ex.flareOrigin()
task.spawn(function()
if where then
local fired = tool:FindFirstChild("FiredValue")
local real, fake
Teleport.busy = true
local function above()
local live = Ex.flareOrigin()
return CFrame.new((live or where) + Vector3.new(0, 12, 0))
end
local hb = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local live = getHRP()
if not live then return end
real = live.CFrame
fake = above()
pcall(function() live.CFrame = fake end)
end)
local rs = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local live = getHRP()
if live and real then pcall(function() live.CFrame = real end) end
end)
local shot = false
pcall(function()
for _ = 1, 5 do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
local flare = tool:FindFirstChild("FlareGunServer")
flare = flare and flare:FindFirstChild("FlarePart")
local drop = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SupplyDB")
local function launched()
if not flare then return false end
local ok, v = pcall(function()
local trail = flare:FindFirstChildOfClass("Trail")
return (flare.Position - Vector3.new(1000, 1000, 1000)).Magnitude > 25
or (trail ~= nil and trail.Enabled == true)
end)
return ok and v == true
end
local t0, goneAt = os.clock(), nil
while os.clock() - t0 < 6 do
if fired and fired:IsA("BoolValue") and fired.Value == true then shot = true end
local called = false
pcall(function() called = drop ~= nil and drop.Value == true end)
if called then break end
if not goneAt and launched() then goneAt = os.clock() end
if goneAt and os.clock() - goneAt >= 0.6 then break end
if not flare and shot and os.clock() - t0 >= 1.4 then break end
RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
end
end)
hb:Disconnect()
rs:Disconnect()
local back = getHRP()
if back and real and fake and (back.Position - fake.Position).Magnitude < 0.05 then
pcall(function() back.CFrame = real end)
end
Teleport.busy = false
Ex.flareStatus = (shot or not fired)
and ("fired from above " .. tostring(who) .. " - the drop should land on them")
or ("held above " .. tostring(who) .. " but the shot did not register")
notify(Ex.flareStatus, 7)
return
end
if Ex.flareFrom and Ex.flareFrom ~= "" then
Ex.flareStatus = who or "the picked player is not in the world"
notify(Ex.flareStatus, 6)
return
end
pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
Ex.flareStatus = "signalled for a supply drop, from where you stand"
notify(Ex.flareStatus, 6)
end)
end
Ex.carryMax = 50
Ex.CARRY_FOR = 1.2
Ex.MARK_HOLD = 3
Ex.MARK_STILL = 2.5
Ex.MARK_MIN = 3
Ex.MARK_SIZE = 4
Ex.markParts = {}
Ex.MARK_RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 58, 70)
Ex.MARK_FONT = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold)
function Ex.aimIgnore()
if Ex.aimIgnoreAt and os.clock() - Ex.aimIgnoreAt < 1 and Ex.aimParams then return Ex.aimParams end
local ignore = {}
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr.Character then ignore[#ignore + 1] = plr.Character end
end
local r = rake()
if r then ignore[#ignore + 1] = r end
for _, t in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do ignore[#ignore + 1] = t end
local params = Ex.aimParams or RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
Ex.aimParams, Ex.aimIgnoreAt = params, os.clock()
return params
end
function Ex.trapAim()
local c = cam()
if not c then return nil end
local origin, dir
if IS_MOBILE then
origin, dir = c.CFrame.Position, c.CFrame.LookVector
else
local ray = LocalPlayer:GetMouse().UnitRay
origin, dir = ray.Origin, ray.Direction
end
local params = Ex.aimIgnore()
local hit
pcall(function() hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, dir * 300, params) end)
if not hit then return nil end
local floor
pcall(function()
floor = Workspace:Raycast(hit.Position + hit.Normal * 0.6 + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), params)
end)
return floor and floor.Position or hit.Position
end
function Ex.restGoal(box, at)
local lift = box.Size.Y * 0.5
pcall(function()
local model = box:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
if model then
local cf, size = model:GetBoundingBox()
lift = math.max(lift, box.Position.Y - (cf.Position.Y - size.Y * 0.5))
end
end)
return Vector3.new(at.X, at.Y + lift + 0.05, at.Z)
end
Ex.FLAT = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
function Ex.markPart(name, colour)
local p = Ex.markParts[name]
if p and p.Parent then return p end
p = make("Part", {
Name = "RKTrapMark_" .. name, Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false,
CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false, Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Color = colour, Transparency = 1,
Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 1, 1),
}, Workspace)
Ex.markParts[name] = p
return p
end
function Ex.dropMarkPart(name, fade)
local p = Ex.markParts[name]
Ex.markParts[name] = nil
if not p then return end
if fade and p.Parent then
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(0.3), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
for _, d in ipairs(p:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Beam") then d.Enabled = false end
if d:IsA("BillboardGui") then d.Enabled = false end
end
end)
task.delay(0.35, function() pcall(function() p:Destroy() end) end)
else
pcall(function() p:Destroy() end)
end
end
function Ex.showCharge(at, k)
local guide = Ex.markPart("guide", Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
local fill = Ex.markPart("fill", Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
local d = Ex.MARK_SIZE
pcall(function()
guide.Size = Vector3.new(0.06, d + 0.3, d + 0.3)
guide.CFrame = CFrame.new(at + Vector3.new(0, 0.05, 0)) * Ex.FLAT
guide.Transparency = 0.78
local s = 0.4 + (d - 0.4) * k
fill.Size = Vector3.new(0.08, s, s)
fill.CFrame = CFrame.new(at + Vector3.new(0, 0.07, 0)) * Ex.FLAT
fill.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1):Lerp(Ex.MARK_RED, k)
fill.Transparency = 0.6 - 0.35 * k
end)
end
function Ex.hideCharge()
if Ex.markParts.guide then Ex.dropMarkPart("guide") end
if Ex.markParts.fill then Ex.dropMarkPart("fill") end
end
function Ex.setMark(at)
Ex.clearMark(false)
Ex.areaMark = at
local disc = Ex.markPart("disc", Ex.MARK_RED)
local d = Ex.MARK_SIZE
pcall(function()
disc.CFrame = CFrame.new(at + Vector3.new(0, 0.06, 0)) * Ex.FLAT
disc.Size = Vector3.new(0.12, 0.6, 0.6)
disc.Transparency = 0.25
TweenService:Create(disc, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
{ Size = Vector3.new(0.12, d, d) }):Play()
local base = make("Attachment", { Name = "Base" }, disc)
local top = make("Attachment", { Name = "Top", Position = Vector3.new(14, 0, 0) }, disc)
local tag = make("Attachment", { Name = "Tag", Position = Vector3.new(3.4, 0, 0) }, disc)
make("Beam", {
Attachment0 = base, Attachment1 = top, FaceCamera = true, LightEmission = 1,
Color = ColorSequence.new(Ex.MARK_RED), Width0 = 0.9, Width1 = 0.2, Segments = 1,
Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.25), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }),
}, disc)
local bb = make("BillboardGui", {
Adornee = tag, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 24), AlwaysOnTop = true,
MaxDistance = 300, LightInfluence = 0,
}, disc)
local pill = make("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = Ex.MARK_RED, BackgroundTransparency = 0.12 }, bb)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, pill)
make("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Transparency = 0.35, Thickness = 1.2 }, pill)
make("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
FontFace = Ex.MARK_FONT, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
Text = "TRAP HERE" }, pill)
end)
Ex.trapAreaStatus = "spot marked - place a trap anywhere you are allowed"
notify("spot marked - now place your trap anywhere you're allowed (within "
.. Ex.carryMax .. " studs of the mark) and it lands on the mark", 6)
end
function Ex.clearMark(fade)
Ex.areaMark = nil
if Ex.markParts.disc then Ex.dropMarkPart("disc", fade) end
end
function Ex.areaFrame()
local disc = Ex.markParts.disc
if disc and disc.Parent then
pcall(function() disc.Transparency = 0.3 + 0.14 * math.sin(os.clock() * 4) end)
end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local tool = char and char:FindFirstChild("RakeTrap")
local placing = false
if tool then
local pr = tool:FindFirstChild("Progress")
pcall(function() placing = pr and pr.Value > 0 and pr.Value < 100 end)
end
local aim, me = nil, myPos()
if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") and not placing and not Ex.areaCarrying then aim = Ex.trapAim() end
local away = aim and me and (Vector3.new(aim.X, me.Y, aim.Z) - me).Magnitude
local ok = aim and away and away >= Ex.MARK_MIN and away <= Ex.carryMax
and not (Ex.areaMark and (aim - Ex.areaMark).Magnitude < 6)
if not ok then
Ex.areaHover = nil
Ex.hideCharge()
return
end
local now = os.clock()
local h = Ex.areaHover
if not h or (aim - h.at).Magnitude > Ex.MARK_STILL then
h = { at = aim, since = now }
Ex.areaHover = h
end
local k = math.clamp((now - h.since) / Ex.MARK_HOLD, 0, 1)
Ex.showCharge(h.at, k)
if k >= 1 then
Ex.areaHover = nil
Ex.hideCharge()
Ex.setMark(h.at)
end
end
function Ex.carryTrap(model, box, mark)
Ex.areaCarrying = true
task.spawn(function()
local goal = Ex.restGoal(box, mark)
local t0 = os.clock()
while os.clock() - t0 < Ex.CARRY_FOR and model.Parent and box.Parent and Ex.trapArea do
if typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then
pcall(sethiddenproperty, LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", 1000)
end
Ex.moveTrap(model, box, goal)
task.wait(0.05)
end
local owned = true
if typeof(isnetworkowner) == "function" then
pcall(function() owned = isnetworkowner(box) end)
end
Ex.releaseModel(model)
Ex.areaCarrying = false
if owned then
Ex.clearMark(true)
Ex.trapAreaStatus = "trap set on the mark"
else
Ex.trapAreaStatus = "the server kept that trap - stay near it while it lands"
end
notify(Ex.trapAreaStatus, 4)
end)
end
function Ex.setTrapArea(on)
Ex.trapArea = on
if not on then
if Ex.areaConn then Ex.areaConn:Disconnect() Ex.areaConn = nil end
Ex.areaHover, Ex.areaSeen = nil, nil
Ex.hideCharge()
Ex.clearMark(false)
Ex.trapAreaStatus = "off"
return
end
if Ex.areaConn then return end
Ex.areaSeen = {}
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do Ex.areaSeen[m] = true end
Ex.trapAreaStatus = "hold the trap and aim at a spot for " .. Ex.MARK_HOLD .. " seconds"
notify("Bypass Trap Area: hold the trap and keep your aim on a spot for "
.. Ex.MARK_HOLD .. "s - it marks red. Then place your trap anywhere you're allowed "
.. "and it lands on the mark", 8)
Ex.areaConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.trapArea then return end
pcall(Ex.areaFrame)
if os.clock() - (Ex.areaScanAt or 0) < 0.15 then return end
Ex.areaScanAt = os.clock()
local me = myPos()
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
if not Ex.areaSeen[m] then
Ex.areaSeen[m] = true
local who
pcall(function() who = m:GetAttribute("Player") end)
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if Ex.areaMark and not Ex.areaCarrying and who == LocalPlayer.Name
and box and box:IsA("BasePart") and me and (box.Position - me).Magnitude < 25 then
local far = (Vector3.new(Ex.areaMark.X, box.Position.Y, Ex.areaMark.Z) - box.Position).Magnitude
if far <= Ex.carryMax then
Ex.carryTrap(m, box, Ex.areaMark)
else
notify(string.format("the mark is %dm from that trap - place within %dm of it",
math.floor(far), Ex.carryMax), 5)
end
end
end
end
for m in pairs(Ex.areaSeen) do
if not m.Parent then Ex.areaSeen[m] = nil end
end
end)
addCleanup("traparea", function() Ex.setTrapArea(false) end)
end
function Ex.grabFlare()
local gun = flarePart()
local hrp = getHRP()
if not (gun and hrp) then return false, "no flare gun spawned" end
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then
return false, "this executor has no firetouchinterest"
end
local ok = pcall(function()
firetouchinterest(hrp, gun, 0)
task.wait()
firetouchinterest(hrp, gun, 1)
end)
return ok, ok and "touched the flare gun" or "the touch was refused"
end
function Ex.removeWalls()
local filter = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Filter")
local gone = 0
for _, name in ipairs({ "ServerInvisibleWalls", "InvisibleWalls" }) do
local folder = filter and filter:FindFirstChild(name)
if folder then
for _, v in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
if v:IsA("BasePart") then
pcall(function() v:Destroy() end)
gone = gone + 1
end
end
end
end
if gone == 0 then
notify("no invisible-wall folders in this map", 4)
return
end
notify("removed " .. gone .. " wall parts", 4)
end
Ex.hideDrop = 12
function Ex.hideFinish(hrp)
Ex.hideActive = false
if Ex.hideConn then Ex.hideConn:Disconnect() Ex.hideConn = nil end
if Ex.hideBP then pcall(function() Ex.hideBP:Destroy() end) Ex.hideBP = nil end
if hrp and Ex.hideReturn then
pcall(function()
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(Ex.hideReturn) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation
end)
end
if Ex.hideNoclipWas ~= nil then
local userWants = Toggles.RK_Noclip and Toggles.RK_Noclip.Value
if not userWants then pcall(function() Move.setNoclip(Ex.hideNoclipWas) end) end
Ex.hideNoclipWas = nil
end
Ex.hideReturn, Ex.hideGoal, Ex.hideRoot = nil, nil, nil
end
function Ex.hideJump(pos)
if not (Ex.hideActive and Ex.hideGoal) then return false end
Ex.hideReturn = pos
Ex.hideGoal = pos - Vector3.new(0, Ex.hideDrop, 0)
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp then
pcall(function()
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(Ex.hideGoal) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation
end)
end
if Ex.hideBP then pcall(function() Ex.hideBP.Position = Ex.hideGoal end) end
return true
end
Teleport.hiddenTo = function(cf) return Ex.hideJump(cf.Position) end
Teleport.hidden = function() return Ex.hidden or Ex.hideActive end
Move.flingPaused = function() return Teleport.busy or Ex.hidden or Ex.hideActive end
function Ex.setHide(on)
on = on and true or false
if on == Ex.hidden then return end
Ex.hidden = on
if not on then
local hrp = getHRP()
Ex.hideFinish(rawequal(hrp, Ex.hideRoot) and hrp or nil)
return
end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then Ex.hidden = false return end
if Ex.hideActive then Ex.hideFinish(nil) end
Ex.hideRoot = hrp
Ex.hideReturn = hrp.Position
Ex.hideGoal = hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, Ex.hideDrop, 0)
Ex.hideNoclipWas = Move.noclip
if not Move.noclip then pcall(function() Move.setNoclip(true) end) end
Ex.hideBP = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
Ex.hideBP.Name = "RKHideBP"
Ex.hideBP.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
Ex.hideBP.P = 100000
Ex.hideBP.D = 2000
Ex.hideBP.Position = Ex.hideGoal
Ex.hideBP.Parent = hrp
Ex.hideActive = true
Ex.hideConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.hidden then return end
local live = getHRP()
if not (live and rawequal(live, Ex.hideRoot)) then return end
if Ex.hideBP and Ex.hideBP.Parent then
Ex.hideBP.Position = Ex.hideGoal
pcall(function() live.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
end
end)
addCleanup("hide", function()
Ex.hidden = false
if Ex.hideActive then
local live = getHRP()
Ex.hideFinish(rawequal(live, Ex.hideRoot) and live or nil)
end
end)
end
Ex.killGap     = 0.3
Ex.killTickGap = 0.03
Ex.killCatch   = 7
Ex.springGap   = 0.06
Ex.killDrag    = 80
Ex.killRange   = 200
function Ex.killSay(text)
if Ex.killSaid == text then return end
Ex.killSaid = text
notify(text, 4)
end
function Ex.allTraps()
local out, seen = {}, {}
for _, m in ipairs(trapModels()) do
if not seen[m] then seen[m] = true out[#out + 1] = m end
end
for _, m in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
if not seen[m] and looksLikeTrap(m) then seen[m] = true out[#out + 1] = m end
end
return out
end
function Ex.rakeTrapped(r)
if not r then return nil end
local hum = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local flagged = false
if hum then
local ok, v = pcall(function() return hum:GetAttribute("iTRPED2") end)
flagged = ok and v == true
end
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
local hit = pcall(function() return m:GetAttribute("Activated") end)
and m:GetAttribute("Activated") == true
if hit and m:GetAttribute("HitChar") == "Rake" then return m end
end
local hrp = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return nil end
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart")
and (box.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= Ex.killCatch then
return m
end
end
return flagged and Ex.allTraps()[1] or nil
end
function Ex.rakeGone(r)
if not r then return false end
local dead = r:FindFirstChild("Dead")
local inactive = r:FindFirstChild("Inactive")
if dead and dead:IsA("BoolValue") and dead.Value then return true end
if inactive and inactive:IsA("BoolValue") and inactive.Value then return true end
return false
end
function Ex.pickTrap(alsoRusty, except, at)
local me = myPos()
if not me then return nil, 0 end
local mine, mineD, rusty = nil, nil, 0
local best, bestD = nil, nil
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart") and m ~= except then
local d = (box.Position - me).Magnitude
if m:GetAttribute("ServerSpawned") == true and d <= Ex.killDrag then
rusty = rusty + 1
end
local rank = (alsoRusty and at) and (box.Position - at).Magnitude or d
if d <= Ex.killDrag and (not bestD or rank < bestD) then
best, bestD = m, rank
end
if not mineD or d < mineD then mine, mineD = m, d end
end
end
if alsoRusty then return best, rusty end
if Ex.instaArmed and not Ex.instaAny then
local own, ownD
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart") and m ~= except and m:GetAttribute("Player") == LocalPlayer.Name then
local d = (box.Position - me).Magnitude
if d <= Ex.killDrag and (not ownD or d < ownD) then own, ownD = m, d end
end
end
if own then return own, rusty end
end
return best or mine, rusty
end
Ex.LEAD_STUDS = 1.5
function Ex.trapGoal(r, box)
local hrp = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not (hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart")) then return nil end
local dir
local hum = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then
local md = hum.MoveDirection
if md.Magnitude > 0.1 then dir = Vector3.new(md.X, 0, md.Z).Unit end
end
if not dir then
local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
local flat = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
if flat.Magnitude > 1 then dir = flat.Unit end
end
local ahead = dir and (dir * Ex.LEAD_STUDS) or Vector3.zero
local gx, gz = hrp.Position.X + ahead.X, hrp.Position.Z + ahead.Z
local floor
pcall(function()
if not Ex.trapListAt or os.clock() - Ex.trapListAt > 1 then
Ex.trapListAt, Ex.trapList = os.clock(), Ex.allTraps()
end
local ignore = { r, LocalPlayer.Character }
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.trapList) do ignore[#ignore + 1] = m end
local params = RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
local hit = Workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(gx, hrp.Position.Y + 2, gz), Vector3.new(0, -14, 0), params)
if hit then floor = hit.Position.Y end
end)
if not floor then
for _, name in ipairs({ "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }) do
local p = r:FindFirstChild(name)
if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
local sole = p.Position.Y - p.Size.Y * 0.5
if not floor or sole < floor then floor = sole end
end
end
end
floor = floor or (hrp.Position.Y - hrp.Size.Y * 0.5 - 2)
local lift = box.Size.Y * 0.5
pcall(function()
local model = box:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
if model then
local cf, size = model:GetBoundingBox()
local bottom = cf.Position.Y - size.Y * 0.5
lift = math.max(lift, box.Position.Y - bottom)
end
end)
return Vector3.new(gx, floor + lift + 0.05, gz)
end
Ex.HOLD_FOR = 0.35
Ex.held = {}
Ex.trapWas = {}
function Ex.releaseModel(model)
if not model then return end
Ex.held[model] = nil
for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
local was = d:IsA("BasePart") and Ex.trapWas[d]
if was then
pcall(function()
d.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
d.Anchored = was.anchored
d.CanCollide = was.collide
end)
Ex.trapWas[d] = nil
end
end
end
function Ex.releaseTrapBP(box)
if not box then return end
local model = box:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model") or box.Parent
Ex.releaseModel(model)
end
function Ex.clearTrapBP()
for model in pairs(Ex.held) do Ex.releaseModel(model) end
Ex.held = {}
for d, was in pairs(Ex.trapWas) do
if d and d.Parent then
pcall(function() d.Anchored = was.anchored d.CanCollide = was.collide end)
end
end
Ex.trapWas = {}
if Ex.holdConnA then Ex.holdConnA:Disconnect() Ex.holdConnA = nil end
if Ex.holdConnB then Ex.holdConnB:Disconnect() Ex.holdConnB = nil end
end
function Ex.placeHeld(model, h)
local box = h.box
if not (box and box.Parent) then return end
pcall(function()
local piv = model:GetPivot()
if piv.UpVector.Y < 0.97 then
local look = piv.LookVector
local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
piv = CFrame.lookAt(piv.Position, piv.Position + flat)
model:PivotTo(piv)
end
model:PivotTo(CFrame.new(h.goal - box.Position) * model:GetPivot())
box.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
box.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end)
end
function Ex.holdStep()
local now = os.clock()
for model, h in pairs(Ex.held) do
if not model.Parent then
Ex.held[model] = nil
elseif now - h.at > Ex.HOLD_FOR then
Ex.releaseModel(model)
else
Ex.placeHeld(model, h)
end
end
end
function Ex.moveTrap(model, box, goal)
if not (model and model:IsA("Model")) then
model = box:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
if not model then return end
end
pcall(function()
for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
if Ex.trapWas[d] == nil then Ex.trapWas[d] = { anchored = d.Anchored, collide = d.CanCollide } end
if d.CanCollide then d.CanCollide = false end
if d.Anchored then d.Anchored = false end
end
end
end)
local h = Ex.held[model]
if h then
h.goal, h.at, h.box = goal, os.clock(), box
else
h = { box = box, goal = goal, at = os.clock() }
Ex.held[model] = h
end
Ex.placeHeld(model, h)
Ex.moveAskedAt = os.clock()
if not Ex.holdConnA then
Ex.holdConnA = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function() pcall(Ex.holdStep) end)
Ex.holdConnB = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() pcall(Ex.holdStep) end)
end
addCleanup("trapbp", function() Ex.clearTrapBP() end)
end
function Ex.touchTrap(r, box, edge)
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return end
if Ex.touchOf ~= r or not Ex.touchList then
Ex.touchOf, Ex.touchList = r, {}
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
Ex.touchList[#Ex.touchList + 1] = d
if #Ex.touchList >= 24 then break end
end
end
end
for _, part in ipairs(Ex.touchList) do
if part.Parent then pcall(firetouchinterest, part, box, edge) end
end
end
function Ex.springTrap(r, box)
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return 0 end
if Ex.springOf ~= r or not Ex.springList then
Ex.springOf, Ex.springList = r, {}
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
Ex.springList[#Ex.springList + 1] = d
if #Ex.springList >= 24 then break end
end
end
end
if Ex.springing then return 0 end
Ex.springing = true
local fired = #Ex.springList
task.spawn(function()
for _, part in ipairs(Ex.springList) do
if part.Parent and box.Parent then
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function()
firetouchinterest(part, box, 0)
task.wait()
firetouchinterest(part, box, 1)
end)
end)
end
end
task.wait(0.1)
Ex.springing = false
end)
return fired
end
Ex.trapPlayers = false
Ex.trapWho = {}
Ex.trapRange = 60
Ex.trapPhase = false
Ex.trapTurn = 0
function Ex.rustyTraps()
local out = {}
for _, m in ipairs(trapModels()) do
if m:GetAttribute("ServerSpawned") == true then
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart") then
out[#out + 1] = { model = m, box = box }
end
end
end
return out
end
function Ex.playerNames()
local out = {}
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LocalPlayer then out[#out + 1] = plr.Name end
end
table.sort(out)
return out
end
function Ex.trapTargets()
local out = {}
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LocalPlayer and Ex.trapWho[plr.Name] then
local char = plr.Character
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local me = myPos()
if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") and hum and hum.Health > 0
and me and (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude <= Ex.trapRange then
out[#out + 1] = { player = plr, char = char, at = hrp.Position }
end
end
end
return out
end
function Ex.charTrapped(char, name)
local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
if m:GetAttribute("Activated") == true and m:GetAttribute("HitChar") == name then
return m
end
end
if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") then
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart")
and (box.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= Ex.killCatch then
return m
end
end
end
return nil
end
function Ex.nearestRusty(except, busy)
local me = myPos()
if not me then return nil, 0 end
local best, bestD, seen = nil, nil, 0
local now = os.clock()
for _, t in ipairs(Ex.rustyTraps()) do
if t.model ~= except and not (busy and (busy[t.model] or 0) > now) then
local d = (t.box.Position - me).Magnitude
if d <= Ex.trapRange then
seen = seen + 1
if not bestD or d < bestD then best, bestD = t, d end
end
end
end
return best, seen
end
function Ex.nearestOwn(except)
local me = myPos()
if not me then return nil, false, 0 end
local mine = LocalPlayer.Name
local bestOwn, bestOwnD = nil, nil
local bestAny, bestAnyD = nil, nil
local rusty = 0
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
if m ~= except then
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart") then
local who
pcall(function() who = m:GetAttribute("Player") end)
local d = (box.Position - me).Magnitude
if d <= Ex.trapRange then
if who == mine then
if not bestOwnD or d < bestOwnD then
bestOwn, bestOwnD = { model = m, box = box }, d
end
elseif m:GetAttribute("ServerSpawned") == true then
rusty = rusty + 1
end
if not bestAnyD or d < bestAnyD then
bestAny, bestAnyD = { model = m, box = box }, d
end
end
end
end
end
if bestOwn then return bestOwn, true, rusty end
return bestAny, false, rusty
end
function Ex.setTrapPlayers(on)
Ex.trapPlayers = on
if not on then
if Ex.trapConn2 then Ex.trapConn2:Disconnect() Ex.trapConn2 = nil end
Ex.trapWant, Ex.trapWantModel, Ex.trapTurn = nil, nil, 0
Ex.trapStatus = "off"
if not Ex.killRake then Ex.clearTrapBP() end
return
end
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then
notify("this executor has no firetouchinterest, so a trap cannot be sprung", 6)
Ex.trapPlayers = false
return
end
Ex.trapWant, Ex.trapWantModel, Ex.trapTurn = nil, nil, 0
Ex.trapFocus, Ex.trapBusy, Ex.trapTouchAt, Ex.trapPhaseOf = nil, {}, {}, {}
if Ex.trapConn2 then return end
Ex.trapConn2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.trapPlayers then return end
if typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then
pcall(sethiddenproperty, LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", 1000)
end
if os.clock() - (Ex.trapTick or 0) < Ex.killTickGap then return end
Ex.trapTick = os.clock()
local targets = Ex.trapTargets()
if #targets == 0 then
Ex.trapStatus = next(Ex.trapWho)
and ("nobody selected is alive within " .. Ex.trapRange .. "m") or "nobody selected"
Ex.trapWant, Ex.trapWantModel = nil, nil
return
end
Ex.trapTurn = (Ex.trapTurn or 0) % #targets + 1
local t = targets[Ex.trapTurn]
local name = t.player.Name
if Ex.trapFocus then
local still = false
for _, tt in ipairs(targets) do
if tt.player.Name == Ex.trapFocus then still = true break end
end
if not still then Ex.trapFocus = nil end
end
local caught = Ex.charTrapped(t.char, name)
if caught then
Ex.trapBusy = Ex.trapBusy or {}
Ex.trapBusy[caught] = os.clock() + 3
if Ex.trapFocus == name then Ex.trapFocus = nil end
local heldBox = caught:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if heldBox and heldBox:IsA("BasePart") then
local fired = Ex.springTrap(t.char, heldBox)
Ex.trapStatus = string.format("%s is in a trap - %d contacts", name, fired)
else
Ex.trapStatus = name .. " is in a trap"
end
return
end
if Ex.trapFocus and Ex.trapFocus ~= name then return end
Ex.trapFocus = name
local pick, seen = Ex.nearestRusty(nil, Ex.trapBusy)
if not pick then
Ex.trapStatus = seen > 0 and "no rusty trap in range"
or ("no rusty trap within " .. Ex.trapRange .. " studs of you")
return
end
local model, box = pick.model, pick.box
if model ~= Ex.trapWantModel then
local oldbox = Ex.trapWantModel and Ex.trapWantModel:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if oldbox then Ex.releaseTrapBP(oldbox) end
Ex.trapWant, Ex.trapWantModel, Ex.trapWantAt = nil, model, nil
end
local goal = Ex.trapGoal(t.char, box)
if goal then
Ex.moveTrap(model, box, goal)
Ex.trapWant = true
Ex.trapWantAt = Ex.trapWantAt or os.clock()
end
if goal and Ex.trapWantAt and os.clock() - Ex.trapWantAt >= 0.3 then
Ex.trapWantAt = os.clock()
local owned = true
if typeof(isnetworkowner) == "function" then
pcall(function() owned = isnetworkowner(box) end)
end
if owned then
Ex.trapStatus = "trap on " .. name .. " - it should snap"
else
Ex.trapStatus = "server still owns this trap - move closer to it"
end
elseif Ex.trapStatus == nil or Ex.trapStatus == "off" then
Ex.trapStatus = "dragging a rusty trap under " .. name
end
Ex.trapTouchAt, Ex.trapPhaseOf = Ex.trapTouchAt or {}, Ex.trapPhaseOf or {}
if os.clock() - (Ex.trapTouchAt[name] or 0) >= Ex.springGap then
Ex.trapTouchAt[name] = os.clock()
Ex.trapPhaseOf[name] = not Ex.trapPhaseOf[name]
Ex.touchTrap(t.char, box, Ex.trapPhaseOf[name] and 0 or 1)
end
end)
addCleanup("trapplayers", function() Ex.setTrapPlayers(false) end)
end
function Ex.trapReadout()
if not Ex.trapPlayers then return "off" end
return Ex.trapStatus or "starting"
end
function Ex.setKillRake(on)
Ex.killRake = on
if not on then
if Ex.killConn then Ex.killConn:Disconnect() Ex.killConn = nil end
Ex.killSaid, Ex.killWant, Ex.killTrap = nil, nil, nil
if not Ex.trapPlayers then Ex.clearTrapBP() end
return
end
Ex.killSaid, Ex.killWant, Ex.killTrap = nil, nil, nil
if Ex.killConn then return end
Ex.killConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.killRake then return end
local r = rake()
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not (hrp and hum) or Fight.rakeHp(hum) <= 0 then
Ex.killTrap, Ex.killWant, Ex.killWantModel = nil, nil, nil
Ex.killSay("no Rake in the world")
return
end
Fight.aimHitPart(hrp)
if os.clock() - (Ex.killTick or 0) < Ex.killTickGap then return end
Ex.killTick = os.clock()
local caught = Ex.rakeTrapped(r)
if not caught then
local me = myPos()
local away = me and (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude or math.huge
if away > Ex.killDrag then
Ex.killSay(string.format("the Rake is %dm away - waiting until he is within %dm",
math.floor(away), Ex.killDrag))
return
end
end
local model, rusty = Ex.pickTrap(caught ~= nil, caught, hrp.Position)
if caught then
if os.clock() - (Ex.stackLast or 0) < 1.5 then
model = nil
elseif model then
Ex.stackLast = os.clock()
end
end
if model ~= Ex.killWantModel then
local oldbox = Ex.killWantModel and Ex.killWantModel:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if oldbox then Ex.releaseTrapBP(oldbox) end
Ex.killWant, Ex.killWantModel, Ex.killWantAt = nil, model, nil
end
Ex.killTrap = model or caught
if caught then
local heldBox = caught:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if heldBox and heldBox:IsA("BasePart") then
Ex.killPhase = not Ex.killPhase
Ex.touchTrap(r, heldBox, Ex.killPhase and 0 or 1)
end
local me = myPos()
if me and (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude < Ex.killRange
and Fight.stick()
and os.clock() - (Ex.killLast or 0) >= Ex.killGap then
Ex.killLast = os.clock()
Fight.swing(hrp)
end
end
local box = model and model:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if not (box and box:IsA("BasePart")) then
if caught then
Ex.killSay("he is in a trap - springing it")
else
Ex.killSay(rusty > 0
and ("no trap within " .. Ex.killDrag .. " studs - walk nearer one")
or "no trap anywhere - buy a RakeTrap and put one down")
end
return
end
local me = myPos()
if me and (box.Position - me).Magnitude > Ex.killDrag then
Ex.killSay("the trap is too far away - walk over to it")
return
end
if Ex.killWant or (box.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > Ex.killCatch then
local goal = Ex.trapGoal(r, box)
if goal then
Ex.moveTrap(model, box, goal)
Ex.killWant = true
Ex.killWantAt = Ex.killWantAt or os.clock()
end
if goal and Ex.killWantAt and os.clock() - Ex.killWantAt >= 0.3 then
Ex.killWantAt = os.clock()
local owned = (box.Position - goal).Magnitude <= 6
if typeof(isnetworkowner) == "function" then
pcall(function() owned = isnetworkowner(box) end)
end
if owned then
Ex.killSay(string.format("trap moved onto the Rake (%.1f studs off)",
(box.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude))
else
Ex.killSay("that one is server owned - it will not move, lure him onto it")
end
end
if os.clock() - (Ex.killTouchLast or 0) >= Ex.springGap then
Ex.killTouchLast = os.clock()
Ex.killPhase = not Ex.killPhase
Ex.touchTrap(r, box, Ex.killPhase and 0 or 1)
end
return
end
Ex.killWant = nil
Ex.killSay("the trap is on him - springing it")
end)
addCleanup("killloop", function()
if Ex.killConn then Ex.killConn:Disconnect() Ex.killConn = nil end
end)
end
local Kill = {
on = false,
radius = 1000,
CATCH = 7,
HOLD = 4,
OUT_FOR = 0.3,
CAP = 16,
LANDING = 3,
win = nil,
said = "", saidAt = nil,
log = {},
}
function Kill.flat(a, b)
return Vector3.new(a.X - b.X, 0, a.Z - b.Z).Magnitude
end
function Kill.owns(part)
if typeof(isnetworkowner) ~= "function" or not part then return nil end
local ok, v = pcall(isnetworkowner, part)
if not ok then return nil end
return v == true
end
function Kill.flag(r, name)
local v = r and r:FindFirstChild(name)
return (v and v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value) and true or false
end
function Kill.isMine(m)
if not m then return false end
local who, spawned
pcall(function() who = m:GetAttribute("Player") end)
pcall(function() spawned = m:GetAttribute("ServerSpawned") end)
if type(who) == "string" then return who == LocalPlayer.Name end
return spawned ~= true
end
function Kill.traps()
if not Kill.trapList or os.clock() - (Kill.trapListAt or 0) > 0.25 then
Kill.trapList, Kill.trapListAt = Ex.allTraps(), os.clock()
end
return Kill.trapList
end
function Kill.heldBy(hum, root)
local flagged, tp = false, nil
pcall(function() flagged = hum:GetAttribute("iTRPED2") == true end)
pcall(function() tp = hum:GetAttribute("TrapPos") end)
local still = false
pcall(function() still = hum.WalkSpeed <= 1 end)
local best, bestD
for _, m in ipairs(Kill.traps()) do
local box = m.Parent and m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart") then
local d = Kill.flat(box.Position, root.Position)
if d <= Kill.CATCH and (not bestD or d < bestD) then
local sprung = false
pcall(function()
sprung = m:GetAttribute("Activated") == true and m:GetAttribute("HitChar") == "Rake"
end)
local atPos = typeof(tp) == "Vector3" and Kill.flat(tp, box.Position) <= Kill.HOLD
if (flagged and (sprung or atPos)) or (sprung and still and d <= Kill.HOLD) then
best, bestD = m, d
end
end
end
end
if not best then return nil end
return best, best:FindFirstChild("HitBox"), Kill.isMine(best)
end
function Kill.nearestMine(root)
local best, gap = nil, math.huge
for _, m in ipairs(Kill.traps()) do
if Kill.isMine(m) then
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart") then
local d = Kill.flat(box.Position, root.Position)
if d < gap then best, gap = m, d end
end
end
end
return best, gap
end
function Kill.say(text, quiet)
if Kill.said == text then return end
if quiet and os.clock() - (Kill.saidAt or 0) < 2 then return end
Kill.said, Kill.saidAt = text, os.clock()
notify(text, 5)
end
function Kill.verdict(killed)
if killed then
notify("Successfully killed", 6)
else
notify("Failed - wait 30 sec and then re-enable if you have a second trap", 8)
end
end
function Kill.probe(label)
local w = Kill.win
