local bits = { string.format("%.3f %s", os.clock(), label) }
pcall(function()
local root, box, hum = w and w.root, w and w.box, w and w.hum
if root then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("root anchored=%s receiveAge=%.3f assemblyRoot=%s owner=%s",
tostring(root.Anchored), root.ReceiveAge,
root.AssemblyRootPart and root.AssemblyRootPart:GetFullName() or "nil",
tostring(Kill.owns(root)))
end
if box and box.Parent then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("trap mine=%s anchored=%s assemblyRoot=%s owner=%s sameAssembly=%s",
tostring(w.mine), tostring(box.Anchored),
box.AssemblyRootPart and box.AssemblyRootPart:GetFullName() or "nil",
tostring(Kill.owns(box)),
tostring(root and box.AssemblyRootPart == root.AssemblyRootPart))
end
if hum then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("hum state=%s health=%.1f walk=%.1f writes=%d",
tostring(hum:GetState()), hum.Health, hum.WalkSpeed, w.writes or 0)
end
end)
Kill.log[#Kill.log + 1] = table.concat(bits, " | ")
while #Kill.log > 200 do table.remove(Kill.log, 1) end
if typeof(writefile) == "function" then
pcall(ensureFolder, "Rake")
pcall(ensureFolder, "Rake/dumps")
pcall(writefile, "Rake/dumps/instakill-log.txt", table.concat(Kill.log, "\n"))
end
end
function Kill.write()
local w = Kill.win
if not w then return end
local hum, root = w.hum, w.root
if not (hum and hum.Parent and root and root.Parent) then return end
local own = Kill.owns(root)
if own == true and not w.ownerAt then
w.ownerAt = os.clock()
Kill.probe("owner")
end
if own ~= nil then w.owner = own end
if not (w.mine or own == true) then return end
pcall(function() hum.Health = 0 end)
w.writes = w.writes + 1
if own == true then
pcall(function()
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
if hum:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
end
end)
end
end
function Kill.open(r, hum, root, trap, box, mine)
local tp
pcall(function() tp = hum:GetAttribute("TrapPos") end)
local hp = hum.Health
if not (hp and hp > 0) then hp = Fight.instaHp or hum.MaxHealth end
local w = {
r = r, hum = hum, root = root, trap = trap, box = box, mine = mine, tp = tp,
opened = os.clock(), writes = 0, hp = hp, owner = nil,
}
Kill.win = w
Fight.instaWriting, Fight.instaHp = true, hp
w.hpConn = hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(v)
if v and v > 0 then w.hp = v Fight.instaHp = v end
end)
w.stateConn = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead and not w.localDeadAt then
w.localDeadAt = os.clock()
Kill.probe("died on this screen")
end
end)
Kill.probe("caught" .. (mine and " (your trap)" or " (not yours)"))
if mine then
Kill.say("IN YOUR TRAP - firing")
elseif Kill.owns(root) == nil then
Kill.say("he is in a trap that is not yours - it has to be YOUR trap")
else
Kill.say("he is in a trap that is not yours - firing only if he becomes yours")
end
Kill.write()
end
function Kill.close(how)
local w = Kill.win
if not w then return end
Kill.win = nil
Kill.lastTrap = w.trap
if w.hpConn then w.hpConn:Disconnect() end
if w.stateConn then w.stateConn:Disconnect() end
Fight.instaWriting = false
local t = os.clock() - w.opened
local owner = (w.ownerAt and "YOU") or (w.owner == false and "the server") or "unknown"
Kill.probe("close " .. how)
if how == "KILLED" then
Kill.kills = (Kill.kills or 0) + 1
Kill.probe(string.format("KILLED: Dead %.1fs after the catch (owner %s%s)", t, owner,
w.localDeadAt and string.format(", fell on your screen at +%.2fs", w.localDeadAt - w.opened) or ""))
Kill.verdict(true)
return
end
if w.hum and w.hum.Parent and w.hp and w.hp > 0 then
pcall(function() w.hum.Health = w.hp end)
end
if how == "OFF" then return end
local why = (how == "OUT" and "he left the round (Inactive, not Dead)")
or (how == "GONE" and "he was removed")
or (how == "CAP" and ("the hold ran past " .. Kill.CAP .. "s"))
or string.format("he walked out after %.1fs", t)
Kill.probe(string.format("NOT landed: %s (owner %s, %d writes%s%s)", why, owner, w.writes,
w.localDeadAt and ", he fell on YOUR screen only" or "",
w.mine and "" or ", not your trap"))
Kill.verdict(false)
end
function Kill.watch()
local w = Kill.win
local now = os.clock()
if Kill.flag(w.r, "Dead") then return Kill.close("KILLED") end
if Kill.flag(w.r, "Inactive") then return Kill.close("OUT") end
local gone = not (w.r.Parent and w.root.Parent) or rake() ~= w.r
if w.localDeadAt then
if now - w.localDeadAt > Kill.LANDING then return Kill.close(gone and "GONE" or "WALKED") end
return
end
if gone then return Kill.close("GONE") end
if now - w.opened > Kill.CAP then return Kill.close("CAP") end
local inIt = w.box and w.box.Parent and Kill.flat(w.box.Position, w.root.Position) <= Kill.HOLD
if inIt then
w.outSince = nil
else
w.outSince = w.outSince or now
if now - w.outSince >= Kill.OUT_FOR then return Kill.close("WALKED") end
end
end
function Kill.check()
local r = rake()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local root = hum and (hum.RootPart or r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
if not (hum and root) then
Kill.say("armed - no Rake in the world", true)
return
end
if Ex.rakeGone(r) then
Kill.say("armed - he is down / in his cave", true)
return
end
local trap, box, mine = Kill.heldBy(hum, root)
if trap and trap ~= Kill.lastTrap then
return Kill.open(r, hum, root, trap, box, mine)
end
local near, gap = Kill.nearestMine(root)
if not near then
Kill.say("armed - place a trap where he walks (it has to be YOURS)", true)
return
end
local nbox = near:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if not Ex.killRake and nbox and nbox:IsA("BasePart") and gap <= Ex.killCatch
and os.clock() - (Kill.springAt or 0) >= 0.5 then
Kill.springAt = os.clock()
pcall(Ex.springTrap, r, nbox)
end
end
function Kill.tick()
local r = rake()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum ~= Kill.sigHum then
if Kill.sigConn then Kill.sigConn:Disconnect() Kill.sigConn = nil end
Kill.sigHum = hum
if hum then
Kill.sigConn = hum:GetAttributeChangedSignal("iTRPED2"):Connect(function()
local v
pcall(function() v = hum:GetAttribute("iTRPED2") end)
if v == true then Kill.lastTrap = nil end
if Kill.on and not Kill.win then
pcall(Kill.check)
pcall(Kill.write)
end
end)
end
end
if Kill.win then return Kill.watch() end
Kill.check()
end
function Kill.readout()
if not Kill.on then return "off" end
return Kill.said ~= "" and Kill.said or "armed"
end
function Kill.watchTimer()
if Kill.timerConn then return end
local t = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Timer")
if not (t and t:IsA("ValueBase")) then return end
Kill.timerConn = t.Changed:Connect(function(v)
if not Kill.on then return end
if type(v) == "number" and v < 0 and (Kill.lastTimer or 0) >= 0 then
notify(string.format("timer went to %d - he is out of the round", v), 6)
end
Kill.lastTimer = type(v) == "number" and v or 0
end)
end
function Kill.set(on)
if on and Kill.v2 and Kill.v2.on and Toggles.RK_InstaKillV2 then
pcall(function() Toggles.RK_InstaKillV2:SetValue(false) end)
end
Kill.on = on
Ex.instaArmed = on and true or false
if not on then
if Kill.conn then Kill.conn:Disconnect() Kill.conn = nil end
if Kill.stepConn then Kill.stepConn:Disconnect() Kill.stepConn = nil end
if Kill.sigConn then Kill.sigConn:Disconnect() Kill.sigConn = nil end
if Kill.timerConn then Kill.timerConn:Disconnect() Kill.timerConn = nil end
Kill.sigHum = nil
if Kill.win then Kill.close("OFF") end
Fight.instaWriting = false
return
end
if Kill.conn then return end
Kill.said, Kill.saidAt, Kill.win, Kill.lastTrap = "", nil, nil, nil
notify(string.format("Insta Kill armed - fires the moment he is caught in YOUR trap "
.. "(isnetworkowner: %s, sethiddenproperty: %s)",
typeof(isnetworkowner) == "function" and "yes" or "no",
typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" and "yes" or "no"), 7)
Kill.watchTimer()
Kill.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Kill.on then return end
if typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then
pcall(sethiddenproperty, LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", Kill.radius)
end
pcall(Kill.tick)
pcall(Kill.write)
end)
Kill.stepConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if Kill.on and Kill.win then pcall(Kill.write) end
end)
addCleanup("instakill", function() Kill.set(false) end)
end
do
local K2 = {
on = false,
SWING = 0.25,
PRIME = 1.5,
PRIME_AT = 6,
HOLD = 4, OUT_FOR = 0.3,
CAP = 16,
LANDING = 8,
AFTER = 10,
win = nil, after = nil, log = {},
}
function K2.simulating(root)
local ok, v = pcall(function() return (not root.Anchored) and root.ReceiveAge <= 0.005 end)
return ok and v == true
end
function K2.attr(inst, k)
local v
pcall(function() v = inst:GetAttribute(k) end)
return v
end
function K2.probe(label)
local w = K2.win or K2.after
local bits = { string.format("%.3f %s", os.clock(), label) }
pcall(function()
local r, root, hum, box = w and w.r, w and w.root, w and w.hum, w and w.box
if root then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("root receiveAge=%.3f anchored=%s simulating=%s isnetworkowner=%s",
root.ReceiveAge, tostring(root.Anchored), tostring(K2.simulating(root)), tostring(Kill.owns(root)))
end
if box and box.Parent and root then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("trap mine=%s sameAssembly=%s", tostring(w.mine),
tostring(box.AssemblyRootPart == root.AssemblyRootPart))
end
if hum then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("hum state=%s health=%.1f walk=%.1f writes=%d hits=%d",
tostring(hum:GetState()), hum.Health, hum.WalkSpeed, w.writes or 0, w.hits or 0)
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("iTRPED2=%s iTRPED3=%s HomeRun=%s",
tostring(K2.attr(hum, "iTRPED2")), tostring(K2.attr(hum, "iTRPED3")), tostring(K2.attr(hum, "HomeRun")))
end
if r then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("server Dead=%s Inactive=%s inWorld=%s",
tostring(Kill.flag(r, "Dead")), tostring(Kill.flag(r, "Inactive")), tostring(r.Parent ~= nil))
end
end)
K2.log[#K2.log + 1] = table.concat(bits, " | ")
while #K2.log > 300 do table.remove(K2.log, 1) end
if typeof(writefile) == "function" then
pcall(ensureFolder, "Rake")
pcall(ensureFolder, "Rake/dumps")
pcall(writefile, "Rake/dumps/instakill-v2-log.txt", table.concat(K2.log, "\n"))
end
end
function K2.stick()
local tool = Fight.stick()
if tool then return tool end
local hum = getHum()
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local stowed = bp and bp:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName)
if hum and stowed and os.clock() - (K2.equipAt or 0) > 0.5 then
K2.equipAt = os.clock()
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(stowed) end)
end
return nil
end
function K2.hit(root)
local me = myPos()
if not (me and root and root.Parent) or (root.Position - me).Magnitude > Fight.range then return false end
if not K2.stick() then return false end
Fight.aimHitPart(root)
local ok, sent = pcall(Fight.swing, root)
return ok and sent and true or false
end
function K2.write()
local w = K2.win
if not w then return end
local hum, root = w.hum, w.root
if not (hum and hum.Parent and root and root.Parent) then return end
local sim = K2.simulating(root)
if sim and not w.simAt then
w.simAt = os.clock()
K2.probe("this client is simulating him")
end
pcall(function() hum.Health = 0 end)
w.writes = w.writes + 1
if sim then
pcall(function()
hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
if hum:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
end
end)
end
end
function K2.open(r, hum, root, trap, box, mine)
local hp = hum.Health
if not (hp and hp > 0) then hp = Fight.instaHp or hum.MaxHealth end
local w = { r = r, hum = hum, root = root, trap = trap, box = box, mine = mine,
opened = os.clock(), writes = 0, hits = 0, hp = hp, hitAt = 0 }
K2.win, K2.after = w, nil
Fight.instaWriting, Fight.instaHp = true, hp
w.hpConn = hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(v)
if v and v > 0 then w.hp = v Fight.instaHp = v end
end)
w.stateConn = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead and not w.localDeadAt then
w.localDeadAt = os.clock()
K2.probe("died on this screen")
end
end)
K2.probe("caught" .. (mine and " (your trap)" or " (rusty / someone else's trap)"))
Kill.say("V2: he is in a trap - stun stick + kill")
local okBox = box and box:IsA("BasePart")
if okBox and not Ex.killRake then pcall(Ex.springTrap, r, box) end
if K2.hit(root) then w.hits = w.hits + 1 w.hitAt = os.clock() end
K2.write()
end
function K2.close(how)
local w = K2.win
if not w then return end
K2.win = nil
Kill.lastTrap2 = w.trap
if w.hpConn then w.hpConn:Disconnect() end
if w.stateConn then w.stateConn:Disconnect() end
Fight.instaWriting = false
local t = os.clock() - w.opened
local sim = w.simAt and string.format("you ran his physics from +%.2fs", w.simAt - w.opened)
or "the server kept his physics"
K2.probe("close " .. how)
if how == "KILLED" then
Kill.kills = (Kill.kills or 0) + 1
K2.probe(string.format("KILLED: Dead %.1fs after the catch (%s, %d hits)", t, sim, w.hits))
Kill.verdict(true)
return
end
if w.hum and w.hum.Parent and w.hp and w.hp > 0 and not w.localDeadAt then
pcall(function() w.hum.Health = w.hp end)
end
if how == "OFF" then return end
K2.after = { r = w.r, hum = w.hum, root = w.root, box = w.box, mine = w.mine,
from = os.clock(), writes = w.writes, hits = w.hits, localDead = w.localDeadAt ~= nil }
local why = (how == "OUT" and "he left the round (Inactive, not Dead)")
or (how == "GONE" and "his model left the world")
or (how == "CAP" and ("the hold ran past " .. K2.CAP .. "s"))
or (how == "LANDING" and ("he fell on YOUR screen but the server had not set Dead after " .. K2.LANDING .. "s - still watching"))
or string.format("he walked out after %.1fs", t)
K2.probe(string.format("not confirmed: %s (%s, %d hits, %d writes)", why, sim, w.hits, w.writes))
end
function K2.aftermath()
local a = K2.after
if not a then return end
local now = os.clock()
if Kill.flag(a.r, "Dead") then
K2.after = nil
K2.probe(string.format("aftermath: server set Dead %.1fs after the window closed", now - a.from))
Kill.kills = (Kill.kills or 0) + 1
Kill.verdict(true)
return
end
if not a.r.Parent or rake() ~= a.r then
K2.after = nil
K2.probe("aftermath: his model left the world")
Kill.verdict(false)
return
end
if now - (a.lastLog or 0) >= 1 then
a.lastLog = now
K2.probe("aftermath")
end
if now - a.from > K2.AFTER then
K2.after = nil
K2.probe(a.localDead and "aftermath: he only fell on YOUR screen - the server never set Dead"
or "aftermath: nothing from the server")
Kill.verdict(false)
end
end
function K2.watch()
local w = K2.win
local now = os.clock()
if Kill.flag(w.r, "Dead") then return K2.close("KILLED") end
if Kill.flag(w.r, "Inactive") then return K2.close("OUT") end
local gone = not (w.r.Parent and w.root.Parent) or rake() ~= w.r
if w.localDeadAt then
if now - w.localDeadAt > K2.LANDING then return K2.close(gone and "GONE" or "LANDING") end
return
end
if gone then return K2.close("GONE") end
if now - w.opened > K2.CAP then return K2.close("CAP") end
if now - w.hitAt >= K2.SWING and K2.hit(w.root) then
w.hits = w.hits + 1
w.hitAt = now
end
local inIt = w.box and w.box.Parent and Kill.flat(w.box.Position, w.root.Position) <= K2.HOLD
if inIt then
w.outSince = nil
else
w.outSince = w.outSince or now
if now - w.outSince >= K2.OUT_FOR then return K2.close("WALKED") end
end
end
function K2.tick()
local r = rake()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local root = hum and (hum.RootPart or r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
if hum ~= K2.sigHum then
if K2.sigConn then K2.sigConn:Disconnect() K2.sigConn = nil end
K2.sigHum = hum
if hum then
K2.sigConn = hum:GetAttributeChangedSignal("iTRPED2"):Connect(function()
if K2.attr(hum, "iTRPED2") == true then Kill.lastTrap2 = nil end
if K2.on and not K2.win then pcall(K2.tick) pcall(K2.write) end
end)
end
end
if K2.win then return K2.watch() end
pcall(K2.aftermath)
if not (hum and root) or Ex.rakeGone(r) then return end
local trap, box, mine = Kill.heldBy(hum, root)
if trap and trap ~= Kill.lastTrap2 then
return K2.open(r, hum, root, trap, box, mine)
end
if os.clock() - (K2.primeAt or 0) >= K2.PRIME then
for _, m in ipairs(Kill.traps()) do
local b = m.Parent and m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if b and b:IsA("BasePart") and Kill.flat(b.Position, root.Position) <= K2.PRIME_AT then
K2.primeAt = os.clock()
K2.hit(root)
break
end
end
end
end
function K2.set(on)
K2.on = on
if not on then
if K2.conn then K2.conn:Disconnect() K2.conn = nil end
if K2.stepConn then K2.stepConn:Disconnect() K2.stepConn = nil end
if K2.sigConn then K2.sigConn:Disconnect() K2.sigConn = nil end
K2.sigHum = nil
if K2.win then K2.close("OFF") end
K2.after = nil
Ex.instaArmed, Ex.instaAny = false, false
Fight.instaWriting = false
return
end
if Kill.on and Toggles.RK_InstaKillRake then
pcall(function() Toggles.RK_InstaKillRake:SetValue(false) end)
end
Ex.instaArmed, Ex.instaAny = true, true
if K2.conn then return end
K2.win, K2.after, Kill.lastTrap2 = nil, nil, nil
notify("Insta Kill V2 armed - any trap: stun stick + kill while he is held "
.. "(keep the stun stick on you)", 7)
K2.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not K2.on then return end
if typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then
pcall(sethiddenproperty, LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", Kill.radius)
end
pcall(K2.tick)
pcall(K2.write)
end)
K2.stepConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if K2.on and K2.win then pcall(K2.write) end
end)
addCleanup("instakillv2", function() K2.set(false) end)
end
Kill.v2 = K2
end
Ex.shockKeys = { "SHOCK_CHANCE", "SHOCK_DAMAGE", "SHOCK_TAKE", "ZAP_CHANCE",
"ZAP_DAMAGE", "ShockChance", "ShockDamage", "shockChance" }
function Ex.shockParts()
local out = {}
local folder = pathTo(Workspace, "Map", "PowerStation", "StationFolder")
if not folder then return out end
local shocker = folder:FindFirstChild("Shocker")
if shocker then
for _, p in ipairs(shocker:GetDescendants()) do
if p:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = p end
end
end
local zap = folder:FindFirstChild("ZapPart")
if zap and zap:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = zap end
return out
end
function Ex.patchShockTable()
if typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then return 0 end
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok then return 0 end
local hits = 0
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" then
for _, key in ipairs(Ex.shockKeys) do
local got, has = pcall(function() return type(rawget(v, key)) == "number" end)
if got and has then
pcall(function() v[key] = 0 end)
hits = hits + 1
end
end
end
end
return hits
end
Ex.shockLift = 4
function Ex.setBypassShock(on)
Ex.bypassShock = on
if not on then
if Ex.shockConn then Ex.shockConn:Disconnect() Ex.shockConn = nil end
Ex.shockStatus = "off"
return
end
if Ex.shockConn then return end
Ex.shockConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if not Ex.bypassShock then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
local over = false
for _, part in ipairs(Ex.shockParts()) do
local half = part.Size * 0.5
local rel = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(hrp.Position)
if math.abs(rel.X) <= half.X + 1 and math.abs(rel.Z) <= half.Z + 1
and rel.Y < half.Y + 8 and rel.Y > -half.Y - 12 then
over = true
local top = part.Position.Y + half.Y + Ex.shockLift
if hrp.Position.Y < top then
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, top, hrp.Position.Z)
* (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
if v.Y < 0 then
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
end
end
end
end
Ex.shockStatus = over and "floating over the water" or "clear"
end)
addCleanup("shock", function() Ex.setBypassShock(false) end)
end
function Ex.stationPrompt()
local part = pathTo(Workspace, "Map", "PowerStation", "StationFolder", "StationGUIPart")
return part and part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true) or nil
end
function Ex.setSilentRepair(on)
Ex.silentRepair = on
if not on then
if Ex.repairConn then Ex.repairConn:Disconnect() Ex.repairConn = nil end
local prompt = Ex.stationPrompt()
if prompt and Ex.repairSaved then
pcall(function()
prompt.HoldDuration = Ex.repairSaved.hold
prompt:SetAttribute("ActivateTime", Ex.repairSaved.activate)
end)
end
Ex.repairSaved = nil
Ex.repairStatus = "off"
return
end
if Ex.repairConn then return end
Ex.repairConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Ex.silentRepair then return end
if os.clock() - (Ex.repairLast or 0) < 0.5 then return end
Ex.repairLast = os.clock()
local prompt = Ex.stationPrompt()
if not prompt then
Ex.repairStatus = "the power station is not loaded from here"
return
end
if not Ex.repairSaved then
Ex.repairSaved = {
hold = prompt.HoldDuration,
activate = prompt:GetAttribute("ActivateTime"),
}
end
pcall(function()
prompt:SetAttribute("ActivateTime", 0)
prompt.HoldDuration = 0
prompt:SetAttribute("Unavailable", false)
prompt:SetAttribute("Unavailable2", false)
prompt:SetAttribute("Busy", false)
prompt.Enabled = true
prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
if prompt.MaxActivationDistance < 30 then prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 30 end
end)
Ex.repairStatus = "station ready - hold E to start, then walk away"
end)
addCleanup("silentrepair", function() Ex.setSilentRepair(false) end)
end
Ex.TOWER = {
Lights = { "Map", "ObservationTower", "Lights" },
Hatch  = { "Map", "ObservationTower", "Door" },
Radar  = { "Map", "ObservationTower", "Radar" },
SafeHouseDoor = { "Map", "SafeHouse", "Door" },
SafeHouseLight = { "Map", "SafeHouse", "Light" },
}
function Ex.firePrompt(prompt)
Ex.promptWas, Ex.promptTok = Ex.promptWas or {}, Ex.promptTok or {}
local keep = Ex.promptWas[prompt]
if not keep then
pcall(function()
keep = { prompt.HoldDuration, prompt.RequiresLineOfSight, prompt.MaxActivationDistance }
end)
Ex.promptWas[prompt] = keep
end
local tok = {}
Ex.promptTok[prompt] = tok
task.delay(0.6, function()
if Ex.promptTok[prompt] ~= tok then return end
Ex.promptTok[prompt], Ex.promptWas[prompt] = nil, nil
if not keep then return end
pcall(function()
if prompt.Parent then
prompt.HoldDuration, prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = keep[1], keep[2]
prompt.MaxActivationDistance = keep[3]
end
end)
end)
pcall(function()
prompt:SetAttribute("Unavailable", false)
prompt:SetAttribute("Unavailable2", false)
prompt:SetAttribute("Busy", false)
prompt:SetAttribute("Busy2", false)
prompt.Enabled = true
prompt.HoldDuration = 0
prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
if prompt.MaxActivationDistance < 100000 then prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 100000 end
end)
if typeof(fireproximityprompt) ~= "function" then return false end
if pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt) then return true end
if pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt, 1) then return true end
return false
end
function Ex.trigger(what)
local path = Ex.TOWER[what]
if not path then return end
local root = pathTo(Workspace, table.unpack(path))
if not root then
notify(what .. ": not loaded from here", 4)
return
end
local prompts, fired = 0, 0
for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
prompts = prompts + 1
if Ex.firePrompt(d) then fired = fired + 1 end
end
end
local bindables = 0
for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BindableEvent") and (d.Name == "Event" or d.Name == "Toggle" or d.Name == "ToggleE") then
if pcall(function() d:Fire() end) then bindables = bindables + 1 end
end
end
if prompts == 0 and bindables == 0 then
notify(what .. ": nothing to trigger here", 5)
elseif fired == 0 and bindables == 0 then
notify(what .. ": found " .. prompts .. " prompt(s) but this executor has no fireproximityprompt", 6)
else
notify(string.format("%s: %d/%d prompt(s), %d event(s)", what, fired, prompts, bindables), 4)
end
end
function Ex.repairNow()
local prompt = Ex.stationPrompt()
if not prompt then
notify("the power station is not loaded from here", 4)
return
end
if typeof(fireproximityprompt) ~= "function" then
notify("this executor has no fireproximityprompt", 5)
return
end
notify(Ex.firePrompt(prompt) and "repair triggered" or "the prompt refused", 4)
end
function Ex.thirdPerson()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local sw = char and pathTo(char, "RagdollTime", "RagdollSwitch")
if not sw then
notify("no RagdollSwitch on your character", 4)
return
end
sw.Value = true
sw.Value = false
end
local Round = { alerted = false }
function Round.night()
local v = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Night")
return v and v.Value or false
end
function Round.timer()
local v = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Timer")
return v and v.Value or nil
end
function Round.target()
local r = rake()
local t = r and r:FindFirstChild("TargetVal")
local val = t and t.Value
return val and tostring(val.Parent) or nil
end
function Round.line()
local t = Round.timer()
local bits = {}
bits[#bits + 1] = (Round.night() and "Time Until Day : " or "Time Until Night : ")
.. tostring(t or "?")
bits[#bits + 1] = "Rake's Target : " .. (Round.target() or "nobody")
return table.concat(bits, "\n")
end
function Round.watchBloodHour()
if Round.bloodBound then return end
Round.bloodBound = true
bind(RunService.Heartbeat, function()
if os.clock() - (Round.bloodAt or 0) < 0.5 then return end
Round.bloodAt = os.clock()
local on = false
local v = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("InitiateBloodHour")
if v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then
local ok, val = pcall(function() return v.Value end)
on = ok and val == true
end
if Round.bloodWas == nil then Round.bloodWas = on return end
if on and not Round.bloodWas and Round.bloodNotify ~= false then
notify("BLOOD HOUR IS STARTING", 6)
if Round.alert then pcall(Round.alert, "siren") end
end
Round.bloodWas = on
end)
end
local Wep = {
on = false, speed = 0.6, hue = 0, clock = 0,
color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80),
style = "Glow", mode = "Custom", strength = 0.45,
count = 0, status = "off",
tags = {}, hidden = {}, parts = {}, last = 0,
}
Wep.STYLES = { "Glow", "Neon", "Ghost", "Outline", "Solid", "Glass", "Pulse" }
Wep.MODES = { "Custom", "Rainbow", "Rainbow Wave" }
Wep.MATERIAL = {
Neon = Enum.Material.Neon, Ghost = Enum.Material.ForceField, Glass = Enum.Material.Glass,
}
Wep.BUDGET = 4
function Wep.targets()
local out = {}
local function take(inst)
if #out >= Wep.BUDGET then return end
out[#out + 1] = inst
end
local function walk(inst, depth)
if depth > 5 or #out >= Wep.BUDGET then return end
for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Model") then
take(d)
elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
take(d)
walk(d, depth + 1)
elseif d:IsA("Folder") or d:IsA("Tool") or d:IsA("Accessory") then
walk(d, depth + 1)
end
end
end
for _, root in ipairs(Wep.roots()) do
if root:IsA("Model") then
take(root)
elseif root:IsA("BasePart") then
take(root)
walk(root, 1)
else
walk(root, 1)
end
end
return out
end
function Wep.roots()
local out = {}
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char then
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Tool") then out[#out + 1] = d end
end
end
local c = cam()
if c then
for _, d in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Folder") then
out[#out + 1] = d
end
end
end
return out
end
function Wep.restoreParts()
for part, was in pairs(Wep.parts) do
if part and part.Parent then
pcall(function()
part.Material = was.material
part.Color = was.color
part.Transparency = was.transparency
if was.textureId ~= nil then part.TextureID = was.textureId end
end)
end
end
Wep.parts = {}
end
function Wep.clear()
Wep.restoreParts()
for hl in pairs(Wep.tags) do
if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
end
Wep.tags = {}
Esp.reserved = 0
for inst, was in pairs(Wep.hidden) do
if inst then
if was.parent then pcall(function() inst.Parent = was.parent end) end
end
if inst and inst.Parent then
pcall(function()
if was.transparency ~= nil then inst.Transparency = was.transparency end
if was.textureId ~= nil then inst.TextureId = was.textureId end
end)
end
end
Wep.hidden = {}
Wep.count = 0
end
addCleanup("wepchams", function() Wep.clear() end)
function Wep.current(i)
if Wep.mode == "Rainbow" then return Color3.fromHSV(Wep.hue, 1, 1) end
if Wep.mode == "Rainbow Wave" then return Color3.fromHSV((Wep.hue + (i or 0) * 0.09) % 1, 1, 1) end
return Wep.color
end
function Wep.look()
local s = math.clamp(Wep.strength or 0.45, 0, 1)
local st = Wep.style
if st == "Pulse" then s = s * (0.45 + 0.55 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(Wep.clock * 4))) end
if st == "Neon" then return 1 - s * 0.5, 0 end
if st == "Ghost" then return 1, 1 - (0.2 + 0.6 * s) end
if st == "Outline" then return 1, 1 - (0.25 + 0.75 * s) end
if st == "Solid" then return 1 - (0.4 + 0.6 * s), 0 end
if st == "Glass" then return 1 - s * 0.35, 0.3 end
return 1 - s * 0.85, 0
end
function Wep.setStyle(v)
Wep.style = v or "Glow"
if not Wep.MATERIAL[Wep.style] then Wep.restoreParts() end
Wep.last = 0
end
function Wep.claimParts(roots)
local mat = Wep.MATERIAL[Wep.style]
if not mat then return end
local live = {}
for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
local list = root:GetDescendants()
if root:IsA("BasePart") then list[#list + 1] = root end
for _, d in ipairs(list) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
local was = Wep.parts[d]
if not was and d.Transparency < 0.95 then
was = { material = d.Material, color = d.Color, transparency = d.Transparency }
if d:IsA("MeshPart") then was.textureId = d.TextureID end
Wep.parts[d] = was
end
if was then
live[d] = true
pcall(function()
d.Material = mat
if d:IsA("MeshPart") and d.TextureID ~= "" then d.TextureID = "" end
if Wep.style == "Glass" then
d.Transparency = 0.25 + (1 - Wep.strength) * 0.4
elseif Wep.style == "Ghost" then
d.Transparency = 0
else
d.Transparency = was.transparency
end
end)
end
end
end
end
for part, was in pairs(Wep.parts) do
if not live[part] then
if part.Parent then
pcall(function()
part.Material, part.Color, part.Transparency = was.material, was.color, was.transparency
if was.textureId ~= nil then part.TextureID = was.textureId end
end)
end
Wep.parts[part] = nil
end
end
end
function Wep.rescan()
local color = Wep.current()
local fillT, lineT = Wep.look()
local live, n = {}, 0
pcall(Wep.claimParts, Wep.roots())
for _, d in ipairs(Wep.targets()) do
n = n + 1
live[d] = true
local hl = d:FindFirstChild("RKWepCham")
if not hl then
hl = make("Highlight", {
Name = "RKWepCham", Adornee = d,
DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop,
}, d)
Wep.tags[hl] = true
end
hl.FillColor, hl.OutlineColor = color, color
hl.FillTransparency = fillT
hl.OutlineTransparency = lineT
end
for _, root in ipairs(Wep.roots()) do
for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
if Wep.hidden[d] == nil then
Wep.hidden[d] = { parent = d.Parent }
pcall(function() d.Parent = nil end)
end
elseif d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
if Wep.hidden[d] == nil then Wep.hidden[d] = { transparency = d.Transparency } end
pcall(function() d.Transparency = 1 end)
elseif d:IsA("SpecialMesh") and d.TextureId ~= "" then
if Wep.hidden[d] == nil then Wep.hidden[d] = { textureId = d.TextureId } end
pcall(function() d.TextureId = "" end)
end
end
end
Wep.count = n
Esp.reserved = n
Wep.status = n > 0 and (n .. " lit") or "nothing in your hands"
for hl in pairs(Wep.tags) do
if hl and (not hl.Parent or not live[hl.Parent]) then
pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end)
Wep.tags[hl] = nil
end
end
end
function Wep.repaint()
local fillT, lineT = Wep.look()
local i = 0
for hl in pairs(Wep.tags) do
if hl and hl.Parent then
i = i + 1
local color = Wep.current(i)
hl.FillColor, hl.OutlineColor = color, color
hl.FillTransparency, hl.OutlineTransparency = fillT, lineT
end
end
if Wep.MATERIAL[Wep.style] then
local j = 0
for part in pairs(Wep.parts) do
j = j + 1
if part.Parent then
local color = Wep.current(j)
if part.Color ~= color then pcall(function() part.Color = color end) end
end
end
end
end
function Wep.set(on)
Wep.on = on
if not on then
if Wep.conn then Wep.conn:Disconnect() Wep.conn = nil end
Wep.clear()
Wep.status = "off"
Esp.reserved = 0
return
end
if Wep.conn then return end
Wep.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
if not Wep.on then return end
Wep.clock = Wep.clock + dt
if Wep.mode ~= "Custom" then Wep.hue = (Wep.hue + dt * Wep.speed * 0.5) % 1 end
local now = os.clock()
if now - Wep.last >= 0.25 then
Wep.last = now
Wep.rescan()
end
Wep.repaint()
end)
addCleanup("wepchamsloop", function()
if Wep.conn then Wep.conn:Disconnect() Wep.conn = nil end
end)
end
local Comfort = {
zoom = false, minZoom = 0.5, maxZoom = 2000,
prompts = false, promptDist = 25, promptHold = {},
quiet = false, muted = {},
noJumpscare = false,
status = "off",
}
Comfort.CAM_EVENTS = { "CamEnvShakeEffect", "CamEnvShakeEffect2" }
Comfort.cutBy = {}
Comfort.wrapGate = {}
Comfort.ownFns = {}
function Comfort.shakeSignal(name)
local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(name)
if not ev then return nil end
return ev:IsA("BindableEvent") and ev.Event or ev.OnClientEvent
end
function Comfort.cutEvent(name)
Comfort.wrapGate[name] = true
if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then return 0 end
local sig = Comfort.shakeSignal(name)
if not sig then return 0 end
local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, sig)
if not ok or type(conns) ~= "table" then return 0 end
local held = Comfort.cutBy[name] or {}
local seen = {}
for _, c in ipairs(held) do seen[c] = true end
for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
local fn
pcall(function() fn = c.Function end)
if not seen[c] and not (fn and Comfort.ownFns[fn]) then
local done = false
pcall(function()
if c.Disable then c:Disable() done = true end
end)
if not done and type(fn) == "function" then
pcall(function()
local gate = function(...)
if Comfort.wrapGate[name] then return end
return fn(...)
end
Comfort.ownFns[gate] = true
c:Disconnect()
sig:Connect(gate)
done = true
end)
end
if done then
held[#held + 1] = c
seen[c] = true
end
end
end
Comfort.cutBy[name] = held
return #held
end
function Comfort.healEvent(name)
Comfort.wrapGate[name] = false
local held = Comfort.cutBy[name]
if not held then return end
Comfort.cutBy[name] = nil
for _, c in ipairs(held) do
pcall(function() if c.Enable then c:Enable() end end)
end
end
function Comfort.syncCuts()
local want = {}
if Comfort.noBob then
want.CamEnvShakeEffect, want.CamEnvShakeEffect2 = true, true
end
if Comfort.noJumpscare then
want.CamEnvShakeEffect, want.CamEnvShakeEffect2 = true, true
end
local cut = 0
for _, name in ipairs(Comfort.CAM_EVENTS) do
if want[name] then cut = cut + (Comfort.cutEvent(name) or 0) else Comfort.healEvent(name) end
end
return cut
end
addCleanup("camcuts", function()
Comfort.noBob, Comfort.noJumpscare = false, false
Comfort.syncCuts()
end)
function Comfort.tintables()
local out = {}
for _, holder in ipairs({ Lighting, cam() }) do
if holder then
for _, d in ipairs(holder:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and d.Name ~= "RKGrade" and d.Name ~= "RKRtxCC"
and d.Name ~= "RKKillFlash" then
out[#out + 1] = d
end
end
end
end
return out
end
function Comfort.setNoTint(on)
Comfort.noTint = on
if not on then
for cc, was in pairs(Comfort.tintWas or {}) do
if cc and cc.Parent then
pcall(function()
cc.TintColor = was.tint
cc.Saturation = was.sat
end)
end
end
Comfort.tintWas = nil
if Comfort.tintConn then Comfort.tintConn:Disconnect() Comfort.tintConn = nil end
return
end
Comfort.tintWas = Comfort.tintWas or {}
if Comfort.tintConn then return end
local function hold()
local n = 0
for _, cc in ipairs(Comfort.tintables()) do
pcall(function()
if Comfort.tintWas[cc] == nil then
Comfort.tintWas[cc] = {
tint = cc.TintColor, sat = cc.Saturation,
bright = cc.Brightness, enabled = cc.Enabled,
}
end
if cc.TintColor ~= Color3.new(1, 1, 1) then
cc.TintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
n = n + 1
end
if cc.Saturation < 0 then cc.Saturation = 0 end
end)
end
Comfort.tintHeld = n
end
hold()
Comfort.tintConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Comfort.noTint then return end
if os.clock() - (Comfort.tintLast or 0) < 0.3 then return end
Comfort.tintLast = os.clock()
hold()
end)
addCleanup("notint", function() Comfort.setNoTint(false) end)
end
Comfort.bobMethod = "Game + Stabilize"
Comfort.BOB_METHODS = { "Game + Stabilize", "Game Settings", "Stabilize Camera" }
function Comfort.bobTables()
local out = {}
if typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then return out end
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok then return out end
local seen = {}
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" then
local got, b = pcall(rawget, v, "BOB_SETTINGS")
if got and type(b) == "table" and not seen[b] then
seen[b] = true
out[#out + 1] = b
end
end
end
return out
end
function Comfort.patchBob(on)
if not on then
for _, e in ipairs(Comfort.bobSaved or {}) do
pcall(function() e.t[e.k] = e.v end)
end
Comfort.bobTbl, Comfort.bobSaved, Comfort.bobCount = nil, nil, nil
return 0
end
if Comfort.bobSaved then return Comfort.bobCount or 0 end
local tables = Comfort.bobTables()
if #tables == 0 then return -1 end
local saved, keys, seen = {}, {}, {}
local function walk(t, depth)
if depth > 3 or seen[t] then return end
seen[t] = true
pcall(function()
for k, v in pairs(t) do
if type(v) == "number" and v ~= 0 then
saved[#saved + 1] = { t = t, k = k, v = v, z = 0 }
t[k] = 0
keys[tostring(k)] = true
elseif type(v) == "boolean" and v == true and tostring(k):lower():find("enable") then
saved[#saved + 1] = { t = t, k = k, v = v, z = false }
t[k] = false
keys[tostring(k)] = true
elseif type(v) == "table" then
walk(v, depth + 1)
end
end
end)
end
for _, b in ipairs(tables) do walk(b, 1) end
local names = {}
for k in pairs(keys) do names[#names + 1] = k end
table.sort(names)
Comfort.bobKeys = table.concat(names, ", ")
Comfort.bobTbl, Comfort.bobSaved, Comfort.bobCount = tables, saved, #saved
return #saved
end
function Comfort.reassertBob()
for _, e in ipairs(Comfort.bobSaved or {}) do
pcall(function() if e.t[e.k] ~= e.z then e.t[e.k] = e.z end end)
end
end
Comfort.STAB_TAU = 0.6
Comfort.SHAKE_TURN = math.rad(8)
function Comfort.watchShake(on)
Comfort.shakeWatch = Comfort.shakeWatch or {}
if not on then
for name, conn in pairs(Comfort.shakeWatch) do
pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
Comfort.shakeWatch[name] = nil
end
Comfort.shakeUntil = nil
return
end
for _, name in ipairs(Comfort.CAM_EVENTS) do
local conn = Comfort.shakeWatch[name]
local sig = (not (conn and conn.Connected)) and Comfort.shakeSignal(name)
if sig then
local fn = function(...)
local dur, args = 2.5, table.pack(...)
for i = args.n, 1, -1 do
if type(args[i]) == "number" then dur = args[i] break end
end
Comfort.shakeUntil = math.max(Comfort.shakeUntil or 0,
os.clock() + math.clamp(dur, 0.2, 6) + 0.3)
end
Comfort.ownFns[fn] = true
Comfort.shakeWatch[name] = sig:Connect(fn)
end
end
end
function Comfort.holdShake(c)
local want = Comfort.stabSet
if not want or os.clock() > (Comfort.shakeUntil or 0) then return end
if (Free and Free.on) or (Stretch and Stretch.on) or c.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then return end
local cf = c.CFrame
if cf == want then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
local headPos = (head and head:IsA("BasePart")) and head.Position or (hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
if (cf.Position - headPos).Magnitude > 2.5 then return end
local rot = want - want.Position
local _, angle = (rot:Inverse() * (cf - cf.Position)):ToAxisAngle()
if angle > Comfort.SHAKE_TURN then return end
local pos = cf.Position
if not Comfort.stabUse() and (pos - want.Position).Magnitude <= 1 then pos = want.Position end
c.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * rot
Comfort.shakeHeld = (Comfort.shakeHeld or 0) + 1
end
function Comfort.stabUse()
return Comfort.noBob and Comfort.bobMethod ~= "Game Settings"
end
function Comfort.stabilize(dt)
local c, hrp = cam(), getHRP()
if not (c and hrp) or (Free and Free.on) or (Stretch and Stretch.on)
or c.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then
Comfort.stabOff = nil
return
end
local cf = c.CFrame
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
local headPos = (head and head:IsA("BasePart")) and head.Position or (hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
if (cf.Position - headPos).Magnitude > 2.5 then Comfort.stabOff = nil return end
local here = hrp.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(cf.Position)
local a = 1 - math.exp(-(dt or 1 / 60) / Comfort.STAB_TAU)
local off = Comfort.stabOff
if not off or (here - off).Magnitude > 1.5 then off = here else off = off:Lerp(here, a) end
Comfort.stabOff = off
local pos = hrp.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(off)
local look = cf.LookVector
if math.abs(look.Y) > 0.985 then
c.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * (cf - cf.Position)
else
c.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + look)
end
end
function Comfort.stabEarly(dt)
if not Comfort.noBob then return end
Comfort.stabDt = dt
if Comfort.stabUse() and not Comfort.stabLate then
Comfort.stabilize(dt)
end
local c = cam()
Comfort.stabSet = c and c.CFrame or nil
end
function Comfort.stabLateStep()
if not Comfort.noBob then return end
local c = cam()
if not c then return end
if Comfort.stabUse() then
if Comfort.stabLate then
Comfort.stabilize(Comfort.stabDt)
elseif Comfort.stabSet and Comfort.stabOff and not (Stretch and Stretch.on) and not (Free and Free.on) then
if c.CFrame ~= Comfort.stabSet then
Comfort.stabMiss = (Comfort.stabMiss or 0) + 1
if Comfort.stabMiss > 45 then Comfort.stabLate = true end
else
Comfort.stabMiss = math.max(0, (Comfort.stabMiss or 0) - 1)
end
end
end
pcall(Comfort.holdShake, c)
Comfort.stabSet = nil
end
function Comfort.setStab(on)
if on and not Comfort.stabBound then
Comfort.stabLate, Comfort.stabMiss, Comfort.stabOff = false, 0, nil
local ok = pcall(function()
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RKNoBobEarly", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 3, Comfort.stabEarly)
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RKNoBobLate", Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value - 5, Comfort.stabLateStep)
end)
if ok then
Comfort.stabBound = "bound"
else
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RKNoBobEarly") end)
Comfort.stabBound = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
Comfort.stabLate = true
Comfort.stabDt = dt
Comfort.stabLateStep()
end)
end
addCleanup("nobobstab", function() Comfort.setStab(false) end)
elseif not on and Comfort.stabBound then
if Comfort.stabBound == "bound" then
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RKNoBobEarly") end)
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RKNoBobLate") end)
else
pcall(function() Comfort.stabBound:Disconnect() end)
end
Comfort.stabBound, Comfort.stabOff = nil, nil
end
end
function Comfort.setBobMethod(v)
Comfort.bobMethod = v or "Game + Stabilize"
if not Comfort.noBob then return end
if Comfort.bobMethod == "Stabilize Camera" then
Comfort.patchBob(false)
else
Comfort.bobTries = 0
Comfort.patchBob(true)
end
Comfort.setStab(Comfort.noBob)
end
function Comfort.setNoBob(on)
Comfort.noBob = on
if on then Comfort.bobTries = 0 end
local useGame = Comfort.bobMethod ~= "Stabilize Camera"
local hit = (on and not useGame) and 0 or Comfort.patchBob(on)
Comfort.setStab(on)
Comfort.watchShake(on)
if on then
local stab = Comfort.stabUse() and " + camera stabiliser" or ""
local cut = Comfort.syncCuts() or 0
local shake = cut > 0 and (" | screen shake off (" .. cut .. " shake listener" .. (cut == 1 and "" or "s") .. " cut)")
or " | screen shake: held by the camera filter (no listener to cut yet)"
if not useGame then
notify("view bob off - camera stabiliser" .. shake, 5)
elseif hit > 0 then
notify("view bob off - " .. hit .. " values zeroed (" .. tostring(Comfort.bobKeys or "") .. ")" .. stab .. shake, 6)
elseif hit == 0 then
notify("BOB_SETTINGS was already all zeroes" .. stab .. shake, 5)
else
notify("BOB_SETTINGS not on the heap yet - still looking" .. stab .. shake, 6)
end
end
if not on then
if Comfort.bobConn then Comfort.bobConn:Disconnect() Comfort.bobConn = nil end
if Comfort.bobGuard then Comfort.bobGuard:Disconnect() Comfort.bobGuard = nil end
Comfort.bobbed = nil
Comfort.syncCuts()
return
end
local function hold()
local hum = getHum()
if hum and hum.CameraOffset ~= Vector3.zero then
hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero
end
end
local function rebind()
if Comfort.bobGuard then Comfort.bobGuard:Disconnect() Comfort.bobGuard = nil end
local hum = getHum()
Comfort.bobbed = hum
if hum then
Comfort.bobGuard = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraOffset"):Connect(hold)
hold()
end
end
rebind()
Comfort.syncCuts()
if Comfort.bobConn then return end
Comfort.bobConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not Comfort.noBob then return end
if getHum() ~= Comfort.bobbed then rebind() end
hold()
local useGame = Comfort.bobMethod ~= "Stabilize Camera"
if useGame and not Comfort.bobSaved and (Comfort.bobTries or 0) < 10
and os.clock() - (Comfort.bobHunt or 0) >= 3 then
Comfort.bobHunt = os.clock()
Comfort.bobTries = (Comfort.bobTries or 0) + 1
Comfort.patchBob(true)
end
if useGame and Comfort.bobSaved and os.clock() - (Comfort.bobReAt or 0) >= 1 then
Comfort.bobReAt = os.clock()
Comfort.reassertBob()
end
if os.clock() - (Comfort.cutLast or 0) >= 2 then
Comfort.cutLast = os.clock()
Comfort.syncCuts()
end
end)
addCleanup("nobob", function() Comfort.setNoBob(false) end)
end
function Comfort.applyZoom()
local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
if not lp or not Comfort.zoom then return end
pcall(function()
if lp.CameraMinZoomDistance ~= Comfort.minZoom then
lp.CameraMinZoomDistance = Comfort.minZoom
end
if lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance < Comfort.maxZoom then
lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance = Comfort.maxZoom
end
end)
end
function Comfort.setZoom(on)
Comfort.zoom = on
if on and Toggles.RK_ViewModel and Toggles.RK_ViewModel.Value then
pcall(function() Toggles.RK_ViewModel:SetValue(false) end)
end
local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
if not lp then return end
if Comfort.savedZoom == nil then
Comfort.savedZoom = { min = lp.CameraMinZoomDistance, max = lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance }
end
if not on then
for _, c in ipairs(Comfort.zoomConns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Comfort.zoomConns = nil
pcall(function()
lp.CameraMinZoomDistance = Comfort.savedZoom.min
lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance = Comfort.savedZoom.max
end)
return
end
Comfort.applyZoom()
if Comfort.zoomConns then return end
Comfort.zoomConns = {
lp:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraMinZoomDistance"):Connect(Comfort.applyZoom),
lp:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraMaxZoomDistance"):Connect(Comfort.applyZoom),
}
local char = lp.Character
if char then
Comfort.zoomConns[#Comfort.zoomConns + 1] = char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(d)
if d:IsA("Tool") then
task.defer(Comfort.applyZoom)
task.delay(0.2, Comfort.applyZoom)
end
end)
end
Comfort.zoomConns[#Comfort.zoomConns + 1] =
lp:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraMode"):Connect(function()
if Comfort.zoom and lp.CameraMode ~= Enum.CameraMode.Classic then
pcall(function() lp.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.Classic end)
end
end)
addCleanup("zoomconns", function()
for _, c in ipairs(Comfort.zoomConns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Comfort.zoomConns = nil
end)
end
addCleanup("zoom", function()
if Comfort.savedZoom then Comfort.setZoom(false) end
end)
function Comfort.setPrompts(on)
Comfort.prompts = on
if not on then
for prompt, was in pairs(Comfort.promptHold) do
if prompt and prompt.Parent then
pcall(function()
prompt.HoldDuration = was.hold
prompt.MaxActivationDistance = was.dist
prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = was.los
if was.enabled ~= nil then prompt.Enabled = was.enabled end
if was.click ~= nil then prompt.ClickablePrompt = was.click end
end)
end
end
Comfort.promptHold = {}
if Comfort.promptConn then Comfort.promptConn:Disconnect() Comfort.promptConn = nil end
return
end
if Comfort.promptConn then return end
Comfort.promptConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Comfort.prompts then return end
if os.clock() - (Comfort.promptLast or 0) < 0.4 then return end
Comfort.promptLast = os.clock()
local n = 0
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
n = n + 1
if Comfort.promptHold[d] == nil then
Comfort.promptHold[d] = {
hold = d.HoldDuration, dist = d.MaxActivationDistance,
los = d.RequiresLineOfSight,
enabled = d.Enabled, click = d.ClickablePrompt,
}
end
pcall(function()
d.Enabled = true
d.HoldDuration = 0
d.RequiresLineOfSight = false
d.ClickablePrompt = true
if d.MaxActivationDistance < Comfort.promptDist then
d.MaxActivationDistance = Comfort.promptDist
end
d:SetAttribute("Busy", false)
d:SetAttribute("Busy2", false)
d:SetAttribute("Unavailable", false)
d:SetAttribute("Unavailable2", false)
end)
end
end
Comfort.status = n .. " prompts unlocked"
end)
addCleanup("prompts", function() Comfort.setPrompts(false) end)
end
local QUIET = { running = true, climbing = true, swimming = true,
jumping = true, landhard = true, land_generic = true, freefalling = true,
getting = true, gettingup = true }
function Comfort.setQuiet(on)
Comfort.quiet = on
if not on then
for snd, vol in pairs(Comfort.muted) do
if snd and snd.Parent then pcall(function() snd.Volume = vol end) end
end
Comfort.muted = {}
if Comfort.quietConn then Comfort.quietConn:Disconnect() Comfort.quietConn = nil end
return
end
if Comfort.quietConn then return end
Comfort.quietConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Comfort.quiet then return end
if os.clock() - (Comfort.quietLast or 0) < 1 then return end
Comfort.quietLast = os.clock()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if not char then return end
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Sound") and QUIET[string.lower(d.Name)] and d.Volume > 0 then
if Comfort.muted[d] == nil then Comfort.muted[d] = d.Volume end
d.Volume = 0
end
end
end)
addCleanup("quiet", function() Comfort.setQuiet(false) end)
end
function Comfort.camGrabbed(c, hum)
local r = rake()
if not r then return false end
local subj = c.CameraSubject
if subj and subj ~= hum and subj:IsDescendantOf(r) then return true end
if c.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then return false end
local rhrp = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local me = getHRP()
return (rhrp and me and (rhrp.Position - me.Position).Magnitude < 40) or false
end
function Comfort.camHold()
if not Comfort.noJumpscare then return end
if Free and Free.on then return end
local c, hum = cam(), getHum()
if not (c and hum) or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
pcall(function()
if not Comfort.camGrabbed(c, hum) then return end
Comfort.grabbedAt = os.clock()
if c.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then
c.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
end
if c.CameraSubject ~= hum then c.CameraSubject = hum end
end)
end
function Comfort.scarePart()
local r = rake()
if not r then return nil end
local p = Comfort.camPart
if p and p.Parent and p:IsDescendantOf(r) then return p end
local root = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
p = (root and root:FindFirstChild("CameraPart")) or r:FindFirstChild("CameraPart", true)
Comfort.camPart = (p and p:IsA("BasePart")) and p or nil
return Comfort.camPart
end
function Comfort.camLast()
if not Comfort.noJumpscare then return end
if Free and Free.on then return end
local c, hum = cam(), getHum()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not (c and hum and head) or hum.Health <= 0 then
Comfort.camGood = nil
return
end
pcall(function()
local cf = c.CFrame
local part = Comfort.scarePart()
local onHim = part and (cf.Position - part.Position).Magnitude < 1.5
local stolen = onHim or (os.clock() - (Comfort.grabbedAt or 0) < 0.3
and c.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom)
if not stolen then
Comfort.camGood = { off = cf.Position - head.Position, rot = cf - cf.Position }
return
end
Comfort.grabbedAt = os.clock()
local good = Comfort.camGood
if good then c.CFrame = CFrame.new(head.Position + good.off) * good.rot end
end)
end
Comfort.CAM_STEPS = {
{ name = "RKNoScareEarly", offset = -1, fn = "camHold" },
{ name = "RKNoScare", offset = 1, fn = "camHold" },
{ name = "RKNoScareLast", fn = "camLast" },
}
function Comfort.holdCam()
if Comfort.camBound then return end
local ok = pcall(function()
for _, step in ipairs(Comfort.CAM_STEPS) do
local at = step.offset and (Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + step.offset)
or Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value
RunService:BindToRenderStep(step.name, at, Comfort[step.fn])
end
end)
if ok then
Comfort.camBound = true
else
for _, step in ipairs(Comfort.CAM_STEPS) do
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(step.name) end)
end
if Comfort.camConn then Comfort.camConn:Disconnect() end
Comfort.camConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
Comfort.camHold()
Comfort.camLast()
end)
end
end
function Comfort.dropCam()
if Comfort.camBound then
for _, step in ipairs(Comfort.CAM_STEPS) do
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(step.name) end)
end
Comfort.camBound = false
end
if Comfort.camConn then Comfort.camConn:Disconnect() Comfort.camConn = nil end
Comfort.camGood, Comfort.grabbedAt = nil, nil
end
Comfort.NOT_SCARE = { MotionBlur = true, ESCB = true, ESCC = true }
function Comfort.isScareFx(fx)
if not (fx:IsA("BlurEffect") or fx:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")) then return false end
if Comfort.NOT_SCARE[fx.Name] then return false end
local nvg = false
pcall(function() nvg = fx:GetAttribute("NVG") ~= nil end)
return not nvg
end
Comfort.STINGS = { "Susto" }
function Comfort.holdSting()
Comfort.stingVol = Comfort.stingVol or {}
Comfort.stingGuards = Comfort.stingGuards or {}
for snd, conns in pairs(Comfort.stingGuards) do
if not snd.Parent then
for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Comfort.stingGuards[snd], Comfort.stingVol[snd] = nil, nil
end
end
local ui = realUI()
if not ui then return end
for _, name in ipairs(Comfort.STINGS) do
local snd = ui:FindFirstChild(name)
if snd and snd:IsA("Sound") and not Comfort.stingGuards[snd] then
Comfort.stingVol[snd] = snd.Volume
pcall(function() snd.Volume = 0 snd:Stop() end)
Comfort.stingGuards[snd] = {
snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Volume"):Connect(function()
if Comfort.noJumpscare and snd.Volume > 0 then
Comfort.stingVol[snd] = snd.Volume
snd.Volume = 0
end
end),
snd.Played:Connect(function()
if Comfort.noJumpscare then pcall(function() snd:Stop() end) end
end),
}
end
end
end
function Comfort.dropSting()
for snd, conns in pairs(Comfort.stingGuards or {}) do
for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
local vol = Comfort.stingVol and Comfort.stingVol[snd]
if vol and snd.Parent then pcall(function() snd.Volume = vol end) end
end
Comfort.stingGuards, Comfort.stingVol = {}, {}
end
function Comfort.setNoJumpscare(on)
Comfort.noJumpscare = on
if not on then
Comfort.syncCuts()
Comfort.dropCam()
Comfort.dropSting()
if Comfort.fxConn then Comfort.fxConn:Disconnect() Comfort.fxConn = nil end
local base, uiBase = Comfort.fxBase or {}, Comfort.uiBase or {}
for fx, was in pairs(Comfort.fxOff or {}) do
if fx and fx.Parent and base[fx] then pcall(function() fx.Enabled = was end) end
end
for d, was in pairs(Comfort.uiOff or {}) do
if d and d.Parent and uiBase[d] then pcall(function() d.Visible = was end) end
end
Comfort.fxOff, Comfort.uiOff, Comfort.fxBase, Comfort.uiBase = {}, {}, nil, nil
return
end
Comfort.fxOff = Comfort.fxOff or {}
if not Comfort.fxConn then
Comfort.fxBase, Comfort.uiBase = {}, {}
local c0 = cam()
if c0 then
for _, fx in ipairs(c0:GetChildren()) do
if Comfort.isScareFx(fx) and fx.Enabled then
Comfort.fxBase[fx] = true
end
end
end
local ui0 = realUI()
if ui0 then
for _, d in ipairs(ui0:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("ImageLabel") and d.Visible
and (d.Name == "BloodShot" or d.Name == "BloodShot2"
or d.Name == "GrainImage" or d.Name == "GrainImage2") then
Comfort.uiBase[d] = true
end
end
end
end
Comfort.syncCuts()
Comfort.holdCam()
Comfort.holdSting()
if Comfort.fxConn then return end
Comfort.fxConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not Comfort.noJumpscare then return end
if os.clock() - (Comfort.cutLast or 0) >= 2 then
Comfort.cutLast = os.clock()
Comfort.syncCuts()
Comfort.holdSting()
end
local c = cam()
if c then
for _, fx in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
if Comfort.isScareFx(fx) and fx.Enabled then
if Comfort.fxOff[fx] == nil then Comfort.fxOff[fx] = true end
fx.Enabled = false
end
end
end
local ui = realUI()
if ui then
for _, d in ipairs(ui:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("ImageLabel")
and (d.Name == "BloodShot" or d.Name == "BloodShot2"
or d.Name == "GrainImage" or d.Name == "GrainImage2")
and d.Visible then
Comfort.uiOff = Comfort.uiOff or {}
if Comfort.uiOff[d] == nil then Comfort.uiOff[d] = true end
d.Visible = false
end
end
end
end)
addCleanup("nojumpscare", function() Comfort.setNoJumpscare(false) end)
end
local Sky = {
preset = "Original", on = false, saved = nil, last = 0,
}
local SKY_PRESETS = { "Original", "Bright Day", "Golden Hour", "Sunset", "Overcast",
"Ashfall", "Storm", "Full Moon", "Aurora", "Blood Moon", "Pitch Black",
"Frostbite", "Night Club", "Vaporwave", "Toxic", "Deep Sea" }

local SKY_ANIMATED = { ["Night Club"] = true }
local FULLBRIGHT = {
ClockTime = 14, Brightness = 4, FogEnd = 100000,
FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
}
function Sky.sets()
local out = {}
for _, name in ipairs({ "CurrentLightingProperties", "CurrentLightingProperties2" }) do
local folder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(name)
if folder then out[#out + 1] = folder end
end
return out
end
function Sky.capture()
if Sky.saved then return end
Sky.saved = {}
for _, folder in ipairs(Sky.sets()) do
local row = {}
for _, v in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
if v:IsA("ValueBase") then row[v.Name] = v.Value end
end
Sky.saved[folder] = row
end
end
function Sky.write(look)
for _, folder in ipairs(Sky.sets()) do
for key, value in pairs(look) do
local v = folder:FindFirstChild(key)
if v then
Sky.guard(v, key)
pcall(function() v.Value = value end)
end
end
end
end
Sky.guards = {}
function Sky.wanted()
if Sky.fullbright then return FULLBRIGHT end
if SKY_ANIMATED[Sky.preset] then return Sky.clubLook() end
return SKY_LOOKS[Sky.preset]
end
function Sky.guard(value, key)
if Sky.guards[value] then return end
Sky.guards[value] = value.Changed:Connect(function()
if not Sky.on then return end
if SKY_ANIMATED[Sky.preset] and not Sky.fullbright then return end
if key == "FogEnd" and Fog.on then return end
local look = Sky.wanted()
local want = look and look[key]
if want == nil then return end
if value.Value ~= want then
pcall(function() value.Value = want end)
end
end)
end
function Sky.dropGuards()
for value, conn in pairs(Sky.guards) do
pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
Sky.guards[value] = nil
end
end
function Sky.restore()
Sky.dropGuards()
for folder, row in pairs(Sky.saved or {}) do
if folder and folder.Parent then
for key, value in pairs(row) do
local v = folder:FindFirstChild(key)
if v then pcall(function() v.Value = value end) end
end
end
end
Sky.saved = nil
end
addCleanup("sky", function()
Sky.on = false
Sky.restore()
end)
function Sky.clubLook()
Sky.hue = ((Sky.hue or 0) + 0.004) % 1
local main = Color3.fromHSV(Sky.hue, 1, 1)
local off = Color3.fromHSV((Sky.hue + 0.5) % 1, 1, 1)
return {
ClockTime = 0, Brightness = 2, FogEnd = 800,
FogColor = off, Ambient = main, OutdoorAmbient = off,
}
end
function Sky.setFullbright(on)
Sky.fullbright = on
Sky.capture()
if on then
if Sky.rainConn then Sky.rainConn:Disconnect() Sky.rainConn = nil end
if Sky.rainPart then pcall(function() Sky.rainPart:Destroy() end) Sky.rainPart = nil end
end
Sky.weather(Sky.preset)
Sky.paintSky(Sky.preset)
if on then
Sky.on = true
Sky.write(FULLBRIGHT)
pcall(function()
if Sky.savedShadows == nil then Sky.savedShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows end
Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
end)
Sky.startLoop()
else
if Sky.savedShadows ~= nil then
local was = Sky.savedShadows
Sky.savedShadows = nil
pcall(function() Lighting.GlobalShadows = was end)
end
if Sky.preset == "Original" then
Sky.on = false
Sky.restore()
end
end
end
function Sky.startLoop()
if Sky.conn then return end
Sky.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Sky.on then return end
for _, holder in ipairs({ Lighting, cam() }) do
if holder then
for _, d in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and d.Name ~= "RKGrade" and d.Name ~= "RKRtxCC"
and d.Name ~= "RKKillFlash" and d.Enabled then
pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end)
end
end
end
end
for _, a in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
if a:IsA("Atmosphere") and a.Name ~= "RKAtmos" and a.Name ~= "RKSkyAtmos" then
if Sky.gameAtmos == nil then Sky.gameAtmos = a end
pcall(function() a.Parent = nil end)
end
end
if SKY_ANIMATED[Sky.preset] and not Sky.fullbright then
Sky.write(Sky.clubLook())
return
end
if os.clock() - Sky.last < 1 then return end
Sky.last = os.clock()
local live = Sky.wanted()
if live then Sky.write(live) end
end)
addCleanup("skyloop", function()
if Sky.conn then Sky.conn:Disconnect() Sky.conn = nil end
end)
end

["Bright Day"] = { clouds = { cover = 0.55, density = 0.7,
colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) } },
Sunset = { clouds = { cover = 0.7, density = 0.62,
colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 180) } },
["Blood Moon"] = { clouds = { cover = 0.8, density = 0.9,
colour = Color3.fromRGB(120, 40, 40) } },
Overcast = { clouds = { cover = 0.95, density = 0.85,
colour = Color3.fromRGB(190, 192, 198) } },
["Full Moon"] = { rain = 55 },
["Deep Sea"] = { rain = 110 },
["Golden Hour"] = { clouds = { cover = 0.45, density = 0.55,
colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 226, 190) } },
Ashfall = { rain = 130 },
Storm = { rain = 200 },
Frostbite = { clouds = { cover = 0.9, density = 0.75,
colour = Color3.fromRGB(226, 238, 250) } },
Aurora = { clouds = { cover = 0.35, density = 0.4,
colour = Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 180) } },
}
function Sky.clearWeather()
Sky.clearGrade()
for _, c in ipairs(Sky.cloudGuards or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Sky.cloudGuards, Sky.cloudGuardFor = nil, nil
Sky.cloudSpec = nil
if Sky.clouds then
if Sky.clouds.Name == "RKClouds" then
pcall(function() Sky.clouds:Destroy() end)
elseif Sky.gameClouds then
pcall(function()
Sky.clouds.Cover = Sky.gameClouds.cover
Sky.clouds.Density = Sky.gameClouds.density
Sky.clouds.Color = Sky.gameClouds.colour
if Sky.gameClouds.enabled ~= nil then
Sky.clouds.Enabled = Sky.gameClouds.enabled
end
end)
end
Sky.clouds = nil
Sky.gameClouds = nil
end
Sky.cloudNote = nil
if Sky.rainConn then Sky.rainConn:Disconnect() Sky.rainConn = nil end
if Sky.rainPart then
pcall(function() Sky.rainPart:Destroy() end)
Sky.rainPart = nil
end
end
addCleanup("weather", function() Sky.clearWeather() end)
function Sky.makeClouds(spec)
Sky.cloudNote = nil
local terrain = Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
if not terrain then
Sky.cloudNote = "no Terrain in this place, so there is nowhere to put clouds"
return
end
local existing = terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")
if existing then
if existing.Name ~= "RKClouds" and Sky.gameClouds == nil then
pcall(function()
Sky.gameClouds = { obj = existing, cover = existing.Cover,
density = existing.Density, colour = existing.Color,
enabled = existing.Enabled }
end)
end
Sky.clouds = existing
else
local ok, made = pcall(function() return Instance.new("Clouds") end)
if not ok or not made then
Sky.cloudNote = "this client does not support Clouds"
return
end
made.Name = "RKClouds"
local parented = pcall(function() made.Parent = terrain end)
if not parented then
Sky.cloudNote = "Clouds could not be parented to Terrain"
return
end
Sky.clouds = made
end
Sky.cloudSpec = spec
local function paint()
local c = Sky.clouds
if not (c and c.Parent) then return end
local want = Sky.cloudSpec
if not want then return end
pcall(function()
if c.Enabled ~= true then c.Enabled = true end
end)
pcall(function()
if math.abs(c.Cover - want.cover) > 0.01 then c.Cover = want.cover end
if math.abs(c.Density - want.density) > 0.01 then c.Density = want.density end
if c.Color ~= want.colour then c.Color = want.colour end
end)
end
paint()
if Sky.cloudGuardFor ~= Sky.clouds then
for _, c in ipairs(Sky.cloudGuards or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Sky.cloudGuards = {}
Sky.cloudGuardFor = Sky.clouds
for _, prop in ipairs({ "Enabled", "Cover", "Density", "Color" }) do
local ok, conn = pcall(function()
return Sky.clouds:GetPropertyChangedSignal(prop):Connect(paint)
end)
if ok and conn then Sky.cloudGuards[#Sky.cloudGuards + 1] = conn end
end
end
end
function Sky.cloudReport()
local terrain = Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
if not terrain then return "no Terrain in this place - clouds have nowhere to live" end
local deck = terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")
if not deck then return "no Clouds object on Terrain - this client refused to make one" end
local bits = {}
local ok = pcall(function()
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("cover %.2f density %.2f", deck.Cover, deck.Density)
end)
if not ok then bits[#bits + 1] = "could not read the deck" end
pcall(function()
if deck.Enabled == false then bits[#bits + 1] = "DECK IS DISABLED" end
end)
local c = cam()
if c and c.CFrame.Position.Y > 6900 then
bits[#bits + 1] = "YOU ARE ABOVE THE CLOUD LAYER - fly back down"
end
local atmos = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")
if atmos then
local h, d, mine = 0, 0, atmos.Name == "RKAtmos"
pcall(function() h, d = atmos.Haze, atmos.Density end)
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("%s atmosphere, haze %.1f density %.2f",
mine and "our" or "the game's", h, d)
if h >= 2.5 or d >= 0.6 then
bits[#bits + 1] = "the atmosphere is thick enough to wash them out"
end
else
local fog = 0
pcall(function() fog = Lighting.FogEnd end)
if fog < 2000 then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("no atmosphere and fog ends at %d - the sky is fog", fog)
end
end
local q
pcall(function()
q = UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").SavedQualityLevel
end)
if q ~= nil then
local n = tonumber(tostring(q):match("(%d+)"))
if n == nil then
bits[#bits + 1] = "graphics quality is Automatic - the engine is free to sit under 6, pin it in Esc > Settings"
elseif n < 6 then
bits[#bits + 1] = "graphics quality " .. n .. " - clouds need about 6"
end
end
return "clouds: " .. table.concat(bits, ", ")
end
function Sky.makeRain(rate)
if Sky.rainPart and Sky.rainPart.Parent then
if rate and Sky.rainEmitter then pcall(function() Sky.rainEmitter.Rate = rate end) end
return
end
if not rate then return end
local part = make("Part", {
Name = "RKRain", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false,
CanTouch = false, Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(90, 1, 90),
}, Workspace)
local emitter = make("ParticleEmitter", {
Name = "Drops",
Texture = "rbxassetid://241629053",
Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(190, 205, 225)),
Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.45),
Size = NumberSequence.new(0.16),
Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.7, 0.9),
Rate = rate,
Speed = NumberRange.new(85, 105),
SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(4, 4),
Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 0),
LightEmission = 0.2,
Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, -40, 0),
EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom,
ZOffset = 2,
}, part)
Sky.rainPart, Sky.rainEmitter = part, emitter
Sky.rainConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local c = cam()
if not c or not Sky.rainPart then return end
Sky.rainPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(c.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 42, 0))
end)
end
function Sky.weather(name)
local spec = Sky.WEATHER[name]
if not spec then
Sky.clearWeather()
Sky.grade(name)
return
end
if spec.clouds then
if Sky.rainConn then Sky.rainConn:Disconnect() Sky.rainConn = nil end
if Sky.rainPart then pcall(function() Sky.rainPart:Destroy() end) Sky.rainPart = nil end
Sky.makeClouds(spec.clouds)
elseif spec.rain then
if Sky.clouds then
if Sky.clouds.Name == "RKClouds" then
pcall(function() Sky.clouds:Destroy() end)
elseif Sky.gameClouds then
pcall(function()
Sky.clouds.Cover = Sky.gameClouds.cover
Sky.clouds.Density = Sky.gameClouds.density
Sky.clouds.Color = Sky.gameClouds.colour
if Sky.gameClouds.enabled ~= nil then
Sky.clouds.Enabled = Sky.gameClouds.enabled
end
end)
end
Sky.clouds, Sky.gameClouds = nil, nil
end
for _, c in ipairs(Sky.cloudGuards or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Sky.cloudGuards, Sky.cloudGuardFor, Sky.cloudSpec = nil, nil, nil
if not Sky.fullbright then Sky.makeRain(spec.rain) end
end
Sky.grade(Sky.preset)
end

function Sky.skyBox()
for _, d in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Sky") and d.Name ~= "RKSkybox" then return d end
end
return nil
end
function Sky.paintSky(name)
local spec = Sky.SKYBOX[name]
if not spec then
if Sky.skySaved then
local was = Sky.skySaved
pcall(function()
was.box.CelestialBodiesShown = was.shown
was.box.SunAngularSize = was.sun
was.box.MoonAngularSize = was.moon
end)
Sky.skySaved = nil
end
return
end
local box = Sky.skyBox()
if not box then return end
if Sky.skySaved and Sky.skySaved.box ~= box then
pcall(function()
Sky.skySaved.box.CelestialBodiesShown = Sky.skySaved.shown
Sky.skySaved.box.SunAngularSize = Sky.skySaved.sun
Sky.skySaved.box.MoonAngularSize = Sky.skySaved.moon
end)
Sky.skySaved = nil
end
if not Sky.skySaved then
pcall(function()
Sky.skySaved = {
box = box,
shown = box.CelestialBodiesShown,
sun = box.SunAngularSize,
moon = box.MoonAngularSize,
}
end)
end
pcall(function()
box.CelestialBodiesShown = spec.shown
box.SunAngularSize = spec.sun
box.MoonAngularSize = spec.moon
end)
end
addCleanup("skybox", function() Sky.paintSky(nil) end)
function Sky.clearGrade()
Sky.paintSky(nil)
if Sky.atmos then pcall(function() Sky.atmos:Destroy() end) Sky.atmos = nil end
if Sky.cc then pcall(function() Sky.cc:Destroy() end) Sky.cc = nil end
if Sky.hidden then
for obj, was in pairs(Sky.hidden) do
if obj and obj.Parent then pcall(function() obj.Enabled = was end) end
end
Sky.hidden = nil
end
if Sky.gameAtmos and Sky.gameAtmos.Parent == nil then
pcall(function() Sky.gameAtmos.Parent = Lighting end)
end
Sky.gameAtmos = nil
end
addCleanup("skygrade", function() Sky.clearGrade() end)
function Sky.grade(name)
local spec = Sky.GRADE[name]
if not spec then
Sky.clearGrade()
return
end
for _, a in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
if a:IsA("Atmosphere") and a.Name ~= "RKAtmos" and a.Name ~= "RKSkyAtmos" then
if Sky.gameAtmos == nil then Sky.gameAtmos = a end
pcall(function() a.Parent = nil end)
end
end
if not (Sky.atmos and Sky.atmos.Parent) then
Sky.atmos = make("Atmosphere", { Name = "RKAtmos" }, Lighting)
end
pcall(function()
Sky.atmos.Density = Sky.fullbright and 0 or spec.density
Sky.atmos.Offset = spec.offset
Sky.atmos.Haze = Sky.fullbright and 0 or spec.haze
Sky.atmos.Glare = Sky.fullbright and 0 or spec.glare
Sky.atmos.Color = spec.colour
Sky.atmos.Decay = Sky.fullbright and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or spec.decay
end)
if not (Sky.cc and Sky.cc.Parent) then
Sky.cc = make("ColorCorrectionEffect", { Name = "RKGrade" }, Lighting)
end
pcall(function()
Sky.cc.Enabled = not Sky.fullbright
Sky.cc.TintColor = spec.tint
Sky.cc.Brightness = spec.brightness
Sky.cc.Contrast = spec.contrast
Sky.cc.Saturation = spec.sat
end)
end
function Sky.set(name)
Sky.preset = name
local look = SKY_LOOKS[name] or (SKY_ANIMATED[name] and Sky.clubLook())
if not look then
Sky.clearWeather()
if Sky.fullbright then return end
Sky.on = false
Sky.restore()
if Sky.conn then Sky.conn:Disconnect() Sky.conn = nil end
return
end
Sky.capture()
Sky.on = true
if not Sky.fullbright then Sky.write(look) end
Sky.weather(name)
Sky.paintSky(name)
Sky.startLoop()
if Sky.WEATHER[name] and Sky.WEATHER[name].clouds then
if Sky.cloudNote then
notify(Sky.cloudNote, 5)
else
notify(Sky.cloudReport(), 6)
end
end
end
local SkyGen = (function()
local SkyGen = {
on = false, preset = "Aurora", quality = "High",
bright = 1, stars = 1, clouds = 0, haze = 0.3, spin = 0, sunH = 12, moonPhase = 0.25,
weather = "None", weatherAmt = 1, layers = {},

}
SkyGen.FACES = {
bk = { c = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), r = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) },

ft = { c = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1), r = Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) },

}
SkyGen.FACE_ORDER = { "up", "bk", "rt", "ft", "lf", "dn" }
SkyGen.PROP = { bk = "SkyboxBk", dn = "SkyboxDn", ft = "SkyboxFt", lf = "SkyboxLf", rt = "SkyboxRt", up = "SkyboxUp" }

"Mountains", "Shooting Stars", "Light Rays" }
SkyGen.WEATHERS = { "None", "Snow", "Bubbles", "Embers", "Fireflies" }
SkyGen.QUALITY = { Normal = 256, High = 512, Ultra = 1024 }
SkyGen.QUALITY_NAMES = { "Normal", "High", "Ultra" }
local function C(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
local function set(...) local t = {} for _, k in ipairs({ ... }) do t[k] = true end return t end
SkyGen.PRESETS = {
Aurora = { top = C(3, 7, 22), hor = C(14, 38, 54), a1 = C(60, 255, 170), a2 = C(170, 90, 255),

layers = set("Clouds", "Cirrus", "Mountains"), clouds = 0.85, stars = 0, weather = "Snow" },
Underwater = { top = C(80, 194, 232), hor = C(10, 60, 110), a1 = C(170, 244, 255), a2 = C(12, 84, 140),
layers = set("Light Rays"), clouds = 0, stars = 0, weather = "Bubbles" },

layers = set("Clouds", "Sun", "Planet"), clouds = 0.55, stars = 0, weather = "None" },
["Blood Moon"] = { top = C(12, 2, 4), hor = C(90, 8, 14), a1 = C(255, 64, 50), a2 = C(70, 8, 16),
layers = set("Stars", "Moon", "Clouds", "Mountains"), clouds = 0.25, stars = 0.7, weather = "Embers" },
}
SkyGen.NAMES = { "Aurora", "Galaxy", "Starry Night", "Snow", "Underwater", "Sunset", "Day", "Candy", "Blood Moon", "Custom" }
local noise, exp, sqrt, floor, abs, max, min, sin, cos =
math.noise, math.exp, math.sqrt, math.floor, math.abs, math.max, math.min, math.sin, math.cos
local atan2 = math.atan2
local function clamp01(v) if v < 0 then return 0 elseif v > 1 then return 1 end return v end
local function smooth(e0, e1, x)
local t = (x - e0) / (e1 - e0)
if t < 0 then t = 0 elseif t > 1 then t = 1 end
return t * t * (3 - 2 * t)
end
local function fbm(x, y, z, oct)
local s, a, f = 0, 0.5, 1
for _ = 1, oct do
s = s + noise(x * f, y * f, z * f) * a
a, f = a * 0.5, f * 2.03
end
return s
end
local function hash(i, j, k)
local h = bit32.bxor(i * 374761393, j * 668265263, k * 2246822519)
h = bit32.bxor(h, bit32.rshift(h, 13))
h = (h * 1274126177) % 4294967296
h = bit32.bxor(h, bit32.rshift(h, 16))
return h / 4294967296
end
local function unit(x, y, z) local m = sqrt(x * x + y * y + z * z) return x / m, y / m, z / m end
function SkyGen.dir(face, u, v)
local x, y, z
if face == "up" then
x, y, z = v, 1, u
elseif face == "dn" then
x, y, z = -v, -1, u
else
local f = SkyGen.FACES[face]
x = f.c.X + u * f.r.X
y = -v
z = f.c.Z + u * f.r.Z
end
return unit(x, y, z)
end
SkyGen.STAR_TINTS = {
{ 0.72, 0.82, 1 }, { 0.92, 0.95, 1 }, { 1, 1, 1 }, { 1, 0.94, 0.8 }, { 1, 0.8, 0.6 }, { 1, 0.66, 0.55 },
}
function SkyGen.env(o)
local e = { L = o.layers or {}, stars = o.stars or 1, cover = o.clouds or 0, phase = o.moonPhase or 0.25 }
local function rgb(c) return c.R, c.G, c.B end
e.tr, e.tg, e.tb = rgb(o.top)
e.hr, e.hg, e.hb = rgb(o.hor)
e.ar, e.ag, e.ab = rgb(o.a1)
e.br, e.bg, e.bb = rgb(o.a2)
e.mr, e.mg, e.mb = e.hr * 0.45 + e.tr * 0.55, e.hg * 0.45 + e.tg * 0.55, e.hb * 0.45 + e.tb * 0.55
e.gr, e.gg, e.gb = e.hr * 0.2 + e.tr * 0.05, e.hg * 0.2 + e.tg * 0.05, e.hb * 0.2 + e.tb * 0.05
local lum = e.tr * 0.3 + e.tg * 0.59 + e.tb * 0.11
e.night = lum < 0.1
e.light = (e.hr * 0.3 + e.hg * 0.59 + e.hb * 0.11) > 0.6
local sh = math.rad(o.sunH or 12)
e.sx, e.sy, e.sz = unit(cos(sh) * 0.96, sin(sh), cos(sh) * 0.28)
e.mx, e.my, e.mz = unit(-0.55, 0.62, 0.58)
e.wx, e.wy, e.wz = unit(0.3, 0.85, 0.43)
e.cx, e.cy, e.cz = unit(-0.8, 0.25, 0.3)
e.px, e.py, e.pz = unit(0.55, 0.38, -0.74)
e.pR = 0.13
local ax, ay, az = 0, 1, 0
local e1x, e1y, e1z = ay * e.pz - az * e.py, az * e.px - ax * e.pz, ax * e.py - ay * e.px
e1x, e1y, e1z = unit(e1x, e1y, e1z)
local e2x, e2y, e2z = e.py * e1z - e.pz * e1y, e.pz * e1x - e.px * e1z, e.px * e1y - e.py * e1x
e.e1x, e.e1y, e.e1z, e.e2x, e.e2y, e.e2z = e1x, e1y, e1z, e2x, e2y, e2z
e.meteors = {}
for i = 1, 4 do
local a = i * 1.9 + 0.4
local h = 0.35 + 0.12 * i
local sx, sy, sz = unit(cos(a), h, sin(a))
local tx, ty, tz = unit(-sin(a) * 0.9, -0.42, cos(a) * 0.9)
e.meteors[i] = { sx, sy, sz, tx, ty, tz, 0.12 + 0.05 * (i % 2) }
end
e.bright = o.bright or 1
return e
end
function SkyGen.starField(face, fi, size, e)
local cs = max(4, floor(size / 48))
local n = floor(size / cs)
local cells = {}
if not e.L.Stars or e.stars <= 0 then return cells, cs, n end
local scale = size / 256
for cy = 0, n - 1 do
for cx = 0, n - 1 do
local u = ((cx + 0.5) * cs) / size * 2 - 1
local v = ((cy + 0.5) * cs) / size * 2 - 1
local x, y, z = SkyGen.dir(face, u, v)
if y > 0.01 then
local p = 0.17 * e.stars * smooth(0.01, 0.3, y)
if e.L["Milky Way"] then
local bd = x * e.wx + y * e.wy + z * e.wz
p = p * (1 + 2.5 * exp(-(bd * bd) / 0.03))
end
if hash(cx, cy, fi * 131 + 7) < p then
local b = hash(cy, cx, fi * 131 + 11) ^ 6
local tint = SkyGen.STAR_TINTS[1 + floor(hash(cx + 17, cy, fi * 131 + 3) * 6)]
cells[cy * n + cx] = {
x = cx * cs + hash(cx, cy, fi * 131 + 1) * cs,
y = cy * cs + hash(cy, cx, fi * 131 + 2) * cs,
r = (0.36 + b * 0.75) * scale, b = 0.28 + b * 1.5, spikeLen = cs * 1.4,
cr = tint[1], cg = tint[2], cb = tint[3],
}
end
end
end
end
return cells, cs, n
end
function SkyGen.shade(e, x, y, z, px, py, fi, cells, cs, n)
local L = e.L
local r, g, b
local h = sqrt(x * x + z * z)
local nx, nz = 0, 0
if h > 0 then nx, nz = x / h, z / h end
local sunSide = 0
if L.Sun then sunSide = max(0, nx * e.sx + nz * e.sz) end
if y >= 0 then
local t1 = smooth(0, 0.28, y)
local t2 = smooth(0.18, 1, y)
r = e.hr + (e.mr - e.hr) * t1; g = e.hg + (e.mg - e.hg) * t1; b = e.hb + (e.mb - e.hb) * t1
r = r + (e.tr - r) * t2; g = g + (e.tg - g) * t2; b = b + (e.tb - b) * t2
local band = exp(-y / 0.035) * 0.22
r, g, b = r + e.hr * band, g + e.hg * band, b + e.hb * band
if sunSide > 0 then
local w = sunSide ^ 4 * exp(-y / 0.18) * 0.4
r, g, b = r + e.ar * w, g + e.ag * w * 0.8, b + e.ab * w * 0.6
end
if e.night then
local ag = exp(-abs(y - 0.07) / 0.035) * 0.035
r, g, b = r + ag * 0.4, g + ag, b + ag * 0.5
end
else
local t = smooth(0, 0.22, -y)
r = e.hr + (e.gr - e.hr) * t; g = e.hg + (e.gg - e.hg) * t; b = e.hb + (e.gb - e.hb) * t
end
local sky = y > -0.03
if sky and L.Stars and e.stars > 0 and y > 0.02 then
local d = hash(px, py, fi + 53)
local dens = 0.035 * e.stars
if L["Milky Way"] then
local bd = x * e.wx + y * e.wy + z * e.wz
dens = dens * (1 + 5 * exp(-(bd * bd) / 0.02))
end
if d < dens then
local k = (0.05 + 0.12 * hash(py, px, fi + 59)) * smooth(0.02, 0.2, y)
r, g, b = r + k, g + k, b + k * 1.05
end
end
if sky and L.Nebula then
local f = smooth(-0.1, 0.15, y)
local qx = fbm(x * 1.6 + 1.7, y * 1.6, z * 1.6, 4)
local qz = fbm(x * 1.6, y * 1.6 + 5.2, z * 1.6 + 1.3, 4)
local rr = fbm(x * 1.9 + 4 * qx, y * 1.9 + 4 * qz, z * 1.9 - 3 * qx, 4)
local nn = fbm(x * 2.4 + 3.3 * rr, y * 2.4 + 3.3 * qx, z * 2.4 + 3.3 * qz, 6)
local k1 = smooth(0.0, 0.6, nn) ^ 1.4 * 0.95 * f
local k2 = smooth(0.0, 0.5, rr) ^ 1.3 * 0.6 * f
local knot = max(0, nn - 0.42) ^ 2 * 8 * f
local fil = smooth(0.2, 0.6, abs(fbm(x * 5 + 2, y * 5, z * 5, 4)) * 2.2) * 0.8 * f
r = r * (1 - fil) + e.ar * k1 + e.br * k2 + knot
g = g * (1 - fil) + e.ag * k1 + e.bg * k2 + knot * 0.9
b = b * (1 - fil) + e.ab * k1 + e.bb * k2 + knot
end
if sky and L["Milky Way"] then
local bd = x * e.wx + y * e.wy + z * e.wz
local w = 0.14 + 0.05 * noise(x * 3, y * 3, z * 3)
local band = exp(-(bd * bd) / (w * w)) * smooth(-0.05, 0.12, y)
if band > 0.01 then
local cd = x * e.cx + y * e.cy + z * e.cz
local core = exp(-(1 - cd) / 0.12)
local cloud = 0.55 + 0.45 * fbm(x * 6, y * 6, z * 6, 5)
local clumps = max(0, fbm(x * 14 + 3, y * 14, z * 14, 3)) ^ 2 * 2
local lane = smooth(0.5, 0.85, 1 - abs(fbm(x * 4.5 + 3, y * 4.5, z * 4.5, 5)) * 1.9)
* exp(-(bd * bd) / ((w * 0.55) ^ 2))
local k = band * (cloud + clumps) * (1 - 0.88 * lane) * (0.35 + core * 0.9)
local wr = 0.75 + 0.25 * core
local wb = 1 - 0.3 * core
r = r + (wr * 0.85 + 0.15 * (e.ar + e.br) * 0.5) * k * 0.8
g = g + (0.82 * 0.85 + 0.15 * (e.ag + e.bg) * 0.5) * k * 0.8
b = b + (wb * 0.85 + 0.15 * (e.ab + e.bb) * 0.5) * k * 0.8
end
end
if sky and L.Aurora then
local fade = smooth(-0.02, 0.05, y)
local glow = 0
for k = 1, 4 do
local fold = noise(nx * 3.2, nz * 3.2, k * 2.7)
local base = 0.05 + 0.09 * (k - 1) + 0.05 * sin(3 * atan2(nz, nx) + k * 1.7) + 0.045 * fold
+ 0.02 * noise(nx * 9, nz * 9, k * 5.3)
local d = y - base
local prof = d >= 0 and exp(-d / (0.2 - 0.03 * k)) or exp(d / 0.01)
local rays = 0.25 + 0.75 * clamp01(0.5 + 1.3 * noise(nx * 46 + fold * 2, nz * 46, k * 9 + y * 0.6))
local amp = (k == 1 and 1) or (k == 2 and 0.65) or (k == 3 and 0.4) or 0.25
amp = amp * (0.6 + 0.4 * clamp01(0.5 + fold))
local i = prof * rays * amp * fade
local t = smooth(0, 0.28, d)
local pink = smooth(0.18, 0.4, d) * 0.5
r = r + (e.ar + (e.br - e.ar) * t + pink * 0.9) * i
g = g + (e.ag + (e.bg - e.ag) * t) * i
b = b + (e.ab + (e.bb - e.ab) * t + pink * 0.5) * i
glow = glow + exp(-abs(d) / 0.08) * amp
end
