glow = glow * 0.09 * fade
r, g, b = r + e.ar * glow, g + e.ag * glow, b + e.ab * glow
end
if sky and L["Light Rays"] then
local c1 = abs(noise(x * 7, y * 3, z * 7)) + abs(noise(x * 13 + 5, y * 5, z * 13))
local caus = clamp01(1 - c1 * 1.6) ^ 3 * smooth(0.25, 0.95, y) * 0.85
local shaft = clamp01(fbm(nx * 4, nz * 4, 3.3, 2) * 1.6 + 0.1) ^ 1.5 * smooth(-0.1, 0.5, y) * (1 - smooth(0.55, 1, y)) * 0.35
local k = caus + shaft
r, g, b = r + e.ar * k, g + e.ag * k, b + e.ab * k
end
if sky and L.Planet then
local dp = x * e.px + y * e.py + z * e.pz
if dp > 0.8 then
local u = (x * e.e1x + y * e.e1y + z * e.e1z) / dp
local v = (x * e.e2x + y * e.e2y + z * e.e2z) / dp
local R = e.pR
local rr2 = u * u + v * v
local inside = rr2 < R * R
local nrx, nry, nrz = 0.0, 0.94, 0.34
local den = u * nrx + v * nry + nrz
local ringA, ringBehind = 0, false
if abs(den) > 1e-4 then
local t = nrz / den
local qx, qy, qz = t * u, t * v, t - 1
local rad = sqrt(qx * qx + qy * qy + qz * qz) / R
if rad > 1.35 and rad < 2.25 then
local bands = 0.55 + 0.45 * sin(rad * 38) * sin(rad * 11 + 1)
ringA = smooth(1.35, 1.45, rad) * (1 - smooth(2.1, 2.25, rad)) * bands * 0.8
ringBehind = inside and t > 1 - sqrt(max(0, R * R - rr2))
end
end
local function ring()
if ringA > 0 then
local cr, cg, cb = 0.85 + 0.15 * e.ar, 0.78 + 0.12 * e.ag, 0.68 + 0.12 * e.ab
r = r + (cr - r) * ringA; g = g + (cg - g) * ringA; b = b + (cb - b) * ringA
end
end
if inside then
local wz = sqrt(max(0, R * R - rr2)) / R
local nnx, nny = u / R, v / R
local lam = clamp01(nnx * 0.75 + nny * 0.25 + wz * 0.45)
local bandsP = 0.5 + 0.5 * sin(v / R * 13 + fbm(u * 8, v * 30, 3.1, 3) * 2.5)
local pr = 0.55 + 0.35 * bandsP + 0.1 * e.ar
local pg = 0.42 + 0.3 * bandsP + 0.08 * e.ag
local pb = 0.38 + 0.2 * bandsP + 0.15 * e.ab
local lit = lam ^ 0.8 * 1.05 + 0.04
local rim = (1 - wz) ^ 3 * 0.35
local edge = smooth(R, R * 0.97, sqrt(rr2))
r = r + (pr * lit + e.br * rim - r) * edge
g = g + (pg * lit + e.bg * rim - g) * edge
b = b + (pb * lit + e.bb * rim - b) * edge
if not ringBehind then ring() end
else
ring()
local dr = sqrt(rr2) - R
if dr < R * 0.12 then
local gl = (1 - dr / (R * 0.12)) ^ 2 * 0.25
r, g, b = r + e.br * gl, g + e.bg * gl, b + e.bb * gl
end
end
end
end
if sky and L.Sun then
local sd = clamp01(x * e.sx + y * e.sy + z * e.sz)
local ang = sqrt(max(0, 2 * (1 - sd)))
if ang < 1.2 then
local disc = smooth(0.04, 0.034, ang)
local limb = 1 - 0.35 * (ang / 0.04) ^ 2
local bloom = exp(-ang / 0.09) * 0.7 + exp(-ang / 0.4) * 0.3
local tx = x - sd * e.sx; local ty = y - sd * e.sy; local tz = z - sd * e.sz
local ra = atan2(ty, tx * 0.7 + tz * 0.7)
local rays = (0.5 + 0.5 * noise(cos(ra) * 9, sin(ra) * 9, 1.3)) * exp(-ang / 0.22) * 0.35
local k = bloom + rays
local dl = disc * max(0.3, limb) * 1.6
r = r + e.ar * k + dl * (0.65 + 0.35 * e.ar)
g = g + e.ag * k + dl * (0.65 + 0.35 * e.ag)
b = b + e.ab * k + dl * (0.65 + 0.35 * e.ab)
end
end
if sky and L.Clouds and e.cover > 0 and y > -0.02 then
local q = 1 / (max(y, 0) + 0.1)
local u, w = x * q * 0.8, z * q * 0.8
local c = fbm(u, w, 1.7, 6)
local dens = clamp01((c + e.cover - 0.55) * 2.6)
if dens > 0.001 then
local sxp, szp = e.sx * 0.2, e.sz * 0.2
local sh = 0
for i = 1, 3 do
sh = sh + clamp01((fbm(u + sxp * i, w + szp * i, 1.7, 3) + e.cover - 0.55) * 2.6)
end
local light = exp(-sh * 0.7)
local cr = e.br + (e.ar - e.br) * light
local cg = e.bg + (e.ag - e.bg) * light
local cb = e.bb + (e.ab - e.bb) * light
if L.Sun then
local sd = clamp01(x * e.sx + y * e.sy + z * e.sz)
local lining = sd ^ 10 * (1 - dens) * 1.4
cr, cg, cb = cr + e.ar * lining, cg + e.ag * lining, cb + e.ab * lining
end
local base = 1 - dens * 0.25
cr, cg, cb = cr * base, cg * base, cb * base
local far = (1 - smooth(0, 0.28, y)) * 0.6
cr, cg, cb = cr + (e.hr - cr) * far, cg + (e.hg - cg) * far, cb + (e.hb - cb) * far
local a = dens * smooth(-0.02, 0.1, y) * 0.95
r, g, b = r + (cr - r) * a, g + (cg - g) * a, b + (cb - b) * a
end
end
if sky and L.Cirrus and y > 0.03 then
local q = 1 / (y + 0.35)
local u, w = x * q, z * q
local wu = u + 0.6 * fbm(u * 1.3, w * 1.3, 3.1, 3)
local ww = w + 0.6 * fbm(u * 1.3 + 5, w * 1.3, 3.1, 3)
local su, sw = wu * 0.8 + ww * 0.6, -wu * 0.6 + ww * 0.8
local c = fbm(su * 1.6, sw * 3.2, 7.7, 5)
local a = smooth(0.12, 0.5, c) * 0.2 * smooth(0.06, 0.35, y)
local cr, cg, cb = 0.75 + 0.25 * e.ar, 0.75 + 0.25 * e.ag, 0.78 + 0.22 * e.ab
r, g, b = r + (cr - r) * a, g + (cg - g) * a, b + (cb - b) * a
end
if sky and L.Moon then
local md = clamp01(x * e.mx + y * e.my + z * e.mz)
local ang = sqrt(max(0, 2 * (1 - md)))
local R = 0.045
if ang < 0.6 then
local halo = exp(-ang / 0.07) * 0.28 + exp(-ang / 0.3) * 0.08
halo = halo + exp(-((ang - 0.384) / 0.018) ^ 2) * 0.05
local disc = smooth(R, R * 0.92, ang)
local m = 0
if disc > 0 then
local tx = x - md * e.mx; local ty = y - md * e.my; local tz = z - md * e.mz
local a = (tx * e.mz - tz * e.mx) / R
local c = ty / R
local depth = sqrt(max(0, 1 - a * a - c * c))
local ph = e.phase * math.pi
local lit = clamp01((a * cos(ph) + depth * sin(ph) + 0.05) * 6)
if e.phase < 0.02 then lit = 1 end
local maria = 0.72 + 0.28 * smooth(-0.2, 0.3, fbm(a * 2.2 + 3, c * 2.2, 1.1, 3))
local craters = 0.85 + 0.15 * clamp01(0.5 + fbm(a * 9, c * 9, 4.4, 3) * 1.5)
local limbD = 0.7 + 0.3 * depth
m = (lit * maria * craters * limbD * 1.2 + 0.05) * disc
end
r = r * (1 - disc) + e.ar * (m + halo)
g = g * (1 - disc) + e.ag * (m + halo)
b = b * (1 - disc) + e.ab * (m + halo)
end
end
if sky and L["Shooting Stars"] and y > 0.1 then
for _, mt in ipairs(e.meteors) do
local dx, dy, dz = x - mt[1], y - mt[2], z - mt[3]
local along = dx * mt[4] + dy * mt[5] + dz * mt[6]
if along > 0 and along < mt[7] then
local px2, py2, pz2 = dx - along * mt[4], dy - along * mt[5], dz - along * mt[6]
local off = sqrt(px2 * px2 + py2 * py2 + pz2 * pz2)
local wdt = 0.0016 * (1 - along / mt[7]) + 0.0004
if off < wdt * 3 then
local k = exp(-(off / wdt) ^ 2) * (1 - along / mt[7]) ^ 1.5 * 1.3
r, g, b = r + k, g + k, b + k * 0.95
end
end
end
end
if sky and cells then
local cx, cy = floor(px / cs), floor(py / cs)
for dy = -1, 1 do
for dx = -1, 1 do
local s = cells[(cy + dy) * n + (cx + dx)]
if s and cx + dx >= 0 and cx + dx < n then
local ddx, ddy = px + 0.5 - s.x, py + 0.5 - s.y
local d2 = ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy
local k = s.b * exp(-d2 / (2 * s.r * s.r))
if s.b > 1.3 then
k = k + 0.16 * s.b * exp(-d2 / (20 * s.r * s.r))
local ax, ay = abs(ddx), abs(ddy)
if ax < s.spikeLen and ay < s.spikeLen then
k = k + 0.35 * s.b * (exp(-ay / 0.45) * exp(-ax / (s.spikeLen * 0.35))
+ exp(-ax / 0.45) * exp(-ay / (s.spikeLen * 0.35)))
end
end
if k > 0.003 then r, g, b = r + s.cr * k, g + s.cg * k, b + s.cb * k end
end
end
end
end
if L.Mountains and y < 0.3 and y > -0.05 then
local th = atan2(nz, nx)
local cs1, sn1 = cos(th), sin(th)
local hit = 0
local ridge
local trees = 0.035 + 0.02 * fbm(cs1 * 3, sn1 * 3, 9.9, 3)
+ 0.012 * max(0, noise(cs1 * 160, sn1 * 160, 2.2)) ^ 1.3
+ 0.008 * max(0, noise(cs1 * 420, sn1 * 420, 5.1))
if y < trees then hit, ridge = 1, trees
else
local mid = 0.07 + 0.05 * (1 - abs(fbm(cs1 * 2.4 + 4, sn1 * 2.4, 3.3, 5)))
if y < mid then hit, ridge = 2, mid
else
local far = 0.1 + 0.09 * (1 - abs(fbm(cs1 * 1.6 + 9, sn1 * 1.6, 6.6, 6)))
if y < far then hit, ridge = 3, far end
end
end
if hit > 0 then
local haze = (hit == 1 and 0.08) or (hit == 2 and 0.35) or 0.6
local dark = (hit == 1 and 0.12) or (hit == 2 and 0.2) or 0.3
local mr = e.gr * dark * 4 + (e.hr - e.gr * dark * 4) * haze
local mg = e.gg * dark * 4 + (e.hg - e.gg * dark * 4) * haze
local mb = e.gb * dark * 4 + (e.hb - e.gb * dark * 4) * haze
if hit == 3 and e.light then
local peak = smooth(0.145, 0.185, ridge)
if peak > 0 then
local sn = smooth(ridge - 0.035 * peak, ridge - 0.004, y) * peak * 0.85
mr, mg, mb = mr + (0.95 - mr) * sn, mg + (0.96 - mg) * sn, mb + (1 - mb) * sn
end
end
local rim = exp(-(ridge - y) / 0.003) * ((hit == 3) and 0.1 or 0.04)
local lr, lg, lb = (L.Sun and e.ar or e.hr), (L.Sun and e.ag or e.hg), (L.Sun and e.ab or e.hb)
r, g, b = mr + lr * rim, mg + lg * rim, mb + lb * rim
end
end
local d = (hash(px, py, fi + 31) - 0.5) * (1.8 / 255)
return r * e.bright + d, g * e.bright + d, b * e.bright + d
end
local CRC
local function crcTable()
if CRC then return CRC end
CRC = {}
for i = 0, 255 do
local c = i
for _ = 1, 8 do
if bit32.band(c, 1) == 1 then c = bit32.bxor(0xEDB88320, bit32.rshift(c, 1)) else c = bit32.rshift(c, 1) end
end
CRC[i] = c
end
return CRC
end
local function crc32(buf, from, to)
local t = crcTable()
local c = 0xFFFFFFFF
for i = from, to do
c = bit32.bxor(t[bit32.band(bit32.bxor(c, buffer.readu8(buf, i)), 0xFF)], bit32.rshift(c, 8))
end
return bit32.bxor(c, 0xFFFFFFFF)
end
function SkyGen.png(w, h, chans, raw)
local rawLen = buffer.len(raw)
local blocks = math.max(1, math.ceil(rawLen / 65535))
local zlen = 2 + rawLen + blocks * 5 + 4
local total = 8 + 25 + (12 + zlen) + 12
local b = buffer.create(total)
local o = 0
local function u8(v) buffer.writeu8(b, o, v); o = o + 1 end
local function u32(v)
u8(bit32.band(bit32.rshift(v, 24), 255)); u8(bit32.band(bit32.rshift(v, 16), 255))
u8(bit32.band(bit32.rshift(v, 8), 255)); u8(bit32.band(v, 255))
end
local function str(s) for i = 1, #s do u8(s:byte(i)) end end
u8(137) str("PNG\r\n\026\n")
u32(13); local cs = o; str("IHDR"); u32(w); u32(h)
u8(8); u8(chans == 4 and 6 or 2); u8(0); u8(0); u8(0)
u32(crc32(b, cs, o - 1))
u32(zlen); cs = o; str("IDAT")
u8(0x78); u8(0x01)
local pos = 0
for k = 1, blocks do
local n = math.min(65535, rawLen - pos)
u8(k == blocks and 1 or 0)
u8(bit32.band(n, 255)); u8(bit32.rshift(n, 8))
local nn = bit32.band(bit32.bnot(n), 0xFFFF)
u8(bit32.band(nn, 255)); u8(bit32.rshift(nn, 8))
buffer.copy(b, o, raw, pos, n)
o = o + n; pos = pos + n
end
local a1, a2 = 1, 0
for i = 0, rawLen - 1 do
a1 = (a1 + buffer.readu8(raw, i)) % 65521
a2 = (a2 + a1) % 65521
end
u32(a2 * 65536 + a1)
u32(crc32(b, cs, o - 1))
u32(0); cs = o; str("IEND"); u32(crc32(b, cs, o - 1))
return buffer.tostring(b)
end
local function byte(v)
v = floor(v * 255 + 0.5)
if v < 0 then return 0 elseif v > 255 then return 255 end
return v
end
function SkyGen.face(face, e, size, pause)
local fi = 0
for i, f in ipairs(SkyGen.FACE_ORDER) do if f == face then fi = i end end
local cells, cs, n = SkyGen.starField(face, fi, size, e)
local stride = 1 + size * 3
local raw = buffer.create(stride * size)
for py = 0, size - 1 do
local row = py * stride
buffer.writeu8(raw, row, 0)
local v = (py + 0.5) / size * 2 - 1
for px = 0, size - 1 do
local u = (px + 0.5) / size * 2 - 1
local x, y, z = SkyGen.dir(face, u, v)
local r, g, b = SkyGen.shade(e, x, y, z, px, py, fi, cells, cs, n)
local o = row + 1 + px * 3
buffer.writeu8(raw, o, byte(r))
buffer.writeu8(raw, o + 1, byte(g))
buffer.writeu8(raw, o + 2, byte(b))
end
if pause then pause() end
end
return SkyGen.png(size, size, 3, raw)
end
function SkyGen.sprite(kind)
local n = 32
local stride = 1 + n * 4
local raw = buffer.create(stride * n)
for py = 0, n - 1 do
buffer.writeu8(raw, py * stride, 0)
for px = 0, n - 1 do
local dx, dy = (px + 0.5) / n * 2 - 1, (py + 0.5) / n * 2 - 1
local d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
local a
if kind == "star" then
local ax, ay = abs(dx), abs(dy)
a = clamp01(0.045 / (ax * ay + 0.012) * (1 - d)) * clamp01(1 - d) + clamp01(1 - d * 3) * 0.8
elseif kind == "ring" then
a = clamp01(1 - abs(d - 0.78) / 0.14) + clamp01(0.25 - d) * 0.6
local hx, hy = dx + 0.35, dy + 0.35
a = max(a, clamp01(1 - sqrt(hx * hx + hy * hy) / 0.18))
else
a = clamp01(1 - d) ^ 1.6
end
local o = py * stride + 1 + px * 4
buffer.writeu8(raw, o, 255); buffer.writeu8(raw, o + 1, 255); buffer.writeu8(raw, o + 2, 255)
buffer.writeu8(raw, o + 3, byte(min(1, a)))
end
end
return SkyGen.png(n, n, 4, raw)
end
function SkyGen.can()
return typeof(getcustomasset) == "function" and typeof(writefile) == "function"
and type(buffer) == "table"
end
local function ensure(dir)
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfolder) == "function" and typeof(makefolder) == "function" and not isfolder(dir) then makefolder(dir) end
end)
end
function SkyGen.current()
local p = SkyGen.PRESETS[SkyGen.preset] or SkyGen.PRESETS.Aurora
return {
top = SkyGen.top or p.top, hor = SkyGen.hor or p.hor, a1 = SkyGen.a1 or p.a1, a2 = SkyGen.a2 or p.a2,
layers = SkyGen.layers or p.layers, stars = SkyGen.stars, clouds = SkyGen.clouds, bright = SkyGen.bright,
sunH = SkyGen.sunH, moonPhase = SkyGen.moonPhase,
}
end
function SkyGen.sweep(keep)
if typeof(listfiles) ~= "function" or typeof(delfile) ~= "function" then return end
pcall(function()
for _, f in ipairs(listfiles("Rake/sky")) do
local name = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)$") or ""
if name:match("%.png$") and not name:match("^sprite_") and (not keep or name:sub(1, #keep) ~= keep) then
pcall(delfile, f)
end
end
end)
end
function SkyGen.atmos()
local o = SkyGen.current()
local a = Lighting:FindFirstChild("RKAtmos")
if a and a:IsA("Atmosphere") then
SkyGen.borrowed = true
if SkyGen.ownAtmos then
pcall(function() SkyGen.ownAtmos:Destroy() end)
SkyGen.ownAtmos = nil
end
else
SkyGen.borrowed = false
a = SkyGen.ownAtmos
if not (a and a.Parent) then
a = make("Atmosphere", { Name = "RKSkyAtmos" }, Lighting)
SkyGen.ownAtmos = a
end
end
pcall(function()
a.Density = SkyGen.haze * 0.55
a.Offset = 0.2
a.Haze = 0
a.Glare = 0
a.Color = o.hor
a.Decay = o.hor:Lerp(o.top, 0.5)
end)
end
function SkyGen.hold()
if SkyGen.box and SkyGen.box.Parent ~= Lighting then
pcall(function() SkyGen.box.Parent = Lighting end)
end
for _, d in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
if (d:IsA("Sky") and d ~= SkyGen.box)
or (d:IsA("Atmosphere") and d.Name ~= "RKAtmos" and d.Name ~= "RKSkyAtmos") then
SkyGen.evicted[d] = true
pcall(function() d.Parent = nil end)
end
end
SkyGen.atmos()
end
function SkyGen.release()
if SkyGen.box then pcall(function() SkyGen.box:Destroy() end) SkyGen.box = nil end
if SkyGen.ownAtmos then pcall(function() SkyGen.ownAtmos:Destroy() end) SkyGen.ownAtmos = nil end
for d in pairs(SkyGen.evicted) do pcall(function() d.Parent = Lighting end) end
SkyGen.evicted = {}
if SkyGen.borrowed then
SkyGen.borrowed = false
pcall(function() Sky.grade(Sky.preset) end)
end
end
function SkyGen.applyIds(ids)
if not (SkyGen.box and SkyGen.box.Parent) then
SkyGen.box = make("Sky", { Name = "RKSkybox" }, Lighting)
end
local box = SkyGen.box
pcall(function()
for f, id in pairs(ids) do box[SkyGen.PROP[f]] = id end
box.StarCount = 0
box.CelestialBodiesShown = false
end)
SkyGen.hold()
end
SkyGen.PARTICLES = {
Snow = { sprite = "dot", dir = "down", rate = 150, speed = { 4, 8 }, life = { 7, 10 }, size = { 0.18, 0.38 },
accel = Vector3.new(0, -1.5, 0), spread = 25, light = 0.2, alpha = 0.1, colour = "a1", y = 34 },
Bubbles = { sprite = "ring", dir = "up", rate = 45, speed = { 3, 7 }, life = { 5, 8 }, size = { 0.25, 0.7 },
accel = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), spread = 18, light = 0.3, alpha = 0.25, colour = "a1", y = -24 },
Embers = { sprite = "dot", dir = "up", rate = 40, speed = { 2, 5 }, life = { 4, 7 }, size = { 0.08, 0.2 },
accel = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), spread = 40, light = 1, alpha = 0, colour = "a1", y = -24 },
Fireflies = { sprite = "dot", dir = "up", rate = 18, speed = { 0.5, 1.5 }, life = { 5, 9 }, size = { 0.12, 0.22 },
accel = Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0), spread = 180, light = 1, alpha = 0, colour = "ff", y = -6 },
}
function SkyGen.spriteId(kind)
SkyGen.spriteIds = SkyGen.spriteIds or {}
if SkyGen.spriteIds[kind] then return SkyGen.spriteIds[kind] end
local path = "Rake/sky/sprite_" .. kind .. ".png"
local ok, id = pcall(function()
ensure("Rake"); ensure("Rake/sky")
if not (typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(path)) then writefile(path, SkyGen.sprite(kind)) end
return getcustomasset(path)
end)
if ok and type(id) == "string" then SkyGen.spriteIds[kind] = id; return id end
return nil
end
function SkyGen.dropParticles()
if SkyGen.pConn then SkyGen.pConn:Disconnect() SkyGen.pConn = nil end
if SkyGen.pPart then pcall(function() SkyGen.pPart:Destroy() end) SkyGen.pPart = nil end
end
function SkyGen.makeParticles()
SkyGen.dropParticles()
local spec = SkyGen.PARTICLES[SkyGen.weather]
if not (SkyGen.on and spec) or SkyGen.weatherAmt <= 0 then return end
local tex = SkyGen.spriteId(spec.sprite)
if not tex then return end
local o = SkyGen.current()
local colour = (spec.colour == "ff") and Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 140) or o.a1
local part = make("Part", { Name = "RKSkyFx", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false,
CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false, Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(110, 1, 110) }, Workspace)
make("ParticleEmitter", {
Texture = tex, Color = ColorSequence.new(colour),
Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.1, spec.alpha),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.85, spec.alpha), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }),
Size = NumberSequence.new(spec.size[1], spec.size[2]),
Lifetime = NumberRange.new(spec.life[1], spec.life[2]), Rate = spec.rate * SkyGen.weatherAmt,
Speed = NumberRange.new(spec.speed[1], spec.speed[2]), SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(spec.spread, spec.spread),
Acceleration = spec.accel, LightEmission = spec.light, Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360),
RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-40, 40),
EmissionDirection = (spec.dir == "up") and Enum.NormalId.Top or Enum.NormalId.Bottom,
}, part)
SkyGen.pPart = part
SkyGen.pConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local c = cam()
if c and SkyGen.pPart then
SkyGen.pPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(c.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, spec.y, 0))
end
end)
end
function SkyGen.render()
if SkyGen.busy then SkyGen.again = true return end
SkyGen.busy = true
task.spawn(function()
local ok, err = pcall(function()
ensure("Rake"); ensure("Rake/sky")
repeat
SkyGen.again = false
local e = SkyGen.env(SkyGen.current())
local size = SkyGen.QUALITY[SkyGen.quality] or 512
SkyGen.gen = SkyGen.gen + 1
local tag = string.format("%d_%d_", os.time() % 100000, SkyGen.gen)
local ids, done = {}, 0
SkyGen.status = "painting " .. SkyGen.preset
local slice = os.clock()
local function pause()
if os.clock() - slice > 0.01 then task.wait(); slice = os.clock() end
end
for _, f in ipairs(SkyGen.FACE_ORDER) do
if not SkyGen.on or SkyGen.again then break end
local png = SkyGen.face(f, e, size, pause)
local path = "Rake/sky/" .. tag .. f .. ".png"
writefile(path, png)
ids[f] = getcustomasset(path)
done = done + 1
end
if SkyGen.on and not SkyGen.again and done == 6 then
SkyGen.applyIds(ids)
SkyGen.sweep(tag)
SkyGen.makeParticles()
SkyGen.status = SkyGen.preset
notify("sky ready: " .. SkyGen.preset, 2)
end
until not (SkyGen.again and SkyGen.on)
end)
SkyGen.busy = false
if not ok then
SkyGen.status = "failed: " .. tostring(err)
notify("custom skybox failed: " .. tostring(err), 6)
end
end)
end
function SkyGen.request()
if not SkyGen.on or SkyGen.quiet then return end
SkyGen.wantAt = os.clock() + 0.35
if SkyGen.busy then SkyGen.again = true end
if SkyGen.waiting then return end
SkyGen.waiting = true
task.spawn(function()
while os.clock() < SkyGen.wantAt do task.wait(0.05) end
SkyGen.waiting = false
if SkyGen.on then SkyGen.render() end
end)
end
function SkyGen.set(on)
SkyGen.on = on
if on then
if not SkyGen.can() then
SkyGen.on = false
SkyGen.status = "this executor has no getcustomasset"
notify("custom skybox needs getcustomasset + writefile, which this executor does not have", 6)
return
end
notify("painting the sky...", 2)
SkyGen.request()
if not SkyGen.conn then
local holdAt = 0
SkyGen.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
if not SkyGen.on or not SkyGen.box then return end
if SkyGen.spin > 0 then
SkyGen.angle = ((SkyGen.angle or 0) + dt * SkyGen.spin) % 360
pcall(function() SkyGen.box.SkyboxOrientation = Vector3.new(0, SkyGen.angle, 0) end)
end
if os.clock() - holdAt > 0.5 then
holdAt = os.clock()
SkyGen.hold()
end
end)
end
else
if SkyGen.conn then SkyGen.conn:Disconnect() SkyGen.conn = nil end
SkyGen.again = true
SkyGen.dropParticles()
SkyGen.release()
SkyGen.sweep(nil)
SkyGen.status = "off"
end
end
addCleanup("skygen", function() SkyGen.set(false) end)
function SkyGen.setPreset(v)
SkyGen.preset = v or "Aurora"
local p = SkyGen.PRESETS[SkyGen.preset]
if p and not Library.Loading then
SkyGen.quiet = true
SkyGen.top, SkyGen.hor, SkyGen.a1, SkyGen.a2 = p.top, p.hor, p.a1, p.a2
SkyGen.layers, SkyGen.clouds, SkyGen.stars, SkyGen.weather = p.layers, p.clouds, p.stars, p.weather
pcall(function() Options.RK_SkyTop:SetValueRGB(p.top) end)
pcall(function() Options.RK_SkyHorizon:SetValueRGB(p.hor) end)
pcall(function() Options.RK_SkyAccent1:SetValueRGB(p.a1) end)
pcall(function() Options.RK_SkyAccent2:SetValueRGB(p.a2) end)
pcall(function()
local s = {}
for k in pairs(p.layers) do s[k] = true end
Options.RK_SkyLayers:SetValue(s)
end)
pcall(function() Options.RK_SkyClouds:SetValue(p.clouds) end)
pcall(function() Options.RK_SkyStars:SetValue(p.stars) end)
pcall(function() Options.RK_SkyWeather:SetValue(p.weather) end)
SkyGen.quiet = false
end
SkyGen.request()
end
SkyGen.COLOUR_KEYS = { top = true, hor = true, a1 = true, a2 = true }
function SkyGen.setColour(key, c)
if SkyGen.COLOUR_KEYS[key] then SkyGen[key] = c end
SkyGen.request()
end
function SkyGen.setLayers(v)
local s = {}
for k, on in pairs(v or {}) do if on then s[k] = true end end
SkyGen.layers = s
SkyGen.request()
end
function SkyGen.setWeather(v)
SkyGen.weather = v or "None"
if SkyGen.on and SkyGen.box and not SkyGen.quiet then SkyGen.makeParticles() end
end
return SkyGen
end)()
local Rtx = (function()
local Rtx = {
on = false, preset = "RTX", strength = 1, blur = 0.5, budget = 10,
saved = {}, lights = {}, chosen = {}, all = {}, took = {}, guards = {},
}
local function C(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
Rtx.PRESETS = {
RTX       = { bloom = { 0.55, 24, 2.2 }, rays = { 0.05, 0.5 }, cc = { 0.02, 0.14, 0.12, C(255, 250, 244) },
dof = { 0.14, 60, 70 }, exposure = 0.1, soft = 0.12, grain = 0 },
Realistic = { bloom = { 0.4, 22, 2.3 }, rays = { 0.06, 0.55 }, cc = { 0, 0.18, 0.05, C(255, 246, 236) },
dof = { 0.12, 70, 80 }, exposure = 0.05, soft = 0.1, grain = 0.04 },
Horror    = { bloom = { 0.35, 26, 2.4 }, rays = nil, cc = { -0.05, 0.3, -0.45, C(210, 225, 255) },
dof = { 0.26, 40, 45 }, exposure = -0.15, soft = 0.18, grain = 0.18 },
Ultra     = { bloom = { 0.85, 30, 2.0 }, rays = { 0.09, 0.62 }, cc = { 0.03, 0.2, 0.2, C(255, 248, 238) },
dof = { 0.24, 50, 60 }, exposure = 0.15, soft = 0.08, grain = 0.04 },
Cinematic = { bloom = { 0.5, 26, 2.2 }, rays = { 0.07, 0.55 }, cc = { -0.02, 0.24, -0.06, C(255, 234, 210) },
dof = { 0.32, 40, 45 }, exposure = 0, soft = 0.2, grain = 0.12 },
Vivid     = { bloom = { 0.45, 20, 2.3 }, rays = { 0.04, 0.4 }, cc = { 0.03, 0.12, 0.38, C(255, 255, 255) },
dof = { 0.08, 70, 80 }, exposure = 0.12, soft = 0.12, grain = 0 },
Clean     = { bloom = { 0.3, 18, 2.5 }, rays = nil, cc = { 0.01, 0.08, 0.08, C(255, 255, 255) },
dof = nil, exposure = 0.05, soft = 0.1, grain = 0 },
Noir      = { bloom = { 0.4, 24, 2.2 }, rays = { 0.04, 0.5 }, cc = { 0, 0.32, -1, C(255, 255, 255) },
dof = { 0.2, 45, 50 }, exposure = 0, soft = 0.15, grain = 0.2 },
}
Rtx.NAMES = { "RTX", "Realistic", "Ultra", "Cinematic", "Horror", "Vivid", "Clean", "Noir" }
Rtx.grain = 0
function Rtx.texture(kind)
Rtx.tex = Rtx.tex or {}
if Rtx.tex[kind] ~= nil then return Rtx.tex[kind] or nil end
Rtx.tex[kind] = false
if not (SkyGen.can() and typeof(getcustomasset) == "function") then return nil end
local ok, id = pcall(function()
local n = 128
local stride = 1 + n * 4
local raw = buffer.create(stride * n)
for py = 0, n - 1 do
buffer.writeu8(raw, py * stride, 0)
for px = 0, n - 1 do
local o = py * stride + 1 + px * 4
local v = math.random(0, 255)
local a = math.random()
buffer.writeu8(raw, o, v); buffer.writeu8(raw, o + 1, v); buffer.writeu8(raw, o + 2, v)
buffer.writeu8(raw, o + 3, math.clamp(math.floor(a * 255 + 0.5), 0, 255))
end
end
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfolder) == "function" and typeof(makefolder) == "function" then
if not isfolder("Rake") then makefolder("Rake") end
if not isfolder("Rake/sky") then makefolder("Rake/sky") end
end
end)
local path = "Rake/sky/sprite_rtx_" .. kind .. ".png"
writefile(path, SkyGen.png(n, n, 4, raw))
return getcustomasset(path)
end)
if ok and type(id) == "string" then Rtx.tex[kind] = id return id end
return nil
end
function Rtx.newGui()
local holder
if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
local ok, h = pcall(gethui)
if ok and h then holder = h end
end
if not holder then
local ok, core = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
holder = (ok and core) or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end
local gui = make("ScreenGui", { Name = "RKRtxScreen", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
DisplayOrder = -5, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling }, holder)
addCleanup("gui:RKRtxScreen", function() pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end) end)
return gui
end
function Rtx.overlay()
local g = Rtx.on and Rtx.grain or 0
if g <= 0 then
if Rtx.gui then Rtx.gui.Enabled = false end
return
end
if not (Rtx.gui and Rtx.gui.Parent) then
Rtx.gui = Rtx.newGui()
Rtx.grainImg = make("ImageLabel", { Name = "Grain", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Size = UDim2.new(1, 256, 1, 256), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-128, -128),
ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Tile, TileSize = UDim2.fromOffset(128, 128),
Image = Rtx.texture("grain") or "" }, Rtx.gui)
end
Rtx.gui.Enabled = true
pcall(function()
Rtx.grainImg.ImageTransparency = 1 - math.clamp(g, 0, 1) * 0.35
Rtx.grainImg.Visible = g > 0
end)
if g > 0 and not Rtx.grainConn then
Rtx.grainConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not (Rtx.grainImg and Rtx.grainImg.Parent) then return end
Rtx.grainImg.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-math.random(0, 127), -math.random(0, 127))
end)
elseif g <= 0 and Rtx.grainConn then
Rtx.grainConn:Disconnect()
Rtx.grainConn = nil
end
end
function Rtx.setPreset(v)
Rtx.preset = v or "RTX"
local p = Rtx.PRESETS[Rtx.preset]
if p and not Library.Loading then
pcall(function() Options.RK_RtxGrain:SetValue(p.grain or 0) end)
end
if Rtx.on then pcall(Rtx.apply) end
end
local function readProp(inst, prop)
local ok, v = pcall(function() return inst[prop] end)
if not ok and typeof(gethiddenproperty) == "function" then ok, v = pcall(gethiddenproperty, inst, prop) end
return ok, v
end
function Rtx.write(inst, prop, value)
local key = inst:GetFullName() .. "." .. prop
local okR, cur = readProp(inst, prop)
if Rtx.saved[key] == nil and okR then Rtx.saved[key] = { inst = inst, prop = prop, value = cur } end
if okR and cur == value then Rtx.took[prop] = true return true end
local ok = pcall(function() inst[prop] = value end)
if not ok and typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then
ok = pcall(sethiddenproperty, inst, prop, value)
end
Rtx.took[prop] = ok
return ok
end
function Rtx.effect(class, name)
local e = Lighting:FindFirstChild(name)
if not (e and e:IsA(class)) then e = make(class, { Name = name }, Lighting) end
return e
end
function Rtx.gameShadows(on)
pcall(function()
local gs = getScriptGlobal("GSettings")
if type(gs) ~= "table" then return end
if on then
if Rtx.gsWas == nil then Rtx.gsWas = (gs.Shadows == nil) and "nil" or gs.Shadows end
if gs.Shadows ~= true then
gs.Shadows = true
setScriptGlobal("GSettings", gs)
local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SettingsChangedEvent")
if ev and ev.Fire then ev:Fire("Shadows", true) end
end
elseif Rtx.gsWas ~= nil then
local was = Rtx.gsWas
Rtx.gsWas = nil
if was == "nil" then gs.Shadows = nil else gs.Shadows = was end
setScriptGlobal("GSettings", gs)
local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SettingsChangedEvent")
if ev and ev.Fire then ev:Fire("Shadows", gs.Shadows) end
end
end)
end
function Rtx.pinQuality(on)
if on then
pcall(function()
local r = settings().Rendering
if Rtx.quality == nil then Rtx.quality = (Esp and Esp.qualityWas) or r.QualityLevel end
if r.QualityLevel ~= Enum.QualityLevel.Level21 then r.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level21 end
end)
pcall(function()
local ugs = UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings")
if Rtx.savedQuality == nil then
Rtx.savedQuality = (Esp and Esp.savedQualityWas) or ugs.SavedQualityLevel
end
if ugs.SavedQualityLevel ~= Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel10 then
ugs.SavedQualityLevel = Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel10
end
Rtx.qualityPinned = true
end)
else
local farHolds = Esp and Esp.farRender
if Rtx.quality ~= nil then
if not farHolds then
pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Rtx.quality end)
elseif Esp.qualityWas ~= nil then
Esp.qualityWas = Rtx.quality
end
end
if Rtx.savedQuality ~= nil then
if farHolds and Esp.savedQualityWas ~= nil then
Esp.savedQualityWas = Rtx.savedQuality
else
pcall(function() UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").SavedQualityLevel = Rtx.savedQuality end)
end
end
Rtx.quality, Rtx.savedQuality, Rtx.qualityPinned = nil, nil, false
end
end
local function lightPos(l)
local p = l.Parent
if not p then return nil end
if p:IsA("BasePart") then return p.Position end
if p:IsA("Attachment") then return p.WorldPosition end
return nil
end
function Rtx.track(l)
if not Rtx.all[l] then Rtx.all[l] = true end
end
function Rtx.release(l)
local was = Rtx.lights[l]
if was ~= nil and l.Parent then pcall(function() l.Shadows = was end) end
Rtx.lights[l] = nil
local g = Rtx.guards[l]
if g then pcall(function() g:Disconnect() end) Rtx.guards[l] = nil end
Rtx.chosen[l] = nil
end
function Rtx.claim(l)
if Rtx.lights[l] == nil then
local ok, was = pcall(function() return l.Shadows end)
if not ok then return end
Rtx.lights[l] = was
end
Rtx.chosen[l] = true
pcall(function() if not l.Shadows then l.Shadows = true end end)
if not Rtx.guards[l] then
local ok, c = pcall(function()
return l:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Shadows"):Connect(function()
if Rtx.on and Rtx.chosen[l] and not l.Shadows then pcall(function() l.Shadows = true end) end
end)
end)
if ok then Rtx.guards[l] = c end
end
end
function Rtx.pickShadows()
local c = cam()
local at = c and c.CFrame.Position
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local list = {}
for l in pairs(Rtx.all) do
if not l.Parent then
Rtx.all[l] = nil
Rtx.release(l)
else
local ok, en = pcall(function() return l.Enabled end)
if ok and en then
local mine = char and l:IsDescendantOf(char)
local pos = lightPos(l)
if mine then
list[#list + 1] = { l = l, d = -1 }
elseif pos and at then
local d = (pos - at).Magnitude
if d < 160 then list[#list + 1] = { l = l, d = d } end
end
end
end
end
table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
local want = {}
local n = 0
for _, row in ipairs(list) do
if row.d < 0 or n < Rtx.budget then
want[row.l] = true
if row.d >= 0 then n = n + 1 end
end
end
for l in pairs(Rtx.chosen) do if not want[l] then Rtx.release(l) end end
for l in pairs(want) do Rtx.claim(l) end
Rtx.shadowCount = n
end
function Rtx.apply()
local p = Rtx.PRESETS[Rtx.preset] or Rtx.PRESETS.RTX
local k = Rtx.strength
Rtx.write(Lighting, "Technology", Enum.Technology.Future)
Rtx.write(Lighting, "EnvironmentDiffuseScale", 1)
Rtx.write(Lighting, "EnvironmentSpecularScale", 1)
Rtx.write(Lighting, "ShadowSoftness", p.soft)
if not Sky.fullbright then Rtx.write(Lighting, "GlobalShadows", true) end
local key = Lighting:GetFullName() .. ".ExposureCompensation"
local baseExp = Rtx.saved[key] and Rtx.saved[key].value or Lighting.ExposureCompensation
Rtx.write(Lighting, "ExposureCompensation", baseExp + p.exposure * k)
pcall(function() Rtx.write(game:GetService("MaterialService"), "Use2022Materials", true) end)
local terrain = Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
if terrain then
Rtx.write(terrain, "WaterReflectance", 1)
Rtx.write(terrain, "WaterTransparency", 0.65)
Rtx.write(terrain, "Decoration", true)
end
Rtx.pinQuality(true)
Rtx.gameShadows(true)
local b = Rtx.effect("BloomEffect", "RKRtxBloom")
pcall(function()
b.Enabled = true
b.Intensity, b.Size, b.Threshold = p.bloom[1] * k, p.bloom[2], p.bloom[3]
end)
local rays = Rtx.effect("SunRaysEffect", "RKRtxRays")
pcall(function()
rays.Enabled = p.rays ~= nil
if p.rays then rays.Intensity, rays.Spread = p.rays[1] * k, p.rays[2] end
end)
local cc = Rtx.effect("ColorCorrectionEffect", "RKRtxCC")
pcall(function()
cc.Enabled = true
cc.Brightness, cc.Contrast = p.cc[1] * k, p.cc[2] * k
cc.Saturation = (p.cc[3] < -0.5) and p.cc[3] or p.cc[3] * k
cc.TintColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1):Lerp(p.cc[4], math.min(1, k))
end)
pcall(Rtx.overlay)
local dof = Rtx.effect("DepthOfFieldEffect", "RKRtxDoF")
pcall(function()
local on = p.dof ~= nil and Rtx.blur > 0
and not (Toggles.RK_NoDistanceBlur and Toggles.RK_NoDistanceBlur.Value)
dof.Enabled = on
if on then
dof.FarIntensity = p.dof[1] * Rtx.blur * 2
dof.FocusDistance = p.dof[2]
dof.InFocusRadius = p.dof[3]
dof.NearIntensity = 0
end
end)
end
function Rtx.report()
local bits = {}
for _, prop in ipairs({ "Technology", "Use2022Materials", "Decoration" }) do
bits[#bits + 1] = prop .. (Rtx.took[prop] and " on" or " refused")
end
bits[#bits + 1] = Rtx.qualityPinned and "quality pinned" or "quality NOT pinned - set Esc > Graphics to 10"
if Sky.fullbright then bits[#bits + 1] = "Fullbright is on (it turns shadows off)" end
return table.concat(bits, ", ")
end
function Rtx.set(on)
Rtx.on = on
if on then
pcall(function()
if Toggles.RK_NoShadows and Toggles.RK_NoShadows.Value then
Toggles.RK_NoShadows:SetValue(false)
notify("No Shadows switched off - RTX Shaders needs shadows", 4)
end
end)
Rtx.apply()
task.spawn(function()
local n = 0
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Light") then Rtx.track(d) end
n = n + 1
if n % 4000 == 0 then task.wait() end
end
if Rtx.on then pcall(Rtx.pickShadows) end
end)
if not Rtx.addConn then
Rtx.addConn = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
if Rtx.on and d:IsA("Light") then Rtx.track(d) end
end)
end
if not Rtx.globalGuard then
Rtx.globalGuard = Lighting:GetPropertyChangedSignal("GlobalShadows"):Connect(function()
if Rtx.on and not Sky.fullbright and not Lighting.GlobalShadows then
pcall(function() Lighting.GlobalShadows = true end)
end
end)
end
if not Rtx.conn then
local at, pickAt = 0, 0
Rtx.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Rtx.on then return end
local now = os.clock()
if now - pickAt >= 0.5 then pickAt = now; pcall(Rtx.pickShadows) end
if now - at >= 1 then at = now; pcall(Rtx.apply) end
end)
end
addCleanup("rtx", function() Rtx.set(false) end)
notify("RTX shaders: " .. Rtx.report(), 6)
return
end
if Rtx.conn then Rtx.conn:Disconnect() Rtx.conn = nil end
if Rtx.addConn then Rtx.addConn:Disconnect() Rtx.addConn = nil end
if Rtx.globalGuard then Rtx.globalGuard:Disconnect() Rtx.globalGuard = nil end
for _, name in ipairs({ "RKRtxBloom", "RKRtxRays", "RKRtxCC", "RKRtxDoF" }) do
local e = Lighting:FindFirstChild(name)
if e then pcall(function() e:Destroy() end) end
end
for _, row in pairs(Rtx.saved) do
if row.prop == "GlobalShadows" and row.inst == Lighting and Sky.fullbright then
Sky.savedShadows = row.value
else
local ok = pcall(function() row.inst[row.prop] = row.value end)
if not ok and typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then
pcall(sethiddenproperty, row.inst, row.prop, row.value)
end
end
end
Rtx.saved, Rtx.took = {}, {}
for l in pairs(Rtx.lights) do Rtx.release(l) end
Rtx.lights, Rtx.chosen, Rtx.all, Rtx.guards = {}, {}, {}, {}
Rtx.pinQuality(false)
Rtx.gameShadows(false)
pcall(Rtx.overlay)
if Rtx.grainConn then Rtx.grainConn:Disconnect() Rtx.grainConn = nil end
end
return Rtx
end)()
Stretch = { on = false, amount = 36 }
function Stretch.matrix()
local f = 1 - (Stretch.amount / 100) * 0.7
if f <= 0.05 then f = 0.05 end
return CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, f, 0, 0, 0, 1)
end
function Stretch.apply()
local c = cam()
if not c or not Stretch.on then return end
if Free and Free.on then return end
c.CFrame = c.CFrame * Stretch.matrix()
end
function Stretch.set(on)
Stretch.on = on
if on and not Stretch.conn then
local ok = pcall(function()
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RKStretch", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 5, Stretch.apply)
end)
if ok then
Stretch.conn = "bound"
else
Stretch.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(Stretch.apply)
end
addCleanup("stretch", function() Stretch.set(false) end)
elseif not on and Stretch.conn then
if Stretch.conn == "bound" then
pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RKStretch") end)
else
Stretch.conn:Disconnect()
end
Stretch.conn = nil
end
end
local Spin = { on = false, speed = 20, angle = 0 }
function Spin.set(on)
Spin.on = on
if not on then
if Spin.conn then Spin.conn:Disconnect() Spin.conn = nil end
return
end
if Spin.conn then return end
Spin.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
if not Spin.on then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
Spin.angle = (Spin.angle + dt * Spin.speed) % (math.pi * 2)
local pos = hrp.CFrame.Position
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, Spin.angle, 0)
end)
addCleanup("spin", function() Spin.set(false) end)
end
local Face = { on = false, range = 250 }
function Face.set(on)
Face.on = on
if not on then
if Face.conn then Face.conn:Disconnect() Face.conn = nil end
return
end
if Face.conn then return end
Face.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not Face.on then return end
if Spin.on or (Free and Free.on) then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
local r = rake()
local target = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not (hrp and target) then return end
local at = target.Position
if (at - hrp.Position).Magnitude > Face.range then return end
local flat = Vector3.new(at.X, hrp.Position.Y, at.Z)
if (flat - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 0.1 then return end
pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, flat) end)
end)
addCleanup("face", function() Face.set(false) end)
end
local Moon = { on = false, autoWas = nil, autoHum = nil, conn = nil, yaw = nil }
function Moon.apply(dt)
if not Moon.on then return end
if Spin.on or Face.on then Moon.yaw = nil return end
if Teleport.busy or Fight.flanking then return end
local hum, hrp = getHum(), getHRP()
if not (hum and hrp) or hrp.Anchored then return end
if Moon.autoHum ~= hum then
Moon.autoHum = hum
Moon.autoWas = hum.AutoRotate
end
if hum.AutoRotate then pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = false end) end
Move.autoRotateHeld = os.clock()
local dir = hum.MoveDirection
local flat = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
if flat.Magnitude > 0.05 then
local back = -flat.Unit
local want = math.atan2(-back.X, -back.Z)
local cur = Moon.yaw or want
local diff = (want - cur + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi) - math.pi
Moon.yaw = cur + diff * math.min(1, (dt or 1 / 60) * 14)
end
if not Moon.yaw then return end
local pos = hrp.Position
pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos) * CFrame.Angles(0, Moon.yaw, 0) end)
end
function Moon.set(on)
Moon.on = on
if not on then
if Moon.conn then Moon.conn:Disconnect() Moon.conn = nil end
if Moon.bound then pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RKMoon") end) Moon.bound = nil end
local hum = getHum()
if hum and Moon.autoWas ~= nil then
pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = Moon.autoWas end)
end
Moon.autoWas, Moon.autoHum, Moon.yaw = nil, nil, nil
return
end
if Moon.conn then return end
Moon.bound = pcall(function()
RunService:BindToRenderStep("RKMoon", Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value, function(dt) Moon.apply(dt) end)
end) or nil
Moon.conn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, dt) Moon.apply(dt) end)
addCleanup("moonwalk", function() Moon.set(false) end)
end
local Farm = {
on = false,
speed = 26,
priority = "Scrap",
fleeAt = 90,
avoidAt = 150,
clearBand = 50,
chaseMult = 2,
bloodMult = 1.75,
fleeStep = 60,
trapClear = 14,
touchDist = 6,
CLOSE_ENOUGH = 22,
canJump = true,
holdRadius = 18,
showPath = true, markRakeOn = true,
showRingOn = false,
ringPart = nil, round = nil, roundWhy = nil,
dots = nil, lines = nil, marks = nil,
straightLine = false,
lootRange = 26,
lootAny = true,
lootAt = 0, prizeAt = 0, lastLoot = nil,
measured = 0, rateAt = 0, ratePos = nil,
beaconPart = nil, beaconRing = nil, beaconText = nil,
bloodShelter = true,
committed = nil, committedName = nil, committedAt = 0,
state = "off", why = "", fleeing = false,
target = nil, targetName = "", targetPts = 0,
goal = nil, dest = nil, blocked = false, touching = false,
seen = 0, vetoed = 0, pushExtra = 0,
svNonNil = 0, svTotal = 0,
lastPick = 0, traps = {}, banned = {},
stuck = 0, sampleAt = nil, samplePos = nil,
dodgeUntil = 0, dodgeGoal = nil,
conn = nil,
rk = { part = nil, dist = math.huge, real = math.huge,
chasing = false, active = false, blood = false, why = "" },
}
Farm.PRIORITIES = { "Scrap", "Survival", "Flare Gun" }
Farm.WHEN = { "Always", "Day Only", "Night Only" }
Farm.when = "Always"
Farm.pauseNear, Farm.pauseRange, Farm.PAUSE_HOLD = false, 50, 1.5
function Farm.fleeOnlyWhenChased()
return Farm.priority ~= "Survival"
end
function Farm.wants(s)
if Farm.priority ~= "Flare Gun" then return true end
return s.prize == "flare"
end
Farm.PICK_GAP = 0.15
local function segDist(a, b, p)
local ab = b - a
local len2 = ab:Dot(ab)
if len2 < 1e-4 then return (p - a).Magnitude end
local t = math.clamp((p - a):Dot(ab) / len2, 0, 1)
return (p - (a + ab * t)).Magnitude
end
local MAP_MID, MAP_LEASH
do
local sum, n = Vector3.zero, 0
for _, loc in ipairs(LOCATIONS) do
sum, n = sum + loc.pos, n + 1
end
MAP_MID = n > 0 and (sum / n) or Vector3.zero
MAP_LEASH = 500
end
Farm.WALLS, Farm.wallsAt = nil, 0
local function mapWalls()
if Farm.WALLS and os.clock() - Farm.wallsAt < 15 and (#Farm.WALLS == 0 or Farm.WALLS[1].Parent) then
return Farm.WALLS
end
Farm.WALLS, Farm.wallsAt = {}, os.clock()
local filter = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Filter")
local folder = filter and filter:FindFirstChild("ServerInvisibleWalls")
if folder then
for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then Farm.WALLS[#Farm.WALLS + 1] = d end
end
end
return Farm.WALLS
end
Farm.wallParams = nil
local function wallBlocked(a, b)
local walls = mapWalls()
if #walls == 0 then return false end
local dir = b - a
if dir.Magnitude < 1 then return false end
if not Farm.wallParams then
Farm.wallParams = RaycastParams.new()
Farm.wallParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
pcall(function() Farm.wallParams.RespectCanCollide = false end)
end
Farm.wallParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = walls
Farm.wallCount = #walls
local hit
pcall(function()
hit = Workspace:Raycast(a + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0), dir, Farm.wallParams)
end)
if hit then Farm.wallHits = (Farm.wallHits or 0) + 1 end
return hit ~= nil
end
local function inMap(pos)
return (pos - MAP_MID).Magnitude <= MAP_LEASH
end
local function clampToMap(pos, from)
if inMap(pos) then return pos end
from = from or MAP_MID
for i = 1, 12 do
local back = pos:Lerp(from, i / 12)
if inMap(back) then return back end
end
return from
end
function Farm.readRound()
local st = Farm.round or {}
Farm.round = st
st.night, st.blood, st.timer, st.power = false, false, 0, 100
local n = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Night")
if n and n:IsA("ValueBase") then st.night = n.Value == true end
local b = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("InitiateBloodHour")
if b and b:IsA("ValueBase") then st.blood = b.Value == true end
local t = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Timer")
if t and t:IsA("ValueBase") then st.timer = tonumber(t.Value) or 0 end
local pv = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("PowerValues")
local pl = pv and pv:FindFirstChild("PowerLevel")
if pl and pl:IsA("ValueBase") then st.power = tonumber(pl.Value) or 100 end
return st
end
function Farm.roundSaysShelter()
local st = Farm.round
if not st then return false, nil end
if st.blood and Farm.bloodShelter then
return true, "Blood Hour - not collecting through it"
end
if not Farm.inWindow() then
return true, Farm.when == "Day Only" and "Day Only - sheltering until morning"
or "Night Only - waiting for night"
end
return false, nil
end
function Farm.inWindow()
local st = Farm.round
if not st then return true end
if Farm.when == "Day Only" then return not st.night end
if Farm.when == "Night Only" then return st.night == true end
return true
end
function Farm.playerNear(me)
if not Farm.pauseNear then Farm.pausedFor = nil return nil end
local range = Farm.pauseRange + (Farm.pausedFor and 15 or 0)
local best
for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if p ~= LocalPlayer then
local c = p.Character
local r = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if r and r:IsA("BasePart") and h and h.Health > 0 then
local d = (r.Position - me).Magnitude
if d <= range and (not best or d < best.dist) then
best = { name = (p.DisplayName and p.DisplayName ~= "") and p.DisplayName or p.Name, dist = d, at = os.clock() }
end
end
end
end
if best then
if not Farm.pausedFor then Farm.pauseNew = true end
Farm.pauseUntil = os.clock() + Farm.PAUSE_HOLD
Farm.pausedFor = best
elseif Farm.pausedFor and os.clock() >= (Farm.pauseUntil or 0) then
Farm.pausedFor = nil
end
return Farm.pausedFor
end
function Farm.readRake()
local info = Farm.rk
info.part, info.dist, info.real = nil, math.huge, math.huge
info.chasing, info.active, info.blood, info.why = false, false, false, ""
local r = rake()
if not r then
info.why = "no Rake in the workspace"
return info
end
local part = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not part then
info.why = "the Rake has no root part yet"
return info
end
info.part = part
local me = myPos()
info.real = me and (part.Position - me).Magnitude or math.huge
local blood = r:FindFirstChild("BloodHourMode")
info.blood = blood ~= nil and blood.Value == true
local dead = r:FindFirstChild("Dead")
local inact = r:FindFirstChild("Inactive")
if dead and dead.Value == true then
info.why = "he is dead"
return info
end
if inact and inact.Value == true then
info.why = "he is inactive"
return info
end
info.active = true
info.dist = info.real
local tv = r:FindFirstChild("TargetVal")
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if tv and tv.Value and char then
info.chasing = (tv.Value == char) or tv.Value:IsDescendantOf(char)
end
return info
end
function Farm.fleeRadius(info)
local r = (Farm.priority == "Survival") and Farm.avoidAt or Farm.fleeAt
if Farm.round then
if Farm.round.blood then r = r * 1.5 end
if Farm.round.night then r = r * 1.15 end
if (Farm.round.power or 100) <= 0 then r = r * 1.15 end
end
if info.chasing then r = r * Farm.chaseMult end
if info.blood then r = r * Farm.bloodMult end
return r
end
function Farm.clearRadius(info)
return Farm.fleeRadius(info) + Farm.clearBand
end
function Farm.isRealScrap(m)
if not m:IsA("Model") then return false end
if looksLikeTrap(m) then return false end
local ok, at = pcall(function() return m:GetPivot().Position end)
if ok and not inMap(at) then return false end
if not m:FindFirstChild("PointsVal") then return false end
if not m:FindFirstChild("LevelVal") then return false end
local part = m:FindFirstChild("Scrap")
if not (part and part:IsA("BasePart")) then return false end
return true, part
end
Farm.WANT = { "StunStick", "UV_Lamp", "Vest" }
Farm.ITEMS = {
StunStick = true, UV_Lamp = true, Vest = true, Vitamins = true,
FirstAidKit = true, RakeTrap = true, Tracker = true, Voltmeter = true,
FlareGun = true, Compass = true, Toolbox = true, Watch = true,
Radio = true, HeadLantern = true, Map = true, Candle = true,
Nightvision = true, Monitor = true, Huntsman = true,
UpgradedFlashlight = true, Flashlight = true,
}
function Farm.carrying(name)
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
return (char and char:FindFirstChild(name) ~= nil)
or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name) ~= nil)
end
function Farm.invCount()
local n = 0
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char then
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Tool") then n = n + 1 end
end
end
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if bp then
for _, d in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Tool") then n = n + 1 end
end
end
return n
end
function Farm.itemRank(name)
for i, want in ipairs(Farm.WANT) do
if name == want then return i end
end
return #Farm.WANT + 1
end
function Farm.loot(at, radius)
local out = {}
local look = {}
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do look[#look + 1] = d end
local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
if debris then
for _, d in ipairs(debris:GetDescendants()) do look[#look + 1] = d end
end
local t = Farm.target
if t and t.model and t.model.Parent and t.model ~= debris then
for _, d in ipairs(t.model:GetDescendants()) do look[#look + 1] = d end
end
for _, d in ipairs(look) do
local name = d.Name
if Farm.ITEMS[name] then
local part = d:IsA("BasePart") and d
or (d:IsA("Model") and (d.PrimaryPart or d:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")))
or (d:IsA("Tool") and d:FindFirstChild("Handle"))
local owned = d:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
owned = owned and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(owned) ~= nil
if part and not owned and (part.Position - at).Magnitude <= radius then
out[#out + 1] = { part = part, name = name, rank = Farm.itemRank(name) }
end
end
end
table.sort(out, function(a, b)
if a.rank ~= b.rank then return a.rank < b.rank end
return (a.part.Position - at).Magnitude < (b.part.Position - at).Magnitude
end)
return out
end
function Farm.takeLoot(at, crate)
if os.clock() - (Farm.lootAt or 0) < 0.35 then return nil end
if crate then
Farm.looted = Farm.looted or {}
if Farm.looted[crate] then return nil end
end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return nil end
Farm.lootAt = os.clock()
for _, item in ipairs(Farm.loot(at, Farm.lootRange)) do
if not Farm.carrying(item.name) then
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" or not item.part.Parent then
return nil
end
pcall(function()
firetouchinterest(hrp, item.part, 0)
task.wait()
firetouchinterest(hrp, item.part, 1)
end)
if crate then
Farm.looted = Farm.looted or {}
Farm.looted[crate] = true
end
return item.name
end
end
return nil
end
Farm.magnet = true
Farm.magnetRange = 60
function Farm.pullScrap(me)
if not Farm.magnet then return 0 end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if not char then return 0 end
local probe = Farm.magnetProbe
if probe then
if not (probe.model and probe.model.Parent) then
Farm.magnetProbe = nil
elseif os.clock() - probe.at >= 0.35 then
local ok, at = pcall(function() return probe.model:GetPivot().Position end)
Farm.magnetProbe = nil
if ok and probe.home and (at - probe.home).Magnitude < 10 then
Farm.magnet = false
Farm.magnetWhy = "the server owns the scrap"
notify("magnet off - the server puts the scrap straight back. "
.. "Walking to it instead.", 6)
return 0
end
Farm.magnetWhy = "replicating"
end
end
local spawns = pathTo(Workspace, "Filter", "ScrapSpawns")
if not spawns then return 0 end
local pulled = 0
local to = char:GetPivot()
for _, spawn in ipairs(spawns:GetChildren()) do
for _, m in ipairs(spawn:GetChildren()) do
local ok = Farm.isRealScrap(m)
if ok and not (Farm.magnetProbe and Farm.magnetProbe.model == m) then
local got, at = pcall(function() return m:GetPivot().Position end)
if got and (at - me).Magnitude <= Farm.magnetRange then
local want = (to * CFrame.new(0, -2.2, -3.6)).Position
pcall(function() m:PivotTo(to * CFrame.new(0, -2.2, -3.6)) end)
pulled = pulled + 1
if not Farm.magnetProbe and spawn:IsA("BasePart")
and (want - spawn.Position).Magnitude > 25 then
Farm.magnetProbe = {
model = m, want = want, home = spawn.Position, at = os.clock(),
}
end
end
end
end
end
return pulled
end
function Farm.prizes(out)
local gun = flarePart()
if gun and inMap(gun.Position) and not Farm.carrying("FlareGun") then
out[#out + 1] = {
model = gun, part = gun, pos = gun.Position,
pts = 60, name = "Flare Gun", prize = "flare",
}
end
local crates = pathTo(Workspace, "Debris", "SupplyCrates")
if crates then
for _, box in ipairs(crates:GetChildren()) do
local hit = box:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if not (hit and hit:IsA("BasePart")) then
hit = box:IsA("BasePart") and box or box:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end
if hit and inMap(hit.Position)
and not (Farm.looted and Farm.looted[box]) then
out[#out + 1] = {
model = box, part = hit, pos = hit.Position,
pts = 80, name = "Supply Drop", prize = "crate",
}
end
end
end
return out
end
function Farm.anchorOf(spawn, part)
if spawn and spawn:IsA("BasePart") then return spawn.Position end
return part.Position
end
function Farm.scraps()
if Farm.scrapCache and os.clock() - (Farm.scrapAt or 0) < 0.1 then
return Farm.scrapCache
end
local out = {}
Farm.prizes(out)
local spawns = pathTo(Workspace, "Filter", "ScrapSpawns")
if not spawns then return out end
for _, spawn in ipairs(spawns:GetChildren()) do
for _, m in ipairs(spawn:GetChildren()) do
local ok, part = Farm.isRealScrap(m)
if ok then
local pv = m:FindFirstChild("PointsVal")
out[#out + 1] = {
model = m, part = part, spawn = spawn,
pos = Farm.anchorOf(spawn, part),
pts = (pv and tonumber(pv.Value)) or 0,
name = m.Name,
}
end
end
end
Farm.scrapCache, Farm.scrapAt = out, os.clock()
return out
end
function Farm.svCount()
local n, total = 0, 0
local sv = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SValues")
if sv then
for _, v in ipairs(sv:GetChildren()) do
if v:IsA("ObjectValue") then
total = total + 1
if v.Value ~= nil then n = n + 1 end
end
end
end
Farm.svNonNil, Farm.svTotal = n, total
end
function Farm.pathHasTrap(a, b)
for _, m in ipairs(Farm.traps) do
local hb = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if hb and hb:IsA("BasePart") and segDist(a, b, hb.Position) < Farm.trapClear then
return true
end
end
return false
end
function Farm.pruneBans()
local now = os.clock()
for m, until_ in pairs(Farm.banned) do
if until_ <= now or not m.Parent then Farm.banned[m] = nil end
end
end
function Farm.pickScrap(me, info, radius)
Farm.pruneBans()
local best, bestScore = nil, -math.huge
local seen, vetoed = 0, 0
local threat = info.active and info.part ~= nil
and (info.chasing or not Farm.fleeOnlyWhenChased()
or info.dist < Farm.clearRadius(info))
for _, s in ipairs(Farm.scraps()) do
seen = seen + 1
local blocked = Farm.banned[s.model] ~= nil or not Farm.wants(s)
or Farm.blockedNear(s.pos)
if not blocked and threat
and segDist(me, s.pos, info.part.Position) < radius then
blocked = true
end
if not blocked and Farm.pathHasTrap(me, s.pos) then blocked = true end
if blocked then
vetoed = vetoed + 1
else
local score = -(s.pos - me).Magnitude + s.pts * 1.5
if threat then
local pass = segDist(me, s.pos, info.part.Position)
if pass < radius * 1.6 then
score = score - (radius * 1.6 - pass) * 2.5
end
end
if Farm.target and Farm.target.model == s.model then score = score + 60 end
if score > bestScore then best, bestScore = s, score end
end
end
Farm.seen, Farm.vetoed = seen, vetoed
return best
end
Farm.DEST_RETIRE = 12
Farm.COMMIT = 6
Farm.FLEE_GRACE = 6
function Farm.commitTo(pos, name)
Farm.committed, Farm.committedName = pos, name
Farm.committedAt = os.clock()
end
function Farm.heldDest(fleeing)
if not Farm.committed then return nil end
if fleeing then return nil end
if os.clock() - (Farm.committedAt or 0) > Farm.COMMIT then return nil end
if Farm.destRetired(Farm.committed) then return nil end
return Farm.committed, Farm.committedName
end
Farm.NO_GO = {
{ folder = "SafeHouse",        msg = "SafehouseMSG",        name = "Safe House" },
{ folder = "PowerStation",     msg = "StationMSG",          name = "Power Station" },
{ folder = "ObservationTower", msg = "ObservationTowerMSG", name = "Observation Tower" },
}
Farm.standoff = 10
Farm.BOX_REACH = 60
Farm.BOX_LIFT = 30
Farm.BOX_MAX = 120
function Farm.markerNamed(msg)
local points = pathTo(Workspace, "Filter", "LocationPoints")
local p = points and points:FindFirstChild(msg)
return (p and p:IsA("BasePart")) and p or nil
end
function Farm.noGoBoxes()
if Farm.boxes and os.clock() - (Farm.boxesAt or 0) < 30 then return Farm.boxes end
Farm.boxes, Farm.boxesAt = {}, os.clock()
local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
if not map then return Farm.boxes end
for _, spec in ipairs(Farm.NO_GO) do
local holder = map:FindFirstChild(spec.folder)
local marker = Farm.markerNamed(spec.msg)
local at = marker and marker.Position or nil
if holder then
local lo, hi
for _, d in ipairs(holder:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Size.Magnitude < 400 then
local p = d.Position
local counts = (at == nil)
or (math.abs(p.X - at.X) <= Farm.BOX_REACH
and math.abs(p.Z - at.Z) <= Farm.BOX_REACH
and p.Y <= at.Y + Farm.BOX_LIFT)
if counts then
if not lo then
lo, hi = p, p
else
lo = Vector3.new(math.min(lo.X, p.X), math.min(lo.Y, p.Y), math.min(lo.Z, p.Z))
hi = Vector3.new(math.max(hi.X, p.X), math.max(hi.Y, p.Y), math.max(hi.Z, p.Z))
end
end
end
end
if lo and (hi - lo).Magnitude > 8
and math.max(hi.X - lo.X, hi.Z - lo.Z) <= Farm.BOX_MAX then
Farm.boxes[#Farm.boxes + 1] = { name = spec.name, lo = lo, hi = hi }
end
end
end
return Farm.boxes
end
Farm.boxPad = 6
function Farm.insideNoGo(pos, pad)
pad = (pad or 0) + Farm.boxPad
for _, b in ipairs(Farm.noGoBoxes()) do
if pos.X > b.lo.X - pad and pos.X < b.hi.X + pad
and pos.Z > b.lo.Z - pad and pos.Z < b.hi.Z + pad then
return b
end
end
return nil
end
function Farm.outsideOf(b, from, pad)
local cx = (b.lo.X + b.hi.X) * 0.5
local cz = (b.lo.Z + b.hi.Z) * 0.5
local dx, dz = from.X - cx, from.Z - cz
local halfX = (b.hi.X - b.lo.X) * 0.5 + pad
local halfZ = (b.hi.Z - b.lo.Z) * 0.5 + pad
if math.abs(dx) / math.max(halfX, 0.1) > math.abs(dz) / math.max(halfZ, 0.1) then
return Vector3.new(cx + (dx >= 0 and halfX or -halfX), from.Y, from.Z)
end
return Vector3.new(from.X, from.Y, cz + (dz >= 0 and halfZ or -halfZ))
end
function Farm.retireDest(pos, why)
if not pos then return end
Farm.deadDests = Farm.deadDests or {}
for _, d in ipairs(Farm.deadDests) do
if (d.pos - pos).Magnitude < 8 then
d.until_ = os.clock() + Farm.DEST_RETIRE
return
end
end
Farm.deadDests[#Farm.deadDests + 1] = { pos = pos, until_ = os.clock() + Farm.DEST_RETIRE }
notify("cannot reach " .. (Farm.targetName ~= "" and Farm.targetName or "that spot")
.. " - " .. (why or "stuck") .. ", trying elsewhere", 5)
end
function Farm.destRetired(pos)
if not Farm.deadDests then return false end
local now = os.clock()
for i = #Farm.deadDests, 1, -1 do
local d = Farm.deadDests[i]
if now > d.until_ then
table.remove(Farm.deadDests, i)
elseif (d.pos - pos).Magnitude < 8 then
return true
end
end
return false
end
Farm.groundParams = nil
function Farm.terrainY(pos)
if not Farm.groundParams then
Farm.groundParams = RaycastParams.new()
Farm.groundParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
Farm.groundParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { Workspace.Terrain }
end
local hit
pcall(function()
hit = Workspace:Raycast(pos + Vector3.new(0, 150, 0),
Vector3.new(0, -400, 0), Farm.groundParams)
end)
return hit and hit.Position.Y or pos.Y
end
Farm.MSG_NAMES = {
{ msg = "SafehouseMSG",        name = "Safe House" },
{ msg = "StationMSG",          name = "Power Station" },
{ msg = "BaseCampMSG",         name = "Base Camp" },
{ msg = "ShopMSG",             name = "Shop" },
{ msg = "ObservationTowerMSG", name = "Observation Tower" },
}
function Farm.allLandmarks()
if Farm.marksList and os.clock() - (Farm.marksAt or 0) < 30 then return Farm.marksList end
Farm.marksList, Farm.marksAt = {}, os.clock()
local boxes = Farm.noGoBoxes()
local function boxNamed(name)
for _, b in ipairs(boxes) do
if b.name == name then return b end
end
return nil
end
for _, spec in ipairs(Farm.MSG_NAMES) do
local marker = Farm.markerNamed(spec.msg)
if marker then
local at = marker.Position
at = Vector3.new(at.X, Farm.terrainY(at) + 3, at.Z)
Farm.marksList[#Farm.marksList + 1] =
{ name = spec.name, pos = clampToMap(at), box = boxNamed(spec.name) }
end
end
if #Farm.marksList > 0 then return Farm.marksList end
for _, loc in ipairs(LOCATIONS) do
Farm.marksList[#Farm.marksList + 1] = { name = loc.name, pos = clampToMap(loc.pos) }
end
return Farm.marksList
end
function Farm.landmarks()
local all = Farm.allLandmarks()
local out = {}
for _, loc in ipairs(all) do
if not Farm.blockList[loc.name] then out[#out + 1] = loc end
end
if #out == 0 then return all end
return out
end
function Farm.shelter(me, info, radius)
local best, bestName, bestScore = nil, nil, -math.huge
for _, loc in ipairs(Farm.landmarks()) do
if not Farm.destRetired(loc.pos) and inMap(loc.pos) then
local score = -(loc.pos - me).Magnitude
if info.active and info.part then
local fromHim = (loc.pos - info.part.Position).Magnitude
if fromHim < radius then
score = score - 1000
else
score = score + fromHim * 0.5
end
end
if score > bestScore then best, bestName, bestScore = loc.pos, loc.name, score end
end
end
return best, bestName
end
function Farm.blameHeading(me)
local g = Farm.goal
if not g then return end
local flat = Vector3.new(g.X - me.X, 0, g.Z - me.Z)
if flat.Magnitude < 1 then return end
Farm.deadEnds = Farm.deadEnds or {}
Farm.deadEnds[#Farm.deadEnds + 1] = { dir = flat.Unit, until_ = os.clock() + 4 }
if #Farm.deadEnds > 8 then table.remove(Farm.deadEnds, 1) end
end
function Farm.deadEndPenalty(me, goal)
if not Farm.deadEnds then return 0 end
local flat = Vector3.new(goal.X - me.X, 0, goal.Z - me.Z)
if flat.Magnitude < 1 then return 0 end
local dir, now, pen = flat.Unit, os.clock(), 0
for i = #Farm.deadEnds, 1, -1 do
local d = Farm.deadEnds[i]
if now > d.until_ then
table.remove(Farm.deadEnds, i)
elseif dir:Dot(d.dir) > 0.8 then
pen = pen - 600
end
end
return pen
end
Farm.MAP_LO = Vector3.new(-450, 0, -380)
Farm.MAP_HI = Vector3.new(130, 0, 320)
Farm.MAP_H = 176
Farm.BLOCK_RADIUS = 110
Farm.BLOCK_FILE = "Ruv&Rill/blacklist.txt"
Farm.blockList = {}
Farm.mapDots = {}
Farm.mapZoom = 1
Farm.MAP_ZOOMS = { 1, 2, 3.5, 6 }
Farm.MAP_FONT = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold)
function Farm.blockSave()
if typeof(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
local out = {}
for name, on in pairs(Farm.blockList) do
if on then out[#out + 1] = name end
end
pcall(ensureFolder, "Ruv&Rill")
pcall(writefile, Farm.BLOCK_FILE, table.concat(out, "\n"))
end
function Farm.blockLoad()
if type(Farm.blockList) ~= "table" then Farm.blockList = {} end
if typeof(isfile) ~= "function" or typeof(readfile) ~= "function" then return end
local ok, has = pcall(isfile, Farm.BLOCK_FILE)
if not (ok and has) then return end
local got, body = pcall(readfile, Farm.BLOCK_FILE)
if not (got and type(body) == "string") then return end
for line in body:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
local name = line:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
if name ~= "" then Farm.blockList[name] = true end
end
end
function Farm.blockToggle(name)
Farm.blockList[name] = not Farm.blockList[name] or nil
Farm.mapScrapAt = 0
Farm.blockSave()
Farm.marksList = nil
Farm.dropKite()
Farm.committed = nil
end
function Farm.blockedNear(pos)
if not pos then return false end
if type(Farm.blockList) ~= "table" then return false end
for _, loc in ipairs(Farm.allLandmarks()) do
if Farm.blockList[loc.name] and (loc.pos - pos).Magnitude <= Farm.BLOCK_RADIUS then
return true
end
end
return false
end
function Farm.mapScale(size)
local spanX = Farm.MAP_HI.X - Farm.MAP_LO.X
local spanZ = Farm.MAP_HI.Z - Farm.MAP_LO.Z
return math.min(size.X / spanX, size.Y / spanZ) * (Farm.mapZoom or 1)
end
function Farm.mapPos(world, size)
local scale = Farm.mapScale(size)
if (Farm.mapZoom or 1) > 1 and Farm.mapCenter then
local c = Farm.mapCenter
return Vector2.new(size.X * 0.5 + (world.X - c.X) * scale, size.Y * 0.5 + (world.Z - c.Z) * scale)
end
local spanX = Farm.MAP_HI.X - Farm.MAP_LO.X
local spanZ = Farm.MAP_HI.Z - Farm.MAP_LO.Z
local offX = (size.X - spanX * scale) * 0.5
local offY = (size.Y - spanZ * scale) * 0.5
return Vector2.new(
offX + (world.X - Farm.MAP_LO.X) * scale,
offY + (world.Z - Farm.MAP_LO.Z) * scale)
end
function Farm.mapDot(name, colour, size, zindex)
local d = Farm.mapDots[name]
if d and d.Parent then return d end
d = make("Frame", {
Name = "Dot_" .. name, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), BackgroundColor3 = colour,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = zindex or 4,
}, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, d)
Farm.mapDots[name] = d
return d
end
function Farm.mapRing(name, colour, zindex, fill)
local d = Farm.mapDots[name]
if d and d.Parent then return d end
d = make("Frame", {
Name = "Ring_" .. name, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10),
BackgroundColor3 = colour, BackgroundTransparency = fill or 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = zindex or 4,
}, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, d)
make("UIStroke", { Name = "Edge", Color = colour, Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.3,
ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, d)
Farm.mapDots[name] = d
return d
end
function Farm.mapSeg(f, a, b, thick)
local dx, dy = b.X - a.X, b.Y - a.Y
local len = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((a.X + b.X) * 0.5, (a.Y + b.Y) * 0.5)
f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(len + (thick or 2) * 0.5, thick or 2)
f.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx))
f.Visible = len > 0.5
end
function Farm.mapPin(loc)
local dot = make("TextButton", {
Name = "Pin_" .. loc.name, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "",
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(44, 49, 62), BorderSizePixel = 0,
ZIndex = 8,
}, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, dot)
make("UIStroke", { Thickness = 2, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Transparency = 0.05,
ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, dot)
local label = make("TextLabel", {
Name = "Tag_" .. loc.name, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(88, 13), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
FontFace = Farm.MAP_FONT, TextSize = 10, ZIndex = 7,
TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 34, 44), Text = loc.name,
TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), TextStrokeTransparency = 0.45,
}, Farm.mapFrame)
dot.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
Farm.blockToggle(loc.name)
notify((Farm.blockList[loc.name] and "blacklisted " or "allowed ") .. loc.name, 4)
end)
return { dot = dot, label = label, loc = loc }
end
function Farm.mapChip(parent, text, pos, w)
local b = make("TextButton", {
Name = "Chip_" .. text, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = pos, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w or 20, 20),
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), BackgroundTransparency = 0.25, BorderSizePixel = 0,
AutoButtonColor = true, Text = text, FontFace = Farm.MAP_FONT, TextSize = 13,
TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 34, 44), ZIndex = 20,
}, parent)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, b)
make("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.2,
ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, b)
return b
end
function Farm.setMapZoom(step)
local levels = Farm.MAP_ZOOMS
local i = 1
for k, z in ipairs(levels) do if z == Farm.mapZoom then i = k break end end
i = math.clamp(i + step, 1, #levels)
Farm.mapZoom = levels[i]
if Farm.mapZoomLbl then Farm.mapZoomLbl.Text = (Farm.mapZoom == 1) and "fit" or (tostring(Farm.mapZoom) .. "x") end
if Farm.mapZoom > 1 and not Farm.mapCenter then Farm.mapCenter = myPos() end
end
function Farm.buildMap(gb)
if Farm.mapFrame and Farm.mapFrame.Parent then return end
local holder = gb.Container
if not holder then return end
Farm.mapFrame = make("Frame", {
Name = "RKFarmMap", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, Farm.MAP_H),
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(214, 221, 233), BackgroundTransparency = 0.12,
BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = 50, ClipsDescendants = true,
}, holder)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9) }, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(214, 224, 242)) }, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Thickness = 1.2, Transparency = 0.15,
ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, Farm.mapFrame)
local plus = Farm.mapChip(Farm.mapFrame, "+", UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 6))
Farm.mapZoomLbl = Farm.mapChip(Farm.mapFrame, "fit", UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 6), 28)
Farm.mapZoomLbl.TextSize = 10; Farm.mapZoomLbl.AutoButtonColor = false
local minus = Farm.mapChip(Farm.mapFrame, "-", UDim2.new(1, -58, 0, 6))
plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Farm.setMapZoom(1) end)
minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Farm.setMapZoom(-1) end)
Farm.mapZoomLbl.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Farm.setMapZoom(-10) end)
make("TextLabel", { Name = "North", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 5),
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), Text = "N", FontFace = Farm.MAP_FONT, TextSize = 11,
TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 76, 90), ZIndex = 20 }, Farm.mapFrame)
Farm.mapInfo = make("TextLabel", { Name = "Info", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1),
Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -4), Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 13), Text = "", FontFace = Farm.MAP_FONT,
TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 62, 76),
TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5, ZIndex = 20 }, Farm.mapFrame)
Farm.mapScaleBar = make("Frame", { Name = "ScaleBar", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 2), BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 62, 76), ZIndex = 20 }, Farm.mapFrame)
Farm.mapScaleLbl = make("TextLabel", { Name = "ScaleLbl", BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 11), Text = "", FontFace = Farm.MAP_FONT,
TextSize = 9, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 62, 76), ZIndex = 20 }, Farm.mapFrame)
Farm.mapPins = {}
for _, loc in ipairs(Farm.allLandmarks()) do
Farm.mapPins[loc.name] = Farm.mapPin(loc)
end
pcall(function() gb:Resize() end)
end
function Farm.mapGrid()
if Farm.mapLines then return end
Farm.mapLines = {}
for i = 1, 14 do
Farm.mapLines[i] = make("Frame", {
Name = "Grid" .. i, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 130, 150),
BackgroundTransparency = 0.8, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2,
Visible = false,
}, Farm.mapFrame)
end
end
function Farm.paintGrid(size)
Farm.mapGrid()
local n = 0
local function line(x, y, w, h)
n = n + 1
local f = Farm.mapLines[n]
if not f then return end
f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
f.Visible = true
end
for world = -400, 100, 100 do
local top = Farm.mapPos(Vector3.new(world, 0, Farm.MAP_LO.Z), size)
local bot = Farm.mapPos(Vector3.new(world, 0, Farm.MAP_HI.Z), size)
line(top.X, top.Y, 1, bot.Y - top.Y)
end
for world = -300, 300, 100 do
local left = Farm.mapPos(Vector3.new(Farm.MAP_LO.X, 0, world), size)
local right = Farm.mapPos(Vector3.new(Farm.MAP_HI.X, 0, world), size)
line(left.X, left.Y, right.X - left.X, 1)
end
for i = n + 1, #Farm.mapLines do Farm.mapLines[i].Visible = false end
end
function Farm.mapRefreshLists()
Farm.mapScrapList = {}
pcall(function()
for _, sp in ipairs(Farm.scraps()) do
if not sp.prize then
Farm.mapScrapList[#Farm.mapScrapList + 1] = { pos = sp.pos, blocked = Farm.blockedNear(sp.pos) }
end
end
end)
Farm.mapTrapList = {}
pcall(function()
local traps = pathTo(Workspace, "Debris", "Traps")
for _, m in ipairs(traps and traps:GetChildren() or {}) do
local pos
if m:IsA("Model") then pos = m:GetPivot().Position elseif m:IsA("BasePart") then pos = m.Position end
if pos then
local rusty = false
pcall(function() rusty = m:GetAttribute("ServerSpawned") == true end)
Farm.mapTrapList[#Farm.mapTrapList + 1] = { pos = pos, rusty = rusty }
end
end
end)
end
function Farm.paintScrap(size)
Farm.scrapDots = Farm.scrapDots or {}
local n = 0
for _, sp in ipairs(Farm.mapScrapList or {}) do
n = n + 1
local d = Farm.scrapDots[n]
if not (d and d.Parent) then
d = make("Frame", {
Name = "Scrap" .. n, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3,
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(56, 140, 220),
}, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, d)
make("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.35 }, d)
Farm.scrapDots[n] = d
end
local at = Farm.mapPos(sp.pos, size)
d.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X, at.Y)
d.BackgroundTransparency = sp.blocked and 0.78 or 0.05
d.Visible = true
end
for i = n + 1, #Farm.scrapDots do Farm.scrapDots[i].Visible = false end
end
function Farm.paintTraps(size)
Farm.trapDots = Farm.trapDots or {}
local n = 0
for _, t in ipairs(Farm.mapTrapList or {}) do
n = n + 1
local d = Farm.trapDots[n]
if not (d and d.Parent) then
d = make("Frame", { Name = "Trap" .. n, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Rotation = 45,
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 5 }, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.2 }, d)
Farm.trapDots[n] = d
end
local at = Farm.mapPos(t.pos, size)
d.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X, at.Y)
d.BackgroundColor3 = t.rusty and Color3.fromRGB(150, 96, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(226, 120, 40)
d.Visible = true
end
for i = n + 1, #Farm.trapDots do Farm.trapDots[i].Visible = false end
end
function Farm.paintRoute(size)
Farm.routeSegs = Farm.routeSegs or {}
local pts = {}
if Farm.on or Farm.macro.rec or Farm.macro.playing then
local ok, got = pcall(Farm.pathPoints)
if ok and type(got) == "table" then pts = got end
end
local n = 0
for i = 1, math.min(#pts - 1, 48) do
n = n + 1
local f = Farm.routeSegs[n]
if not (f and f.Parent) then
f = make("Frame", { Name = "Route" .. n, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(226, 160, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 0.15, ZIndex = 4 }, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, f)
Farm.routeSegs[n] = f
end
Farm.mapSeg(f, Farm.mapPos(pts[i], size), Farm.mapPos(pts[i + 1], size), 2)
end
for i = n + 1, #Farm.routeSegs do Farm.routeSegs[i].Visible = false end
end
Farm.TRAIL_MAX, Farm.TRAIL_GAP = 36, 5
function Farm.noteTrail()
local me = myPos()
if not me then return end
Farm.mapTrail = Farm.mapTrail or {}
local last = Farm.mapTrail[#Farm.mapTrail]
if last and (last - me).Magnitude < Farm.TRAIL_GAP then return end
if last and (last - me).Magnitude > 80 then Farm.mapTrail = {} end
Farm.mapTrail[#Farm.mapTrail + 1] = me
if #Farm.mapTrail > Farm.TRAIL_MAX then table.remove(Farm.mapTrail, 1) end
end
function Farm.paintTrail(size)
Farm.trailDots = Farm.trailDots or {}
local list = Farm.mapTrail or {}
for i, pos in ipairs(list) do
local d = Farm.trailDots[i]
if not (d and d.Parent) then
d = make("Frame", { Name = "Trail" .. i, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3),
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 165, 95), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3 }, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, d)
Farm.trailDots[i] = d
end
local at = Farm.mapPos(pos, size)
d.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X, at.Y)
d.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25 + 0.7 * (1 - i / #list)
d.Visible = true
end
for i = #list + 1, #Farm.trailDots do Farm.trailDots[i].Visible = false end
end
function Farm.paintHeading(name, part, colour, size, z)
local at = Farm.mapPos(part.Position, size)
local look = part.CFrame.LookVector
local dir = Vector2.new(look.X, look.Z)
local needle = Farm.mapDots[name .. "Needle"]
if not (needle and needle.Parent) then
needle = make("Frame", { Name = name .. "Needle", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = colour, ZIndex = z - 1 }, Farm.mapFrame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, needle)
Farm.mapDots[name .. "Needle"] = needle
end
if dir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
dir = dir.Unit
Farm.mapSeg(needle, at, at + dir * 10, 2.5)
else
needle.Visible = false
end
return at
end
function Farm.paintMap(dt)
if not (Farm.mapFrame and Farm.mapFrame.Parent) then return end
dt = math.min(dt or 1 / 60, 0.1)
if os.clock() - (Farm.trailAt or 0) > 0.4 then
Farm.trailAt = os.clock()
pcall(Farm.noteTrail)
end
if Library and Library.Toggled == false then return end
local size = Farm.mapFrame.AbsoluteSize
if size.X < 20 then return end
Farm.mapClock = (Farm.mapClock or 0) + dt
local me = myPos()
if (Farm.mapZoom or 1) > 1 and me then
local c = Farm.mapCenter or me
Farm.mapCenter = c:Lerp(me, 1 - math.exp(-dt * 6))
end
if os.clock() - (Farm.mapScrapAt or 0) > 1 then
Farm.mapScrapAt = os.clock()
Farm.mapRefreshLists()
end
Farm.paintGrid(size)
pcall(Farm.paintScrap, size)
pcall(Farm.paintTraps, size)
pcall(Farm.paintTrail, size)
pcall(Farm.paintRoute, size)
local scale = Farm.mapScale(size)
local mid = size.X / 2
for name, pin in pairs(Farm.mapPins or {}) do
local at = Farm.mapPos(pin.loc.pos, size)
pin.dot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X, at.Y)
local off = Farm.blockList[name]
pin.dot.BackgroundColor3 = off and Color3.fromRGB(214, 58, 58) or Color3.fromRGB(44, 49, 62)
pin.label.TextColor3 = off and Color3.fromRGB(190, 50, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(30, 34, 44)
local zone = Farm.mapRing("zone_" .. name, Color3.fromRGB(214, 58, 58), 2, 0.88)
zone.Visible = off and true or false
if off then
local r = Farm.BLOCK_RADIUS * scale * 2
zone.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(r, r)
zone.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X, at.Y)
end
if at.X > mid then
pin.label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
pin.label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X - 8, at.Y)
pin.label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
else
pin.label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
pin.label.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X + 8, at.Y)
pin.label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
end
end
local aim = Farm.aimingAt()
if aim then
local d = Farm.mapDot("goal", Color3.fromRGB(226, 160, 36), 7, 9)
local at = Farm.mapPos(aim, size)
d.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X, at.Y)
d.Visible = true
elseif Farm.mapDots.goal then
Farm.mapDots.goal.Visible = false
end
local hrp = getHRP()
local meDot = Farm.mapDot("me", Color3.fromRGB(45, 165, 95), 9, 12)
local halo = Farm.mapRing("meHalo", Color3.fromRGB(45, 165, 95), 11, 0.8)
if hrp then
local at = Farm.paintHeading("me", hrp, Color3.fromRGB(45, 165, 95), size, 12)
meDot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X, at.Y); meDot.Visible = true
halo.Position = meDot.Position; halo.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(17, 17); halo.Visible = true
else
meDot.Visible, halo.Visible = false, false
if Farm.mapDots.meNeedle then Farm.mapDots.meNeedle.Visible = false end
end
local r = rake()
local rh = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local rDot = Farm.mapDot("rake", Color3.fromRGB(230, 60, 60), 10, 14)
local pulse = Farm.mapRing("rakePulse", Color3.fromRGB(230, 60, 60), 13)
local dist
if rh and rh:IsA("BasePart") then
local at = Farm.paintHeading("rake", rh, Color3.fromRGB(230, 60, 60), size, 14)
rDot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(at.X, at.Y); rDot.Visible = true
local chasing = false
pcall(function()
local tv = r:FindFirstChild("TargetVal")
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if tv and tv.Value and char then chasing = (tv.Value == char) or tv.Value:IsDescendantOf(char) end
end)
local period = chasing and 0.6 or 1.4
local ph = (Farm.mapClock % period) / period
pulse.Position = rDot.Position
pulse.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10 + ph * (chasing and 30 or 22), 10 + ph * (chasing and 30 or 22))
local edge = pulse:FindFirstChild("Edge")
if edge then edge.Transparency = ph end
pulse.Visible = true
if me then dist = (rh.Position - me).Magnitude end
else
rDot.Visible, pulse.Visible = false, false
if Farm.mapDots.rakeNeedle then Farm.mapDots.rakeNeedle.Visible = false end
end
if Farm.mapInfo then
local bits = { #(Farm.mapScrapList or {}) .. " scrap", #(Farm.mapTrapList or {}) .. " traps" }
bits[#bits + 1] = dist and ("rake " .. math.floor(dist) .. "m") or "rake not out"
Farm.mapInfo.Text = table.concat(bits, "  -  ")
end
if Farm.mapScaleBar then
local studs = 100
for _, s in ipairs({ 10, 25, 50, 100, 200 }) do
if s * scale >= 24 then studs = s break end
end
Farm.mapScaleBar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(studs * scale + 0.5), 2)
Farm.mapScaleLbl.Text = studs .. "m"
end
end
Farm.MACRO_RATE = 0.12
Farm.MACRO_GAP = 7
Farm.MACRO_MAX = 600
Farm.MACRO_JUMP = 60
Farm.MACRO_HIT = 7
Farm.MACRO_STUCK = 8
Farm.MACRO_FILE = "Ruv&Rill/walkmacro.txt"
Farm.macro = {
rec = false, playing = false, recorded = false, pts = {},
i = 1, dir = 1, laps = 0, at = 0, hitAt = 0,
}
function Farm.macroCount()
return #Farm.macro.pts
end
function Farm.macroClear()
Farm.macro.rec, Farm.macro.playing = false, false
Farm.macro.pts, Farm.macro.i, Farm.macro.dir, Farm.macro.laps = {}, 1, 1, 0
Farm.macro.recorded = false
Farm.macroDisarm()
Farm.hidePath()
pcall(function()
if Toggles.RK_WalkMacro then
Toggles.RK_WalkMacro.Value = false
