Toggles.RK_WalkMacro:Display()
end
end)
end
function Farm.macroRecord(me)
local m = Farm.macro
if not m.rec then return end
if os.clock() - m.at < Farm.MACRO_RATE then return end
m.at = os.clock()
if #m.pts >= Farm.MACRO_MAX then
m.rec = false
notify("macro is full at " .. Farm.MACRO_MAX .. " points - recording stopped", 6)
return
end
local last = m.pts[#m.pts]
if last then
local step = (me - last).Magnitude
if step < Farm.MACRO_GAP then return end
if step > Farm.MACRO_JUMP then
notify("that was a jump, not a step - macro cleared", 6)
Farm.macroClear()
return
end
end
m.pts[#m.pts + 1] = Farm.onGround(me)
end
function Farm.macroStep(me)
local m = Farm.macro
if #m.pts == 0 then
m.playing = false
return nil
end
if #m.pts == 1 then return m.pts[1] end
local goal = m.pts[m.i]
if not goal then
m.i, m.dir = 1, 1
return m.pts[1]
end
if (me - goal).Magnitude <= Farm.MACRO_HIT then
m.hitAt = os.clock()
m.i = m.i + m.dir
if m.i > #m.pts then
m.i, m.dir = #m.pts - 1, -1
m.laps = m.laps + 1
elseif m.i < 1 then
m.i, m.dir = 2, 1
m.laps = m.laps + 1
end
elseif os.clock() - (m.hitAt or 0) > Farm.MACRO_STUCK then
m.hitAt = os.clock()
local ni = m.i + m.dir
if ni > #m.pts then m.i, m.dir = #m.pts - 1, -1
elseif ni < 1 then m.i, m.dir = 2, 1
else m.i = ni end
end
return m.pts[m.i]
end
function Farm.macroSave()
if typeof(writefile) ~= "function" then return false end
local out = {}
for _, p in ipairs(Farm.macro.pts) do
out[#out + 1] = string.format("%.1f,%.1f,%.1f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z)
end
pcall(ensureFolder, "Ruv&Rill")
return (pcall(writefile, Farm.MACRO_FILE, table.concat(out, "\n")))
end
function Farm.macroLoad()
if typeof(isfile) ~= "function" or typeof(readfile) ~= "function" then return 0 end
local ok, has = pcall(isfile, Farm.MACRO_FILE)
if not (ok and has) then return 0 end
local got, body = pcall(readfile, Farm.MACRO_FILE)
if not (got and type(body) == "string") then return 0 end
local pts = {}
for line in body:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
local x, y, z = line:match("^(-?[%d%.]+),(-?[%d%.]+),(-?[%d%.]+)$")
local nx, ny, nz = tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)
if nx and ny and nz then pts[#pts + 1] = Vector3.new(nx, ny, nz) end
end
if #pts > 0 then
Farm.macro.pts, Farm.macro.i, Farm.macro.dir = pts, 1, 1
Farm.macro.recorded = false
end
return #pts
end
function Farm.macroArm()
if Farm.macroConn then return end
Farm.macroConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local m = Farm.macro
if not (m.rec or m.playing) then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
local hum = getHum()
if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
local me = hrp.Position
Farm.macroRecord(me)
if not Farm.on then
if os.clock() - (Farm.macroTrapAt or 0) > 1 then
Farm.macroTrapAt = os.clock()
Farm.traps = trapModels()
end
if m.playing then
local goal = Farm.macroStep(me)
if goal then Farm.walkTo(goal) end
end
Farm.drawPath()
end
end)
addCleanup("walkmacro", function()
if Farm.macroConn then Farm.macroConn:Disconnect() Farm.macroConn = nil end
end)
end
function Farm.macroDisarm()
if Farm.macroConn then Farm.macroConn:Disconnect() Farm.macroConn = nil end
if not Farm.on then
Farm.stopWalking()
Farm.hidePath()
Farm.hideBeacon()
end
end
function Farm.macroSet(on)
local m = Farm.macro
if on then
m.rec, m.playing, m.recorded = true, false, true
m.pts, m.i, m.dir, m.laps = {}, 1, 1, 0
m.at, m.hitAt = 0, os.clock()
Farm.macroArm()
notify("recording - walk the route, then switch it off to run it", 6)
return
end
m.rec = false
if not m.recorded then
m.playing = false
Farm.macroDisarm()
notify("nothing recorded this session - switch it on and walk the route", 6)
return
end
if #m.pts < 2 then
m.playing = false
Farm.macroDisarm()
notify("nothing recorded - walk further with it on", 5)
return
end
m.playing = true
m.i, m.dir, m.hitAt = 1, 1, os.clock()
Farm.macroSave()
Farm.macroArm()
notify(#m.pts .. " points recorded - walking it", 5)
end
Farm.KITE_HOLD = 24
Farm.KITE_MIN = 70
Farm.KITE_MEMORY = 2
Farm.KITE_AHEAD = 110
Farm.AHEAD_AT = 70
Farm.KITE_PICK = 90
Farm.KITE_DROP = 55
function Farm.standAt(pos, from)
local b = Farm.insideNoGo(pos, 0)
if not b then return pos end
from = from or pos
local cx = (b.lo.X + b.hi.X) * 0.5
local cz = (b.lo.Z + b.hi.Z) * 0.5
local halfX = (b.hi.X - b.lo.X) * 0.5 + Farm.standoff
local halfZ = (b.hi.Z - b.lo.Z) * 0.5 + Farm.standoff
local dx, dz = from.X - cx, from.Z - cz
if math.abs(dx) / math.max(halfX, 0.1) > math.abs(dz) / math.max(halfZ, 0.1) then
return Vector3.new(cx + (dx >= 0 and halfX or -halfX), pos.Y, cz)
end
return Vector3.new(cx, pos.Y, cz + (dz >= 0 and halfZ or -halfZ))
end
function Farm.kiteRefused(name, extra)
for _, n in ipairs(Farm.kiteRecent or {}) do
if n == name then return true end
end
for _, n in ipairs(extra or {}) do
if n == name then return true end
end
return false
end
Farm.LEG_SKIP = 1.3
function Farm.legStart(from, to, corridor)
local d = to - from
if d.Magnitude < 1 then return from end
local skip = math.min((corridor or 90) * Farm.LEG_SKIP, d.Magnitude * 0.5)
return from + d.Unit * skip
end
Farm.KITE_STALE = 900
function Farm.kiteScore(from, loc, info, radius, skipMemory, extra)
if Farm.destRetired(loc.pos) then return nil end
for _, n in ipairs(extra or {}) do
if n == loc.name then return nil end
end
if not skipMemory and Farm.kiteRefused(loc.name) then return nil end
local away = (loc.pos - from).Magnitude
if away < Farm.KITE_MIN then return nil end
if wallBlocked(from, loc.pos) then return nil end
local score = 0
if info and info.part then
local him = info.part.Position
local corridor = math.min(radius, Farm.KITE_PICK)
if segDist(Farm.legStart(from, loc.pos, corridor), loc.pos, him) < corridor then
return nil
end
score = score + (loc.pos - him).Magnitude
local toGoal = loc.pos - from
local fromHim = from - him
if toGoal.Magnitude > 1 and fromHim.Magnitude > 1 then
score = score + toGoal.Unit:Dot(fromHim.Unit) * 140
end
end
score = score - away * 0.35
score = score + Farm.deadEndPenalty(from, loc.pos)
return score
end
function Farm.pickKite(from, info, radius, skipMemory, extra)
local best, bestScore = nil, -math.huge
for _, loc in ipairs(Farm.landmarks()) do
local score = Farm.kiteScore(from, loc, info, radius, skipMemory, extra)
if score and score > bestScore then best, bestScore = loc, score end
end
if best then return best end
if not skipMemory then return Farm.pickKite(from, info, radius, true, extra) end
for _, loc in ipairs(Farm.landmarks()) do
local sameSpot = false
for _, n in ipairs(extra or {}) do
if n == loc.name then sameSpot = true end
end
if not sameSpot and not wallBlocked(from, loc.pos)
and (loc.pos - from).Magnitude >= Farm.KITE_MIN then
local score = -(loc.pos - from).Magnitude
if info and info.part then
score = (loc.pos - info.part.Position).Magnitude
end
if Farm.kiteRefused(loc.name) then score = score - Farm.KITE_STALE end
if score > bestScore then best, bestScore = loc, score end
end
end
return best
end
function Farm.rememberKite(name)
Farm.kiteRecent = Farm.kiteRecent or {}
table.insert(Farm.kiteRecent, 1, name)
while #Farm.kiteRecent > Farm.KITE_MEMORY do
table.remove(Farm.kiteRecent)
end
end
function Farm.lookAhead(goal, info, radius)
if not goal then return end
if Farm.kiteNext and Farm.kiteNext.name == goal.name then Farm.kiteNext = nil end
if not Farm.kiteNext then
Farm.kiteNext = Farm.pickKite(goal.pos, info, radius, false, { goal.name })
end
local nxt = Farm.kiteNext
if not nxt then return end
local me = myPos()
if not me then return end
if (me - goal.pos).Magnitude > Farm.KITE_AHEAD then return end
Farm.planAhead(Farm.kiteStand or goal.pos, Farm.standAt(nxt.pos, goal.pos), nxt.name)
end
function Farm.planAhead(from, to, key)
if Farm.aheadFor == key or Farm.aheadBusy then return end
Farm.aheadBusy = true
task.spawn(function()
local ok, points = pcall(function()
local ps = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
local path = ps:CreatePath({
AgentRadius = 4, AgentHeight = 6,
AgentCanJump = Farm.canJump ~= false, WaypointSpacing = 6,
})
path:ComputeAsync(from, to)
if path.Status ~= Enum.PathStatus.Success then return nil end
return path:GetWaypoints()
end)
if ok and points and #points > 1 then
Farm.aheadPoints, Farm.aheadFor = points, key
else
Farm.aheadPoints, Farm.aheadFor = nil, nil
end
Farm.aheadBusy = false
end)
end
function Farm.nextScrap(current, info, radius)
if not (current and current.pos) then return nil end
local from = current.pos
local best, bestScore = nil, -math.huge
for _, s in ipairs(Farm.scraps()) do
if s.model ~= current.model and Farm.banned[s.model] == nil and Farm.wants(s)
and not Farm.blockedNear(s.pos) then
local vetoed = false
if info.active and info.part
and (not Farm.fleeOnlyWhenChased() or info.chasing)
and segDist(from, s.pos, info.part.Position) < radius then
vetoed = true
end
if not vetoed and Farm.pathHasTrap(from, s.pos) then vetoed = true end
if not vetoed then
local score = -(s.pos - from).Magnitude + s.pts * 1.5
if score > bestScore then best, bestScore = s, score end
end
end
end
return best
end
function Farm.adoptPath(goal, points)
Farm.dropPath()
Farm.dest = goal
Farm.points, Farm.point = points, 2
Farm.pathFail = 0
Farm.repathAt = os.clock()
end
function Farm.kite(me, info, radius)
local k = Farm.kiteTo
if k then
local stand = Farm.kiteStand or k.pos
if (me - stand).Magnitude <= Farm.KITE_HOLD then
Farm.rememberKite(k.name)
local nxt = Farm.kiteNext
if nxt and not Farm.kiteScore(me, nxt, info, radius, false, { k.name }) then
nxt = nil
end
nxt = nxt or Farm.pickKite(me, info, radius, false, { k.name })
if nxt and Farm.aheadPoints and Farm.aheadFor == nxt.name then
Farm.pendingAhead = { name = nxt.name, points = Farm.aheadPoints }
end
Farm.kiteTo, Farm.kiteNext = nxt, nil
Farm.kiteStand = nxt and Farm.standAt(nxt.pos, k.pos) or nil
Farm.kiteAt = os.clock()
Farm.kiteTurns = (Farm.kiteTurns or 0) + 1
Farm.aheadPoints, Farm.aheadFor = nil, nil
k = Farm.kiteTo
elseif Farm.destRetired(k.pos)
or (info.part and segDist(
Farm.legStart(me, stand, math.min(radius, Farm.KITE_DROP)),
stand, info.part.Position) < math.min(radius, Farm.KITE_DROP))
or wallBlocked(me, k.pos) then
Farm.kiteTo, Farm.kiteNext, Farm.kiteStand = nil, nil, nil
Farm.aheadPoints, Farm.aheadFor = nil, nil
k = nil
end
end
if not k then
k = Farm.pickKite(me, info, radius)
Farm.kiteTo, Farm.kiteAt = k, os.clock()
Farm.kiteStand = k and Farm.standAt(k.pos, me) or nil
Farm.kiteNext, Farm.pendingAhead = nil, nil
end
if k then Farm.lookAhead(k, info, radius) end
return k
end
function Farm.dropKite()
Farm.kiteTo, Farm.kiteNext, Farm.kiteStand = nil, nil, nil
Farm.aheadPoints, Farm.aheadFor, Farm.pendingAhead = nil, nil, nil
Farm.kiteRecent = nil
end
function Farm.fleeGoal(me, rakePos)
local r = rake()
local rh = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if rh and rh:IsA("BasePart") then
local v = rh.AssemblyLinearVelocity
local flat = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
if flat.Magnitude > 1 then
if flat.Magnitude > 30 then flat = flat.Unit * 30 end
rakePos = rakePos + flat * 0.7
end
end
local house = LOCATIONS[1] and LOCATIONS[1].pos
local hereDist = (me - rakePos).Magnitude
local best, bestScore = nil, -math.huge
for i = 0, 15 do
local a = (i / 16) * math.pi * 2
local goal = me + Vector3.new(math.cos(a), 0, math.sin(a)) * Farm.fleeStep
local score = (goal - rakePos).Magnitude
if score < hereDist then score = score - 1000 end
if Farm.pathHasTrap(me, goal) then score = score - 500 end
score = score + Farm.deadEndPenalty(me, goal)
if not inMap(goal) or wallBlocked(me, goal) then score = score - 100000 end
if house then
local closes = (me - house).Magnitude - (goal - house).Magnitude
score = score + math.clamp(closes, -Farm.fleeStep, Farm.fleeStep) * 0.35
end
if score > bestScore then best, bestScore = goal, score end
end
return best or (me + (me - rakePos).Unit * Farm.fleeStep)
end
Farm.SIDESTEPS = { 18, -18, 32, -32, 46, -46 }
Farm.REPATH = 1.2
Farm.WAYPOINT_HIT = 5
function Farm.clearPoint(me, wp)
local box = Farm.insideNoGo(wp, 0)
if box and Farm.goalBox and box.name == Farm.goalBox.name then box = nil end
if box then
local out = Farm.outsideOf(box, wp, Farm.standoff)
if not Farm.pathHasTrap(me, out) then return out end
return nil
end
if not Farm.pathHasTrap(me, wp) then return wp end
local flat = Vector3.new(wp.X - me.X, 0, wp.Z - me.Z)
if flat.Magnitude < 1 then return nil end
local fwd = flat.Unit
local side = Vector3.new(-fwd.Z, 0, fwd.X)
for _, off in ipairs(Farm.SIDESTEPS) do
local try = wp + side * off
if not Farm.pathHasTrap(me, try) then return try end
end
return nil
end
function Farm.dropPath()
Farm.path, Farm.points, Farm.point = nil, nil, 1
if Farm.blockConn then Farm.blockConn:Disconnect() Farm.blockConn = nil end
end
function Farm.repath(from, to)
if Farm.computing then return false end
Farm.computing = true
local want = to
task.spawn(function()
local ok, path = pcall(function()
local ps = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
local path = ps:CreatePath({
AgentRadius = 4,
AgentHeight = 6,
AgentCanJump = Farm.canJump ~= false,
WaypointSpacing = 6,
})
path:ComputeAsync(from, want)
return path
end)
local list
if ok and path and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
pcall(function() list = path:GetWaypoints() end)
end
if Farm.dest == want then
if list and #list > 1 then
Farm.dropPath()
Farm.path = path
Farm.blockConn = path.Blocked:Connect(function()
Farm.repathAt = 0
end)
Farm.points, Farm.point = list, 2
Farm.pathFail = 0
elseif list then
Farm.dropPath()
else
Farm.pathFail = (Farm.pathFail or 0) + 1
if Farm.pathFail >= 3 then Farm.dropPath() end
end
end
Farm.computing = false
end)
return true
end
function Farm.walkTo(goal, mustMove)
local hum, hrp = getHum(), getHRP()
if not (hum and hrp and goal) then return end
goal = clampToMap(goal, hrp.Position)
Farm.goalBox = Farm.insideNoGo(goal, 0)
local me = hrp.Position
if Farm.dest ~= goal then
Farm.dest = goal
Farm.dropPath()
Farm.repathAt = 0
end
local now = os.clock()
if now - (Farm.repathAt or 0) >= Farm.REPATH then
if Farm.repath(me, goal) then Farm.repathAt = now end
end
if not Farm.points then
if (Farm.pathFail or 0) >= 6 then
if Farm.priority == "Survival" then
Farm.retireDest((Farm.fleeing and Farm.kiteTo and Farm.kiteTo.pos)
or Farm.committed or goal, "no route to it")
elseif Farm.target and Farm.target.model then
Farm.banned[Farm.target.model] = os.clock() + Farm.DEST_RETIRE
Farm.target = nil
end
Farm.pathFail = 0
end
Farm.blocked = (Farm.pathFail or 0) >= 3
Farm.goal = goal
local near = (goal - me).Magnitude <= Farm.CLOSE_ENOUGH
if not (Farm.straightLine or mustMove or near) then
pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(me) end)
return
end
if near then
pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(goal) end)
return
end
local step = Farm.clearPoint(me, goal) or goal
if wallBlocked(me, step) then
local flat = Vector3.new(step.X - me.X, 0, step.Z - me.Z)
if flat.Magnitude > 1 then
local fwd = flat.Unit
local side = Vector3.new(-fwd.Z, 0, fwd.X)
local reach = math.min(flat.Magnitude, 26)
for _, off in ipairs(Farm.SIDESTEPS) do
local try = me + fwd * reach + side * off
if not wallBlocked(me, try) then
step = try
break
end
end
end
end
pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(step) end)
return
end
while Farm.point <= #Farm.points do
local wp = Farm.points[Farm.point]
if (Vector3.new(wp.Position.X, me.Y, wp.Position.Z) - me).Magnitude > Farm.WAYPOINT_HIT then
break
end
if wp.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end) end
Farm.point = Farm.point + 1
end
if Farm.point > #Farm.points then
Farm.blocked = false
Farm.goal = goal
pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(goal) end)
return
end
local wp = Farm.points[Farm.point]
local step = Farm.clearPoint(me, wp.Position)
if step and step ~= wp.Position
and (Vector3.new(step.X, me.Y, step.Z) - me).Magnitude <= Farm.WAYPOINT_HIT then
Farm.point = Farm.point + 1
return
end
if not step then
Farm.blocked = true
Farm.dropPath()
Farm.repathAt = 0
if mustMove then pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(goal) end) end
return
end
Farm.blocked = false
Farm.goal = step
if wp.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end) end
pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(step) end)
end
function Farm.stopWalking()
Farm.goal, Farm.dest, Farm.blocked = nil, nil, false
Farm.dropPath()
Farm.repathAt, Farm.pathFail = 0, 0
local hum, hrp = getHum(), getHRP()
if hum and hrp then pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(hrp.Position) end) end
end
function Farm.touch(part)
local hrp = getHRP()
if not (hrp and part) then return end
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return end
if Farm.touching then return end
Farm.touching = true
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function()
firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 0)
task.wait()
firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 1)
end)
Farm.touching = false
end)
end
function Farm.trackStuck(me)
local now = os.clock()
if not Farm.sampleAt then
Farm.sampleAt, Farm.samplePos = now, me
return
end
if now - Farm.sampleAt < 0.5 then return end
if Farm.state == "collecting" or (Farm.dodgeGoal and now < Farm.dodgeUntil) then
Farm.sampleAt, Farm.samplePos = now, me
return
end
local moved = (me - Farm.samplePos).Magnitude
Farm.sampleAt, Farm.samplePos = now, me
if Farm.goal and moved < 2 then
Farm.stuck = Farm.stuck + 1
else
Farm.stuck = 0
end
if Farm.stuck == 2 and Farm.goal then
Farm.blameHeading(me)
Farm.dropPath()
Farm.repathAt = 0
local hum = getHum()
if hum then pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end) end
end
if Farm.stuck == 3 and Farm.goal then
local d = Farm.goal - me
local flat = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z)
if flat.Magnitude > 0.1 then
local side = Vector3.new(-flat.Unit.Z, 0, flat.Unit.X) * 18
Farm.dodgeGoal, Farm.dodgeUntil = me + side, now + 1.2
end
elseif Farm.stuck >= 6 then
if Farm.target and Farm.target.model then
Farm.banned[Farm.target.model] = now + 12
end
Farm.target, Farm.stuck = nil, 0
end
end
function Farm.think(me)
local info = Farm.readRake()
Farm.readRound()
Farm.roundWhy = nil
Farm.traps = trapModels()
Farm.svCount()
Farm.trackStuck(me)
Farm.macroRecord(me)
local radius = Farm.fleeRadius(info)
local clear = Farm.clearRadius(info)
local worthFleeing = info.active
and (not Farm.fleeOnlyWhenChased() or info.chasing)
if info.chasing then Farm.fleeHoldUntil = os.clock() + Farm.FLEE_GRACE end
if Farm.fleeing and not worthFleeing and info.active and info.part
and info.dist < clear and os.clock() < (Farm.fleeHoldUntil or 0) then
local stand = Farm.kiteStand
if not (stand and (me - stand).Magnitude <= Farm.holdRadius) then
worthFleeing = true
end
end
if worthFleeing then
if info.dist <= radius then
Farm.fleeing = true
elseif info.dist >= clear then
Farm.fleeing = false
end
else
Farm.fleeing = false
end
if Farm.fleeing and info.part then
Farm.target, Farm.targetName, Farm.targetPts = nil, "", 0
Farm.state = "fleeing"
Farm.why = info.chasing and "he is chasing YOU"
or (info.blood and "Blood Hour, he is inside the ring")
or "he is inside the flee ring"
local k = Farm.kite(me, info, radius)
if k then
Farm.targetName = k.name
local goal = Farm.kiteStand or Farm.standAt(k.pos, me)
local ahead = Farm.pendingAhead
if ahead and ahead.name == k.name then
Farm.adoptPath(goal, ahead.points)
Farm.aheadUsed = (Farm.aheadUsed or 0) + 1
end
Farm.pendingAhead = nil
Farm.why = Farm.why .. " - running to " .. k.name
.. (Farm.kiteNext and (", then " .. Farm.kiteNext.name) or "")
Farm.walkTo(goal, true)
else
Farm.targetName = "open ground"
Farm.why = Farm.why .. " - no landmark clear of him"
Farm.walkTo(Farm.fleeGoal(me, info.part.Position), true)
end
return
end
if Farm.kiteTo then Farm.dropKite() end
local inWin = Farm.inWindow()
if inWin ~= Farm.winWas and (Farm.winWas ~= nil or not inWin) then
notify(inWin and "Auto Farm - farming again"
or ("Auto Farm - " .. (Farm.when == "Day Only" and "Day Only, sheltering until morning"
or "Night Only, waiting for night")), 4)
end
Farm.winWas = inWin
local near = (not Farm.macro.rec) and Farm.playerNear(me) or nil
if near then
if Farm.pauseNew then
Farm.pauseNew = false
if os.clock() - (Farm.pauseToldAt or -99) > 20 then
Farm.pauseToldAt = os.clock()
notify("Auto Farm paused - " .. near.name .. " is close", 3)
end
end
Farm.state = "paused"
Farm.why = os.clock() - near.at < 0.5
and string.format("%s is %d studs away", near.name, math.floor(near.dist))
or (near.name .. " was close - going again in a moment")
Farm.stopWalking()
return
end
if Farm.macro.playing then
Farm.target, Farm.targetPts = nil, 0
local goal = Farm.macroStep(me)
if goal then
Farm.state = "macro"
Farm.targetName = string.format("point %d of %d", Farm.macro.i, #Farm.macro.pts)
Farm.why = string.format("%s, %d lap%s",
Farm.macro.dir > 0 and "outbound" or "back",
Farm.macro.laps, Farm.macro.laps == 1 and "" or "s")
Farm.walkTo(goal)
return
end
end
if Farm.macro.rec then
Farm.state = "recording"
Farm.targetName = ""
Farm.why = #Farm.macro.pts .. " points - switch it off to run it"
Farm.stopWalking()
return
end
if Farm.macro.playing and #Farm.macro.pts < 2 then
Farm.macro.playing = false
end
if Farm.dodgeGoal and os.clock() < Farm.dodgeUntil then
Farm.state = "unsticking"
Farm.why = "no progress for " .. Farm.stuck .. " samples"
Farm.walkTo(Farm.dodgeGoal)
return
end
Farm.dodgeGoal = nil
local shelterNow, shelterWhy = Farm.roundSaysShelter()
if Farm.priority == "Survival" or shelterNow then
Farm.target, Farm.targetPts = nil, 0
if shelterNow then Farm.roundWhy = shelterWhy else Farm.roundWhy = nil end
local inHere = Farm.insideNoGo(me, 0)
if inHere then
Farm.state, Farm.why = "walking", "getting out of " .. inHere.name
Farm.targetName = "outside " .. inHere.name
Farm.committed = nil
local out = Farm.outsideOf(inHere, me, Farm.standoff + 10)
Farm.dest, Farm.goal, Farm.blocked = out, out, false
Farm.dropPath()
local hum = getHum()
if hum then
pcall(function() hum:MoveTo(out) end)
if Farm.stuck >= 2 then pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end) end
end
return
end
local goal, name = Farm.shelter(me, info, radius)
if not goal then
Farm.deadDests = nil
goal, name = Farm.shelter(me, info, radius)
end
if not goal then
Farm.state, Farm.why = "idle", "nowhere to shelter"
Farm.stopWalking()
return
end
local held, heldName = Farm.heldDest(Farm.fleeing)
if held then
goal, name = held, heldName
else
Farm.commitTo(goal, name)
end
Farm.targetName = name or ""
local landmark = goal
goal = Farm.standAt(goal, me)
Farm.why = Farm.roundWhy
or (info.active and "keeping away, heading for " .. Farm.targetName)
or ("holding " .. Farm.targetName)
if (me - goal).Magnitude <= Farm.holdRadius then
Farm.state = "holding"
Farm.stuck = 0
Farm.committed = nil
Farm.stopWalking()
else
Farm.state = "walking"
if Farm.stuck >= 4 then
Farm.retireDest(landmark, "cannot get to it from here")
Farm.stuck = 0
Farm.committed = nil
Farm.stopWalking()
return
end
Farm.walkTo(goal)
end
return
end
local best = Farm.pickScrap(me, info, radius)
local keep = Farm.target
if keep then
local still, part = Farm.isRealScrap(keep.model)
if not (still and keep.model.Parent) then
keep = nil
else
keep.part = part
keep.spawn = keep.spawn or keep.model.Parent
keep.pos = Farm.anchorOf(keep.spawn, part)
local barred = Farm.banned[keep.model] ~= nil
or (info.active and info.part
and (info.chasing or not Farm.fleeOnlyWhenChased()
or info.dist < clear)
and segDist(me, keep.pos, info.part.Position) < radius)
or Farm.pathHasTrap(me, keep.pos)
if barred then
keep = nil
elseif best and best.model ~= keep.model then
local a = -(keep.pos - me).Magnitude + keep.pts * 1.5
local b = -(best.pos - me).Magnitude + best.pts * 1.5
if b > a + 25 then keep = nil end
end
end
end
local prev = Farm.target
Farm.target = keep or best
if Farm.target and (not prev or Farm.target.model ~= prev.model)
and Farm.aheadPoints and Farm.aheadFor == Farm.target.model
and Farm.aheadPoints[1] and (Farm.aheadPoints[1].Position - me).Magnitude < 15 then
Farm.adoptPath(Farm.target.pos, Farm.aheadPoints)
Farm.aheadUsed = (Farm.aheadUsed or 0) + 1
Farm.aheadPoints, Farm.aheadFor = nil, nil
end
local tgt = Farm.target
if not tgt then
Farm.state = "idle"
Farm.nextTarget = nil
Farm.targetName, Farm.targetPts = "", 0
if Farm.priority == "Flare Gun" then
Farm.why = "the flare gun is not out"
else
Farm.why = Farm.seen == 0 and "no scrap on the map right now"
or ("all " .. Farm.seen .. " vetoed: too near him, or a trap on the way")
end
Farm.stopWalking()
return
end
Farm.targetName, Farm.targetPts = tgt.name, tgt.pts
Farm.nextTarget = (not tgt.prize) and Farm.nextScrap(tgt, info, radius) or nil
if Farm.nextTarget and (tgt.pos - me).Magnitude <= Farm.AHEAD_AT then
Farm.planAhead(tgt.pos, Farm.nextTarget.pos, Farm.nextTarget.model)
end
if (tgt.pos - me).Magnitude <= Farm.touchDist then
Farm.state = "collecting"
Farm.why = "standing on it"
if not tgt.prize then
if Farm.collectModel ~= tgt.model then
Farm.collectModel, Farm.collectSince = tgt.model, os.clock()
elseif os.clock() - (Farm.collectSince or 0) > 5 then
Farm.banned[tgt.model] = os.clock() + Farm.DEST_RETIRE
Farm.collectModel, Farm.target = nil, nil
return
end
end
Farm.touch(tgt.part)
Farm.walkTo(tgt.pos)
else
Farm.collectModel = nil
Farm.state = "walking"
Farm.why = info.active and string.format("he is %d away", math.floor(info.real))
or info.why
Farm.walkTo(tgt.pos)
end
end
Farm.pathParts = {}
Farm.PATH_WIDTH = 0.7
Farm.PATH_RISE  = 0.18
Farm.PATH_MAX   = 300
function Farm.showRing(centre, radius, colour)
if not (Farm.ringPart and Farm.ringPart.Parent) then
Farm.ringPart = make("Part", {
Name = "RKFarmRing", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Transparency = 0.82,
Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder,
}, Workspace)
end
local p = Farm.ringPart
p.Size = Vector3.new(0.6, radius * 2, radius * 2)
p.CFrame = CFrame.new(centre) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
p.Color = colour
fadeAtEye(p, 0.82, 4)
end
function Farm.hideRing()
if Farm.ringPart then Farm.ringPart.Transparency = 1 end
end
addCleanup("farmvis", function()
if Farm.ringPart then pcall(function() Farm.ringPart:Destroy() end) Farm.ringPart = nil end
end)
function Farm.markRake()
if not (Farm.on and Farm.markRakeOn) then
if Farm.rakeGlow then
pcall(function() Farm.rakeGlow:Destroy() end)
Farm.rakeGlow = nil
end
return
end
local r = rake()
if not r then
if Farm.rakeGlow then Farm.rakeGlow.Adornee = nil end
return
end
if not (Farm.rakeGlow and Farm.rakeGlow.Parent) then
Farm.rakeGlow = make("Highlight", {
Name = "RKFarmRake", FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60),
OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 190),
FillTransparency = 0.6, OutlineTransparency = 0,
DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop,
}, Workspace)
end
if Farm.rakeGlow.Adornee ~= r then Farm.rakeGlow.Adornee = r end
fadeGlow(Farm.rakeGlow, r, 0.6, 22)
end
addCleanup("farmrake", function()
if Farm.rakeGlow then pcall(function() Farm.rakeGlow:Destroy() end) Farm.rakeGlow = nil end
end)
function Farm.hidePath()
for _, p in ipairs(Farm.pathParts) do
pcall(function() p.Transparency = 1 end)
end
end
addCleanup("farmpath", function()
for _, p in ipairs(Farm.pathParts) do pcall(function() p:Destroy() end) end
Farm.pathParts = {}
end)
local function spline(p0, p1, p2, p3, t)
local t2, t3 = t * t, t * t * t
return (p1 * 2
+ (-p0 + p2) * t
+ (p0 * 2 - p1 * 5 + p2 * 4 - p3) * t2
+ (-p0 + p1 * 3 - p2 * 3 + p3) * t3) * 0.5
end
Farm.groundCache, Farm.groundCount = {}, 0
function Farm.onGround(pos)
local kx, ky, kz = math.floor(pos.X * 2), math.floor(pos.Y), math.floor(pos.Z * 2)
local key = ((kx + 8192) * 16384 + (kz + 8192)) * 4096 + (ky + 2048)
local gy = Farm.groundCache[key]
if gy then return Vector3.new(pos.X, gy, pos.Z) end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if not Farm.groundParams then
Farm.groundParams = RaycastParams.new()
Farm.groundParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
end
if Farm.groundChar ~= char then
Farm.groundChar = char
Farm.groundParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
end
local hit
pcall(function()
hit = Workspace:Raycast(pos + Vector3.new(0, 12, 0), Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), Farm.groundParams)
end)
local out = (hit and (hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.35, 0))) or pos
if hit then
if Farm.groundCount > 6000 then Farm.groundCache, Farm.groundCount = {}, 0 end
Farm.groundCache[key] = out.Y
Farm.groundCount = Farm.groundCount + 1
end
return out
end
function Farm.aimingAt()
if Farm.dest then return Farm.dest end
if Farm.committed then return Farm.committed end
if Farm.target and Farm.target.pos then return Farm.target.pos end
return nil
end
function Farm.pathPoints()
local out = {}
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return out end
local m = Farm.macro
if m.rec then
for _, p in ipairs(m.pts) do out[#out + 1] = p end
out[#out + 1] = hrp.Position
return out
end
if m.playing and #m.pts > 1 then
out[1] = hrp.Position
local i = m.i
for _ = 1, math.min(#m.pts, 40) do
local p = m.pts[i]
if not p then break end
out[#out + 1] = p
i = i + m.dir
if i > #m.pts or i < 1 then break end
end
return out
end
out[1] = hrp.Position
if Farm.points then
for i = Farm.point, #Farm.points do
out[#out + 1] = Farm.points[i].Position
end
end
local at = Farm.aimingAt()
if at and (#out < 2 or (out[#out] - at).Magnitude > 3) then
out[#out + 1] = at
end
return out
end
function Farm.pathSeg(i)
local p = Farm.pathParts[i]
if p and p.Parent then return p end
p = make("Part", {
Name = "RKFarmPath", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Transparency = 1,
TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth,
Size = Vector3.new(Farm.PATH_WIDTH, 0.15, 1),
}, Workspace)
Farm.pathParts[i] = p
return p
end
function Farm.drawPath()
local macro = Farm.macro.rec or Farm.macro.playing
if not (Farm.showPath or macro) then
if #Farm.pathParts > 0 then Farm.hidePath() end
Farm.hideBeacon()
return
end
local raw = Farm.pathPoints()
if #raw < 2 then
Farm.hidePath()
Farm.hideBeacon()
return
end
local pts = {}
for i = 1, #raw - 1 do
local p0 = raw[math.max(i - 1, 1)]
local p1, p2 = raw[i], raw[i + 1]
local p3 = raw[math.min(i + 2, #raw)]
local steps = math.clamp(math.floor((p2 - p1).Magnitude / 2) + 4, 6, 16)
for step = 0, steps - 1 do
pts[#pts + 1] = spline(p0, p1, p2, p3, step / steps)
end
end
pts[#pts + 1] = raw[#raw]
local n = #pts
local used, prev = 0, Farm.onGround(pts[1])
for i = 2, n do
if used >= Farm.PATH_MAX then break end
local cur = Farm.onGround(pts[i])
local flat = Vector3.new(cur.X - prev.X, 0, cur.Z - prev.Z)
local len = flat.Magnitude
if len > 0.05 then
used = used + 1
local part = Farm.pathSeg(used)
local mid = Vector3.new(
(prev.X + cur.X) * 0.5,
(prev.Y + cur.Y) * 0.5 + Farm.PATH_RISE,
(prev.Z + cur.Z) * 0.5)
part.Size = Vector3.new(Farm.PATH_WIDTH, 0.15, len + Farm.PATH_WIDTH)
part.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(mid, mid + flat)
local a = i / n
if macro then
part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(120, 20, 20), a)
else
part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 255, 140):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(60, 130, 255), a)
end
fadeAtEye(part, math.clamp(0.25 + a * 0.35, 0, 0.85), 6)
end
prev = cur
end
for i = used + 1, #Farm.pathParts do
pcall(function() Farm.pathParts[i].Transparency = 1 end)
end
local aim = (not macro) and Farm.aimingAt() or nil
if aim then
local at = Farm.onGround(aim)
Farm.beacon(at)
else
Farm.hideBeacon()
end
end
function Farm.beacon(at)
if not (Farm.beaconPart and Farm.beaconPart.Parent) then
Farm.beaconPart = make("Part", {
Name = "RKFarmBeacon", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 90),
Transparency = 0.4, Size = Vector3.new(0.7, 26, 0.7),
}, Workspace)
Farm.beaconRing = make("Part", {
Name = "RKFarmBeaconRing", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 90),
Transparency = 0.55, Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder,
Size = Vector3.new(0.3, 7, 7),
}, Workspace)
local gui = make("BillboardGui", {
Name = "RKFarmBeaconTag", AlwaysOnTop = true,
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(230, 40),
StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 16, 0),
}, Farm.beaconPart)
Farm.beaconText = make("TextLabel", {
BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 15,
TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 226, 140),
TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4, Text = "",
}, gui)
end
Farm.beaconPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(at + Vector3.new(0, 13, 0))
fadeAtEye(Farm.beaconPart, 0.4, 10)
Farm.beaconRing.CFrame = CFrame.new(at + Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0))
* CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
fadeAtEye(Farm.beaconRing, 0.55, 6)
local me = myPos()
local label = Farm.targetName ~= "" and Farm.targetName or "destination"
if me then
local away = (at - me).Magnitude
local rate = Farm.measured or 0
local eta = (rate > 1) and (away / rate) or nil
label = string.format("%s\n%dm%s", label, math.floor(away),
eta and string.format("  -  %ds", math.ceil(eta)) or "")
end
if Farm.beaconText then Farm.beaconText.Text = label end
end
function Farm.hideBeacon()
if Farm.beaconPart then Farm.beaconPart.Transparency = 1 end
if Farm.beaconRing then Farm.beaconRing.Transparency = 1 end
if Farm.beaconText then Farm.beaconText.Text = "" end
end
addCleanup("farmbeacon", function()
if Farm.beaconPart then pcall(function() Farm.beaconPart:Destroy() end) end
if Farm.beaconRing then pcall(function() Farm.beaconRing:Destroy() end) end
Farm.beaconPart, Farm.beaconRing, Farm.beaconText = nil, nil, nil
end)
function Farm.speedFix()
if not Farm.on or Move.speedOn then return end
local hum = getHum()
if not hum then return end
if Farm.baseWalk == nil and hum.WalkSpeed > 0 then
Farm.baseWalk = hum.WalkSpeed
end
local ws = hum.WalkSpeed
if ws <= 0.05 and Farm.speed > 0.05 then return end
if math.abs(ws - Farm.speed) > 0.01 then
pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = Farm.speed end)
end
end
function Farm.bindSpeed()
if Farm.walkConn then Farm.walkConn:Disconnect() Farm.walkConn = nil end
local hum = getHum()
Farm.boundHum = hum
if hum then
Farm.walkConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(Farm.speedFix)
Farm.speedFix()
end
end
function Farm.armSpeed()
if Farm.speedLoop then return end
Farm.speedLoop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not Farm.on then return end
if getHum() ~= Farm.boundHum then
Farm.animOn = false
Farm.bindSpeed()
end
Farm.speedFix()
if os.clock() - (Farm.animLast or 0) >= 0.25 then
Farm.animLast = os.clock()
local hum = getHum()
local fast = (not Move.speedOn) and hum and Farm.baseWalk
and hum.WalkSpeed > Farm.baseWalk + 2 or false
if fast ~= Farm.animOn then
Farm.animOn = fast
Move.setRunAnim(fast)
end
end
if Farm.animOn then Move.holdAnimSpeed() end
end)
Farm.bindSpeed()
addCleanup("farmspeed", function() Farm.disarmSpeed() end)
end
function Farm.disarmSpeed()
if Farm.speedLoop then Farm.speedLoop:Disconnect() Farm.speedLoop = nil end
if Farm.walkConn then Farm.walkConn:Disconnect() Farm.walkConn = nil end
Farm.boundHum = nil
if Farm.animOn then
Farm.animOn = false
Move.setRunAnim(false)
end
Farm.restoreWalk()
end
function Farm.push(dt)
Farm.speedBy = Move.speedOn and "the Walk Speed slider"
or string.format("WalkSpeed %d", math.floor(Farm.speed))
end
function Farm.restoreWalk()
local hum = getHum()
if hum and Farm.baseWalk and not Move.speedOn then
pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = Farm.baseWalk end)
end
Farm.baseWalk = nil
end
addCleanup("farmwalk", function() Farm.restoreWalk() end)
function Farm.standDown(state, why)
local first = Farm.state ~= state or Farm.why ~= why
Farm.state, Farm.why = state, why
if first and LocalPlayer.Character and getHum() then
Farm.stopWalking()
end
Farm.hidePath()
Farm.hideBeacon()
Farm.hideRing()
Farm.markRake()
end
function Farm.set(on)
if not on and Farm.on and Farm.stats.time >= 30 and not Library.Loading and not Library.Unloaded then
local line = Farm.statLine()
if line then notify("Auto Farm off - " .. line, 6) end
end
Farm.on = on
if not on then
Farm.pausedFor, Farm.pauseUntil, Farm.pauseNew, Farm.winWas = nil, nil, nil, nil
if Farm.statConn then Farm.statConn:Disconnect() Farm.statConn = nil end
Farm.statFolder = nil
if Farm.conn then Farm.conn:Disconnect() Farm.conn = nil end
Farm.stopWalking()
Farm.target, Farm.targetName, Farm.targetPts = nil, "", 0
Farm.fleeing, Farm.stuck, Farm.dodgeGoal = false, 0, nil
Farm.sampleAt, Farm.samplePos = nil, nil
Farm.state, Farm.why = "off", ""
Farm.blocked, Farm.touching = false, false
Farm.deadDests, Farm.deadEnds = nil, nil
Farm.committed, Farm.committedName = nil, nil
Farm.dropKite()
Farm.kiteTurns, Farm.aheadUsed = 0, 0
Farm.disarmSpeed()
Farm.hidePath()
Farm.markRake()
Farm.hideRing()
Farm.hideBeacon()
Farm.lastLoot = nil
Farm.rateAt, Farm.ratePos, Farm.measured = 0, nil, 0
Farm.crateTries, Farm.looted, Farm.crateBase = nil, nil, nil
return
end
if Farm.conn then return end
Farm.armSpeed()
Farm.lastPick, Farm.stuck = 0, 0
Farm.sampleAt, Farm.samplePos = nil, nil
Farm.dropKite()
Farm.kiteTurns, Farm.aheadUsed = 0, 0
Farm.rateAt, Farm.ratePos, Farm.measured = 0, nil, 0
if Farm.macroCount() == 0 then Farm.macroLoad() end
Farm.wallHits, Farm.pathFail = 0, 0
Farm.errors, Farm.lastError = 0, nil
Farm.crateTries, Farm.looted, Farm.crateBase = nil, nil, nil
Farm.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
if not Farm.on then return end
local char, hum, hrp = LocalPlayer.Character, getHum(), getHRP()
pcall(Farm.statTick, dt, hum)
if not (char and hum and hrp) then
return Farm.standDown("waiting", "no character")
end
if hum.Health <= 0 then
return Farm.standDown("waiting", "you are dead")
end
local downed = char:FindFirstChild("Downed")
if downed and downed.Value == true then
return Farm.standDown("waiting", "you are downed")
end
if Move.fly then
return Farm.standDown("standing down", "Fly is driving the character")
end
if Free.on then
return Farm.standDown("standing down", "FreeCam owns the character")
end
if Teleport.busy then
return Farm.standDown("standing down", "a teleport is in flight")
end
if Move.noclip then
return Farm.standDown("standing down",
"Noclip has the rig falling through the floor")
end
if hrp.Anchored then
return Farm.standDown("standing down", Ex.hidden
and "Hide has you anchored under the map"
or "the character is anchored")
end
if hum.WalkSpeed <= 0.05 then
if (Farm.speed or 0) <= 0.05 then
return Farm.standDown("holding still", "the Speed slider is at 0")
end
return Farm.standDown("standing down", "something has WalkSpeed at 0")
end
local now = os.clock()
if now - Farm.lastPick >= Farm.PICK_GAP then
Farm.lastPick = now
local ok, err = pcall(Farm.think, hrp.Position)
if not ok then
Farm.lastError = tostring(err)
Farm.errors = (Farm.errors or 0) + 1
if Farm.errors == 1 then
notify("auto farm error: " .. Farm.lastError, 10)
end
end
end
Farm.push(dt)
Farm.drawPath()
Farm.markRake()
if not Farm.ratePos then
Farm.rateAt, Farm.ratePos = os.clock(), hrp.Position
elseif os.clock() - Farm.rateAt >= 0.25 then
local moved = hrp.Position - Farm.ratePos
Farm.measured = Vector3.new(moved.X, 0, moved.Z).Magnitude
/ (os.clock() - Farm.rateAt)
Farm.rateAt, Farm.ratePos = os.clock(), hrp.Position
end
if Farm.showRingOn and Farm.rk then
local r = Farm.fleeRadius(Farm.rk)
Farm.showRing(Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, hrp.Position.Y - 2.6, hrp.Position.Z), r,
Farm.fleeing and Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 170, 255))
else
Farm.hideRing()
end
if Farm.target and Farm.target.part and not Farm.target.part.Parent then
Farm.target, Farm.targetName, Farm.targetPts = nil, "", 0
end
if Farm.lootAny and not Farm.pausedFor and os.clock() - (Farm.walkLootAt or 0) >= 0.4 then
Farm.walkLootAt = os.clock()
local got = Farm.takeLoot(hrp.Position, nil)
if got then Farm.lastLoot = got end
end
if Farm.priority == "Scrap" and not Farm.pausedFor and os.clock() - (Farm.magnetAt or 0) >= 0.2 then
Farm.magnetAt = os.clock()
Farm.pulled = Farm.pullScrap(hrp.Position)
local t = Farm.target
if t and t.prize and t.part and t.part.Parent
and (t.pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= Farm.touchDist + 4 then
if t.prize == "flare" then
if os.clock() - (Farm.prizeAt or 0) >= 0.5 then
Farm.prizeAt = os.clock()
Ex.grabFlare()
end
elseif t.prize == "crate" then
Farm.crateBase = Farm.crateBase or {}
if Farm.crateBase[t.model] == nil then
Farm.crateBase[t.model] = Farm.invCount()
end
if os.clock() - (Farm.prizeAt or 0) >= 0.5 then
Farm.prizeAt = os.clock()
Ex.openCrate(t.model)
Farm.crateTries = Farm.crateTries or {}
Farm.crateTries[t.model] = (Farm.crateTries[t.model] or 0) + 1
end
local got = Farm.takeLoot(t.pos, t.model)
if not got and Farm.invCount() > (Farm.crateBase[t.model] or 0) then
got = "supply item"
end
if got then
Farm.lastLoot = got
Farm.looted = Farm.looted or {}
Farm.looted[t.model] = true
notify("took " .. got .. " - this crate is spent", 4)
Farm.target, Farm.targetName = nil, ""
elseif (Farm.crateTries and Farm.crateTries[t.model] or 0) >= 8 then
Farm.looted = Farm.looted or {}
Farm.looted[t.model] = true
Farm.target, Farm.targetName = nil, ""
notify("nothing in that crate we can use - moving on", 4)
end
end
end
end
end)
addCleanup("autofarm", function() Farm.set(false) end)
end
addCleanup("autofarm_stop", function()
Farm.on = false
if Farm.conn then Farm.conn:Disconnect() Farm.conn = nil end
Farm.stopWalking()
end)
Farm.stats = { time = 0, scrap = 0, points = 0, deaths = 0 }
Farm.STAT_REACH = 14
Farm.statsCard = true
function Farm.statGone(d)
if not (Farm.on and d:IsA("Model")) then return end
local pv = d:FindFirstChild("PointsVal")
if not (pv and d:FindFirstChild("LevelVal")) then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
local ok, at = pcall(function() return d:GetPivot().Position end)
if not ok or (at - hrp.Position).Magnitude > Farm.STAT_REACH then return end
local pts = tonumber(pv.Value) or 0
local spawn = d.Parent
task.defer(function()
if spawn and spawn.Parent then
Farm.stats.scrap = Farm.stats.scrap + 1
Farm.stats.points = Farm.stats.points + pts
end
end)
end
function Farm.statWatch()
local spawns = pathTo(Workspace, "Filter", "ScrapSpawns")
if rawequal(spawns, Farm.statFolder) then return end
if Farm.statConn then Farm.statConn:Disconnect() Farm.statConn = nil end
Farm.statFolder = spawns
if spawns then Farm.statConn = spawns.DescendantRemoving:Connect(Farm.statGone) end
end
function Farm.statTick(dt, hum)
local s = Farm.stats
s.time = s.time + (tonumber(dt) or 0)
if hum then
local dead = hum.Health <= 0
if dead and not Farm.statDead then s.deaths = s.deaths + 1 end
Farm.statDead = dead
end
if os.clock() - (Farm.statAt or 0) >= 1 then
Farm.statAt = os.clock()
Farm.statWatch()
end
end
function Farm.num(n)
n = math.floor((tonumber(n) or 0) + 0.5)
if n >= 10000 then return string.format("%.1fk", n / 1000) end
return tostring(n)
end
function Farm.statValues()
local s = Farm.stats
local t = math.floor(s.time)
local v = {}
v.time = string.format("%d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(t / 3600), math.floor(t % 3600 / 60), t % 60)
v.scrap = string.format("%d  <font color=\"#78808E\">%s pts</font>", s.scrap, Farm.num(s.points))
if s.time >= 60 then
local h = s.time / 3600
v.perHour = math.floor(s.scrap / h + 0.5)
v.rate = string.format("%d  <font color=\"#78808E\">%s pts</font>", v.perHour, Farm.num(s.points / h))
else
v.rate = "-"
end
v.deaths = tostring(s.deaths)
v.state = (not Farm.on) and "off" or (Farm.pausedFor and "paused" or tostring(Farm.state or ""))
return v
end
function Farm.statLine()
local s = Farm.stats
if s.time < 1 and s.scrap == 0 and s.deaths == 0 then return nil end
local v = Farm.statValues()
return string.format("%s farmed, %d scrap (%s pts)%s, %d death%s", v.time, s.scrap, Farm.num(s.points),
v.perHour and (", " .. v.perHour .. " an hour") or "", s.deaths, s.deaths == 1 and "" or "s")
end
function Farm.readout()
if not Farm.on then return "off" end
local out = Farm.state
if Farm.why ~= "" then out = out .. " - " .. Farm.why end
local me = myPos()
if Farm.priority == "Scrap" and Farm.target and me then
out = out .. string.format("\ntarget %s, %d pts, %d studs",
Farm.targetName, Farm.targetPts,
math.floor((Farm.target.pos - me).Magnitude))
if Farm.nextTarget then
out = out .. ", then " .. Farm.nextTarget.name
.. (Farm.aheadFor == Farm.nextTarget.model and " (planned)" or "")
end
elseif Farm.priority == "Survival" and Farm.targetName ~= "" and Farm.dest and me then
out = out .. string.format("\nheading for %s, %d studs",
Farm.targetName, math.floor((Farm.dest - me).Magnitude))
end
if Farm.macro.rec or Farm.macro.playing then
out = out .. string.format("\nmacro: %d points%s",
Farm.macroCount(),
Farm.macro.playing and string.format(", point %d, %d laps",
Farm.macro.i, Farm.macro.laps) or " and recording")
end
if Farm.kiteTo then
out = out .. "\nkiting to " .. Farm.kiteTo.name
.. (Farm.kiteNext and (", then " .. Farm.kiteNext.name) or ", next not chosen yet")
out = out .. string.format("\n%d legs, %d planned ahead%s",
Farm.kiteTurns or 0, Farm.aheadUsed or 0,
Farm.aheadFor and (" | planning " .. tostring(Farm.aheadFor)) or "")
end
if Farm.blocked then
out = out .. "\na trap sits on the way - holding until it clears"
end
local info = Farm.rk
if info.part then
out = out .. string.format("\nRake %d studs, %s%s%s",
math.floor(info.real),
info.active and "hunting" or "not hunting",
info.chasing and ", ON YOU" or "",
info.blood and ", BLOOD HOUR" or "")
else
out = out .. "\nno Rake in the workspace"
end
if Farm.priority == "Scrap" then
out = out .. string.format("\n%d scrap up, %d vetoed", Farm.seen, Farm.vetoed)
end
local st = Farm.round
if st then
out = out .. string.format("\n%s | timer %d%s%s",
st.night and "NIGHT" or "day", math.floor(st.timer),
st.blood and " | BLOOD HOUR" or "",
(st.power or 100) <= 0 and " | power out" or "")
end
if Farm.lastLoot then out = out .. "\nlast pickup: " .. Farm.lastLoot end
out = out .. "\nspeed by " .. (Farm.speedBy or "nothing")
if Farm.points then
out = out .. string.format("\npath: waypoint %d of %d", Farm.point, #Farm.points)
elseif (Farm.pathFail or 0) > 0 then
out = out .. "\nno path - steering straight at it"
end
if (Farm.errors or 0) > 0 then
out = out .. string.format("\n%d ERRORS, last: %s",
Farm.errors, tostring(Farm.lastError))
end
out = out .. string.format("\nborder: %d barriers, %d lines refused this run",
Farm.wallCount or 0, Farm.wallHits or 0)
out = out .. "\nmagnet: " .. (Farm.magnet
and ("on - " .. (Farm.magnetWhy or "not yet proven"))
or ("off - " .. (Farm.magnetWhy or "off")))
out = out .. string.format("\nSValues %d/%d set%s", Farm.svNonNil, Farm.svTotal,
Farm.svNonNil == 0 and " (as dumped: not the live list)" or " - worth a look")
return out
end
local Misc = {
noMotionBlur = false, noShadows = false, noDistanceBlur = false, noRakeSound = false, rakeNotify = false,
muteMusic = false, muteChase = false,
forceChat = false, nametags = false, sixthSense = false,
hideDeathMsg = false, blockPopups = false, forcePc = false,
freezeLook = false,
saved = {}, status = "off", last = 0,
}
Misc.FPS_CAPS = { "60", "120", "144", "165", "240", "360", "Unlimited" }
Misc.fpsCap = "240"
function Misc.setFps(on)
Misc.fpsOn = on and true or false
if typeof(setfpscap) ~= "function" then
if on and not Library.Loading then
notify("this executor has no setfpscap - the FPS Unlocker cannot work here", 5)
end
return
end
if on then
if Misc.fpsWas == nil then
local ok, v = pcall(function()
local g = getgenv().getfpscap
return type(g) == "function" and g() or nil
end)
v = ok and tonumber(v) or nil
if v == nil or v ~= v then v = 60 elseif v == math.huge then v = 0 end
Misc.fpsWas = v
end
pcall(setfpscap, Misc.fpsCap == "Unlimited" and 0 or (tonumber(Misc.fpsCap) or 240))
elseif Misc.fpsWas ~= nil then
pcall(setfpscap, Misc.fpsWas)
Misc.fpsWas = nil
end
end
addCleanup("fpscap", function() if Misc.fpsOn then Misc.setFps(false) end end)
function Misc.gsettings()
local gs = getScriptGlobal("GSettings")
if type(gs) ~= "table" then
gs = {}
setScriptGlobal("GSettings", gs)
end
return gs
end
function Misc.remember(key, value)
if Misc.saved[key] == nil then Misc.saved[key] = value end
return Misc.saved[key]
end
function Misc.setGS(key, wanted, default)
local gs = Misc.gsettings()
Misc.gsNil = Misc.gsNil or {}
if Misc.saved["gs:" .. key] == nil then
local orig = gs[key]
Misc.saved["gs:" .. key] = (orig == nil) and Misc.gsNil or orig
end
gs[key] = wanted
setScriptGlobal("GSettings", gs)
pcall(function()
local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SettingsChangedEvent")
if ev and ev.Fire then ev:Fire(key, wanted) end
end)
end
function Misc.skipIntro()
setScriptGlobal("IsLoading", nil)
setScriptGlobal("SLoaded", true)
pcall(function() LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("Started", true) end)
local gui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
local removed = 0
if gui then
for _, g in ipairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
if g:IsA("ScreenGui")
and (g.Name == "IntroGUI" or (g:FindFirstChild("LoadingFrame") and g:FindFirstChild("MenuFrame"))) then
pcall(function() g:Destroy() end)
removed = removed + 1
end
end
end
pcall(function()
local died = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DiedEvent")
if died and died.Fire then died:Fire(false, true) end
end)
local starter = game:GetService("StarterGui")
pcall(function() starter:SetCore("TopbarEnabled", true) end)
for _, kind in ipairs({ Enum.CoreGuiType.Health, Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat }) do
pcall(function() starter:SetCoreGuiEnabled(kind, true) end)
end
notify(removed > 0 and ("skipped the intro (" .. removed .. " screens)") or "nothing to skip", 4)
end
function Misc.soundGroup(name)
local ss = game:GetService("SoundService")
local g = ss and ss:FindFirstChild(name)
return (g and (g:IsA("SoundGroup") or g:IsA("Sound"))) and g or nil
end
function Misc.setMute(name, key, on)
local g = Misc.soundGroup(name)
if not g then return end
local was = Misc.remember("vol:" .. name, g.Volume)
pcall(function() g.Volume = on and 0 or (was > 0 and was or 1) end)
end
function Misc.setUiMute(name, on, store)
local ui = realUI()
local snd = ui and ui:FindFirstChild(name)
if not (snd and snd:IsA("Sound")) then return false end
store[snd] = store[snd] == nil and snd.Volume or store[snd]
if on then
pcall(function()
snd.Volume = 0
snd:Stop()
end)
else
pcall(function() snd.Volume = store[snd] or 0.5 end)
end
return true
end
function Misc.setNoChase(on)
Misc.noChase = on
Misc.chaseSaved = Misc.chaseSaved or {}
Misc.setMute("ChaseMusic", "ChaseMusic", on)
Misc.setUiMute("Chase", on, Misc.chaseSaved)
if not on then
if Misc.chaseConn then Misc.chaseConn:Disconnect() Misc.chaseConn = nil end
return
end
if Misc.chaseConn then return end
Misc.chaseConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Misc.noChase then return end
if os.clock() - (Misc.chaseLast or 0) < 0.5 then return end
Misc.chaseLast = os.clock()
Misc.setUiMute("Chase", true, Misc.chaseSaved)
Misc.setMute("ChaseMusic", "ChaseMusic", true)
end)
addCleanup("nochase", function() Misc.setNoChase(false) end)
end
function Misc.sixthSenseMarker(on)
local marker = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("SixthSenseGamepass")
if on then
if not marker then
marker = make("BoolValue", { Name = "SixthSenseGamepass", Value = true }, LocalPlayer)
marker:SetAttribute("RKMarker", true)
end
pcall(function() marker.Value = true end)
elseif marker and marker:GetAttribute("RKMarker") == true then
pcall(function() marker:Destroy() end)
end
end
function Misc.enforce()
if Misc.conn then return end
Misc.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if os.clock() - Misc.last < 0.5 then return end
Misc.last = os.clock()
local gui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if Misc.noMotionBlur then
local c = cam()
if c then
for _, fx in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
if (fx:IsA("BlurEffect") and (fx.Name == "MotionBlur" or fx.Name == "ESCB"))
or (fx:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") and fx.Name == "ESCC") then
pcall(function() fx.Enabled = false end)
end
end
end
end
if Misc.noDistanceBlur then
for _, fx in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
if fx:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") and fx.Enabled then pcall(function() fx.Enabled = false end) end
end
local c = cam()
if c then
for _, fx in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
if fx:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") and fx.Enabled then pcall(function() fx.Enabled = false end) end
end
end
end
if Misc.noRakeSound then
local r = rake()
if r then
Misc.rakeSounds = Misc.rakeSounds or setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Sound") and d.Volume > 0 then
if Misc.rakeSounds[d] == nil then Misc.rakeSounds[d] = d.Volume end
pcall(function() d.Volume = 0 end)
end
end
end
end
if Misc.rakeNotify then
local r = rake()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local alive = (hum and Fight.rakeHp(hum) > 0) and true or false
if Misc.rakeWas ~= nil then
if alive and not Misc.rakeWas then
notify("THE RAKE HAS SPAWNED", 5)
if Round.alert then pcall(Round.alert, "alarm") end
elseif not alive and Misc.rakeWas then
notify("THE RAKE HAS DIED", 5)
if Round.alert then pcall(Round.alert, "fall") end
end
end
Misc.rakeWas = alive
end
if Misc.noShadows and not Rtx.on and Lighting.GlobalShadows then
pcall(function() Lighting.GlobalShadows = false end)
end
if Misc.hideDeathMsg and gui then
for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
if d.Name == "DeathMsg" and d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.Visible then
d.Visible = false
end
end
end
if Misc.blockPopups and gui then
for _, g in ipairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
if g:IsA("ScreenGui") and (g.Name == "_fav" or g.Name == "_fav2") then
pcall(function() g:Destroy() end)
end
end
end
if Misc.sixthSense then Misc.sixthSenseMarker(true) end
if Misc.freezeLook then
pcall(function()
local remote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SetLookAngles")
if remote and remote.FireServer then remote:FireServer(0, 0) end
end)
end
end)
addCleanup("miscloop", function()
if Misc.conn then Misc.conn:Disconnect() Misc.conn = nil end
end)
end
function Misc.set(field, value, gsKey)
if field == "noShadows" and value then
pcall(function()
if Rtx.on and Toggles.RK_Rtx and Toggles.RK_Rtx.Value then
Toggles.RK_Rtx:SetValue(false)
notify("RTX Shaders switched off - No Shadows is on", 4)
end
end)
end
Misc[field] = value
Misc.enforce()
if gsKey then
if value then
Misc.setGS(gsKey, field == "forceChat" or field == "nametags" or field == "sixthSense")
else
local orig = Misc.saved["gs:" .. gsKey]
if orig ~= nil then
Misc.saved["gs:" .. gsKey] = nil
local gs = Misc.gsettings()
if orig == Misc.gsNil then gs[gsKey] = nil else gs[gsKey] = orig end
setScriptGlobal("GSettings", gs)
pcall(function()
local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SettingsChangedEvent")
if ev and ev.Fire then ev:Fire(gsKey, gs[gsKey]) end
end)
end
end
end
if field == "muteMusic" then Misc.setMute("GameMusic", "GameMusic", value) end
if field == "muteChase" then Misc.setMute("ChaseMusic", "ChaseMusic", value) end
if field == "sixthSense" and not value then Misc.sixthSenseMarker(false) end
if field == "forceChat" and value then
pcall(function()
game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, true)
end)
end
if field == "noShadows" and not value then
Misc.restoreShadows()
elseif field == "noShadows" and value then
Misc.remember("shadows", Lighting.GlobalShadows)
end
if field == "noRakeSound" and not value then
for d, vol in pairs(Misc.rakeSounds or {}) do
if d and d.Parent then pcall(function() d.Volume = vol end) end
end
Misc.rakeSounds = nil
end
end
function Misc.setForcePc(on)
Misc.forcePc = on
if on then
Misc.remember("device", getScriptGlobal("deviceType"))
setScriptGlobal("deviceType", "PC")
setScriptGlobal("inputType", "PC")
else
local was = Misc.saved["device"]
if was ~= nil then
setScriptGlobal("deviceType", was)
setScriptGlobal("inputType", was)
end
end
pcall(function()
local ev = getScriptGlobal("InputTypeChangeEvent")
if ev and ev.Fire then ev:Fire(on and "PC" or Misc.saved["device"]) end
end)
end
function Misc.restoreShadows()
local was = Misc.saved["shadows"]
if was == nil then return end
Misc.saved["shadows"] = nil
if Sky.fullbright then
if Sky.savedShadows == false then Sky.savedShadows = was end
return
end
pcall(function() Lighting.GlobalShadows = was end)
end
addCleanup("misc", function()
Misc.setMute("GameMusic", "GameMusic", false)
Misc.setMute("ChaseMusic", "ChaseMusic", false)
Misc.sixthSenseMarker(false)
if Misc.forcePc then Misc.setForcePc(false) end
Misc.restoreShadows()
end)
local Scare = {
noScreen = false, noHeartbeat = false, noScream = false, screams = {},
beatGuards = {},
static = { on = false, guards = {}, muted = {}, conn = nil, last = 0 },
stickSound = "", overlays = {}, muted = {}, sticks = {},
}
Scare.OVERLAYS = {
BloodShot = true, BloodShot2 = true, GrainImage = true, GrainImage2 = true,
DefaultOverlay = true, DefaultOverlay2 = true,
}
function Scare.setNoScreen(on)
Scare.noScreen = on
if not on then
for obj, was in pairs(Scare.overlays) do
if obj and obj.Parent then
pcall(function()
if obj:IsA("GuiObject") then obj.Visible = was else obj.Enabled = was end
end)
end
end
Scare.overlays = {}
if Scare.screenConn then Scare.screenConn:Disconnect() Scare.screenConn = nil end
return
end
if Scare.screenConn then return end
Scare.screenConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not Scare.noScreen then return end
local ui = realUI()
if ui then
for _, d in ipairs(ui:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("ImageLabel") and Scare.OVERLAYS[d.Name] and d.Visible then
if Scare.overlays[d] == nil then Scare.overlays[d] = true end
d.Visible = false
end
end
end
local c = cam()
if c then
for _, fx in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
if Comfort.isScareFx(fx) and fx.Enabled then
if Scare.overlays[fx] == nil then Scare.overlays[fx] = true end
fx.Enabled = false
end
end
end
end)
addCleanup("noscreen", function() Scare.setNoScreen(false) end)
end
function Scare.setNoScream(on)
Scare.noScream = on
if not on then
for snd, vol in pairs(Scare.screams or {}) do
if snd and snd.Parent then pcall(function() snd.Volume = vol end) end
end
Scare.screams = {}
if Scare.screamConn then Scare.screamConn:Disconnect() Scare.screamConn = nil end
return
end
Scare.screams = Scare.screams or {}
if Scare.screamConn then return end
Scare.screamConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Scare.noScream then return end
if os.clock() - (Scare.screamLast or 0) < 0.4 then return end
Scare.screamLast = os.clock()
local r = rake()
if not r then return end
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Sound") and string.find(string.lower(d.Name), "scream", 1, true) then
if Scare.screams[d] == nil then Scare.screams[d] = d.Volume end
if d.Volume > 0 then d.Volume = 0 end
if d.Playing then d.Playing = false end
end
end
end)
addCleanup("noscream", function() Scare.setNoScream(false) end)
end
function Scare.holdUi(state, snd)
if not snd or state.guards[snd] then return end
state.guards[snd] = {
snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Volume"):Connect(function()
if state.on and snd.Volume > 0 then snd.Volume = 0 end
end),
snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Playing"):Connect(function()
if state.on and snd.Playing then
snd.Playing = false
snd.TimePosition = 0
end
end),
snd.Played:Connect(function()
if state.on then pcall(function() snd:Stop() end) end
end),
}
end
function Scare.uiSound(name)
local ui = realUI()
local snd = ui and ui:FindFirstChild(name)
return (snd and snd:IsA("Sound")) and snd or nil
end
function Scare.setNoStatic(on)
local state = Scare.static
state.on = on
if not on then
for snd, conns in pairs(state.guards) do
for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
local was = state.muted[snd]
if was and snd and snd.Parent then
pcall(function() snd.Volume = was end)
end
end
state.guards, state.muted = {}, {}
if state.conn then state.conn:Disconnect() state.conn = nil end
return
end
local function grab()
local snd = Scare.uiSound("Static")
if not snd then return false end
if state.muted[snd] == nil then state.muted[snd] = snd.Volume end
pcall(function()
snd.Volume = 0
snd:Stop()
end)
Scare.holdUi(state, snd)
return true
end
if not grab() then
notify("no sound called Static in the UI yet - it will latch when the "
.. "round UI builds", 5)
end
if state.conn then return end
state.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not state.on then return end
if os.clock() - (state.last or 0) < 1 then return end
state.last = os.clock()
grab()
end)
addCleanup("nostatic", function() Scare.setNoStatic(false) end)
end
function Scare.beatSound()
local ui = realUI()
local snd = ui and ui:FindFirstChild("Heartbeat")
return (snd and snd:IsA("Sound")) and snd or nil
end
function Scare.holdBeat(snd)
if not snd or Scare.beatGuards[snd] then return end
Scare.beatGuards[snd] = {
snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Volume"):Connect(function()
if Scare.noHeartbeat and snd.Volume > 0 then snd.Volume = 0 end
end),
snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Playing"):Connect(function()
if Scare.noHeartbeat and snd.Playing then
snd.Playing = false
snd.TimePosition = 0
end
end),
snd.Played:Connect(function()
if Scare.noHeartbeat then pcall(function() snd:Stop() end) end
end),
}
end
function Scare.setNoHeartbeat(on)
Scare.noHeartbeat = on
Scare.beatGuards = Scare.beatGuards or {}
if not on then
for snd, conns in pairs(Scare.beatGuards) do
for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
if snd and snd.Parent then
local was = Scare.muted[snd]
if was then pcall(function() snd.Volume = was end) end
end
end
Scare.beatGuards, Scare.muted = {}, {}
if Scare.beatConn then Scare.beatConn:Disconnect() Scare.beatConn = nil end
return
end
local snd = Scare.beatSound()
if snd then
if Scare.muted[snd] == nil then Scare.muted[snd] = snd.Volume end
pcall(function()
snd.Volume = 0
snd:Stop()
end)
Scare.holdBeat(snd)
end
if Scare.beatConn then return end
Scare.beatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Scare.noHeartbeat then return end
if os.clock() - (Scare.beatLast or 0) < 1 then return end
Scare.beatLast = os.clock()
local live = Scare.beatSound()
if live and not Scare.beatGuards[live] then
if Scare.muted[live] == nil then Scare.muted[live] = live.Volume end
pcall(function() live.Volume = 0 live:Stop() end)
Scare.holdBeat(live)
end
end)
addCleanup("noheartbeat", function() Scare.setNoHeartbeat(false) end)
end
function Scare.setStickSound(id)
id = tostring(id or ""):gsub("%s", "")
if id ~= "" and not id:find("rbxassetid") then
id = "rbxassetid://" .. id:gsub("%D", "")
end
Scare.stickSound = id
local tool = Fight.stick()
if not tool then
notify("equip the stun stick first", 4)
return
end
local n = 0
for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Sound") then
if Scare.sticks[d] == nil then Scare.sticks[d] = d.SoundId end
pcall(function() d.SoundId = (id ~= "" and id) or Scare.sticks[d] end)
n = n + 1
end
end
notify(id == "" and ("restored " .. n .. " sounds") or ("replaced " .. n .. " sounds"), 4)
end
addCleanup("sticksound", function()
for snd, was in pairs(Scare.sticks) do
if snd and snd.Parent then pcall(function() snd.SoundId = was end) end
end
end)
local Dev = {
console = "", consoleOut = "", dumping = false, fullLimit = 250000,
}
local function shortPath(inst)
local ok, full = pcall(function() return inst:GetFullName() end)
return ok and full or tostring(inst)
end
local function describe(v, depth)
depth = depth or 0
local t = typeof(v)
if t == "Instance" then return "<" .. v.ClassName .. "> " .. shortPath(v) end
if t == "table" then
if depth > 2 then return "{...}" end
local bits = {}
for k, item in pairs(v) do
bits[#bits + 1] = tostring(k) .. " = " .. describe(item, depth + 1)
if #bits > 12 then bits[#bits + 1] = "..." break end
end
return "{ " .. table.concat(bits, ", ") .. " }"
end
if t == "Vector3" then return string.format("Vector3(%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", v.X, v.Y, v.Z) end
if t == "CFrame" then
local p = v.Position
return string.format("CFrame(%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", p.X, p.Y, p.Z)
end
if t == "string" then return string.format("%q", v) end
return t .. "(" .. tostring(v) .. ")"
end
function Dev.dumpRemotes()
local lines = { "-- every remote the client can see", "" }
local seen = 0
local function walk(root, label)
local ok, list = pcall(function() return root:GetDescendants() end)
if not ok then return end
local mine = {}
for _, d in ipairs(list) do
if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") or d:IsA("RemoteFunction")
or d:IsA("UnreliableRemoteEvent") or d:IsA("BindableEvent")
or d:IsA("BindableFunction") then
mine[#mine + 1] = string.format("  [%s] %s", d.ClassName, shortPath(d))
end
end
table.sort(mine)
if #mine > 0 then
lines[#lines + 1] = label .. "  (" .. #mine .. ")"
for _, l in ipairs(mine) do lines[#lines + 1] = l end
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
seen = seen + #mine
end
end
walk(ReplicatedStorage, "ReplicatedStorage")
walk(Workspace, "Workspace")
walk(LocalPlayer, "Player")
walk(game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst"), "ReplicatedFirst")
lines[#lines + 1] = "total " .. seen
saveDump("remotes.txt", table.concat(lines, "\n"))
end
Dev.CONFIG_MARKERS = {
"STAMINA", "NVG", "SPRINT", "WALK", "RUN", "SPEED", "JUMP", "COOLDOWN",
"DAMAGE", "MALFUNCTION", "VITAMIN", "BOOST", "TAKE", "REGEN", "STUN",
"TRAP", "SHOCK", "HEALTH", "REACH",
}
function Dev.looksLikeConfig(key)
if type(key) ~= "string" then return false end
local up = string.upper(key)
for _, m in ipairs(Dev.CONFIG_MARKERS) do
if up:find(m, 1, true) then return true end
end
return false
end
function Dev.dumpConfigs()
if typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then
notify("this executor has no getgc, so there is nothing to walk", 6)
return
end
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok or type(all) ~= "table" then
notify("getgc was refused", 6)
return
end
local lines = {
"-- every gameplay config table the heap will show us",
"-- a table is listed when any key matches: " .. table.concat(Dev.CONFIG_MARKERS, " "),
"",
}
local seen, tables, keys = {}, 0, 0
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" and not seen[v] then
seen[v] = true
local hit, rows = false, {}
pcall(function()
for key, value in pairs(v) do
local kind = type(value)
if kind == "number" or kind == "boolean" or kind == "string" then
rows[#rows + 1] = string.format("    %-28s %-8s %s",
tostring(key), kind, tostring(value))
if Dev.looksLikeConfig(key) then hit = true end
end
end
end)
if hit and #rows > 0 then
tables = tables + 1
keys = keys + #rows
table.sort(rows)
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("table #%d  (%d readable keys)", tables, #rows)
for _, row in ipairs(rows) do lines[#lines + 1] = row end
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
end
end
end
if tables == 0 then
notify("no config tables on the heap yet - walk around, sprint, and try again", 7)
return
end
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("-- %d tables, %d keys", tables, keys)
saveDump("configs.txt", table.concat(lines, "\n"))
end
function Dev.dumpNumbers()
local lines = { "-- every number the game keeps on you", "" }
local function scan(holder, label)
if not holder then return end
local ok, attrs = pcall(function() return holder:GetAttributes() end)
if ok and attrs then
for k, v in pairs(attrs) do
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("%-12s attr   %-24s = %s", label, k, tostring(v))
end
end
for _, child in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("ValueBase") then
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("%-12s %-6s %-24s = %s",
label, child.ClassName:gsub("Value", ""), child.Name, tostring(child.Value))
end
end
end
scan(LocalPlayer, "Player")
scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats"), "leaderstats")
scan(LocalPlayer.Character, "Character")
scan(getHum(), "Humanoid")
scan(ReplicatedStorage, "ReplicatedStorage")
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
lines[#lines + 1] = "-- the tables getgc can see, which is where stamina lives"
local t = Stam.find("STAMINA_REGEN") or Stam.find("stamina")
if t then
local keys = {}
for k, v in pairs(t) do
if type(v) ~= "table" and type(v) ~= "function" then
keys[#keys + 1] = string.format("  %-20s = %s", tostring(k), tostring(v))
end
end
table.sort(keys)
for _, l in ipairs(keys) do lines[#lines + 1] = l end
else
lines[#lines + 1] = "  not found - move around once, then dump again"
end
local hum = getHum()
if hum then
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("Humanoid  hp %.1f/%.1f  walk %.1f  jump %.1f  rig %s",
hum.Health, hum.MaxHealth, hum.WalkSpeed, hum.JumpPower, hum.RigType.Name)
end
saveDump("numbers.txt", table.concat(lines, "\n"))
end
local Rec = { on = false, t0 = 0, lines = {}, conns = {}, max = 6000, hp = nil }
local function recVal(v, depth)
depth = depth or 0
local t = typeof(v)
if t == "Instance" then
local ok, n = pcall(function() return v:GetFullName() end)
return ok and n or "<instance>"
end
if t == "Vector3" then return string.format("(%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", v.X, v.Y, v.Z) end
if t == "CFrame" then
local p = v.Position
return string.format("CF(%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", p.X, p.Y, p.Z)
end
if t == "table" and depth < 2 then
local parts = {}
for k, x in pairs(v) do
parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(k) .. "=" .. recVal(x, depth + 1)
if #parts >= 8 then parts[#parts + 1] = "..." break end
end
return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. "}"
end
if t == "string" then return string.format("%q", v) end
return tostring(v)
end
function Rec.log(fmt, ...)
if not Rec.on then return end
if #Rec.lines >= Rec.max then return end
local ok, text = pcall(string.format, fmt, ...)
Rec.lines[#Rec.lines + 1] = string.format("%8.3f  %s",
os.clock() - Rec.t0, ok and text or tostring(fmt))
end
function Rec.hold(conn)
if conn then Rec.conns[#Rec.conns + 1] = conn end
end
function Rec.watchValue(v, label)
if not v:IsA("ValueBase") then return end
Rec.log("watch  %s = %s", label, recVal(v.Value))
Rec.hold(v.Changed:Connect(function(new)
Rec.log("VALUE  %s = %s", label, recVal(new))
end))
end
function Rec.watchAttributes(inst, label)
local ok, attrs = pcall(function() return inst:GetAttributes() end)
if ok and attrs then
for k, val in pairs(attrs) do Rec.log("attr   %s @%s = %s", label, k, recVal(val)) end
end
Rec.hold(inst.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(name)
local got, val = pcall(function() return inst:GetAttribute(name) end)
Rec.log("ATTR   %s @%s = %s", label, name, got and recVal(val) or "?")
end))
end
function Rec.watchRake(r)
r = r or rake()
if not r then return false end
if Rec.rakeOn == r then return true end
Rec.rakeOn = r
Rec.log("note   Rake attached: %s", r:GetFullName())
Rec.watchAttributes(r, "Rake")
local hum = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then
Rec.hp = hum.Health
Rec.log("note   Monster %0.1f / %0.1f, walk %0.1f", hum.Health, hum.MaxHealth, hum.WalkSpeed)
Rec.watchAttributes(hum, "Monster")
Rec.hold(hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(h)
local was = Rec.hp or h
Rec.hp = h
Rec.log("HEALTH %0.1f  (%+0.1f)", h, h - was)
end))
Rec.hold(hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(old, new)
Rec.log("STATE  %s -> %s", tostring(old), tostring(new))
end))
Rec.hold(hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
Rec.log("WALK   Monster WalkSpeed = %0.1f", hum.WalkSpeed)
end))
end
for _, c in ipairs(r:GetChildren()) do
if c:IsA("ValueBase") then Rec.watchValue(c, "Rake." .. c.Name) end
end
local hrp = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") then
Rec.hold(hrp:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
Rec.log("ANCHOR Rake.HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = %s", tostring(hrp.Anchored))
end))
end
Rec.hold(r.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
Rec.log("ADD    Rake + [%s] %s", d.ClassName, d.Name)
if d:IsA("ValueBase") and d.Parent == r then Rec.watchValue(d, "Rake." .. d.Name) end
end))
Rec.hold(r.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(d)
Rec.log("GONE   Rake - [%s] %s", d.ClassName, d.Name)
end))
Rec.hold(r.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
Rec.log("RAKE   ancestry -> %s", parent and parent:GetFullName() or "nil (DESTROYED)")
end))
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Sound") then
Rec.hold(d.Played:Connect(function() Rec.log("SOUND  Rake %s", d.Name) end))
end
end
return true
end
function Rec.watchTrap(m, label)
if Rec.seenTrap[m] then return end
Rec.seenTrap[m] = true
Rec.watchAttributes(m, label)
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
if box and box:IsA("BasePart") then
Rec.hold(box.Touched:Connect(function(hit)
Rec.log("TOUCH  %s hitbox <- %s", label, hit and hit:GetFullName() or "?")
end))
Rec.hold(box.TouchEnded:Connect(function(hit)
Rec.log("UNTCH  %s hitbox <- %s", label, hit and hit.Name or "?")
end))
end
for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Sound") then
Rec.hold(d.Played:Connect(function() Rec.log("SOUND  %s %s", label, d.Name) end))
elseif d:IsA("ValueBase") then
Rec.watchValue(d, label .. "." .. d.Name)
end
end
Rec.hold(m.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
Rec.log("TRAP   %s ancestry -> %s", label,
parent and parent:GetFullName() or "nil (DESTROYED)")
end))
end
function Rec.watchTraps()
Rec.trapN = 0
for _, m in ipairs(Ex.allTraps()) do
Rec.trapN = Rec.trapN + 1
Rec.watchTrap(m, "trap" .. Rec.trapN)
end
Rec.log("note   %d trap%s down at arm time", Rec.trapN, Rec.trapN == 1 and "" or "s")
local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
local traps = debris and debris:FindFirstChild("Traps")
if traps then
Rec.hold(traps.ChildAdded:Connect(function(m)
Rec.trapN = Rec.trapN + 1
local label = "trap" .. Rec.trapN
Rec.log("TRAP   + %s placed -> %s", m.Name, label)
task.spawn(function() Rec.watchTrap(m, label) end)
end))
Rec.hold(traps.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(m)
Rec.log("TRAP   - %s gone", m.Name)
end))
end
end
function Rec.watchRemotes()
local n = 0
for _, d in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") or d:IsA("UnreliableRemoteEvent") then
n = n + 1
local name = d.Name
Rec.hold(d.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
local parts = {}
local a = table.pack(...) for i = 1, a.n do parts[i] = recVal(a[i]) end
Rec.log("S->C   %s(%s)", name, table.concat(parts, ", "))
end))
elseif d:IsA("BindableEvent") then
local name = d.Name
Rec.hold(d.Event:Connect(function(...)
local parts = {}
local a = table.pack(...) for i = 1, a.n do parts[i] = recVal(a[i]) end
Rec.log("BIND   %s(%s)", name, table.concat(parts, ", "))
end))
end
end
Rec.log("note   %d remote events watched", n)
end
function Rec.watchMe()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char then
Rec.hold(char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
if d:IsA("Tool") or d:IsA("ValueBase") then
Rec.log("ME     + [%s] %s", d.ClassName, d.Name)
end
end))
Rec.hold(char.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(d)
if d:IsA("Tool") then Rec.log("ME     - Tool %s", d.Name) end
end))
end
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if bp then
Rec.hold(bp.ChildAdded:Connect(function(d) Rec.log("BAG    + %s", d.Name) end))
Rec.hold(bp.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(d) Rec.log("BAG    - %s", d.Name) end))
end
if not Rec.watchMeBound then
Rec.watchMeBound = true
Rec.hold(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() Rec.watchMe() end))
end
end
function Rec.arm()
if Rec.on then
notify("already recording - stop it first", 4)
return
end
Rec.on, Rec.t0, Rec.lines, Rec.conns = true, os.clock(), {}, {}
Rec.seenTrap, Rec.rakeOn, Rec.trapN = {}, nil, 0
Rec.watchMeBound = false
Rec.log("=== armed ===")
local who = "?"
pcall(function()
if typeof(identifyexecutor) == "function" then who = tostring((identifyexecutor())) end
end)
Rec.log("note   executor: %s", who)
if not Rec.watchRake() then
Rec.log("note   no Rake yet - waiting for him to spawn")
end
Rec.watchTraps()
Rec.watchRemotes()
Rec.watchMe()
Rec.log("=== recording. Now do the thing. ===")
Rec.hold(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Rec.on then return end
local r = rake()
if r and Rec.rakeOn ~= r then
Rec.hp = nil
Rec.watchRake(r)
end
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return end
if Rec.hp == nil then Rec.hp = hum.Health end
if Rec.hp and math.abs(hum.Health - Rec.hp) > 0.01 then
local was = Rec.hp
Rec.hp = hum.Health
Rec.log("HEALTH %0.1f  (%+0.1f)  [sampled]", hum.Health, hum.Health - was)
end
end))
notify("recording - run the kill now, then press Stop", 8)
end
function Rec.disarm()
if not Rec.on then
notify("not recording", 4)
return
end
Rec.log("=== stopped ===")
Rec.on = false
for _, c in ipairs(Rec.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Rec.conns = {}
local body = table.concat(Rec.lines, "\n")
local header = table.concat({
"-- what happened, in order, with a clock in seconds from arming",
"-- HEALTH lines carry the delta: one hit of 400 and eight of 50",
"-- look nothing alike, and that is the question this exists to answer.",
"-- S->C lines are server to client remote traffic WITH arguments.",
"-- What is NOT here: the arguments another script SENDS. Reading those",
"-- needs a namecall hook and this game kicks for one.",
"",
}, "\n")
saveDump("kill-recording.txt", header .. body)
notify(#Rec.lines .. " events recorded", 6)
end
addCleanup("recorder", function()
if Rec.on then
Rec.on = false
for _, c in ipairs(Rec.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Rec.conns = {}
end
end)
function Dev.dumpEverything()
if Dev.dumping then
notify("already dumping", 4)
return
end
Dev.dumping = true
notify("dumping the whole game - a few seconds", 5)
task.spawn(function()
local lines, count, spent = {}, 0, 0
local services = {
"Workspace", "ReplicatedStorage", "ReplicatedFirst", "Lighting",
"Players", "StarterGui", "StarterPack", "StarterPlayer", "SoundService",
"Teams", "Chat", "TextChatService", "MaterialService",
"CollectionService", "ProximityPromptService",
}
local function detail(inst)
local bits = {}
if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("pos=(%.0f,%.0f,%.0f) size=(%.1f,%.1f,%.1f)",
inst.Position.X, inst.Position.Y, inst.Position.Z,
inst.Size.X, inst.Size.Y, inst.Size.Z)
if inst.Anchored then bits[#bits + 1] = "anchored" end
elseif inst:IsA("ValueBase") then
local ok, v = pcall(function() return inst.Value end)
bits[#bits + 1] = "= " .. (ok and tostring(v) or "?")
elseif inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton") or inst:IsA("TextBox") then
local t = inst.Text
if t ~= "" then bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("%q", #t > 90 and (t:sub(1, 90) .. "...") or t) end
elseif inst:IsA("Humanoid") then
bits[#bits + 1] = string.format("hp=%.0f/%.0f walk=%.0f", inst.Health, inst.MaxHealth, inst.WalkSpeed)
end
local ok, attrs = pcall(function() return inst:GetAttributes() end)
if ok and attrs then
for k, v in pairs(attrs) do bits[#bits + 1] = "@" .. k .. "=" .. tostring(v) end
end
return #bits > 0 and ("  " .. table.concat(bits, " ")) or ""
end
local function walk(inst, depth)
count = count + 1
spent = spent + 1
if count > Dev.fullLimit then return true end
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("%s[%s] %s%s",
string.rep("  ", depth), inst.ClassName, inst.Name, detail(inst))
if spent >= 3000 then
spent = 0
task.wait()
end
local ok, kids = pcall(function() return inst:GetChildren() end)
if not ok then return false end
for _, k in ipairs(kids) do
if walk(k, depth + 1) then return true end
end
return false
end
lines[#lines + 1] = "-- the whole game, every service, full depth"
lines[#lines + 1] = "-- place " .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. "  job " .. tostring(game.JobId)
local stopped = false
for _, name in ipairs(services) do
local got, svc = pcall(function() return game:GetService(name) end)
if got and svc then
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
lines[#lines + 1] = "==== " .. name .. " ===="
if walk(svc, 0) then stopped = true break end
end
end
local gui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if gui and not stopped then
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
lines[#lines + 1] = "==== PlayerGui ===="
walk(gui, 0)
end
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("-- %d instances%s", count,
stopped and (" (stopped at the " .. Dev.fullLimit .. " cap)") or "")
saveDump("everything.txt", table.concat(lines, "\n"))
Dev.dumping = false
end)
end
function Dev.caps()
local names = {
"hookmetamethod", "getnamecallmethod", "checkcaller", "getgc", "getconnections",
"isnetworkowner", "sethiddenproperty",
"setclipboard", "writefile", "readfile", "isfile", "isfolder", "makefolder",
"getrawmetatable", "setreadonly", "fireproximityprompt", "firetouchinterest",
"gethui", "identifyexecutor", "setfpscap", "getcustomasset",
}
local lines = { "-- what this executor can do", "" }
for _, n in ipairs(names) do
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("  %-22s %s", n,
typeof(getfenv(0)[n]) == "function" and "yes" or "no")
end
local ok, who = pcall(function() return identifyexecutor() end)
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
lines[#lines + 1] = "executor: " .. (ok and tostring(who) or "unknown")
