lines[#lines + 1] = "Drawing: " .. tostring(HAS_DRAWING)
lines[#lines + 1] = "mobile: " .. tostring(Library.IsMobile)
lines[#lines + 1] = "place: " .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. "  job: " .. tostring(game.JobId)
saveDump("executor.txt", table.concat(lines, "\n"))
end
function Dev.run(src)
if not src or src == "" then return end
local fn, err = loadstring(src)
if not fn then
Dev.consoleOut = "compile error: " .. tostring(err)
notify(Dev.consoleOut, 6)
return
end
local ok, res = pcall(fn)
Dev.consoleOut = ok and ("ok: " .. tostring(res)) or ("error: " .. tostring(res))
notify(Dev.consoleOut, 6)
end
Dev.stringLimit = 40
Dev.loadedCap = 1500
Dev.scanCap = 120000
Dev.scanSlice = 0.008
Dev.scanState = ""
function Dev.info(fn, what)
if typeof(debug) ~= "table" then return nil end
if typeof(debug.info) == "function" then
local ok, v = pcall(debug.info, fn, what)
if ok then return v end
end
if typeof(debug.getinfo) == "function" then
local ok, t = pcall(debug.getinfo, fn)
if ok and type(t) == "table" then
if what == "s" then return t.source or t.short_src end
if what == "l" then
local n = t.linedefined
if n == nil or n < 0 then n = t.currentline end
return n
end
end
end
return nil
end
function Dev.canReadConstants()
return typeof(getgc) == "function"
and typeof(debug) == "table"
and typeof(debug.getconstants) == "function"
end
function Dev.dumpLoaded(filter)
if not Dev.canReadConstants() then
notify("this executor has no debug.getconstants", 7)
return
end
filter = (filter or ""):lower()
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok then
notify("getgc failed", 5)
return
end
notify("scanning the heap", 3)
local blocks, scanned, kept, sources = {}, 0, 0, {}
local slice, capped = os.clock(), false
for _, f in pairs(all) do
if type(f) == "function" then
if scanned >= Dev.scanCap then capped = true break end
scanned = scanned + 1
if os.clock() - slice >= Dev.scanSlice then
Dev.scanState = string.format("scanning: %d looked at, %d kept", scanned, kept)
task.wait()
slice = os.clock()
end
local src = Dev.info(f, "s")
if type(src) ~= "string" or src == "" then src = "(unknown chunk)" end
if src ~= "[C]" then
local gotK, consts = pcall(debug.getconstants, f)
if gotK and type(consts) == "table" then
local rows, hit, mine = {}, filter == "", false
if filter ~= "" and src:lower():find(filter, 1, true) then hit = true end
for _, c in pairs(consts) do
local t = type(c)
if t == "string" and #c > 0 and #c <= 160 then
rows[#rows + 1] = string.format("%q", c)
if c:find("RK_", 1, true) == 1 or c == "RKCham"
or c == "Obsidian" or c == "ObsidianLib" then
mine = true
end
if filter ~= "" and c:lower():find(filter, 1, true) then hit = true end
elseif t == "number" or t == "boolean" then
rows[#rows + 1] = tostring(c)
end
end
if hit and typeof(debug.getupvalues) == "function" then
local gotU, ups = pcall(debug.getupvalues, f)
if gotU and type(ups) == "table" then
for _, u in pairs(ups) do
local t = type(u)
if t == "string" and #u <= 160 then
rows[#rows + 1] = "up " .. string.format("%q", u)
if filter ~= "" and u:lower():find(filter, 1, true) then hit = true end
elseif t == "number" or t == "boolean" then
rows[#rows + 1] = "up " .. tostring(u)
end
end
end
end
if hit and not mine and #rows > 1 and kept < Dev.loadedCap then
kept = kept + 1
sources[src] = (sources[src] or 0) + 1
blocks[#blocks + 1] = string.format("-- %s  line %s\n    %s",
src, tostring(Dev.info(f, "l")), table.concat(rows, "  "))
end
end
end
end
end
Dev.scanState = ""
if kept == 0 then
notify(string.format("%d functions scanned%s, none matched%s",
scanned, capped and " (hit the ceiling)" or "",
filter ~= "" and (" \"" .. filter .. "\"") or
" - this executor may not expose constants for chunks"), 9)
return
end
notify(string.format("%d scanned, %d matched - writing", scanned, kept), 4)
local index = {}
for src, n in pairs(sources) do index[#index + 1] = string.format("--   %5d  %s", n, src) end
table.sort(index)
local head = {
"-- constants and upvalues from every non-C function on the heap",
"-- strings AND numbers, because a damage routine's number is the point",
"-- read with getgc + debug.getconstants. No hook.",
filter ~= "" and ("-- filtered to: " .. filter) or "-- UNFILTERED",
string.format("-- %d scanned%s, %d kept%s", scanned,
capped and " (hit the examine ceiling - narrow the filter)" or "",
kept, kept >= Dev.loadedCap and " (result cap - use a filter)" or ""),
"-- this menu's own functions are excluded",
"--",
"-- chunks these came from:",
}
for _, l in ipairs(index) do head[#head + 1] = l end
head[#head + 1] = ""
saveDump("loaded-scripts.txt", table.concat(head, "\n") .. "\n" .. table.concat(blocks, "\n\n"))
end
function Dev.dumpStrings(filter)
if not Dev.canReadConstants() then
notify("this executor has no debug.getconstants - use the spy instead", 7)
return
end
filter = (filter or ""):lower()
local bySource = {}
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok then
notify("getgc failed", 5)
return
end
notify("scanning the heap - this takes a few seconds", 4)
local scanned, kept, since = 0, 0, 0
for _, f in pairs(all) do
if type(f) == "function" then
scanned = scanned + 1
since = since + 1
if since >= 2000 then
since = 0
task.wait()
end
local src = Dev.info(f, "s")
if type(src) == "string" and src ~= "" and src ~= "[C]" then
local gotK, consts = pcall(debug.getconstants, f)
if gotK and type(consts) == "table" then
local strings = {}
for _, c in pairs(consts) do
if type(c) == "string" and #c > 0 and #c <= Dev.stringLimit then
strings[#strings + 1] = c
end
end
if #strings > 0 then
local interesting = filter ~= ""
if not interesting then
for _, c in ipairs(strings) do
local lc = c:lower()
if lc == "fireserver" or lc == "invokeserver"
or lc:find("event", 1, true) or lc:find("remote", 1, true) then
interesting = true
break
end
end
else
interesting = false
for _, c in ipairs(strings) do
if c:lower():find(filter, 1, true) then interesting = true break end
end
end
if interesting then
kept = kept + 1
local line = Dev.info(f, "l")
local key = src
bySource[key] = bySource[key] or {}
table.insert(bySource[key], {
line = tonumber(line) or -1,
strings = strings,
})
end
end
end
end
end
end
local lines = {
"-- string constants from the game's own client functions",
"-- read with getgc + debug.getconstants, no namecall hook involved",
"--",
"-- a function holding a remote name AND a short literal is a call shape:",
"--   \"StunStick\" ... \"S\" ... \"H\"   ->  Event:FireServer(\"S\") / (\"H\", part)",
"",
}
if filter ~= "" then
lines[#lines + 1] = "-- filtered to functions mentioning: " .. filter
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
end
local sources = {}
for src in pairs(bySource) do sources[#sources + 1] = src end
table.sort(sources)
for _, src in ipairs(sources) do
lines[#lines + 1] = "==== " .. src .. " ===="
local rows = bySource[src]
table.sort(rows, function(a, b) return a.line < b.line end)
for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
local seen, uniq = {}, {}
for _, c in ipairs(row.strings) do
if not seen[c] then
seen[c] = true
uniq[#uniq + 1] = string.format("%q", c)
end
end
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("  line %-6s %s",
row.line >= 0 and tostring(row.line) or "?", table.concat(uniq, ", "))
end
lines[#lines + 1] = ""
end
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("-- %d functions scanned, %d kept", scanned, kept)
local name = filter ~= "" and ("strings-" .. filter .. ".txt") or "clientstrings.txt"
local ok, where = saveDump(name, table.concat(lines, "\n"))
notify(string.format("%d functions scanned, %d kept  -  %s",
scanned, kept, ok and tostring(where) or "could not be saved"), 8)
end
function Dev.watchDetector()
local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ExploitEvent2")
if not ev or not ev:IsA("BindableEvent") then return end
bind(ev.Event, function(...)
Dev.flagged = (Dev.flagged or 0) + 1
local bits = {}
for i = 1, select("#", ...) do bits[#bits + 1] = describe((select(i, ...))) end
Dev.lastFlag = table.concat(bits, ", ")
notify("DETECTOR FIRED - turn off anything using the namecall hook", 10)
end)
end
local function newGui(name, order)
local holder
if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
local got, h = pcall(gethui)
if got and h then holder = h end
end
if not holder then
local got, core = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
holder = (got and core) or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end
local gui = make("ScreenGui", {
Name = name, ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
DisplayOrder = order or 900, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
}, holder)
addCleanup("gui:" .. name, function() pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end) end)
return gui
end
local Cross = { on = false, size = 3, color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) }
function Cross.build()
if Cross.gui and Cross.gui.Parent then return end
Cross.gui = newGui("RKCross", 901)
Cross.dot = make("Frame", {
Name = "Dot", BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = Cross.color,
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Cross.size, Cross.size),
}, Cross.gui)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, Cross.dot)
end
function Cross.set(on)
Cross.on = on
if on then
Cross.build()
Cross.dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Cross.size, Cross.size)
Cross.dot.BackgroundColor3 = Cross.color
end
if Cross.gui then Cross.gui.Enabled = on end
end
local Synth = { RATE = 44100, ids = {} }
function Synth.tanh(x) local e = math.exp(2 * x) return (e - 1) / (e + 1) end
Synth.SOUNDS = {
tick = { dur = 0.09, f = function(t, ph)
local f = 1100 + 420 * math.exp(-t / 0.012)
return f, math.sin(ph) * math.exp(-t / 0.018) end },
pop = { dur = 0.13, f = function(t, ph)
local f = 170 + 360 * math.exp(-t / 0.018)
return f, (math.sin(ph) + 0.2 * math.sin(ph * 2)) * math.min(1, t / 0.003) * math.exp(-t / 0.03) end },
bubble = { dur = 0.12, f = function(t, ph)
local f = 980 - 600 * math.exp(-t / 0.02)
return f, math.sin(ph) * math.min(1, t / 0.004) * math.exp(-t / 0.035) end },
chime = { dur = 0.5, f = function(t, ph)
return 1318.5, (math.sin(ph) * 0.6 + math.sin(ph * 1.4983) * 0.3 + math.sin(ph * 2) * 0.12)
* math.min(1, t / 0.004) * math.exp(-t / 0.16) end },
bell = { dur = 0.65, f = function(t, ph)
return 880, (math.sin(ph) * math.exp(-t / 0.35) + 0.5 * math.sin(ph * 2.76) * math.exp(-t / 0.18)
+ 0.25 * math.sin(ph * 5.4) * math.exp(-t / 0.08)) * math.min(1, t / 0.003) end },
thump = { dur = 0.16, f = function(t, ph)
local f = 70 + 90 * math.exp(-t / 0.03)
local click = (t < 0.004) and (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.25 or 0
return f, math.sin(ph) * math.exp(-t / 0.05) + click end },
glass = { dur = 0.32, f = function(t, ph)
local v = 1 + 0.004 * math.sin(t * 60)
return 2600 * v, (math.sin(ph) * 0.6 + math.sin(ph * 1.5) * 0.35 + math.sin(ph * 2) * 0.2)
* math.min(1, t / 0.002) * math.exp(-t / 0.09) end },
blip = { dur = 0.08, f = function(t, ph)
return 760, Synth.tanh(math.sin(ph) * 2.5) / Synth.tanh(2.5) * 0.7 * math.exp(-t / 0.03) end },
coin = { dur = 0.32, f = function(t, ph)
local f = (t < 0.055) and 987.8 or 1318.5
return f, math.sin(ph) * math.min(1, t / 0.003) * math.exp(-math.max(0, t - 0.055) / 0.12) end },
puff = { dur = 0.07, noise = true, f = function(t)
return 0, math.exp(-t / 0.016) end },
thunder = { dur = 2.6, sample = function(t, st)
st.a = (st.a or 0) + ((math.random() * 2 - 1) - (st.a or 0)) * 0.5
st.b = (st.b or 0) + ((math.random() * 2 - 1) - (st.b or 0)) * 0.025
st.c = (st.c or 0) + (st.b - (st.c or 0)) * 0.08
local crack = st.a * math.exp(-t / 0.06) * 0.9
local roll = st.c * 9 * math.min(1, t / 0.12) * math.exp(-t / 0.9)
* (0.75 + 0.25 * math.sin(t * 7.3) * math.sin(t * 2.1))
return crack + roll end },
boom = { dur = 1.6, sample = function(t, st)
st.ph = (st.ph or 0) + 2 * math.pi * (32 + 70 * math.exp(-t / 0.18)) / 44100
st.n = (st.n or 0) + ((math.random() * 2 - 1) - (st.n or 0)) * 0.06
return math.sin(st.ph) * math.exp(-t / 0.45) * math.min(1, t / 0.004) + st.n * 3 * math.exp(-t / 0.25) end },
alarm = { dur = 0.62, f = function(t, ph)
local first = t < 0.2
local s = first and t or (t - 0.24)
if s < 0 then return 1174.7, 0 end
local len = first and 0.2 or 0.38
local env = math.min(1, s / 0.004, (len - s) / 0.012) * math.exp(-s / 0.3)
return first and 784 or 1174.7, Synth.tanh(math.sin(ph) * 1.6) / Synth.tanh(1.6) * math.max(0, env) end },
fall = { dur = 0.9, f = function(t, ph)
local first = t < 0.18
local s = first and t or (t - 0.2)
if s < 0 then return 784, 0 end
local len = first and 0.18 or 0.7
local env = math.min(1, s / 0.003, (len - s) / 0.015) * math.exp(-s / (first and 0.14 or 0.3))
return first and 1174.7 or 784, (math.sin(ph) * 0.8 + math.sin(ph * 2) * 0.15 + math.sin(ph * 3) * 0.05)
* math.max(0, env) end },
siren = { dur = 1.8, f = function(t, ph)
local f = 164.8 * (1 + 0.335 * math.min(1, t / 1.3))
local pulse = 0.7 + 0.3 * math.sin(t * 2 * math.pi * 5.5)
local env = math.min(1, t / 0.3, (1.8 - t) / 0.4)
local v = math.sin(ph) * 0.7 + math.sin(ph * 2) * 0.35 + math.sin(ph * 3) * 0.2 + math.sin(ph * 4) * 0.1
return f, Synth.tanh(v * 1.5) * pulse * math.max(0, env) end },
}
function Synth.wav(key)
local spec = Synth.SOUNDS[key]
local rate = Synth.RATE
local n = math.floor(spec.dur * rate)
local samples, peak, ph, lp, st = {}, 1e-6, 0, 0, {}
for i = 1, n do
local t = (i - 1) / rate
local f, v
if spec.sample then f, v = 0, spec.sample(t, st) else f, v = spec.f(t, ph) end
if spec.noise then
lp = lp + ((math.random() * 2 - 1) - lp) * 0.18
v = lp * v
end
ph = ph + 2 * math.pi * f / rate
local edge = math.min(1, t / 0.002, (spec.dur - t) / 0.002)
v = v * math.max(0, edge)
samples[i] = v
if math.abs(v) > peak then peak = math.abs(v) end
end
local b = buffer.create(44 + n * 2)
local function str(o, s2) for k = 1, #s2 do buffer.writeu8(b, o + k - 1, s2:byte(k)) end end
str(0, "RIFF"); buffer.writeu32(b, 4, 36 + n * 2); str(8, "WAVE")
str(12, "fmt "); buffer.writeu32(b, 16, 16); buffer.writeu16(b, 20, 1); buffer.writeu16(b, 22, 1)
buffer.writeu32(b, 24, rate); buffer.writeu32(b, 28, rate * 2); buffer.writeu16(b, 32, 2); buffer.writeu16(b, 34, 16)
str(36, "data"); buffer.writeu32(b, 40, n * 2)
local g = 0.82 / peak
for i = 1, n do
buffer.writei16(b, 44 + (i - 1) * 2, math.clamp(math.floor(samples[i] * g * 32767 + 0.5), -32768, 32767))
end
return buffer.tostring(b)
end
function Synth.id(key)
if Synth.ids[key] ~= nil then return Synth.ids[key] or nil end
Synth.ids[key] = false
if not (Synth.SOUNDS[key] and typeof(getcustomasset) == "function" and typeof(writefile) == "function"
and type(buffer) == "table") then return nil end
local ok, id = pcall(function()
if typeof(isfolder) == "function" and typeof(makefolder) == "function" then
if not isfolder("Rake") then makefolder("Rake") end
if not isfolder("Rake/sounds") then makefolder("Rake/sounds") end
end
local path = "Rake/sounds/v1-" .. key .. ".wav"
if not (typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(path)) then writefile(path, Synth.wav(key)) end
return getcustomasset(path)
end)
if ok and type(id) == "string" and id ~= "" then Synth.ids[key] = id; return id end
return nil
end
Round.alertOn, Round.alertVolume, Round.alertSnd = true, 0.7, {}
Round.ALERT_FALLBACK = { alarm = "rbxassetid://3744371342", fall = "rbxassetid://3779045779",
siren = "rbxassetid://108120460785710" }
function Round.alert(key, force)
if not (Round.alertOn or force) then return end
local id = Synth.id(key) or Round.ALERT_FALLBACK[key]
if not id then return end
local s = Round.alertSnd[key]
if not (s and s.Parent) then
s = Instance.new("Sound")
s.Name = "RKAlert"
s.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
Round.alertSnd[key] = s
end
pcall(function()
s.SoundId = id
s.Volume = Round.alertVolume
s.TimePosition = 0
s:Play()
end)
end
function Round.testAlerts()
Round.alert("alarm", true)
task.delay(0.9, function() Round.alert("fall", true) end)
task.delay(2, function() Round.alert("siren", true) end)
end
addCleanup("alertsounds", function()
for _, snd in pairs(Round.alertSnd) do pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end) end
Round.alertSnd = {}
end)

local HIT_EFFECTS = { "Combo", "Shockwave", "Pulse", "Glow Puff", "Stars", "Lightning", "Slash", "Damage Number",
"Cross", "Spikes", "Ring", "Burst", "Sparks", "Starburst", "Confetti" }
local HIT_COLOUR_MODES = { "Preset", "Custom", "Rainbow" }
local HIT_SOUND_NAMES = {}
for _, row in ipairs(HIT_SOUNDS) do HIT_SOUND_NAMES[#HIT_SOUND_NAMES + 1] = row[1] end
local Hit = {
on = false, ping = true, sound = "rbxassetid://3779045779",
soundName = "Click",
life = 0.55, size = 14, colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80),
marks = {}, count = 0, lastHealth = nil, watched = nil,
stickNames = {},
effect = "Combo", colourMode = "Preset", fxSize = 1, volume = 0.6, pitch = 1,
}
function Hit.build()
if Hit.gui and Hit.gui.Parent then return end
Hit.gui = newGui("RKHits", 902)
Hit.snd = make("Sound", {
Name = "RKPing", SoundId = Hit.sound, Volume = Hit.volume,
}, Hit.gui)
end
function Hit.fxColour(default)
if Hit.colourMode == "Custom" then return Hit.colour end
if Hit.colourMode == "Rainbow" then
Hit.hueStep = ((Hit.hueStep or 0) + 0.037) % 1
return Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() * 0.35 + Hit.hueStep) % 1, 0.85, 1)
end
return default or Hit.colour
end
function Hit.tex(kind)
local id = SkyGen.can() and SkyGen.spriteId(kind) or nil
return id or "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
end
function Hit.fxPart(cf, size)
local p = Instance.new("Part")
p.Anchored, p.CanCollide, p.CanQuery, p.CanTouch, p.CastShadow = true, false, false, false, false
p.Size, p.CFrame = size or Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2), cf
p.Transparency = 1
return p
end
function Hit.emitAt(origin, cfg)
local p = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.new(origin))
local pe = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
pe.Enabled = false
pe.Texture = cfg.texture
pe.Color = ColorSequence.new(cfg.colour)
pe.LightEmission = cfg.light or 1
pe.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cfg.size[1]), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, cfg.size[2]) })
pe.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.05), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, 0.35),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
pe.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(cfg.life[1], cfg.life[2])
pe.Speed = NumberRange.new(cfg.speed[1], cfg.speed[2])
pe.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
pe.Drag = cfg.drag or 0
pe.Acceleration = cfg.accel or Vector3.zero
pe.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
pe.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-(cfg.spin or 0), cfg.spin or 0)
pe.Parent = p
p.Parent = Workspace
pcall(function() pe:Emit(cfg.n) end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p, cfg.life[2] + 0.3) end)
end
function Hit.flash(origin, colour, s)
local p = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.new(origin))
local l = Instance.new("PointLight")
l.Color, l.Range, l.Brightness, l.Shadows = colour, 9 * s, 4, false
l.Parent = p
p.Parent = Workspace
pcall(function() TweenService:Create(l, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Brightness = 0 }):Play() end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p, 0.35) end)
end
function Hit.bloomPart(cf, shape, material, colour, from, to, life)
local p = Hit.fxPart(cf, from)
p.Shape, p.Material, p.Color, p.Transparency = shape, material, colour, 0.1
p.Parent = Workspace
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(life, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), { Size = to, Transparency = 1 }):Play()
end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p, life + 0.05) end)
end
function Hit.beam(a, b, colour, thick, life)
local len = (b - a).Magnitude
if len < 0.01 then return end
local p = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.lookAt((a + b) / 2, b), Vector3.new(thick, thick, len))
p.Material, p.Color, p.Transparency = Enum.Material.Neon, colour, 0
p.Parent = Workspace
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(life, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
{ Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(thick * 0.3, thick * 0.3, len) }):Play()
end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p, life + 0.05) end)
end
Hit.NUMBER_FONT = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.Heavy)
function Hit.damageNumber(origin, dmg, colour, s)
local p = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.new(origin + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)))
p.Parent = Workspace
local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(96 * s), math.floor(38 * s))
bb.AlwaysOnTop, bb.LightInfluence = true, 0
bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 1.4, 0, 0)
bb.Adornee = p
bb.Parent = p
local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
t.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
t.TextScaled = true
t.FontFace = Hit.NUMBER_FONT
t.Text = (dmg and dmg > 0) and ("-" .. tostring(math.floor(dmg + 0.5))) or "HIT"
t.TextColor3 = colour
t.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 10, 12)
t.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
t.Parent = bb
local sc = Instance.new("UIScale")
sc.Scale = 0.4
sc.Parent = t
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(sc, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Back), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
TweenService:Create(bb, TweenInfo.new(0.95, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint),
{ StudsOffset = bb.StudsOffset + Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0) }):Play()
TweenService:Create(t, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In, 0, false, 0.5),
{ TextTransparency = 1, TextStrokeTransparency = 1 }):Play()
end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p, 1.05) end)
end
function Hit.burst3D(worldPos, dmg)
if typeof(worldPos) ~= "Vector3" then return end
local kind = Hit.effect
local S = Hit.fxSize or 1
local debris = game:GetService("Debris")
local origin = worldPos + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
local base = Hit.fxColour(Hit.colour)
Hit.flash(origin, base, S)
local function spawn(size, shape, col, vel, life)
local p = Instance.new("Part")
p.Size, p.Shape, p.Material, p.Color = size * S, shape, Enum.Material.Neon, Hit.fxColour(col)
p.CanCollide, p.CanQuery, p.CanTouch, p.CastShadow = true, false, false, false
p.CFrame = CFrame.new(origin)
* CFrame.Angles(math.random() * 6, math.random() * 6, math.random() * 6)
p.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel * math.sqrt(S)
p.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-18, 18), math.random(-18, 18), math.random(-18, 18))
p.Parent = Workspace
local lt = life or 2
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(lt, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
{ Transparency = 1, Size = p.Size * 0.25 }):Play()
end)
pcall(function() debris:AddItem(p, lt) end)
end
local BALL, BLOCK, CYL = Enum.PartType.Ball, Enum.PartType.Block, Enum.PartType.Cylinder
local RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
local ORANGE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 60)
local AQUA = Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255)
local function shockwave(col)
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(origin - Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)), CYL,
Enum.Material.ForceField, col, Vector3.new(0.2, 1, 1), Vector3.new(0.2, 13 * S, 13 * S), 0.5)
end
local function puff(col)
Hit.emitAt(origin, { texture = Hit.tex("dot"), n = 16, colour = col, size = { 0.6 * S, 2.4 * S },
life = { 0.45, 0.75 }, speed = { 3 * S, 8 * S }, drag = 4 })
end
if kind == "Combo" then
shockwave(Hit.fxColour(AQUA)); puff(base); Hit.damageNumber(origin, dmg, base, S)
elseif kind == "Shockwave" then
shockwave(Hit.fxColour(AQUA))
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(origin) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)), CYL, Enum.Material.Neon,
Hit.fxColour(AQUA), Vector3.new(0.08, 0.5, 0.5), Vector3.new(0.04, 6 * S, 6 * S), 0.3)
elseif kind == "Pulse" then
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(origin), BALL, Enum.Material.ForceField, base,
Vector3.new(0.6, 0.6, 0.6), Vector3.new(9, 9, 9) * S, 0.45)
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(origin), BALL, Enum.Material.Neon, base,
Vector3.new(0.4, 0.4, 0.4), Vector3.new(2.2, 2.2, 2.2) * S, 0.22)
elseif kind == "Glow Puff" then
puff(base)
elseif kind == "Stars" then
Hit.emitAt(origin, { texture = Hit.tex("star"), n = 11, colour = Hit.fxColour(ORANGE),
size = { 0.9 * S, 0 }, life = { 0.5, 0.85 }, speed = { 9 * S, 16 * S }, spin = 220,
accel = Vector3.new(0, -14, 0) })
elseif kind == "Lightning" then
for _ = 1, 4 do
local dir = Vector3.new(math.random() * 2 - 1, math.random() * 1.2 - 0.2, math.random() * 2 - 1)
dir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
local a = origin
for k = 1, 5 do
local jitter = Vector3.new(math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.5, math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8
local b = a + (dir * 0.95 + jitter) * S
Hit.beam(a, b, Hit.fxColour(Color3.fromRGB(170, 210, 255)), 0.13 * S * (1 - k * 0.12), 0.26)
a = b
end
end
elseif kind == "Slash" then
local c = cam()
if c then
local tilt = math.random() * math.pi
local r, u = c.CFrame.RightVector, c.CFrame.UpVector
local ax = r * math.cos(tilt) + u * math.sin(tilt)
local ay = -r * math.sin(tilt) + u * math.cos(tilt)
local R = 2.4 * S
local prev
for i = 0, 12 do
local ang = math.rad(-75 + i * 12.5)
local pt = origin + (ax * math.cos(ang) + ay * math.sin(ang)) * R - ax * R * 0.6
if prev then
local w = 0.05 + 0.2 * math.sin(math.pi * i / 12)
Hit.beam(prev, pt, Hit.fxColour(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), w * S, 0.3)
end
prev = pt
end
end
elseif kind == "Damage Number" then
Hit.damageNumber(origin, dmg, base, S)
elseif kind == "Confetti" then
for i = 1, 22 do
local a = math.rad(math.random(0, 359))
spawn(Vector3.new(0.36, 0.36, 0.08), BLOCK, Color3.fromHSV(math.random(), 0.9, 1),
Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * (10 + math.random() * 10), 22 + math.random() * 12,
math.sin(a) * (10 + math.random() * 10)), 2.4)
end
elseif kind == "Starburst" then
for i = 1, 18 do
local a = (i / 18) * math.pi * 2
spawn(Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3), BALL, ORANGE,
Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * 26, 16 + math.random() * 8, math.sin(a) * 26), 2.2)
end
elseif kind == "Ring" then
for i = 1, 18 do
local a = (i / 18) * math.pi * 2
spawn(Vector3.new(0.32, 0.32, 0.32), BALL, AQUA,
Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * 24, 5, math.sin(a) * 24), 2.2)
end
elseif kind == "Burst" then
for i = 1, 18 do
local d = Vector3.new(math.random() * 2 - 1, math.random() * 2 - 1, math.random() * 2 - 1)
d = d.Magnitude > 0.1 and d.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
spawn(Vector3.new(0.4, 0.4, 0.4), BALL, RED, d * (20 + math.random() * 12), 2.2)
end
elseif kind == "Spikes" then
for i = 1, 9 do
local a = (i / 9) * math.pi * 2
spawn(Vector3.new(0.18, 0.18, 1.4), BLOCK, Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120),
Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * 20, 14 + math.random() * 8, math.sin(a) * 20), 2.2)
end
elseif kind == "Sparks" then
for i = 1, 16 do
local a = math.rad(math.random(0, 359))
spawn(Vector3.new(0.22, 0.22, 0.22), BALL, ORANGE,
Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * (16 + math.random() * 12), 20 + math.random() * 14,
math.sin(a) * (16 + math.random() * 12)), 1.9)
end
else
for i = 1, 12 do
local a = math.rad(math.random(0, 359))
spawn(Vector3.new(0.3, 0.3, 0.3), BALL, Hit.colour,
Vector3.new(math.cos(a) * (14 + math.random() * 8), 18 + math.random() * 10,
math.sin(a) * (14 + math.random() * 8)), 2.0)
end
end
end
function Hit.testEffect()
local c = cam()
if not c then return end
local at = c.CFrame.Position + c.CFrame.LookVector * 9 - Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
pcall(Hit.burst3D, at, math.random(8, 30))
end
function Hit.mark(worldPos, dmg)
Hit.build()
local c = cam()
if not c then return end
Hit.count = Hit.count + 1
if Hit.on then pcall(Hit.burst3D, worldPos, dmg) end
if Hit.ping and Hit.snd and (os.clock() - (Fight.stickAt or -1e9) < 0.7) then
Hit.play()
end
end
function Hit.play()
if not Hit.snd then return end
pcall(function()
Hit.snd.SoundId = Hit.sound
Hit.snd.Volume = Hit.volume
Hit.snd.PlaybackSpeed = Hit.pitch * (1 + (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.06)
Hit.snd.TimePosition = 0
Hit.snd:Play()
end)
end
function Hit.setSound(name)
for _, row in ipairs(HIT_SOUNDS) do
if row[1] == name then
local id = row[2]
local key = id:match("^synth:(.+)$")
if key then
id = Synth.id(key)
if not id then
id = "rbxassetid://3779045779"
if not Hit.synthWarned then
Hit.synthWarned = true
notify("the soft hit sounds need getcustomasset - using Click instead", 5)
end
end
end
Hit.soundName, Hit.sound = row[1], id
if Hit.snd then pcall(function() Hit.snd.SoundId = id end) end
return
end
end
end
function Hit.testSound()
Hit.build()
if not Hit.snd then
notify("could not build the sound", 4)
return
end
Hit.play()
task.delay(0.6, function()
local ok, len = pcall(function() return Hit.snd.TimeLength end)
if ok and type(len) == "number" and len > 0 then
notify(string.format("%s - %.2fs, loaded", Hit.soundName, len), 5)
else
notify(Hit.soundName .. " did not load - that id does not stream "
.. "on this place, pick another", 7)
end
end)
end
function Hit.stickSounds()
local out = {}
local roots = {}
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if char then roots[#roots + 1] = char end
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if bp then roots[#roots + 1] = bp end
for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
local tool = root:FindFirstChild("StunStick")
if tool then
for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Sound") then out[#out + 1] = d end
end
end
end
return out
end
Hit.STICK_IDS = {
["85436509690486"] = true,
["108172345948550"] = true,
["80998052021754"] = true,
["96128258093313"] = true,
["113998838754185"] = true,
["101164100"] = true,
["129695352137285"] = true,
}
Hit.held = {}
function Hit.soundKey(snd)
local ok, id = pcall(function() return snd.SoundId end)
return ok and tostring(id or ""):match("(%d+)%s*$") or nil
end
function Hit.isStickSound(snd)
local key = Hit.soundKey(snd)
if key and Hit.STICK_IDS[key] then return true end
if snd:FindFirstAncestor(Fight.toolName) then
if key then Hit.STICK_IDS[key] = true end
return true
end
if Hit.stickNames[snd.Name] ~= true then return false end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
return char ~= nil and snd:IsDescendantOf(char)
end
function Hit.muteGroup()
if Hit.group and Hit.group.Parent then return Hit.group end
Hit.group = make("SoundGroup", { Name = "RKMuted", Volume = 0 }, game:GetService("SoundService"))
return Hit.group
end
function Hit.hush(snd)
local h = Hit.held[snd]
if not h then
h = { conns = {} }
pcall(function() h.vol, h.group = snd.Volume, snd.SoundGroup end)
Hit.held[snd] = h
local function silence()
pcall(function()
if snd.SoundGroup ~= Hit.group then snd.SoundGroup = Hit.muteGroup() end
if snd.Volume ~= 0 then snd.Volume = 0 end
if snd.Playing then snd:Stop() end
end)
end
pcall(function() h.conns[#h.conns + 1] = snd.Played:Connect(silence) end)
pcall(function() h.conns[#h.conns + 1] = snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Volume"):Connect(silence) end)
pcall(function() h.conns[#h.conns + 1] = snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Playing"):Connect(silence) end)
pcall(function() h.conns[#h.conns + 1] = snd.Destroying:Connect(function()
for _, c in ipairs(h.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Hit.held[snd] = nil
end) end)
end
pcall(function()
snd.SoundGroup = Hit.muteGroup()
snd.Volume = 0
if snd.Playing then snd:Stop() end
end)
end
function Hit.consider(d)
if d:IsA("Sound") and Hit.isStickSound(d) then Hit.hush(d) end
end
function Hit.muteStick(on)
if on then
for _, snd in ipairs(Hit.stickSounds()) do
Hit.stickNames[snd.Name] = true
Hit.hush(snd)
end
else
for snd, h in pairs(Hit.held) do
for _, c in ipairs(h.conns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
if snd and snd.Parent then
pcall(function()
snd.SoundGroup = h.group
if h.vol and h.vol > 0 then snd.Volume = h.vol end
end)
end
end
Hit.held, Hit.stickNames = {}, {}
end
end
addCleanup("stickmute", function() Hit.muteStick(false) end)
function Hit.setPing(on)
Hit.ping = on
if not on then
if Hit.muteConn then Hit.muteConn:Disconnect() Hit.muteConn = nil end
for _, c in ipairs(Hit.cloneConns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Hit.cloneConns, Hit.cloneConn = nil, nil
if Hit.swingConn then Hit.swingConn:Disconnect() Hit.swingConn = nil end
Hit.activatedTool = nil
if Hit.pingLoop and not Hit.on then
Hit.pingLoop:Disconnect() Hit.pingLoop = nil
end
Hit.muteStick(false)
return
end
Hit.build()
Hit.watch()
if not Hit.pingLoop then
Hit.pingLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not (Hit.ping or Hit.on) then return end
if os.clock() - (Hit.watchAt or 0) < 0.5 then return end
Hit.watchAt = os.clock()
Hit.watch()
end)
end
Hit.muteStick(true)
if Hit.muteConn then return end
Hit.swingConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local tool = char and char:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName)
if tool and Hit.activatedTool ~= tool then
Hit.activatedTool = tool
pcall(function() tool.Activated:Connect(function() Fight.stickAt = os.clock() end) end)
end
end)
local n = 0
for _ in pairs(Hit.stickNames) do n = n + 1 end
if n > 0 then
notify("stun stick muted - " .. n .. " of its own sounds", 4)
end
Hit.muteConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Hit.ping then return end
if os.clock() - (Hit.muteLast or 0) < 1 then return end
Hit.muteLast = os.clock()
Hit.muteStick(true)
end)
Hit.cloneConns = {}
for _, root in ipairs({ Workspace, LocalPlayer, game:GetService("SoundService") }) do
local ok, c = pcall(function()
return root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
if not Hit.ping or not d:IsA("Sound") then return end
Hit.consider(d)
task.defer(function() if Hit.ping and d.Parent then Hit.consider(d) end end)
end)
end)
if ok and c then Hit.cloneConns[#Hit.cloneConns + 1] = c end
end
Hit.cloneConn = Hit.cloneConns[1]
task.spawn(function()
local n = 0
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("Sound") then Hit.consider(d) end
n = n + 1
if n % 4000 == 0 then task.wait() end
end
end)
addCleanup("stickmutehold", function() Hit.setPing(false) end)
end
function Hit.watch()
local r = rake()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum == Hit.watched then return end
if Hit.conn then Hit.conn:Disconnect() Hit.conn = nil end
Hit.watched = hum
Hit.lastHealth = hum and hum.Health or nil
if not hum then return end
Hit.conn = hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(now)
if Fight.instaWriting then
Hit.lastHealth = Fight.instaHp or now
return
end
if not (Hit.on or Hit.ping) then
Hit.lastHealth = now
return
end
local before = Hit.lastHealth or now
Hit.lastHealth = now
if now >= before then return end
local part = Fight.lastTarget
if not (part and part.Parent and r and part:IsDescendantOf(r)) then
part = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
if part then Hit.mark(part.Position, before - now) end
end)
end
function Hit.step()
local c = cam()
if not c then return end
local now = os.clock()
for i = #Hit.marks, 1, -1 do
local row = Hit.marks[i]
local age = now - row.born
if age >= Hit.life or not row.frame.Parent then
pcall(function() row.frame:Destroy() end)
table.remove(Hit.marks, i)
else
local sp, on = c:WorldToViewportPoint(row.at)
row.frame.Visible = on
if on then
row.frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sp.X, sp.Y)
local fade = age / Hit.life
for _, child in ipairs(row.frame:GetChildren()) do
local stroke = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
if stroke then
stroke.Transparency = fade
elseif child:IsA("Frame") then
child.BackgroundTransparency = fade
end
end
for _, b in ipairs(row.bits or {}) do
if b.frame.Parent then
if b.grow then
local g = b.from * (1 + fade * 2.2)
b.frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(g, g)
elseif b.fly then
local d = Hit.size * (0.15 + fade * 1.7)
b.frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(b.ang) * d,
0.5, math.sin(b.ang) * d)
elseif b.shrink then
b.frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
math.max(1, b.len * (1 - fade)), 2)
elseif b.spark then
local d = Hit.size * (0.2 + fade * 1.9)
b.frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(b.ang) * d,
0.5, math.sin(b.ang) * d)
b.frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, b.len * (1 - fade)), 2)
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
function Hit.set(on)
Hit.on = on
if not on then
if Hit.conn then Hit.conn:Disconnect() Hit.conn = nil end
if Hit.loop then Hit.loop:Disconnect() Hit.loop = nil end
if Hit.pingLoop and not Hit.ping then Hit.pingLoop:Disconnect() Hit.pingLoop = nil end
Hit.watched = nil
for _, row in ipairs(Hit.marks) do pcall(function() row.frame:Destroy() end) end
Hit.marks = {}
return
end
Hit.build()
Hit.watch()
if Hit.loop then return end
Hit.loop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not Hit.on then return end
Hit.watch()
Hit.step()
end)
addCleanup("hitmarks", function() Hit.set(false) end)
end
local KillFx = { on = false, style = "Lightning Strike", size = 1, sound = true, r = nil, wasDead = false }
KillFx.STYLES = { "Lightning Strike", "Explosion", "Soul Rise", "Shockwave", "Random" }
function KillFx.isDead(r)
if not r then return false end
local dead = r:FindFirstChild("Dead")
if dead and dead:IsA("BoolValue") and dead.Value then return true end
local hum = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
return hum ~= nil and Fight.rakeHp(hum) <= 0
end
function KillFx.playSound(key, at, vol)
if not KillFx.sound then return end
local id = Synth.id(key)
if not id then return end
local p = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.new(at))
local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
snd.SoundId, snd.Volume, snd.RollOffMaxDistance, snd.RollOffMinDistance = id, vol or 1, 900, 40
snd.Parent = p
p.Parent = Workspace
pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p, 4) end)
end
function KillFx.screenFlash(amount)
local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
cc.Name = "RKKillFlash"
cc.Brightness = amount
cc.Contrast = -0.1
cc.Parent = Lighting
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(cc, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Brightness = 0, Contrast = 0 }):Play()
end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(cc, 0.6) end)
end
function KillFx.light(at, colour, range, time)
local p = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.new(at))
local l = Instance.new("PointLight")
l.Color, l.Range, l.Brightness, l.Shadows = colour, range, 10, true
l.Parent = p
p.Parent = Workspace
pcall(function() TweenService:Create(l, TweenInfo.new(time, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Brightness = 0 }):Play() end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p, time + 0.1) end)
end
function KillFx.boltPath(a, b, depth, rough)
local pts = { a, b }
local disp = (b - a).Magnitude * rough
for _ = 1, depth do
local np = { pts[1] }
for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
local p, q = pts[i], pts[i + 1]
np[#np + 1] = (p + q) / 2 + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * disp,
(math.random() - 0.5) * disp * 0.4, (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * disp)
np[#np + 1] = q
end
pts, disp = np, disp * 0.55
end
return pts
end
function KillFx.drawPath(pts, colour, thick, folder)
local segs = {}
for i = 1, #pts - 1 do
local a, b = pts[i], pts[i + 1]
local len = (b - a).Magnitude
if len > 0.01 then
local p = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.lookAt((a + b) / 2, b), Vector3.new(thick, thick, len + thick * 0.5))
p.Material, p.Color, p.Transparency = Enum.Material.Neon, colour, 0
p.Parent = folder
segs[#segs + 1] = p
end
end
return segs
end
function KillFx.lightning(at, S)
local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
folder.Name = "RKKillBolt"
folder.Parent = Workspace
local sky = at + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 30, 140 * S, (math.random() - 0.5) * 30)
local main = KillFx.boltPath(sky, at, 6, 0.12)
local segs = KillFx.drawPath(main, Color3.fromRGB(215, 230, 255), 0.55 * S, folder)
for _ = 1, 5 do
local from = main[math.random(4, #main - 8)]
local to = from + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 30, -(10 + math.random() * 25), (math.random() - 0.5) * 30) * S
for _, p in ipairs(KillFx.drawPath(KillFx.boltPath(from, to, 4, 0.2), Color3.fromRGB(180, 205, 255), 0.22 * S, folder)) do
segs[#segs + 1] = p
end
end
task.spawn(function()
for _, step in ipairs({ { 0.07, 1 }, { 0.05, 0 }, { 0.08, 1 }, { 0.04, 0 }, { 0.06, 1 } }) do
for _, p in ipairs(segs) do p.Transparency = (step[2] == 1) and 0 or 1 end
task.wait(step[1])
end
for _, p in ipairs(segs) do
pcall(function() TweenService:Create(p, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Transparency = 1 }):Play() end)
end
task.wait(0.5)
pcall(function() folder:Destroy() end)
end)
KillFx.screenFlash(0.45)
KillFx.light(at + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), Color3.fromRGB(200, 220, 255), 80 * S, 0.9)
local scorch = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.new(at - Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)), Vector3.new(0.1, 9 * S, 9 * S))
scorch.Shape, scorch.Material, scorch.Color, scorch.Transparency = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Enum.Material.Neon, Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 60), 0.1
scorch.Parent = Workspace
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(scorch, TweenInfo.new(0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Color = Color3.fromRGB(20, 16, 14) }):Play()
TweenService:Create(scorch, TweenInfo.new(5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In, 0, false, 1.5), { Transparency = 1 }):Play()
end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(scorch, 6.6) end)
Hit.emitAt(at, { texture = Hit.tex("star"), n = 26, colour = Color3.fromRGB(200, 225, 255), size = { 1.2 * S, 0 },
life = { 0.5, 1 }, speed = { 18 * S, 34 * S }, spin = 260, accel = Vector3.new(0, -30, 0) })
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(at - Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)), Enum.PartType.Cylinder,
Enum.Material.ForceField, Color3.fromRGB(170, 200, 255), Vector3.new(0.2, 2, 2), Vector3.new(0.2, 30 * S, 30 * S), 0.7)
KillFx.playSound("thunder", at, 1)
end
function KillFx.explosion(at, S)
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(at), Enum.PartType.Ball, Enum.Material.Neon, Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 40),
Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), Vector3.new(16, 16, 16) * S, 0.65)
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(at), Enum.PartType.Ball, Enum.Material.Neon, Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 200),
Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), Vector3.new(7, 7, 7) * S, 0.3)
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(at - Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)), Enum.PartType.Cylinder,
Enum.Material.ForceField, Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 80), Vector3.new(0.2, 3, 3), Vector3.new(0.2, 40 * S, 40 * S), 0.8)
Hit.emitAt(at, { texture = Hit.tex("dot"), n = 30, colour = Color3.fromRGB(70, 64, 60), size = { 3 * S, 9 * S },
life = { 1.6, 2.6 }, speed = { 4 * S, 12 * S }, drag = 1.5, light = 0, accel = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0) })
Hit.emitAt(at, { texture = Hit.tex("dot"), n = 40, colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 90), size = { 0.5 * S, 0 },
life = { 0.6, 1.2 }, speed = { 25 * S, 50 * S }, accel = Vector3.new(0, -50, 0) })
KillFx.screenFlash(0.3)
KillFx.light(at, Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 80), 70 * S, 1.2)
KillFx.playSound("boom", at, 1)
end
function KillFx.soul(at, S)
local beam = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.new(at + Vector3.new(0, 30 * S, 0)), Vector3.new(4 * S, 70 * S, 4 * S))
beam.Shape, beam.Material, beam.Color, beam.Transparency = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, Enum.Material.Neon, Color3.fromRGB(190, 225, 255), 1
beam.CFrame = CFrame.new(at + Vector3.new(0, 30 * S, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
beam.Size = Vector3.new(70 * S, 3 * S, 3 * S)
beam.Parent = Workspace
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(beam, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Transparency = 0.55 }):Play()
TweenService:Create(beam, TweenInfo.new(1.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In, 0, false, 0.6),
{ Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(70 * S, 0.2, 0.2) }):Play()
end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(beam, 2.4) end)
local p = Hit.fxPart(CFrame.new(at))
local pe = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
pe.Texture = Hit.tex("star")
pe.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(200, 230, 255), Color3.fromRGB(150, 120, 255))
pe.LightEmission = 1
pe.Size = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2 * S), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0.9 * S), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0) })
pe.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 0.1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
pe.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1.6, 2.6)
pe.Speed = NumberRange.new(6 * S, 12 * S)
pe.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(25, 25)
pe.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
pe.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
pe.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-90, 90)
pe.Rate = 60
pe.Parent = p
p.Parent = Workspace
task.delay(1.2, function() pcall(function() pe.Enabled = false end) end)
pcall(function() game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(p, 4) end)
KillFx.light(at + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), Color3.fromRGB(190, 220, 255), 50 * S, 2)
KillFx.playSound("chime", at, 0.9)
end
function KillFx.shockwave(at, S)
for i, d in ipairs({ { 0, 60 }, { 0.15, 45 } }) do
task.delay(d[1], function()
Hit.bloomPart(CFrame.new(at - Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90)), Enum.PartType.Cylinder,
Enum.Material.ForceField, (i == 1) and Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 120),
Vector3.new(0.3, 2, 2), Vector3.new(0.3, d[2] * S, d[2] * S), 0.9)
end)
end
Hit.emitAt(at - Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), { texture = Hit.tex("dot"), n = 34, colour = Color3.fromRGB(120, 110, 100),
size = { 1.5 * S, 5 * S }, life = { 0.8, 1.4 }, speed = { 20 * S, 34 * S }, drag = 3, light = 0 })
KillFx.screenFlash(0.2)
KillFx.light(at, Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 100), 60 * S, 0.8)
KillFx.playSound("boom", at, 0.8)
end
function KillFx.play(at)
local style = KillFx.style
if style == "Random" then style = KillFx.STYLES[math.random(1, 4)] end
local S = KillFx.size
local ok, err = pcall(function()
if style == "Explosion" then KillFx.explosion(at, S)
elseif style == "Soul Rise" then KillFx.soul(at, S)
elseif style == "Shockwave" then KillFx.shockwave(at, S)
else KillFx.lightning(at, S) end
end)
if not ok then notify("kill effect failed: " .. tostring(err), 5) end
end
function KillFx.test()
local c = cam()
if not c then return end
KillFx.play(c.CFrame.Position + c.CFrame.LookVector * 30 + Vector3.new(0, -2, 0))
end
function KillFx.set(on)
KillFx.on = on
if not on then
if KillFx.conn then KillFx.conn:Disconnect() KillFx.conn = nil end
return
end
KillFx.r, KillFx.wasDead = rake(), KillFx.isDead(rake())
if KillFx.conn then return end
KillFx.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not KillFx.on then return end
local r = rake()
local dead = KillFx.isDead(r)
if r ~= KillFx.r then
KillFx.r, KillFx.wasDead = r, dead
return
end
if dead and not KillFx.wasDead and r then
local hrp = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") then KillFx.play(hrp.Position) end
end
KillFx.wasDead = dead
end)
addCleanup("killfx", function() KillFx.set(false) end)
end
local Items = { status = "idle" }
function Items.find(name)
local bag = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local tool = bag and bag:FindFirstChild(name)
if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then return tool, false end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
tool = char and char:FindFirstChild(name)
if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then return tool, true end
return nil, false
end
function Items.listens(tool)
if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then return nil end
local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, tool.Activated)
if not ok or type(conns) ~= "table" then return nil end
return #conns > 0, #conns
end
function Items.isOut(tool)
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
return tool and char and tool.Parent == char
end
function Items.probe()
local out = {}
out[#out + 1] = "-- tools you are carrying, and whether they can be used from the bag"
if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then
out[#out + 1] = "getconnections is not available - this cannot be answered here"
end
local seen = 0
for _, where in ipairs({ LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"), LocalPlayer.Character }) do
if where then
for _, t in ipairs(where:GetChildren()) do
if t:IsA("Tool") then
seen = seen + 1
local listens, n = Items.listens(t)
out[#out + 1] = string.format("\n%s  (%s)", t.Name, where.ClassName)
out[#out + 1] = string.format("  Activated listeners: %s",
(listens == nil) and "unreadable" or tostring(n or 0))
out[#out + 1] = string.format("  usable from the bag: %s",
(listens == nil) and "unknown" or (listens and "YES" or "NO"))
local kids = {}
for _, d in ipairs(t:GetChildren()) do
kids[#kids + 1] = d.ClassName .. " " .. d.Name
end
out[#out + 1] = "  children: " .. table.concat(kids, ", ")
end
end
end
end
if seen == 0 then out[#out + 1] = "\nyou are not carrying any tools" end
saveDump("tool-probe.txt", table.concat(out, "\n"))
notify(seen .. " tools checked - see Rake/dumps/tool-probe.txt", 6)
end
local Heal = { on = false, hold = false, at = 55, conn = nil, last = 0, next = 0,
busy = false, status = "off" }
Heal.SETTLE = 0.6
Heal.START_WAIT = 1.5
Heal.QUIET = 0.6
Heal.MAX_HOLD = 12
Heal.AFTER_OK = 3
Heal.AFTER_FAIL = 15
Heal.HOLD_AT = 90
function Heal.value(tool, name)
local v = tool and tool:FindFirstChild(name)
if v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then return v.Value end
return nil
end
function Heal.animating(hum)
local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
local ok, tracks = pcall(function()
if animator then return animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() end
return hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
end)
if not (ok and tracks) then return false end
for _, track in ipairs(tracks) do
local name = tostring(track.Name or "")
if string.find(name, "Heal", 1, true) and not string.find(name, "Stop", 1, true) then
return true
end
end
return false
end
function Heal.blocked(hum)
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if not (char and hum) or hum.Health <= 0 then return "you are dead" end
local downed = char:FindFirstChild("Downed")
if downed and downed:IsA("ValueBase") and downed.Value == true then return "you are downed" end
local tool = Items.find("FirstAidKit")
if not tool then return "no First Aid Kit" end
local uses = tonumber(Heal.value(tool, "Uses"))
if uses and uses <= 0 then return "the First Aid Kit is empty" end
if Heal.value(tool, "Doing") == true then return "the First Aid Kit is already in use" end
if Items.listens(tool) == false then
return "the First Aid Kit does not listen for Activated - it cannot be used from here"
end
return nil
end
function Heal.use()
if Heal.busy then return "busy" end
local hum = getHum()
local why = Heal.blocked(hum)
if why then return why end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local tool, equipped = Items.find("FirstAidKit")
local was = (not equipped) and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or nil
Heal.busy = true
local hp0 = hum.Health
local done0 = Heal.value(tool, "Done") == true
local outcome = "the kit did not start healing"
local ok, err = pcall(function()
if not equipped then hum:EquipTool(tool) end
task.wait(Heal.SETTLE)
if tool.Parent ~= char then outcome = "the kit was put away" return end
if not tool.Enabled then outcome = "the kit is not ready" return end
tool:Activate()
local t0, started, quiet = os.clock(), false, nil
local prog = tonumber(Heal.value(tool, "Progress")) or 0
while true do
task.wait(0.1)
if getHum() ~= hum or hum.Health <= 0 then outcome = "you died" return end
if tool.Parent ~= char then
outcome = (tool.Parent == nil and hum.Health > hp0) and "healed"
or "the kit was put away"
return
end
if not done0 and Heal.value(tool, "Done") == true then outcome = "healed" return end
local nowProg = tonumber(Heal.value(tool, "Progress")) or 0
local running = Heal.value(tool, "Doing") == true or nowProg > prog
or Heal.animating(hum)
prog = nowProg
if running then
started, quiet = true, nil
elseif started then
quiet = quiet or os.clock()
if os.clock() - quiet >= Heal.QUIET then
outcome = (hum.Health > hp0) and "healed" or "the heal was interrupted"
return
end
end
local age = os.clock() - t0
if not started and age > Heal.START_WAIT then
if hum.Health > hp0 + 0.5 then outcome = "healed" end
return
end
if age > Heal.MAX_HOLD then
outcome = (hum.Health > hp0) and "healed" or "the heal took too long"
return
end
end
end)
if not ok then outcome = "could not heal (" .. tostring(err) .. ")" end
if tool.Parent == char then
pcall(function() tool:Deactivate() end)
if not equipped then
pcall(function()
hum:UnequipTools()
if was and was.Parent then hum:EquipTool(was) end
end)
end
elseif tool.Parent == nil and was and was.Parent and not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(was) end)
end
Heal.busy = false
return outcome
end
function Heal.line()
local at = Heal.on and Heal.at or -1
if Heal.hold then at = math.max(at, Heal.HOLD_AT) end
return at
end
function Heal.say(text)
if text == Heal.said then return end
Heal.said = text
notify(text, 5)
end
function Heal.tick()
local hum = getHum()
if not (hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum.MaxHealth > 0) then return end
local pct = hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth * 100
if pct > Heal.line() then
Heal.status = string.format("watching - %d%%", math.floor(pct))
return
end
Heal.status = "healing"
task.spawn(function()
local outcome = Heal.use()
Heal.status = outcome
local now = os.clock()
if outcome == "healed" then
Heal.next, Heal.said = now + Heal.AFTER_OK, nil
local uses = tonumber(Heal.value(Items.find("FirstAidKit"), "Uses"))
notify("Healed" .. (uses and (" - " .. uses .. (uses == 1 and " use" or " uses") .. " left") or ""), 4)
elseif outcome == "you are downed" or outcome == "busy" then
Heal.next = now + 2
else
Heal.next = now + Heal.AFTER_FAIL
Heal.say((Heal.hold and "Hold Health: " or "Auto Heal: ") .. outcome)
end
end)
end
function Heal.watch()
if not (Heal.on or Heal.hold) then
if Heal.conn then Heal.conn:Disconnect() Heal.conn = nil end
Heal.status = "off"
return
end
if Heal.conn then return end
Heal.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not (Heal.on or Heal.hold) or Heal.busy then return end
local now = os.clock()
if now - Heal.last < 0.5 or now < Heal.next then return end
Heal.last = now
Heal.tick()
end)
addCleanup("autoheal", function()
Heal.on, Heal.hold = false, false
Heal.watch()
end)
end
function Heal.set(on)
Heal.on = on
Heal.watch()
end
function Heal.setHold(on)
local was = Heal.hold
Heal.hold = on
if on and not was and not Heal.holdSaid then
Heal.holdSaid = true
notify("Hold Health keeps you topped up with your First Aid Kit: it heals you below "
.. Heal.HOLD_AT .. "%", 6)
end
Heal.watch()
end
local UV = { on = false, status = "off", conn = nil, at = 0, warned = false,
wasHolding = nil }
function UV.lamp()
return (Items.find("UV_Lamp"))
end
function UV.isOn(tool)
local flag = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("On")
return flag and flag:IsA("ValueBase") and flag.Value == true
end
function UV.blocked(tool)
local recharging = tool:FindFirstChild("Recharging")
if recharging and recharging:IsA("ValueBase") and recharging.Value == true then
return "recharging"
end
local canUse = tool:FindFirstChild("CanUse")
if canUse and canUse:IsA("ValueBase") and canUse.Value == false then
return "the lamp says it cannot be used"
end
local power = tool:FindFirstChild("PowerLevel")
if power and power:IsA("ValueBase") and (tonumber(power.Value) or 1) <= 0 then
return "out of power"
end
return nil
end
function UV.set(on)
local was = UV.on
UV.on = on
if not on then
if UV.conn then UV.conn:Disconnect() UV.conn = nil end
if not was then UV.status = "off" return end
local tool = UV.lamp()
local back = UV.wasHolding
UV.wasHolding = nil
if tool then
if UV.isOn(tool) and Items.isOut(tool) then
pcall(function() tool.Enabled = true tool:Activate() end)
end
task.delay(0.3, function()
pcall(function()
local hum = getHum()
if not hum then return end
hum:UnequipTools()
if back and back.Parent then hum:EquipTool(back) end
end)
end)
end
UV.status = "off"
return
end
local tool = UV.lamp()
if not tool then
UV.status = "no UV lamp in your bag"
notify(UV.status, 5)
UV.on = false
return
end
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
UV.wasHolding = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or nil
if not UV.warned then
UV.warned = true
notify("the lamp goes IN YOUR HANDS while this is on - a tool in the bag "
.. "does not run at all. Your weapon comes back when you switch it off.", 9)
end
if UV.conn then return end
UV.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not UV.on then return end
if os.clock() - UV.at < 1 then return end
UV.at = os.clock()
local live = UV.lamp()
if not live then
UV.status = "the lamp is gone"
return
end
if not Items.isOut(live) then
local hum = getHum()
if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(live) end) end
end
local why = UV.blocked(live)
if why then
UV.status = why
if why == "out of power" or why == "recharging" then
notify("UV lamp ran out - swapping back to your weapon", 4)
local tg = Toggles.RK_BindUV
if tg then pcall(function() tg:SetValue(false) end) end
if UV.on then UV.set(false) end
end
return
end
if UV.isOn(live) then
UV.status = "on"
return
end
pcall(function()
live.Enabled = true
live:Activate()
end)
UV.status = "switching it on"
end)
addCleanup("silentuv", function() UV.set(false) end)
end
local Xray = { on = false, level = 0.72, was = {}, conn = nil, at = 0, count = 0 }
Xray.SKIP = { Rake = true, Debris = true, Filter = true, Cameras = true }
function Xray.apply()
local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
if not map then return end
local n = 0
for _, folder in ipairs(map:GetChildren()) do
if not Xray.SKIP[folder.Name] then
for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") and not d:IsA("Terrain") then
if Xray.was[d] == nil then
if d.Transparency >= 0.95 then
Xray.was[d] = false
else
Xray.was[d] = d.Transparency
pcall(function() d.Transparency = Xray.level end)
end
end
if Xray.was[d] ~= false then n = n + 1 end
end
end
end
end
Xray.count = n
end
function Xray.restore()
for part, was in pairs(Xray.was) do
if part and part.Parent and was ~= false then
pcall(function() part.Transparency = was end)
end
end
Xray.was, Xray.count = {}, 0
end
function Xray.set(on)
Xray.on = on
if not on then
if Xray.conn then Xray.conn:Disconnect() Xray.conn = nil end
Xray.restore()
return
end
Xray.apply()
if Xray.conn then return end
Xray.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Xray.on then return end
if os.clock() - Xray.at < 4 then return end
Xray.at = os.clock()
for part in pairs(Xray.was) do if not part.Parent then Xray.was[part] = nil end end
Xray.apply()
end)
addCleanup("xray", function() Xray.set(false) end)
end
local Trail = { on = false, colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60), life = 2.5, conn = nil }
function Trail.build()
local r = rake()
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not (hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart")) then return false end
if Trail.obj and Trail.obj.Parent and Trail.a0 and Trail.a0.Parent then return true end
Trail.a0 = make("Attachment", { Name = "RKTrailA0", Position = Vector3.new(0, 1.4, 0) }, hrp)
Trail.a1 = make("Attachment", { Name = "RKTrailA1", Position = Vector3.new(0, -1.4, 0) }, hrp)
Trail.obj = make("Trail", {
Name = "RKTrail", Attachment0 = Trail.a0, Attachment1 = Trail.a1,
Lifetime = Trail.life, LightEmission = 1, LightInfluence = 0,
MinLength = 0.2, FaceCamera = true,
Color = ColorSequence.new(Trail.colour),
Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.25),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
}),
}, hrp)
return true
end
function Trail.clear()
for _, k in ipairs({ "obj", "a0", "a1" }) do
if Trail[k] then pcall(function() Trail[k]:Destroy() end) Trail[k] = nil end
end
end
function Trail.set(on)
Trail.on = on
if not on then
if Trail.conn then Trail.conn:Disconnect() Trail.conn = nil end
Trail.clear()
return
end
Trail.build()
if Trail.conn then return end
Trail.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Trail.on then return end
if os.clock() - (Trail.at or 0) < 1 then return end
Trail.at = os.clock()
if not (Trail.obj and Trail.obj.Parent) then Trail.build() end
if Trail.obj then
Trail.obj.Color = ColorSequence.new(Trail.colour)
Trail.obj.Lifetime = Trail.life
end
end)
addCleanup("raketrail", function() Trail.set(false) end)
end

local function hudGlass(f, radius, transp)
local white = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
make("ImageLabel", {
Name = "Shadow", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Controls/DropShadow.png",
ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice, SliceCenter = Rect.new(10, 10, 118, 118),
ImageColor3 = Hud.GLASS.shadow, ImageTransparency = 0.6,
Size = UDim2.new(1, 30, 1, 30), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-15, -10), ZIndex = 1,
}, f)
local body = make("Frame", { Name = "Body", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = Hud.GLASS.panel, BackgroundTransparency = transp, ZIndex = 2 }, f)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius) }, body)
make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(white, Color3.fromRGB(226, 234, 250)),
Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.1) }) }, body)
local gloss = make("Frame", { Name = "Gloss", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), BackgroundColor3 = white,
BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 2 }, body)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius) }, gloss)
make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.62), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }) }, gloss)
local rim = make("UIStroke", { Color = white, Thickness = 1.2, Transparency = 0,
ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, body)
make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 40, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, 0.72), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0.86),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.35) }) }, rim)
return body, rim
end
local function hudFrame(parent, pos, size)
local f = make("Frame", {
Name = "Panel", Position = pos, Size = size, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = true, Draggable = true,
}, parent)
local body, rim = hudGlass(f, 12, 0.24)
local accent = make("Frame", {
Name = "Accent", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -16),
BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 4,
}, f)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2) }, accent)
Hud.chrome[f] = { stroke = rim, accent = accent, body = body }
return f
end
local function hudText(parent, pos, size, align)
return make("TextLabel", {
BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = pos, Size = size,
FontFace = Hud.FONT, TextSize = 13, TextColor3 = Hud.GLASS.text,
TextXAlignment = align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
RichText = true, Text = "", ZIndex = 4,
}, parent)
end
