function Hud.applyLook()
if not (Hud.gui and Hud.gui.Parent) then return end
local t = Hud.look()
local minimal = Hud.style == "Minimal"
local bar = Hud.style == "Bar"
for f, c in pairs(Hud.chrome) do
if f and f.Parent then
if c.body then
c.body.BackgroundColor3 = Hud.GLASS.panel
c.body.BackgroundTransparency = minimal and 0.55 or 0.24
end
c.stroke.Enabled = true
c.accent.Visible = bar
c.accent.BackgroundColor3 = t.dim
end
end
if Hud.title then Hud.title.TextColor3 = Hud.GLASS.text end
if Hud.barText then Hud.barText.TextColor3 = Hud.GLASS.text end
end
function Hud.setStyle(v)
Hud.style = v
Hud.applyLook()
end
function Hud.setTheme(v)
Hud.theme = v
Hud.applyLook()
end
function Hud.repairInfo()
local sf = pathTo(Workspace, "Map", "PowerStation", "StationFolder")
local using = sf and sf:FindFirstChild("CharUsing")
local who = (using and using:IsA("ObjectValue")) and using.Value or nil
if not who then Hud.repairS = nil return nil end
local name = tostring(who.Name)
pcall(function()
local plr = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(who)
if plr then name = (plr.DisplayName ~= "" and plr.DisplayName) or plr.Name end
end)
if #name > 14 then name = name:sub(1, 13) .. "." end
local prog = sf:FindFirstChild("Progress")
local p = prog and tonumber(prog.Value)
local eta
if p then
local now = os.clock()
local st = Hud.repairS
if not st or st.who ~= who or p < st.p then
st = { who = who, t = now, p = p }
Hud.repairS = st
end
if now - st.t >= 1 and p > st.p then
eta = (100 - p) / ((p - st.p) / (now - st.t))
else
local total = 30
pcall(function()
local prompt = sf.StationGUIPart:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
local a = prompt and tonumber(prompt:GetAttribute("ActivateTime"))
if a and a > 0 then total = a end
end)
eta = (100 - p) / 100 * total
end
eta = math.max(0, math.floor(eta + 0.5))
end
return { who = name, eta = eta }
end
function Hud.layoutRepair(on)
on = on and true or false
if Hud.repairShown == on then return end
Hud.repairShown = on
local d = on and 16 or 0
if Hud.repairLbl then Hud.repairLbl.Visible = on end
local rn, rv = Hud.rowName and Hud.rowName.rake, Hud.stat and Hud.stat.rake
if rn then rn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 92 + d) end
if rv then rv.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 92 + d) end
if Hud.hpLbl then Hud.hpLbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 120 + d) end
if Hud.barText then Hud.barText.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 120 + d) end
if Hud.barBack then Hud.barBack.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 138 + d) end
if Hud.panel then Hud.panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Hud.WIDTH, 156 + d) end
end
function Hud.statValues()
local v = {}
local fps = math.floor(Hud.fps + 0.5)
v.fps = (fps < 30 and "<font color=\"#D24646\">%d</font>" or (fps < 50 and "<font color=\"#C8901E\">%d</font>" or "%d")):format(fps)
local night = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Night")
local timer = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Timer")
local left = (timer and timer:IsA("ValueBase")) and tonumber(timer.Value) or nil
local isNight = (night and night:IsA("ValueBase")) and night.Value == true
local day = isNight and "Night" or "Day"
if left then
v.time = string.format("%s  %d:%02d", day, math.floor(left / 60), math.floor(left % 60))
else
v.time = day
end
local pv = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("PowerValues")
local level = pv and pv:FindFirstChild("PowerLevel")
local station = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("StationPower")
if level and level:IsA("ValueBase") then
local st = (station and station:IsA("ValueBase")) and (station.Value and "" or "  off") or ""
v.power = tostring(math.floor(tonumber(level.Value) or 0)) .. "%" .. st
else
v.power = "-"
end
local rep = Hud.repairInfo()
if rep then
v.power = rep.who .. " is repairing"
v.repair = rep.eta and string.format("ETA: %d sec", rep.eta) or "ETA: working it out"
end
local r = rake()
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hrp and not hrp:IsA("BasePart") then hrp = nil end
local me = myPos()
if r and hrp and me then
local d = (hrp.Position - me).Magnitude
local chasing = false
local tv = r:FindFirstChild("TargetVal")
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
pcall(function()
if tv and tv.Value and char then chasing = (tv.Value == char) or tv.Value:IsDescendantOf(char) end
end)
local text = tostring(math.floor(d)) .. "m" .. (chasing and "  chasing you" or "")
local col = (chasing or d < 60) and "D24646" or (d < 150 and "C8901E" or nil)
v.rake = col and string.format("<font color=\"#%s\">%s</font>", col, text) or text
else
v.rake = "not out"
end
return v
end
function Hud.rakeBar(dt)
if not (Hud.barFill and Hud.hp) then return end
dt = math.min(dt or 1 / 60, 0.1)
local s = Hud.hp
local r = rake()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local alive = hum and hum.MaxHealth > 0
local target = alive and math.clamp(Fight.rakeHp(hum) / hum.MaxHealth, 0, 1) or 0
if target < s.target - 0.002 then s.hold, s.flash = 0.45, 1 end
s.target = target
s.shown = s.shown + (target - s.shown) * (1 - math.exp(-dt * 9))
if s.hold > 0 then
s.hold = s.hold - dt
else
s.trail = s.trail + (s.shown - s.trail) * (1 - math.exp(-dt * 3))
end
if s.trail < s.shown then s.trail = s.shown end
s.flash = math.max(0, s.flash - dt * 4)
Hud.barFill.Size = UDim2.new(s.shown, 0, 1, 0)
Hud.barTrail.Size = UDim2.new(s.trail, 0, 1, 0)
local t = Hud.look()
local col = (s.shown > 0.5) and t.warn:Lerp(t.good, (s.shown - 0.5) / 0.5) or t.bad:Lerp(t.warn, s.shown / 0.5)
Hud.barFill.BackgroundColor3 = col:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), s.flash * 0.65)
if alive then
Hud.barText.Text = string.format("%d / %d", math.floor(s.shown * hum.MaxHealth + 0.5), math.floor(hum.MaxHealth))
else
Hud.barText.Text = "no Rake"
end
end
Hud.CONFIG = {
RK_Hud = true,
RK_ShowDistance = true,
RK_OrbRainbow = true,
RK_SilentRing = true,
RK_FovCircle = true,
RK_ReachRing = true,
RK_FarRender = true,
RK_NoSwingAnim = true,
}
function Hud.isConfig(idx)
if Hud.CONFIG[idx] then return true end
return idx:sub(1, 7) == "RK_Farm"
end
function Hud.modInfo(t)
local f = t and t._feature
if not (f and f.popupControls) then return nil end
for _, c in ipairs(f.popupControls) do
if c.Type == "Dropdown" and not c.Multi and type(c.Value) == "string" and c.Value ~= "" then
return c.Value
end
end
return nil
end
Hud.NO_SUFFIX = { RK_RakeEsp = true, RK_PlayerEsp = true }
function Hud.modules()
local out = {}
for idx, t in pairs(Toggles) do
if t and t.Value and not Hud.isConfig(idx) and t.Type == "Toggle" then
local text = t.Text
if type(text) == "string" and text ~= "" then
out[#out + 1] = { name = text, info = (not Hud.NO_SUFFIX[idx]) and Hud.modInfo(t) or nil }
end
end
end
local function w(m) return #m.name + (m.info and (#m.info * 0.85 + 2) or 0) end
table.sort(out, function(x, y)
local a, b = w(x), w(y)
if a ~= b then return a > b end
return x.name < y.name
end)
return out
end
Hud.MARK_ON = "+"
Hud.MARK_OFF = "-"
Hud.MARK_ACT = ">"
function Hud.bindState(o)
if o.Mode == "Press" then return "action" end
local ok, state = pcall(function() return o:GetState() end)
if not ok then state = o.Toggled end
return state and "on" or "off"
end
Hud.BIND_SKIP = { RK_MenuKey = true }
function Hud.binds()
local rows = {}
for idx, o in pairs(Options) do
if o and o.Type == "KeyPicker" and o.Value and o.Value ~= "None"
and not Hud.BIND_SKIP[idx] then
local name = o.Text
if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
local key = tostring(o.Value)
rows[#rows + 1] = {
plain = "x " .. name .. "  [" .. key .. "]",
name = name, key = key, state = Hud.bindState(o),
}
end
end
end
table.sort(rows, function(x, y)
if #x.plain ~= #y.plain then return #x.plain > #y.plain end
return x.plain < y.plain
end)
local keyCol = "9AA2AE"
local marks = {
on = { Hud.MARK_ON, "3CB46E" },
off = { Hud.MARK_OFF, "D24646" },
action = { Hud.MARK_ACT, "5B78C0" },
}
local out = {}
for i, r in ipairs(rows) do
local m = marks[r.state] or marks.off
out[i] = string.format(
"<font color=\"#%s\">%s</font> %s  <font color=\"#%s\">[%s]</font>",
m[2], m[1], r.name, keyCol, r.key)
end
return out
end
Hud.MOD_H = 21
Hud.modChips = {}
function Hud.richEscape(str)
return (tostring(str):gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;"))
end
function Hud.modChip(name)
local r = Hud.modChips[name]
if r and r.frame.Parent then return r end
local frame = make("Frame", {
Name = "Mod_" .. name, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0),
Position = UDim2.new(1, 240, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(80, Hud.MOD_H - 3),
BackgroundColor3 = Hud.GLASS.panel, BackgroundTransparency = 0.2, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false,
}, Hud.modFrame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7) }, frame)
make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(226, 234, 250)) }, frame)
make("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.25,
ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, frame)
local label = make("TextLabel", {
BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -17, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 0),
FontFace = Hud.FONT_B, TextSize = 12, RichText = true,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
TextColor3 = Hud.GLASS.text, Text = "",
}, frame)
local accent = make("Frame", {
Name = "Accent", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -8), BorderSizePixel = 0,
}, frame)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, accent)
r = { frame = frame, label = label, accent = accent }
Hud.modChips[name] = r
return r
end
function Hud.paintModules(mods)
local minimal = Hud.style == "Minimal"
local widest = 0
local alive = {}
for i, m in ipairs(mods) do
local r = Hud.modChip(m.name)
alive[m.name] = true
local text = m.info and (Hud.richEscape(m.name) .. "  <font color=\"#8C94A4\">" .. Hud.richEscape(m.info) .. "</font>")
or Hud.richEscape(m.name)
if r.text ~= text then r.label.Text = text; r.text = text end
r.label.TextColor3 = Hud.GLASS.text
local a = (#mods > 1) and ((i - 1) / (#mods - 1)) or 0
r.accent.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 76, 90):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(190, 196, 208), a)
r.frame.BackgroundColor3 = Hud.GLASS.panel
r.frame.BackgroundTransparency = minimal and 0.5 or 0.2
local w = math.max(r.label.TextBounds.X + 24, 36)
if w > widest then widest = w end
r.frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, Hud.MOD_H - 3)
local y = (i - 1) * Hud.MOD_H
if r.gone or r.slot == nil then
r.gone = false
r.frame.Visible = true
r.frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, w + 24, 0, y)
TweenService:Create(r.frame, TweenInfo.new(0.38, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), { Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, y) }):Play()
elseif r.slot ~= y then
TweenService:Create(r.frame, TweenInfo.new(0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), { Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, y) }):Play()
end
r.slot = y
end
for name, r in pairs(Hud.modChips) do
if not alive[name] and not r.gone and r.slot ~= nil then
r.gone = true
local w = r.frame.AbsoluteSize.X
TweenService:Create(r.frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
{ Position = UDim2.new(1, w + 24, 0, r.slot) }):Play()
task.delay(0.3, function()
if r.gone then r.frame.Visible = false; r.slot = nil end
end)
end
end
Hud.modFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(widest, 40), math.max(#mods, 1) * Hud.MOD_H)
end
function Hud.setPanels(set)
set = set or {}
Hud.showStats = set.Stats and true or false
Hud.showModules = set.Modules and true or false
Hud.showBinds = set.Keybinds and true or false
if Hud.on then pcall(Hud.paint) end
end
function Hud.paint()
if Hud.panel then Hud.panel.Visible = Hud.showStats ~= false end
if Hud.modFrame then Hud.modFrame.Visible = Hud.showModules ~= false end
if Hud.showStats ~= false then
local v = Hud.statValues()
if Hud.stat then
if Hud.stat.fps then Hud.stat.fps.Text = v.fps end
if Hud.stat.time then Hud.stat.time.Text = v.time end
if Hud.stat.power then Hud.stat.power.Text = v.power end
Hud.layoutRepair(v.repair ~= nil)
if v.repair and Hud.repairLbl then Hud.repairLbl.Text = v.repair end
if Hud.stat.rake then Hud.stat.rake.Text = v.rake end
end
end
if Hud.showModules ~= false then Hud.paintModules(Hud.modules()) end
local binds = Hud.binds()
if Hud.showBinds ~= false and #binds > 0 then
Hud.bindList.Text = table.concat(binds, "\n")
Hud.bindFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Hud.WIDTH, #binds * 16 + 12)
Hud.bindFrame.Visible = true
else
Hud.bindFrame.Visible = false
end
if Hud.farmFrame then
local show = Farm.statsCard and (Farm.on or Farm.stats.time >= 1)
Hud.farmFrame.Visible = show and true or false
if show then
local v = Farm.statValues()
for k, lbl in pairs(Hud.farmStat) do lbl.Text = v[k] or "-" end
Hud.farmState.Text = v.state
end
end
end
Hud.POS_FILE = "Ruv&Rill/hud.txt"
Hud.POS_KEYS = { "panel", "modFrame", "bindFrame", "farmFrame" }
function Hud.posLine()
local out = {}
for _, key in ipairs(Hud.POS_KEYS) do
local f = Hud[key]
if f and f.Parent then
local p = f.Position
out[#out + 1] = string.format("%s=%g,%g,%g,%g", key,
p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset)
end
end
return table.concat(out, "\n")
end
function Hud.savePos()
if Hud.sliding or not Hud.on or Hud.isOut then return end
if typeof(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
local line = Hud.posLine()
if line == "" or line == Hud.posSaved then return end
Hud.posSaved = line
pcall(ensureFolder, "Ruv&Rill")
pcall(writefile, Hud.POS_FILE, line)
end
function Hud.loadPos()
if typeof(isfile) ~= "function" or typeof(readfile) ~= "function" then return end
local ok, has = pcall(isfile, Hud.POS_FILE)
if not (ok and has) then return end
local got, body = pcall(readfile, Hud.POS_FILE)
if not (got and type(body) == "string") then return end
for line in body:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
local key, xs, xo, ys, yo =
line:match("^(%a+)=([%-%d%.e+]+),([%-%d%.e+]+),([%-%d%.e+]+),([%-%d%.e+]+)$")
local f = key and Hud[key]
if f and f.Parent then
local sx, ox = tonumber(xs), tonumber(xo)
local sy, oy = tonumber(ys), tonumber(yo)
if sx and ox and sy and oy then
if math.abs(sx) < 10 and math.abs(sy) < 10 and math.abs(ox) < 1e5 and math.abs(oy) < 1e5 then
f.Position = UDim2.new(sx, ox, sy, oy)
Hud.home[f] = f.Position
end
end
end
end
Hud.posSaved = Hud.posLine()
end
Hud.EDGE = 4
function Hud.clampFrame(f)
if not (f and f.Parent and f.Visible and Hud.gui) then return false end
local view = Hud.gui.AbsoluteSize
if view.X <= 0 or view.Y <= 0 then return false end
local pos = f.AbsolutePosition - Hud.gui.AbsolutePosition
local size = f.AbsoluteSize
local e = Hud.EDGE
local x = math.clamp(pos.X, e, math.max(e, view.X - size.X - e))
local y = math.clamp(pos.Y, e, math.max(e, view.Y - size.Y - e))
local dx, dy = math.floor(x - pos.X + 0.5), math.floor(y - pos.Y + 0.5)
if dx == 0 and dy == 0 then return false end
f.Position = f.Position + UDim2.fromOffset(dx, dy)
return true
end
function Hud.clampAll()
if Hud.sliding or Hud.isOut or not Hud.on then return end
for _, key in ipairs(Hud.POS_KEYS) do
local f = Hud[key]
if Hud.clampFrame(f) then Hud.home[f] = f.Position end
end
end
function Hud.sideOf(f)
return (f == Hud.modFrame) and 1 or -1
end
function Hud.offPos(f, home)
local w = f.AbsoluteSize.X
if w <= 0 then w = Hud.WIDTH end
return UDim2.new(home.X.Scale, home.X.Offset + Hud.sideOf(f) * (w + 80),
home.Y.Scale, home.Y.Offset)
end
function Hud.slide(inward, done)
local wasSliding = Hud.sliding
Hud.sliding = true
local frames = { Hud.panel, Hud.modFrame, Hud.bindFrame, Hud.farmFrame }
local last
for _, f in ipairs(frames) do
if f and f.Parent then
local from, to
if inward then
local home = Hud.home[f] or f.Position
Hud.home[f] = home
from, to = (wasSliding or Hud.isOut) and f.Position or Hud.offPos(f, home), home
else
local home = ((wasSliding or Hud.isOut) and Hud.home[f]) or f.Position
Hud.home[f] = home
from, to = f.Position, Hud.offPos(f, home)
end
f.Position = from
local tw = TweenService:Create(f, Hud.SLIDE, { Position = to })
tw:Play()
last = tw
end
end
Hud.isOut = not inward
if not last then
Hud.sliding = false
if done then done() end
return
end
last.Completed:Connect(function(state)
if state ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then return end
Hud.sliding = false
if done then done() end
end)
end
function Hud.set(on)
Hud.on = on
if on then
Hud.build()
Hud.gui.Enabled = not (Library and Library.Toggled)
Hud.applyLook()
pcall(Hud.paint)
if not (Library and Library.Toggled) then Hud.slide(true) end
else
if Hud.conn then Hud.conn:Disconnect() Hud.conn = nil end
if not (Hud.gui and Hud.gui.Parent) then return end
Hud.slide(false, function()
if not Hud.on and Hud.gui then Hud.gui.Enabled = false end
end)
return
end
if Hud.conn then return end
Hud.frames, Hud.fpsAt = 0, os.clock()
Hud.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
if not Hud.on or not Hud.label then return end
if Hud.showStats ~= false then pcall(Hud.rakeBar, dt) end
pcall(Hud.clampAll)
Hud.frames = Hud.frames + 1
local now = os.clock()
if now - Hud.fpsAt >= 0.5 then
Hud.fps = Hud.frames / (now - Hud.fpsAt)
Hud.frames, Hud.fpsAt = 0, now
end
if now - Hud.last < 0.25 then return end
Hud.last = now
local ok, err = pcall(Hud.paint)
if not ok then Hud.label.Text = "hud error: " .. tostring(err) end
if now - (Hud.posAt or 0) >= 2 then
Hud.posAt = now
pcall(Hud.savePos)
end
end)
addCleanup("hud", function() Hud.set(false) end)
end
local Alerts = { on = false }
function Alerts.scrapCount()
local ok, n = pcall(function()
local c = 0
for _, sp in ipairs(Farm.scraps()) do
if not sp.prize then c = c + 1 end
end
return c
end)
return ok and n or 0
end
function Alerts.set(on)
Alerts.on = on
if not on then
for _, c in ipairs(Alerts.conns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
Alerts.conns = nil
return
end
if Alerts.conns then return end
Alerts.conns = {}
local traps = pathTo(Workspace, "Debris", "Traps")
if traps then
Alerts.conns[#Alerts.conns + 1] = traps.ChildAdded:Connect(function(m)
task.wait(0.15)
if not Alerts.on then return end
local rusty, who = false, nil
pcall(function() rusty = m:GetAttribute("ServerSpawned") == true end)
pcall(function() who = m:GetAttribute("Player") end)
if who == LocalPlayer.Name then return end
notify(rusty and "a rusty trap spawned"
or (type(who) == "string" and (who .. " placed a trap")) or "a trap was placed", 4)
end)
end
local crates = pathTo(Workspace, "Debris", "SupplyCrates")
if crates then
Alerts.conns[#Alerts.conns + 1] = crates.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
if Alerts.on then notify("a supply drop landed", 4) end
end)
end
Alerts.flareWas = flarePart() ~= nil
Alerts.scrapWas = Alerts.scrapCount()
Alerts.conns[#Alerts.conns + 1] = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Alerts.on then return end
if os.clock() - (Alerts.at or 0) < 1 then return end
Alerts.at = os.clock()
local flareNow = flarePart() ~= nil
if flareNow and not Alerts.flareWas then notify("the flare gun spawned", 4) end
Alerts.flareWas = flareNow
local sc = Alerts.scrapCount()
if sc > (Alerts.scrapWas or 0) then
notify(string.format("scrap spawned (%d around)", sc), 3)
end
Alerts.scrapWas = sc
end)
addCleanup("spawnalerts", function() Alerts.set(false) end)
end
local Orbs = {
on = false, count = 6, radius = 7, speed = 1.4, size = 0.8, height = 3,
colour = Color3.fromRGB(120, 190, 255), rainbow = false, hue = 0,
spin = 0, parts = {}, folder = nil,
}
function Orbs.build()
if Orbs.folder and Orbs.folder.Parent then return end
Orbs.folder = make("Folder", { Name = "RKOrbs" }, Workspace)
Orbs.parts = {}
end
function Orbs.clear()
for _, p in ipairs(Orbs.parts) do pcall(function() p:Destroy() end) end
Orbs.parts = {}
if Orbs.folder then
pcall(function() Orbs.folder:Destroy() end)
Orbs.folder = nil
end
end
addCleanup("orbs", function() Orbs.clear() end)
function Orbs.fit()
Orbs.build()
while #Orbs.parts > Orbs.count do
local p = table.remove(Orbs.parts)
pcall(function() p:Destroy() end)
end
while #Orbs.parts < Orbs.count do
local p = make("Part", {
Name = "Orb", Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball, Anchored = true,
CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = Orbs.colour,
Size = Vector3.new(Orbs.size, Orbs.size, Orbs.size),
}, Orbs.folder)
make("PointLight", { Brightness = 2, Range = 10, Color = Orbs.colour }, p)
Orbs.parts[#Orbs.parts + 1] = p
end
end
function Orbs.set(on)
Orbs.on = on
if not on then
if Orbs.conn then Orbs.conn:Disconnect() Orbs.conn = nil end
Orbs.clear()
return
end
Orbs.fit()
if Orbs.conn then return end
Orbs.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
if not Orbs.on then return end
local r = rake()
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then
for _, p in ipairs(Orbs.parts) do
p.Transparency = 1
local light = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("PointLight")
if light then light.Enabled = false end
end
return
end
if #Orbs.parts ~= Orbs.count or not (Orbs.folder and Orbs.folder.Parent) then Orbs.fit() end
Orbs.spin = (Orbs.spin + dt * Orbs.speed) % (math.pi * 2)
local colour = Orbs.colour
if Orbs.rainbow then
Orbs.hue = ((Orbs.hue or 0) + dt * 0.35) % 1
colour = Color3.fromHSV(Orbs.hue, 0.9, 1)
end
local centre = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, Orbs.height, 0)
for i, p in ipairs(Orbs.parts) do
local turn = Orbs.spin + (math.pi * 2) * ((i - 1) / math.max(#Orbs.parts, 1))
local bob = math.sin(turn * 2 + Orbs.spin * 2) * (Orbs.radius * 0.18)
p.Position = centre + Vector3.new(
math.cos(turn) * Orbs.radius, bob, math.sin(turn) * Orbs.radius)
p.Size = Vector3.new(Orbs.size, Orbs.size, Orbs.size)
p.Transparency = 0
p.Color = colour
local light = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("PointLight")
if light then light.Color = colour light.Enabled = true end
end
end)
addCleanup("orbsloop", function()
if Orbs.conn then Orbs.conn:Disconnect() Orbs.conn = nil end
end)
end
local AURA_STYLES = { "Hearts", "Stars", "Diamonds", "Crosses", "Rings", "Sparkles", "Smoke", "Fire" }
local ORBIT_STYLES = { "Ring", "Sphere", "Vertical", "Helix", "Pulse" }
local AURA_TEXTURES = {
Smoke = "rbxasset://textures/particles/smoke_main.dds",
Fire  = "rbxasset://textures/particles/fire_main.dds",
}
local AURA_SHAPES = {
Hearts = {
{ w = 1, h = 1, x = 0,       y = -0.0735, turn = 45, round = false },
{ w = 1, h = 1, x = -0.3536, y = 0.2801,  turn = 0,  round = true },
{ w = 1, h = 1, x = 0.3536,  y = 0.2801,  turn = 0,  round = true },
},
Stars = {
{ w = 1, h = 0.24, x = 0, y = 0, turn = 0,   round = false },
{ w = 1, h = 0.24, x = 0, y = 0, turn = 60,  round = false },
{ w = 1, h = 0.24, x = 0, y = 0, turn = 120, round = false },
},
Diamonds = {
{ w = 1, h = 1, x = 0, y = 0, turn = 45, round = false },
},
Crosses = {
{ w = 1, h = 0.26, x = 0, y = 0, turn = 0, round = false },
{ w = 0.26, h = 1, x = 0, y = 0, turn = 0, round = false },
},
}
local function orbitPos(orbit, i, n, radius, spin, centre)
local base = (math.pi * 2) * ((i - 1) / math.max(n, 1))
local turn = spin + base
if orbit == "Sphere" then
local phi = math.acos(1 - 2 * ((i - 0.5) / math.max(n, 1)))
local theta = spin * 1.3 + base * 3
return centre + Vector3.new(
radius * math.sin(phi) * math.cos(theta),
radius * math.cos(phi),
radius * math.sin(phi) * math.sin(theta))
elseif orbit == "Vertical" then
local yaw = spin * 0.5
local x, y = math.cos(turn) * radius, math.sin(turn) * radius
return centre + Vector3.new(x * math.cos(yaw), y, x * math.sin(yaw))
elseif orbit == "Helix" then
local strand = (i % 2 == 0) and 0 or math.pi
local ang = spin * 1.5 + strand + math.floor((i - 1) / 2) * 0.6
local y = (((i - 1) / math.max(n - 1, 1)) - 0.5) * radius * 2.2
return centre + Vector3.new(math.cos(ang) * radius * 0.8, y,
math.sin(ang) * radius * 0.8)
elseif orbit == "Pulse" then
local r = radius * (0.55 + 0.45 * math.sin(spin * 2))
local bob = math.sin(turn * 2 + spin * 2) * (radius * 0.16)
return centre + Vector3.new(math.cos(turn) * r, bob, math.sin(turn) * r)
end
local bob = math.sin(turn * 2 + spin * 2) * (radius * 0.16)
return centre + Vector3.new(math.cos(turn) * radius, bob, math.sin(turn) * radius)
end
local function makeGlow(A)
A.glowPart = make("Part", {
Name = "AuraGlow", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false,
CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false, Transparency = 1,
Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
}, A.folder)
A.glow = make("PointLight", {
Color = A.colour, Range = 12, Brightness = 0, Shadows = false,
}, A.glowPart)
end
local function updateGlow(A, centre)
if not (A.glow and A.glowPart and A.glowPart.Parent) then return end
A.glowPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(centre)
A.glow.Color = A.colour
local b = A.bloom or 0
A.glow.Enabled = b > 0.02
A.glow.Brightness = b * 4
A.glow.Range = 8 + b * 20
end
local Aura = {
on = false, style = "Hearts", colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 95, 150),
imageId = "", count = 8, radius = 4.2, speed = 1.1, size = 1.15,
height = 0.4, thick = 0.35, spin = 0, orbit = "Ring", intensity = 1, bloom = 0,
built = nil, painted = nil,
sprites = {}, rings = {}, folder = nil, anchor = nil, emitter = nil,
conn = nil,
}
local function auraKey()
if Aura.imageId ~= "" then return "image|" .. Aura.imageId end
return Aura.style
end
function Aura.build()
if Aura.folder and Aura.folder.Parent then return end
Aura.folder = make("Folder", { Name = "RKAura" }, Workspace)
Aura.sprites, Aura.rings = {}, {}
Aura.anchor, Aura.emitter = nil, nil
Aura.built, Aura.painted = nil, nil
end
function Aura.clear()
if Aura.folder then pcall(function() Aura.folder:Destroy() end) end
Aura.folder, Aura.anchor, Aura.emitter = nil, nil, nil
Aura.glow, Aura.glowPart = nil, nil
Aura.sprites, Aura.rings = {}, {}
Aura.built, Aura.painted = nil, nil
end
addCleanup("aura", function() Aura.set(false) end)
function Aura.fit()
Aura.build()
local key = auraKey()
if Aura.built == key then return end
for _, pieces in ipairs(Aura.sprites) do
for _, piece in ipairs(pieces) do pcall(function() piece.part:Destroy() end) end
end
for _, r in ipairs(Aura.rings) do pcall(function() r:Destroy() end) end
if Aura.anchor then pcall(function() Aura.anchor:Destroy() end) end
if Aura.glowPart then pcall(function() Aura.glowPart:Destroy() end) end
Aura.sprites, Aura.rings = {}, {}
Aura.anchor, Aura.emitter, Aura.painted = nil, nil, nil
Aura.glow, Aura.glowPart = nil, nil
local shape = (Aura.imageId == "") and AURA_SHAPES[Aura.style] or nil
if shape then
for _ = 1, Aura.count do
local pieces = {}
for _, spec in ipairs(shape) do
local part = make("Part", {
Name = "AuraPiece", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = Aura.colour,
Size = Vector3.new(spec.w * Aura.size, spec.h * Aura.size,
Aura.thick * Aura.size),
}, Aura.folder)
if spec.round then
make("SpecialMesh", { MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Sphere }, part)
end
pieces[#pieces + 1] = {
part = part,
off = CFrame.new(spec.x * Aura.size, spec.y * Aura.size, 0)
* CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(spec.turn)),
}
end
Aura.sprites[#Aura.sprites + 1] = pieces
end
elseif Aura.imageId == "" and Aura.style == "Rings" then
for _ = 1, 3 do
local ring = make("Part", {
Name = "AuraRing", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = Aura.colour,
Transparency = 0.3, Size = Vector3.new(4, 0.12, 4),
}, Aura.folder)
make("CylinderMesh", {}, ring)
Aura.rings[#Aura.rings + 1] = ring
end
else
local anchor = make("Part", {
Name = "AuraAnchor", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Transparency = 1, Size = Vector3.new(3.5, 5, 3.5),
}, Aura.folder)
local props = {
Name = "AuraParticles",
Color = ColorSequence.new(Aura.colour),
Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1 - Aura.intensity * 0.75),
Size = NumberSequence.new(Aura.size * 1.4),
Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.9, 1.5),
Rate = math.clamp(Aura.count * 4, 8, 120),
Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 3),
SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(40, 40),
Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 180),
RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-40, 40),
LightEmission = Aura.intensity,
Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0),
ZOffset = 1,
}
if Aura.imageId ~= "" then
props.Texture = Aura.imageId
elseif AURA_TEXTURES[Aura.style] then
props.Texture = AURA_TEXTURES[Aura.style]
end
Aura.anchor = anchor
Aura.emitter = make("ParticleEmitter", props, anchor)
end
makeGlow(Aura)
Aura.built = key
Aura.painted = nil
end
function Aura.paint()
local col = Aura.colour
for _, pieces in ipairs(Aura.sprites) do
for _, piece in ipairs(pieces) do piece.part.Color = col end
end
for _, r in ipairs(Aura.rings) do r.Color = col end
if Aura.emitter then Aura.emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(col) end
Aura.painted = col
end
function Aura.setStyle(style)
Aura.style = style
if Aura.on then Aura.fit() end
end
function Aura.setOrbit(o)
Aura.orbit = o
end
function Aura.setSize(v)
Aura.size = v
Aura.built = nil
if Aura.on then Aura.fit() end
end
function Aura.setCount(v)
Aura.count = math.max(1, math.floor(v))
Aura.built = nil
if Aura.on then Aura.fit() end
end
function Aura.setIntensity(v)
Aura.intensity = v
if Aura.emitter then
Aura.emitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1 - v * 0.75)
Aura.emitter.LightEmission = v
end
end
function Aura.setBloom(v)
Aura.bloom = v
end
function Aura.setImage(id)
id = tostring(id or ""):gsub("%s", "")
if id ~= "" and not id:find("rbxasset") then
local digits = id:gsub("%D", "")
id = digits ~= "" and ("rbxassetid://" .. digits) or ""
end
local was = Aura.imageId
Aura.imageId = id
if Aura.on then Aura.fit() end
if id == was then return end
notify(id ~= ""
and ("aura image " .. id .. " - clear the box to go back to " .. Aura.style)
or ("aura back on " .. Aura.style), 5)
end
function Aura.set(on)
Aura.on = on
if not on then
if Aura.conn then Aura.conn:Disconnect() Aura.conn = nil end
Aura.clear()
return
end
Aura.fit()
if Aura.conn then return end
Aura.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
if not Aura.on then return end
local hrp, c = getHRP(), cam()
if not (hrp and c) then
for _, pieces in ipairs(Aura.sprites) do
for _, piece in ipairs(pieces) do piece.part.Transparency = 1 end
end
for _, r in ipairs(Aura.rings) do r.Transparency = 1 end
if Aura.emitter and Aura.emitter.Enabled then Aura.emitter.Enabled = false end
return
end
if Aura.built ~= auraKey() or not (Aura.folder and Aura.folder.Parent) then
Aura.fit()
end
if Aura.painted ~= Aura.colour then Aura.paint() end
Aura.spin = (Aura.spin + dt * Aura.speed) % (math.pi * 2)
local eye = c.CFrame.Position
local centre = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, Aura.height, 0)
for i, pieces in ipairs(Aura.sprites) do
local pos = orbitPos(Aura.orbit, i, #Aura.sprites, Aura.radius, Aura.spin, centre)
local dir = eye - pos
if dir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
local face = (math.abs(dir.Unit.Y) > 0.99)
and CFrame.lookAt(pos, eye, c.CFrame.UpVector)
or CFrame.lookAt(pos, eye)
for _, piece in ipairs(pieces) do
piece.part.CFrame = face * piece.off
fadeAtEye(piece.part, 1 - Aura.intensity, 6)
end
end
end
if #Aura.rings > 0 then
local base = hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
local count = #Aura.rings
for i, r in ipairs(Aura.rings) do
local phase = ((Aura.spin / (math.pi * 2)) + (i - 1) / count) % 1
local width = 2 + phase * 5.5
r.Size = Vector3.new(width, 0.12, width)
r.CFrame = CFrame.new(base + Vector3.new(0, phase * 5.2, 0))
fadeAtEye(r, 1 - (0.75 - phase * 0.7) * Aura.intensity, 5)
end
end
updateGlow(Aura, centre)
if Aura.anchor then
Aura.anchor.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position)
if Aura.emitter and not Aura.emitter.Enabled then Aura.emitter.Enabled = true end
end
end)
addCleanup("auraloop", function()
if Aura.conn then Aura.conn:Disconnect() Aura.conn = nil end
end)
end

RakeAura.radius, RakeAura.spin, centre)

and CFrame.lookAt(pos, eye, c.CFrame.UpVector)
or CFrame.lookAt(pos, eye)

piece.part.CFrame = face * piece.off
fadeAtEye(piece.part, 1 - RakeAura.intensity, 6)
end
end
end
if #RakeAura.rings > 0 then
local base = rh.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
local count = #RakeAura.rings

r.Size = Vector3.new(width, 0.12, width)
r.CFrame = CFrame.new(base + Vector3.new(0, phase * 5.2, 0))
fadeAtEye(r, 1 - (0.75 - phase * 0.7) * RakeAura.intensity, 5)
end

updateGlow(RakeAura, centre)
if RakeAura.anchor then
RakeAura.anchor.CFrame = CFrame.new(rh.Position)
if RakeAura.emitter and not RakeAura.emitter.Enabled then RakeAura.emitter.Enabled = true end
end

local Jump = {
on = false, colour = Color3.fromRGB(255, 95, 150), life = 0.55,
from = 1.5, to = 13, rise = 1.2, last = 0, rings = {}, folder = nil,
conn = nil, humConn = nil, charConn = nil,
}
function Jump.build()
if Jump.folder and Jump.folder.Parent then return end
Jump.folder = make("Folder", { Name = "RKJumpFx" }, Workspace)
Jump.rings = {}
end
function Jump.clear()
if Jump.folder then pcall(function() Jump.folder:Destroy() end) end
Jump.folder, Jump.rings = nil, {}
end
function Jump.pop()
if not Jump.on then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
local now = os.clock()
if now - Jump.last < 0.12 then return end
Jump.last = now
Jump.build()
if #Jump.rings >= 10 then
local old = table.remove(Jump.rings, 1)
if old then pcall(function() old.part:Destroy() end) end
end
local base = CFrame.new(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.8, 0))
local part = make("Part", {
Name = "JumpRing", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false,
CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Color = Jump.colour, Transparency = 0.15,
Size = Vector3.new(Jump.from, 0.15, Jump.from), CFrame = base,
}, Jump.folder)
make("CylinderMesh", {}, part)
Jump.rings[#Jump.rings + 1] = { part = part, base = base, t0 = now }
end
function Jump.attach(hum)
if Jump.humConn then Jump.humConn:Disconnect() Jump.humConn = nil end
if not hum then return end
Jump.humConn = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then Jump.pop() end
end)
end
function Jump.set(on)
Jump.on = on
if not on then
if Jump.humConn then Jump.humConn:Disconnect() Jump.humConn = nil end
if Jump.charConn then Jump.charConn:Disconnect() Jump.charConn = nil end
if Jump.conn then Jump.conn:Disconnect() Jump.conn = nil end
Jump.clear()
return
end
Jump.build()
Jump.attach(getHum())
if not Jump.charConn then
Jump.charConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
task.spawn(function()
local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
if Jump.on then Jump.attach(hum) end
end)
end)
end
if Jump.conn then return end
Jump.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if #Jump.rings == 0 then return end
local now = os.clock()
for i = #Jump.rings, 1, -1 do
local r = Jump.rings[i]
local alpha = (now - r.t0) / Jump.life
if alpha >= 1 or not r.part.Parent then
pcall(function() r.part:Destroy() end)
table.remove(Jump.rings, i)
else
local ease = 1 - (1 - alpha) ^ 3
local width = Jump.from + (Jump.to - Jump.from) * ease
r.part.Size = Vector3.new(width, 0.15, width)
r.part.CFrame = r.base + Vector3.new(0, Jump.rise * ease, 0)
fadeAtEye(r.part, 0.15 + 0.85 * ease, 5)
r.part.Color = Jump.colour
end
end
end)
addCleanup("jumpfx", function() Jump.set(false) end)
end

local booted = false
local function setBackgroundFade(value)
pcall(function()
local main = Library.ScreenGui and Library.ScreenGui:FindFirstChild("Main")
if not main then return end
for _, v in ipairs(main:GetChildren()) do
if v:IsA("ImageLabel") and v.Size == UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) then
v.ImageTransparency = value
break
end
end
end)
end
local function liveLabel()
return { SetText = function() end, SetVisible = function() end,
AddKeyPicker = function(self) return self end }
end
local Grab = {
flare = false, scrap = false,
busy = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
tries = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
status = "off", flareLast = 0, scrapLast = 0,
TRIES = 3,
NEAR = 12,
}
function Grab.touch(part)
local hrp = getHRP()
if not (hrp and part and part.Parent) then return false end
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return false end
if Grab.busy[part] then return false end
Grab.busy[part] = true
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function()
firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 0)
task.wait()
firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 1)
end)
Grab.busy[part] = nil
end)
return true
end
function Grab.canMove()
if Move.fly or Free.on or Ex.hidden or Teleport.busy then return false end
local hrp = getHRP()
return hrp ~= nil and not hrp.Anchored
end
function Grab.fetch(part, label)
if not (part and part.Parent) then return end
local me = myPos()
if not me then return end
local away = (part.Position - me).Magnitude
if away <= Grab.NEAR then
Grab.touch(part)
Grab.status = "on the " .. label
return
end
if not Grab.canMove() then return end
Teleport.go(CFrame.new(part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)))
Grab.status = "going to the " .. label
task.spawn(function()
local t0 = os.clock()
while Teleport.busy and os.clock() - t0 < 8 do task.wait(0.1) end
for _ = 1, 3 do
if not (part and part.Parent) then break end
Grab.touch(part)
task.wait(0.2)
end
Grab.status = "on the " .. label
end)
end
Grab.FLARE_LIFT = 2.5
Grab.FLARE_GAP = 5
Grab.FLARE_JUMPS = 2
function Grab.snapTo(part, lift)
local hrp = getHRP()
if not (hrp and part and part.Parent) or not Grab.canMove() then return false end
Teleport.busy = true
local ok = pcall(function()
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(part.Position + Vector3.new(0, lift, 0)) * hrp.CFrame.Rotation
end)
task.delay(0.1, function() Teleport.busy = false end)
return ok
end
function Grab.setFlare(on)
Grab.flare = on
if not on then
if Grab.flareConn then Grab.flareConn:Disconnect() Grab.flareConn = nil end
Grab.flareGun = nil
return
end
if Grab.flareConn then return end
Grab.flareConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Grab.flare then return end
if os.clock() - Grab.flareLast < 0.25 then return end
Grab.flareLast = os.clock()
if Ex.haveFlare() then
Grab.status = "flare gun collected"
return
end
local gun = flarePart()
if not gun then
Grab.status = "off"
return
end
if Grab.flareGun ~= gun then
Grab.flareGun, Grab.flareJumps, Grab.flareJumpAt = gun, 0, -math.huge
end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
if (gun.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= Grab.NEAR then
if os.clock() - (Grab.flareTouchAt or 0) >= 0.6 then
Grab.flareTouchAt = os.clock()
Grab.touch(gun)
end
Grab.status = "on the flare gun"
return
end
if Grab.flareJumps >= Grab.FLARE_JUMPS then
Grab.status = "the server put you back twice - walk to it"
return
end
if os.clock() - Grab.flareJumpAt < Grab.FLARE_GAP then return end
if Grab.snapTo(gun, Grab.FLARE_LIFT) then
Grab.flareJumps = Grab.flareJumps + 1
Grab.flareJumpAt = os.clock()
Grab.touch(gun)
Grab.status = "jumped onto the flare gun"
end
end)
addCleanup("autoflare", function() Grab.setFlare(false) end)
end
function Grab.setScrap(on)
Grab.scrap = on
if not on then
if Grab.scrapConn then Grab.scrapConn:Disconnect() Grab.scrapConn = nil end
return
end
if Grab.scrapConn then return end
Grab.scrapConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Grab.scrap then return end
if os.clock() - Grab.scrapLast < 0.7 then return end
Grab.scrapLast = os.clock()
local me = myPos()
if not me then return end
local nearest, best = nil, math.huge
local reached = 0
for _, sp in ipairs(Farm.scraps()) do
local part = sp.part
if part and part.Parent and not sp.prize then
local away = (part.Position - me).Magnitude
if away <= Grab.NEAR then
if Grab.touch(part) then reached = reached + 1 end
elseif away < best and not Farm.blockedNear(part.Position) then
nearest, best = part, away
end
end
end
if reached > 0 then
Grab.status = "picking up " .. reached .. " nearby"
elseif nearest then
Grab.status = string.format("nearest scrap %.0fm", best)
Grab.fetch(nearest, "scrap")
else
Grab.status = "no scrap in reach"
end
end)
addCleanup("autoscrap", function() Grab.setScrap(false) end)
end
do
local Bring = {
on = false, range = 1000, max = 3,
held = {}, skip = {}, seq = 0,
brought = 0, got = 0, status = "off",
}
Bring.GIVE_UP = 4
Bring.SKIP = 15
function Bring.spot(i, n, root)
local ang = (n > 1) and ((i - (n + 1) / 2) * 0.35) or 0
return root * CFrame.Angles(0, ang, 0) * CFrame.new(0, -2.2, -4.5)
end
function Bring.release(m, rec)
if m and m.Parent and rec and rec.home then
pcall(function() m:PivotTo(rec.home) end)
end
end
function Bring.releaseAll()
for m, rec in pairs(Bring.held) do Bring.release(m, rec) end
Bring.held = {}
end
function Bring.pick(me)
local list = {}
local spawns = pathTo(Workspace, "Filter", "ScrapSpawns")
if not spawns then return list end
local now = os.clock()
for m, untilAt in pairs(Bring.skip) do
if untilAt < now or not m.Parent then Bring.skip[m] = nil end
end
for _, spawn in ipairs(spawns:GetChildren()) do
for _, m in ipairs(spawn:GetChildren()) do
if not Bring.held[m] and not Bring.skip[m] then
local ok, part = Farm.isRealScrap(m)
if ok and part then
local d = (Farm.anchorOf(spawn, part) - me).Magnitude
if d <= Bring.range then
list[#list + 1] = { m = m, part = part, d = d }
end
end
end
end
end
table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
return list
end
function Bring.go()
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
if Farm.on then
notify("Auto Farm is collecting - its magnet brings scrap already", 4)
return
end
if typeof(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" and not Bring.warned then
Bring.warned = true
notify("Bring Scrap needs firetouchinterest to pick scrap up - this executor "
.. "only lets it show them in front of you", 6)
end
if not Bring.conn then Bring.brought, Bring.got = 0, 0 end
local now, n, added = os.clock(), 0, 0
for _ in pairs(Bring.held) do n = n + 1 end
for _, c in ipairs(Bring.pick(hrp.Position)) do
if n >= Bring.max then break end
local home
pcall(function() home = c.m:GetPivot() end)
Bring.seq = Bring.seq + 1
Bring.held[c.m] = { part = c.part, home = home, at = now, touchAt = 0, seq = Bring.seq }
Bring.brought = Bring.brought + 1
n, added = n + 1, added + 1
end
if n == 0 then
notify(Bring.range >= 1000 and "no scrap on the map right now"
or ("no scrap within " .. math.floor(Bring.range) .. " m"), 3)
return
end
Bring.on = true
if not Bring.conn then
Bring.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() pcall(Bring.step) end)
addCleanup("bringscrap", function() Bring.stop(false) end)
end
end
function Bring.stop(report)
Bring.on = false
if Bring.conn then Bring.conn:Disconnect() Bring.conn = nil end
Bring.releaseAll()
if report and Bring.brought > 0 then
notify(string.format("Bring Scrap - picked up %d of %d", Bring.got, Bring.brought), 3)
end
Bring.status = "off"
end
function Bring.step()
if not Bring.on then return end
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then return end
if Farm.on then Bring.stop(false) return end
local now = os.clock()
for m, rec in pairs(Bring.held) do
if not (m.Parent and rec.part.Parent) then
Bring.held[m] = nil
Bring.got = Bring.got + 1
elseif now - rec.at > Bring.GIVE_UP then
Bring.release(m, rec)
Bring.held[m] = nil
Bring.skip[m] = now + Bring.SKIP
end
end
local order = {}
for m, rec in pairs(Bring.held) do order[#order + 1] = { m = m, rec = rec } end
if #order == 0 then Bring.stop(true) return end
table.sort(order, function(a, b) return a.rec.seq < b.rec.seq end)
local root = hrp.CFrame
for i, o in ipairs(order) do
local cf = Bring.spot(i, #order, root)
pcall(function() o.m:PivotTo(cf) end)
if now - o.rec.touchAt >= 0.5 then
o.rec.touchAt = now
Grab.touch(o.rec.part)
end
end
Bring.status = string.format("holding %d - %d picked up", #order, Bring.got)
end
Grab.Bring = Bring
end
function Move.mover(hrp)
if Move.lv and Move.lv.Parent and Move.lvAtt and Move.lvAtt.Parent then
return Move.lv
end
if Move.lv then pcall(function() Move.lv:Destroy() end) end
if Move.lvAtt then pcall(function() Move.lvAtt:Destroy() end) end
Move.lv, Move.lvAtt = nil, nil
local ok = pcall(function()
Move.lvAtt = make("Attachment", { Name = "RKMoveAtt" }, hrp)
Move.lv = make("LinearVelocity", {
Name = "RKMover", Attachment0 = Move.lvAtt,
RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World,
MaxForce = 100000, VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero,
ForceLimitMode = Enum.ForceLimitMode.Magnitude,
}, hrp)
end)
if ok and Move.lv then
pcall(function()
Move.lv.ForceLimitMode = Enum.ForceLimitMode.PerAxis
Move.lv.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(100000, 0, 100000)
end)
end
return ok and Move.lv or nil
end
function Move.pushLoop()
if Move.pushConn then return end
Move.pushConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
if not Move.speedOn then return end
local mode = Move.speedMode
if not Move.LOOP_MODES[mode] then return end
local function idle()
if Move.lv then pcall(function() Move.lv.Enabled = false end) end
end
if Move.fly or Free.on or Ex.hidden or Teleport.busy then idle() return end
local hum, hrp = getHum(), getHRP()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if not (hum and hrp and char) or hrp.Anchored then idle() return end
local dir = hum.MoveDirection
if dir.Magnitude < 0.1 then idle() return end
local extra = Move.walkSpeed - (Move.baseWalk or 16)
if mode == "Velocity" then
local v = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
pcall(function()
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
Vector3.new(dir.X * Move.walkSpeed, v.Y, dir.Z * Move.walkSpeed)
end)
elseif mode == "Linear Velocity" then
local lv = Move.mover(hrp)
if lv then
pcall(function()
lv.Enabled = true
lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * Move.walkSpeed, 0,
dir.Z * Move.walkSpeed)
end)
end
elseif mode == "Impulse" then
if extra > 0 then
pcall(function()
hrp:ApplyImpulse(dir * extra * hrp.AssemblyMass * dt)
end)
end
elseif mode == "Step" then
if extra > 0 and os.clock() - (Move.stepAt or 0) >= 0.1 then
Move.stepAt = os.clock()
pcall(function()
char:PivotTo(char:GetPivot() + dir * extra * 0.1)
end)
end
else
if extra > 0 then
pcall(function() char:PivotTo(char:GetPivot() + dir * extra * dt) end)
end
end
end)
addCleanup("speedpush", function()
if Move.pushConn then Move.pushConn:Disconnect() Move.pushConn = nil end
end)
end
local UIReport = {}
local function tryBuild(name, fn, tab)
Device.Boot.step(name .. " tab")
local ok, err = pcall(fn, tab)
UIReport[#UIReport + 1] = { name = name, ok = ok, err = not ok and tostring(err) or nil }
if not ok then
Device.Boot.fail(name .. " tab", err)
warn("[Ruv&Rill] " .. name .. " tab failed: " .. tostring(err))
notify(name .. " tab failed - see Settings > Build Report", 10)
end
end

local Dodge = { on = false, reach = 11, hop = 9, gap = 0.6, last = 0, status = "off" }
Dodge.ATTACK_WORDS = { "attack", "swing", "hit", "slash", "claw", "lunge", "bite", "strike", "grab" }
Dodge.LOCO_WORDS = { "idle", "walk", "run", "jump", "fall", "climb", "swim", "sit", "pose", "look" }
function Dodge.isAttackTrack(track)
local name = ""
pcall(function()
name = (tostring(track.Name) .. " " .. tostring(track.Animation and track.Animation.Name or "")):lower()
end)
for _, w in ipairs(Dodge.ATTACK_WORDS) do if name:find(w, 1, true) then return true end end
for _, w in ipairs(Dodge.LOCO_WORDS) do if name:find(w, 1, true) then return false end end
local looped = true
pcall(function() looped = track.Looped end)
return not looped
end
function Dodge.threat(r)
local rh = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hrp = getHRP()
if not (rh and hrp) then return false, "no Rake" end
local d = (rh.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
local box = r:FindFirstChild("AttackHitbox", true)
if box and box:IsA("BasePart") then d = math.min(d, (box.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude) end
if d > Dodge.reach then return false, string.format("swing at %dm - out of reach", math.floor(d)) end
local to = hrp.Position - rh.Position
to = Vector3.new(to.X, 0, to.Z)
local lv = rh.CFrame.LookVector
lv = Vector3.new(lv.X, 0, lv.Z)
if to.Magnitude > 0.1 and lv.Magnitude > 0.1 and lv.Unit:Dot(to.Unit) < 0.35 then
return false, "swing not facing you"
end
return true, d
end
function Dodge.step(r)
local rh = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hrp = getHRP()
if not (rh and hrp) then return end
local away = hrp.Position - rh.Position
away = Vector3.new(away.X, 0, away.Z)
away = away.Magnitude > 0.1 and away.Unit or -hrp.CFrame.LookVector
local side = Vector3.new(-away.Z, 0, away.X)
local params = RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { r, LocalPlayer.Character }
local first = (math.random() < 0.5) and 1 or -1
for _, dir in ipairs({ side * first + away * 0.4, -side * first + away * 0.4, away }) do
local move = dir.Unit * Dodge.hop
local wall = Workspace:Raycast(hrp.Position, move, params)
local spot = hrp.Position + move
local ground = Workspace:Raycast(spot + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), Vector3.new(0, -9, 0), params)
if not wall and ground then
pcall(function()
hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(spot) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
end)
return true
end
end
return false
end
function Dodge.onSwing(source)
if not Dodge.on then return end
if os.clock() - Dodge.last < Dodge.gap then return end
local r = rake()
local hit, why = Dodge.threat(r)
if not hit then Dodge.status = why return end
Dodge.last = os.clock()
Dodge.status = (Dodge.step(r) and "dodged a swing (" .. source .. ")") or "swing coming - no clear spot to step to"
end
function Dodge.attach()
local r = rake()
local hum = r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
if animator and animator ~= Dodge.animator then
if Dodge.animConn then Dodge.animConn:Disconnect() end
Dodge.animator = animator
Dodge.animConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
if Dodge.isAttackTrack(track) then Dodge.onSwing("animation") end
end)
end
local swing = r and r:FindFirstChild("Swing", true)
if swing and swing:IsA("Sound") and swing ~= Dodge.sound then
if Dodge.soundConn then Dodge.soundConn:Disconnect() end
Dodge.sound = swing
Dodge.soundConn = swing:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Playing"):Connect(function()
if swing.Playing then Dodge.onSwing("sound") end
end)
end
end
function Dodge.set(on)
Dodge.on = on
if not on then
for _, k in ipairs({ "conn", "animConn", "soundConn" }) do
if Dodge[k] then Dodge[k]:Disconnect() Dodge[k] = nil end
end
Dodge.animator, Dodge.sound, Dodge.status = nil, nil, "off"
return
end
Dodge.status = "watching for a swing"
if Dodge.conn then return end
local at, wasPlaying = 0, false
Dodge.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Dodge.on then return end
if os.clock() - at > 0.5 then at = os.clock(); pcall(Dodge.attach) end
local snd = Dodge.sound
local playing = snd and snd.Parent and snd.IsPlaying or false
if playing and not wasPlaying then Dodge.onSwing("sound") end
wasPlaying = playing
end)
addCleanup("dodge", function() Dodge.set(false) end)
end
local TrapTimer = { on = false, expect = 8, learned = nil, since = nil, file = "Rake/traptime.txt" }
TrapTimer.FONT = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.Heavy)
TrapTimer.NEAR = 4
function TrapTimer.caught(r)
local root = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not (root and root:IsA("BasePart")) then return false end
local function near(at)
return (Vector3.new(at.X, root.Position.Y, at.Z) - root.Position).Magnitude <= TrapTimer.NEAR
end
local hum = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then
local okF, flag = pcall(function() return hum:GetAttribute("iTRPED2") end)
if okF and flag == true then
local okP, at = pcall(function() return hum:GetAttribute("TrapPos") end)
if not (okP and typeof(at) == "Vector3") or near(at) then return true end
end
end
for _, m in ipairs(trapModels()) do
local box = m:FindFirstChild("HitBox")
local ok, hit = pcall(function()
return m:GetAttribute("Activated") == true and m:GetAttribute("HitChar") == "Rake"
end)
if ok and hit and box and box:IsA("BasePart") and near(box.Position) then return true end
end
return false
end
function TrapTimer.duration()
return TrapTimer.learned or TrapTimer.expect
end
function TrapTimer.board(head)
if TrapTimer.gui and TrapTimer.gui.Parent and TrapTimer.gui.Adornee == head then return end
if TrapTimer.gui then pcall(function() TrapTimer.gui:Destroy() end) end
local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
bb.Name = "RKTrapTimer"
bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 46)
bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4.2, 0)
bb.AlwaysOnTop, bb.LightInfluence = true, 0
bb.Adornee = head
local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
label.FontFace = TrapTimer.FONT
label.TextScaled = true
label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 120)
label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 30, 10)
label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
label.Parent = bb
local back = Instance.new("Frame")
back.Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0, 34)
back.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, 6)
back.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 30, 16)
back.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
back.BorderSizePixel = 0
back.Parent = bb
Instance.new("UICorner", back).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 120)
fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
fill.Parent = back
Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
bb.Parent = head
TrapTimer.gui, TrapTimer.label, TrapTimer.fill = bb, label, fill
end
function TrapTimer.hide()
if TrapTimer.gui then pcall(function() TrapTimer.gui:Destroy() end) end
TrapTimer.gui, TrapTimer.label, TrapTimer.fill = nil, nil, nil
end
function TrapTimer.step()
local r = rake()
local head = r and (r:FindFirstChild("Head") or r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
local gone = (not r) or Ex.rakeGone(r)
local caught = (not gone) and TrapTimer.caught(r)
if caught and not TrapTimer.since then
TrapTimer.since = os.clock()
elseif not caught and TrapTimer.since then
local held = os.clock() - TrapTimer.since
TrapTimer.since = nil
if not gone and held > 0.5 and held < 60 then
TrapTimer.learned = held
pcall(function() if typeof(writefile) == "function" then writefile(TrapTimer.file, string.format("%.2f", held)) end end)
end
end
if not (TrapTimer.since and head and head:IsA("BasePart")) then TrapTimer.hide() return end
TrapTimer.board(head)
local total = TrapTimer.duration()
local left = total - (os.clock() - TrapTimer.since)
if left >= 0 then
TrapTimer.label.Text = string.format("TRAPPED %.1fs", left)
TrapTimer.label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 120)
TrapTimer.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(left / total, 0, 1), 1)
TrapTimer.fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 120)
else
TrapTimer.label.Text = string.format("TRAPPED +%.1fs", -left)
TrapTimer.label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 90)
TrapTimer.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
end
end
function TrapTimer.set(on)
TrapTimer.on = on
if not on then
if TrapTimer.conn then TrapTimer.conn:Disconnect() TrapTimer.conn = nil end
TrapTimer.since = nil
TrapTimer.hide()
return
end
if TrapTimer.learned == nil then
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(TrapTimer.file) then
local v = tonumber(readfile(TrapTimer.file))
if v and v > 0.5 and v < 60 then TrapTimer.learned = v end
end
end)
end
if TrapTimer.conn then return end
TrapTimer.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if TrapTimer.on then pcall(TrapTimer.step) end
end)
addCleanup("traptimer", function() TrapTimer.set(false) end)
end

Callback = function(v) Farm.canJump = v end,
})

Text = "Pause Near Players", Default = false,

Farm.stats = { time = 0, scrap = 0, points = 0, deaths = 0 }

end })
liveLabel(gb, Farm.readout, 0.4)
end
local FakeLag = { on = false, method = "Delay", ms = 250, every = 1.2, hist = {}, restore = nil }
FakeLag.METHODS = { "Delay", "Stutter", "Freeze" }
function FakeLag.busy()
return Teleport.busy or Fight.flanking or (Free and Free.on) or Move.fly
end
function FakeLag.sample(at)
local h = FakeLag.hist
for i = #h, 1, -1 do
if h[i].t <= at then return h[i].cf end
end
return h[1] and h[1].cf or nil
end
function FakeLag.step()
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp ~= FakeLag.root then
FakeLag.root = hrp
FakeLag.hist, FakeLag.frozen, FakeLag.restore, FakeLag.wrote = {}, nil, nil, nil
end
if not hrp or FakeLag.busy() then
FakeLag.hist, FakeLag.frozen = {}, nil
return
end
local now = os.clock()
local real = hrp.CFrame
local h = FakeLag.hist
h[#h + 1] = { t = now, cf = real }
local keep = FakeLag.ms / 1000 + 1
while #h > 2 and now - h[1].t > keep do table.remove(h, 1) end
local fake
if FakeLag.method == "Delay" then
fake = FakeLag.sample(now - FakeLag.ms / 1000)
elseif FakeLag.method == "Stutter" then
local phase = now % math.max(FakeLag.every, FakeLag.ms / 1000 + 0.05)
if phase < FakeLag.ms / 1000 then
FakeLag.frozen = FakeLag.frozen or real
fake = FakeLag.frozen
else
FakeLag.frozen = nil
end
end
if fake and (fake.Position - real.Position).Magnitude > 0.05 then
FakeLag.restore, FakeLag.wrote = real, fake
pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = fake end)
end
end
function FakeLag.unstep()
local r, wrote = FakeLag.restore, FakeLag.wrote
if not r then return end
FakeLag.restore, FakeLag.wrote = nil, nil
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp or hrp ~= FakeLag.root then return end
if FakeLag.busy() then return end
pcall(function()
local moved = wrote and (hrp.Position - wrote.Position).Magnitude > 1e-3
if moved then
hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + (r.Position - wrote.Position)
else
hrp.CFrame = r
end
end)
end
function FakeLag.freezeLoop()
local gen = {}
FakeLag.freezeGen = gen
task.spawn(function()
while FakeLag.on and FakeLag.method == "Freeze" and FakeLag.freezeGen == gen do
task.wait(math.max(0.1, FakeLag.every - FakeLag.ms / 1000))
if not (FakeLag.on and FakeLag.method == "Freeze" and FakeLag.freezeGen == gen) then break end
local hrp = getHRP()
if hrp and not FakeLag.busy() and not hrp.Anchored and (Ex.rootHeld or 0) == 0 then
pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = true end)
task.wait(FakeLag.ms / 1000)
local live = getHRP()
if live and live == hrp and not (Free and Free.on) and (Ex.rootHeld or 0) == 0 then
pcall(function() live.Anchored = false end)
end
end
end
end)
end
function FakeLag.set(on)
FakeLag.on = on
if not on then
if FakeLag.hb then FakeLag.hb:Disconnect() FakeLag.hb = nil end
if FakeLag.rs then FakeLag.rs:Disconnect() FakeLag.rs = nil end
FakeLag.unstep()
FakeLag.hist, FakeLag.frozen = {}, nil
return
end
if FakeLag.method == "Freeze" and not FakeLag.hb then FakeLag.freezeLoop() end
if FakeLag.hb then return end
FakeLag.hb = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if FakeLag.on and FakeLag.method ~= "Freeze" then FakeLag.step() end
end)
FakeLag.rs = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(FakeLag.unstep)
addCleanup("fakelag", function() FakeLag.set(false) end)
end
function FakeLag.setMethod(v)
local was = FakeLag.method
FakeLag.method = v or "Delay"
FakeLag.unstep()
FakeLag.hist, FakeLag.frozen = {}, nil
if FakeLag.on and FakeLag.method == "Freeze" and was ~= "Freeze" then FakeLag.freezeLoop() end
end

local function buildTeleports(page)
local gb = page:AddLeftGroupbox("Places")

gb:AddButton({ Text = loc.name, Func = function()
Teleport.go(CFrame.new(loc.pos))
end })
end
local gb2 = page:AddRightGroupbox("Things")
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Flare Gun", Func = Teleport.toFlare })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Nearest Supply Drop", Func = function()
local crates = pathTo(Workspace, "Debris", "SupplyCrates")
local me = myPos()
if not (crates and me) then notify("no crate down right now", 4) return end
local best, bestDist
for _, box in ipairs(crates:GetChildren()) do
local part = box:FindFirstChild("HitBox") or box:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
if part then
local d = (part.Position - me).Magnitude
if not bestDist or d < bestDist then best, bestDist = part, d end
end
end
if not best then notify("no crate down right now", 4) return end
Teleport.go(CFrame.new(best.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)))
end })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "The Rake", Func = function()
local r = rake()
local hrp = r and r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then notify("no Rake in the world", 4) return end
local me = myPos()
local back = me and (me - hrp.Position) or Vector3.new(0, 0, 30)
if back.Magnitude < 1 then back = Vector3.new(0, 0, 30) end
Teleport.go(CFrame.new(hrp.Position + back.Unit * 40 + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0)))
end, DoubleClick = true })
end
