local Skin = {
style = "Default", on = nil,
saved = nil, extras = nil, parts = nil, eyes = nil, mouth = nil,
hair = nil, blush = nil,
body = nil, skirt = nil, whole = nil,
built = {}, veiled = false,
conn = nil, paintedOn = nil, last = 0,
}
Skin.STYLES = { "Default", "Anime1", "Anime2", "Anime3", "Built", "Bacon Hair" }
Skin.BACON = {
skin  = Color3.fromRGB(245, 205, 48),
shirt = Color3.fromRGB(13, 105, 172),
pants = Color3.fromRGB(75, 151, 75),
hair  = Color3.fromRGB(170, 85, 0),
face  = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20),
}
Skin.BACON_BODY = {
Head   = { w = 1.25, h = 1.0,  d = 1.25, shape = "box", part = "skin"  },
Neck   = { w = 0.3,  h = 0.42, d = 0.3,  shape = "box", part = "skin"  },
TorsoM = { w = 0.72, h = 0.72, d = 0.55, shape = "box", part = "shirt" },
LArmM  = { w = 0.34, h = 0.78, d = 0.34, shape = "box", part = "skin"  },
RArmM  = { w = 0.34, h = 0.78, d = 0.34, shape = "box", part = "skin"  },
LLegM  = { w = 0.38, h = 0.82, d = 0.38, shape = "box", part = "pants" },
RLegM  = { w = 0.38, h = 0.82, d = 0.38, shape = "box", part = "pants" },
}
Skin.PRESETS = {
Anime1 = "4661297252",
Anime2 = "6859666802",
Anime3 = "2513353543",
}
Skin.SKIN_COLOUR = Color3.fromRGB(252, 232, 224)
function Skin.rakeParts(r)
local out = {}
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then out[#out + 1] = d end
end
return out
end
function Skin.clear()
if Skin.parts then
for _, p in ipairs(Skin.parts) do pcall(function() p:Destroy() end) end
end
Skin.parts, Skin.eyes, Skin.hair, Skin.blush, Skin.mouth = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
Skin.body, Skin.skirt, Skin.whole = nil, nil, nil
Skin.built, Skin.veiled = {}, false
if Skin.extras then
for obj, e in pairs(Skin.extras) do
if obj and obj.Parent then
pcall(function()
if e.kind == "enabled" then obj.Enabled = e.was
else obj.Transparency = e.was end
end)
end
end
Skin.extras = nil
end
if Skin.saved then
for part, was in pairs(Skin.saved) do
if part and part.Parent then
pcall(function()
part.Color = was.color
part.Material = was.material
part.Transparency = was.transparency
if was.texture ~= nil then part.TextureID = was.texture end
end)
end
end
end
Skin.saved, Skin.on = nil, nil
end
addCleanup("rakeskin", function() Skin.clear() end)
function Skin.blob(name, colour, sizeV, material, shape)
local p = make("Part", {
Name = name, Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false,
CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = material or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
Color = colour, Size = sizeV,
}, Workspace)
if shape == "box" then
make("SpecialMesh", {
MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Brick, Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
}, p)
elseif shape == "limb" then
make("CylinderMesh", {}, p)
else
make("SpecialMesh", { MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Sphere }, p)
end
return p
end
function Skin.eye(side, size)
local white = Skin.blob("RKEye", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
Vector3.new(size * 0.66, size * 1.02, size * 0.3))
local iris = Skin.blob("RKIris", Color3.fromRGB(126, 92, 214),
Vector3.new(size * 0.44, size * 0.72, size * 0.22), Enum.Material.Neon)
local shine = Skin.blob("RKShine", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
Vector3.new(size * 0.15, size * 0.19, size * 0.12), Enum.Material.Neon)
local lash = Skin.blob("RKLash", Color3.fromRGB(28, 20, 34),
Vector3.new(size * 0.72, size * 0.2, size * 0.32))
return { white = white, iris = iris, shine = shine, lash = lash, side = side }
end
Skin.HAIR = Color3.fromRGB(46, 34, 60)
Skin.DRESS = Color3.fromRGB(58, 62, 96)
Skin.BODY = {
Head   = { w = 1.5,  h = 1.4,  d = 1.45, shape = "ball" },
Neck   = { w = 0.3,  h = 0.42, d = 0.3,  shape = "limb" },
TorsoM = { w = 0.62, h = 0.66, d = 0.5,  shape = "box"  },
LArmM  = { w = 0.26, h = 0.72, d = 0.26, shape = "limb" },
RArmM  = { w = 0.26, h = 0.72, d = 0.26, shape = "limb" },
LLegM  = { w = 0.34, h = 0.78, d = 0.34, shape = "limb" },
RLegM  = { w = 0.34, h = 0.78, d = 0.34, shape = "limb" },
}
function Skin.baconEye(side, size)
local C = Skin.BACON
local eye = {
white = Skin.blob("RKEye", C.face, Vector3.new(size * 0.13, size * 0.2, size * 0.05), nil, "box"),
iris  = Skin.blob("RKIris", C.face, Vector3.new(size * 0.1, size * 0.15, size * 0.04), nil, "box"),
shine = Skin.blob("RKShine", C.face, Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, 0.01), nil, "box"),
lash  = Skin.blob("RKLash", C.face, Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, 0.01), nil, "box"),
side  = side,
}
eye.shine.Transparency = 1
eye.lash.Transparency = 1
return eye
end
function Skin.buildBacon(r)
local head = r:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not head or not head:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
local size = math.max(head.Size.Y, 1.4)
local C = Skin.BACON
Skin.parts = {}
local function keep(p) Skin.parts[#Skin.parts + 1] = p return p end
Skin.body = {}
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
local spec = d:IsA("BasePart") and Skin.BACON_BODY[d.Name]
if spec then
local sz = d.Size
Skin.body[#Skin.body + 1] = {
real = d,
mirror = keep(Skin.blob("RKBody", C[spec.part],
Vector3.new(sz.X * spec.w, sz.Y * spec.h, sz.Z * spec.d),
nil, spec.shape)),
}
end
end
Skin.eyes = { Skin.baconEye(-1, size), Skin.baconEye(1, size) }
for _, e in ipairs(Skin.eyes) do
keep(e.lash) keep(e.white) keep(e.iris) keep(e.shine)
end
Skin.blush = nil
Skin.mouth = keep(Skin.blob("RKMouth", C.face,
Vector3.new(size * 0.34, size * 0.07, size * 0.05), nil, "box"))
Skin.hair = {
cap    = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", C.hair,
Vector3.new(size * 1.3, size * 0.5, size * 1.3), nil, "box")),
fringe = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", C.hair,
Vector3.new(size * 1.1, size * 0.28, size * 0.3), nil, "box")),
left   = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", C.hair,
Vector3.new(size * 0.28, size * 0.5, size * 1.0), nil, "box")),
right  = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", C.hair,
Vector3.new(size * 0.28, size * 0.5, size * 1.0), nil, "box")),
back   = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", C.hair,
Vector3.new(size * 1.2, size * 0.9, size * 0.5), nil, "box")),
}
Skin.skirt = nil
Skin.whole = nil
return true
end
function Skin.build(r)
if Skin.style == "Bacon Hair" then return Skin.buildBacon(r) end
local head = r:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not head or not head:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
local size = math.max(head.Size.Y, 1.4)
Skin.parts = {}
local function keep(p) Skin.parts[#Skin.parts + 1] = p return p end
Skin.body = {}
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
local spec = d:IsA("BasePart") and Skin.BODY[d.Name]
if spec then
local sz = d.Size
local clothed = (d.Name == "TorsoM")
Skin.body[#Skin.body + 1] = {
real = d,
mirror = keep(Skin.blob("RKBody",
clothed and Skin.DRESS or Skin.SKIN_COLOUR,
Vector3.new(sz.X * spec.w, sz.Y * spec.h, sz.Z * spec.d),
nil, spec.shape)),
}
if d.Name == "TorsoM" then
Skin.skirt = {
real = d,
mirror = keep(Skin.blob("RKSkirt", Skin.DRESS,
Vector3.new(sz.X * 0.95, sz.Y * 0.4, sz.Z * 0.8),
nil, "limb")),
}
end
end
end
Skin.eyes = { Skin.eye(-1, size), Skin.eye(1, size) }
for _, e in ipairs(Skin.eyes) do
keep(e.lash) keep(e.white) keep(e.iris) keep(e.shine)
end
Skin.mouth = keep(Skin.blob("RKMouth", Color3.fromRGB(196, 96, 116),
Vector3.new(size * 0.2, size * 0.12, size * 0.14)))
Skin.blush = {
keep(Skin.blob("RKBlush", Color3.fromRGB(255, 168, 178),
Vector3.new(size * 0.34, size * 0.2, size * 0.12))),
keep(Skin.blob("RKBlush", Color3.fromRGB(255, 168, 178),
Vector3.new(size * 0.34, size * 0.2, size * 0.12))),
}
Skin.hair = {
cap   = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", Skin.HAIR,
Vector3.new(size * 1.28, size * 1.18, size * 1.28))),
fringe= keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", Skin.HAIR,
Vector3.new(size * 1.24, size * 0.44, size * 0.5))),
left  = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", Skin.HAIR,
Vector3.new(size * 0.34, size * 1.9, size * 0.42))),
right = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", Skin.HAIR,
Vector3.new(size * 0.34, size * 1.9, size * 0.42))),
back  = keep(Skin.blob("RKHair", Skin.HAIR,
Vector3.new(size * 1.15, size * 2.6, size * 0.62))),
}
return true
end
function Skin.veil()
Skin.built = Skin.built or {}
for _, p in ipairs(Skin.parts or {}) do
if p and p.Parent then
pcall(function()
if Skin.built[p] == nil then Skin.built[p] = p.Transparency end
p.Transparency = 1
end)
end
end
Skin.veiled = true
end
function Skin.unveil()
if not Skin.veiled then return end
Skin.veiled = false
for _, p in ipairs(Skin.parts or {}) do
if p and p.Parent and Skin.built[p] then
pcall(function() p.Transparency = Skin.built[p] end)
end
end
end
function Skin.hold()
local r = rake()
if not (Skin.on and Skin.on ~= "Default" and Skin.parts) then return end
if not r then Skin.veil() return end
local dv = r:FindFirstChild("Dead")
if dv and dv:IsA("BoolValue") and dv.Value then Skin.veil() return end
local head = r:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not (head and head.Parent) then
Skin.veil()
return
end
if Skin.body and #Skin.body > 0 then
local alive = 0
for _, b in ipairs(Skin.body) do
if b.real and b.real.Parent then alive = alive + 1 end
end
if alive == 0 then
Skin.veil()
return
end
end
Skin.unveil()
local size = math.max(head.Size.Y, 1.4)
local cf = head.CFrame
local face = -size * 0.52
for _, b in ipairs(Skin.body or {}) do
if b.real.Parent then
b.mirror.CFrame = b.real.CFrame
if b.mirror.Transparency ~= 0 then b.mirror.Transparency = 0 end
else
b.mirror.Transparency = 1
end
end
if Skin.whole then
local root = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not (root and root:IsA("BasePart")) then root = head end
local base = root.CFrame
for _, w in ipairs(Skin.whole) do
if w.part.Parent then
local bone = w.bone
if bone and bone.Parent and w.boneCF then
w.part.CFrame = bone.CFrame * w.boneCF
else
w.part.CFrame = base * CFrame.new(w.offset) * w.rot
end
end
end
end
if Skin.skirt and Skin.skirt.real.Parent then
Skin.skirt.mirror.CFrame =
Skin.skirt.real.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -Skin.skirt.real.Size.Y * 0.42, 0)
end
for i, e in ipairs(Skin.eyes or {}) do
local at = cf * CFrame.new(e.side * size * 0.29, size * 0.1, face)
e.white.CFrame = at
e.iris.CFrame = at * CFrame.new(0, -size * 0.04, -size * 0.06)
e.shine.CFrame = at * CFrame.new(e.side * -size * 0.09, size * 0.16, -size * 0.11)
e.lash.CFrame = at * CFrame.new(0, size * 0.46, -size * 0.02)
local b = Skin.blush and Skin.blush[i]
if b then b.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(e.side * size * 0.4, -size * 0.24, face * 0.94) end
end
if Skin.mouth then
Skin.mouth.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0, -size * 0.36, face)
end
local h = Skin.hair
if h then
h.cap.CFrame    = cf * CFrame.new(0, size * 0.18, size * 0.04)
h.fringe.CFrame = cf * CFrame.new(0, size * 0.46, face * 0.86)
h.left.CFrame   = cf * CFrame.new(-size * 0.6, -size * 0.42, -size * 0.06)
h.right.CFrame  = cf * CFrame.new(size * 0.6, -size * 0.42, -size * 0.06)
h.back.CFrame   = cf * CFrame.new(0, -size * 0.85, size * 0.42)
end
end
function Skin.apply()
local r = rake()
if not r then return false, "no Rake in the world" end
if Skin.on == Skin.style and Skin.parts then return true end
Skin.clear()
if Skin.style == "Default" then return true, "back to his own skin" end
local headYet = r:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not (headYet and headYet:IsA("BasePart")) then
return false, "his head has not loaded yet - it will retry"
end
Skin.saved = {}
for _, p in ipairs(Skin.rakeParts(r)) do
local ok = pcall(function()
Skin.saved[p] = {
color = p.Color, material = p.Material,
transparency = p.Transparency,
texture = p:IsA("MeshPart") and p.TextureID or nil,
}
p.Color = Skin.SKIN_COLOUR
p.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
if p:IsA("MeshPart") then p.TextureID = "" end
p.Transparency = 1
end)
if not ok then Skin.saved[p] = nil end
end
Skin.extras = {}
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
local kind
if d:IsA("Light") then kind = "enabled"
elseif d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then kind = "transparency"
elseif d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail")
or d:IsA("Smoke") or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Sparkles") then kind = "enabled"
elseif d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui") then kind = "enabled"
end
if kind then
pcall(function()
if kind == "enabled" then
Skin.extras[d] = { kind = kind, was = d.Enabled }
d.Enabled = false
else
Skin.extras[d] = { kind = kind, was = d.Transparency }
d.Transparency = 1
end
end)
end
end
Skin.parts = Skin.parts or {}
Skin.body = Skin.body or {}
local id = tostring(Skin.modelId or ""):gsub("%D", "")
if id == "" then id = Skin.PRESETS[Skin.style] or "" end
if id ~= "" then
local model = Skin.fetch(id)
if model then
local made = Skin.buildFromModel(r, model)
if made > 0 then
Skin.on = Skin.style
Skin.paintedOn = r
Skin.hold()
return true, "wearing model " .. id .. " - " .. made .. " limbs, animated"
end
made = Skin.wearWhole(r, model)
if made > 0 then
Skin.on = Skin.style
Skin.paintedOn = r
Skin.hold()
return true, "wearing model " .. id .. " whole - " .. made
.. " parts, no rig inside it so it will not animate"
end
notify("model " .. id .. " has no parts at all - using the built skin", 8)
else
if not Skin.build(r) then
Skin.on = nil
return false, "his head has not loaded yet - it will retry"
end
Skin.on = Skin.style
Skin.paintedOn = r
Skin.hold()
return true, "fetching model " .. id .. "..."
end
end
if not Skin.build(r) then
Skin.on = nil
return false, "his head has not loaded yet - it will retry"
end
Skin.on = Skin.style
Skin.paintedOn = r
Skin.hold()
return true, Skin.style == "Bacon Hair"
and "bacon hair on - built from parts, so it animates"
or "built skin on - paste a model id for a real character"
end
Skin.modelId = ""
function Skin.fetch(id)
if Skin.fetched and Skin.fetchedId == id then return Skin.fetched end
if Skin.fetching then return nil end
Skin.fetching = true
task.spawn(function()
local ok, objs = pcall(function()
return game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. id)
end)
Skin.fetching = false
if ok and type(objs) == "table" and objs[1] then
Skin.fetched, Skin.fetchedId = objs[1], id
notify("model " .. id .. " loaded - applying", 5)
Skin.on = nil
Skin.apply()
else
Skin.fetched, Skin.fetchedId = nil, nil
notify("model " .. id .. " would not load - it may be private, "
.. "moderated, or not a model", 8)
end
Skin.fetching = false
end)
return nil
end
Skin.RIG_MAP = {
Head = "Head", Torso = "TorsoM", UpperTorso = "TorsoM",
["Left Arm"] = "LArmM", LeftUpperArm = "LArmM", LeftHand = "LArmM",
["Right Arm"] = "RArmM", RightUpperArm = "RArmM", RightHand = "RArmM",
["Left Leg"] = "LLegM", LeftUpperLeg = "LLegM", LeftFoot = "LLegM",
["Right Leg"] = "RLegM", RightUpperLeg = "RLegM", RightFoot = "RLegM",
}
function Skin.buildFromModel(r, model)
local made = 0
for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
local boneName = Skin.RIG_MAP[d.Name]
local bone = boneName and r:FindFirstChild(boneName, true)
if bone and bone:IsA("BasePart") then
local ok, clone = pcall(function() return d:Clone() end)
if ok and clone then
pcall(function()
clone.Name = "RKSkinPart"
clone.Anchored = true
clone.CanCollide = false
clone.CanQuery = false
clone.CanTouch = false
clone.Massless = true
clone.Parent = Workspace
end)
Skin.parts[#Skin.parts + 1] = clone
Skin.body[#Skin.body + 1] = {
real = bone, mirror = clone,
offset = CFrame.new(),
}
made = made + 1
end
end
end
end
return made
end
function Skin.wearWhole(r, model)
local root = r:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return 0 end
local lo, hi
for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
local p = d.Position
if not lo then lo, hi = p, p end
lo = Vector3.new(math.min(lo.X, p.X), math.min(lo.Y, p.Y), math.min(lo.Z, p.Z))
hi = Vector3.new(math.max(hi.X, p.X), math.max(hi.Y, p.Y), math.max(hi.Z, p.Z))
end
end
if not lo then return 0 end
local mid = (lo + hi) * 0.5
local tall = math.max(hi.Y - lo.Y, 0.1)
local top, bottom = root.Position.Y, root.Position.Y
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
top = math.max(top, d.Position.Y + d.Size.Y * 0.5)
bottom = math.min(bottom, d.Position.Y - d.Size.Y * 0.5)
end
end
local scale = math.clamp((top - bottom) / tall, 0.05, 20)
local bones = {}
for _, d in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then bones[#bones + 1] = d end
end
local made = 0
for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
local ok, clone = pcall(function() return d:Clone() end)
if ok and clone then
pcall(function()
clone.Name = "RKSkinPart"
clone.Anchored = true
clone.CanCollide, clone.CanQuery, clone.CanTouch = false, false, false
clone.Massless = true
clone.Size = d.Size * scale
clone.Parent = Workspace
end)
local offset = (d.Position - mid) * scale
local rot = d.CFrame - d.Position
local want = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset) * rot
local bone, best = nil, math.huge
for _, b in ipairs(bones) do
local gap = (b.Position - want.Position).Magnitude
if gap < best then bone, best = b, gap end
end
Skin.parts[#Skin.parts + 1] = clone
Skin.whole = Skin.whole or {}
Skin.whole[#Skin.whole + 1] = {
part = clone,
offset = offset,
rot = rot,
bone = bone,
boneCF = bone and bone.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(want) or nil,
}
made = made + 1
end
end
end
return made
end
function Skin.set(style)
Skin.style = style
local ok, why = Skin.apply()
if why then notify(why, 4) end
if style ~= "Default" and not Skin.conn then
Skin.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if Skin.style == "Default" then return end
if os.clock() - (Skin.last or 0) >= 0.5 then
Skin.last = os.clock()
local r = rake()
if r and Skin.paintedOn ~= r then
Skin.on = nil
Skin.apply()
elseif Skin.saved then
for part, was in pairs(Skin.saved) do
if part and part.Parent then
pcall(function()
if part.Transparency ~= 1 then part.Transparency = 1 end
end)
end
end
for obj, e in pairs(Skin.extras or {}) do
if obj and obj.Parent then
pcall(function()
if e.kind == "enabled" then
if obj.Enabled then obj.Enabled = false end
elseif obj.Transparency ~= 1 then
obj.Transparency = 1
end
end)
end
end
end
end
Skin.hold()
end)
addCleanup("rakeskinloop", function()
if Skin.conn then Skin.conn:Disconnect() Skin.conn = nil end
end)
elseif style == "Default" and Skin.conn then
Skin.conn:Disconnect()
Skin.conn = nil
Skin.paintedOn = nil
end
end
local View = {
on = false, conn = nil, gripInst = nil, gripOrig = nil,
wx = 0, wy = 0, wz = 0, rx = 0, ry = 0, rz = 0,
minZ = nil, maxZ = nil,
}
function View.findGrip()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
if not (char and tool) then return nil end
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if (d:IsA("Motor6D") or d:IsA("Weld")) and d.Part0 and d.Part1 then
local t0 = d.Part0:IsDescendantOf(tool)
local t1 = d.Part1:IsDescendantOf(tool)
if t1 and not t0 then return d end
end
end
return nil
end
function View.restoreGrip()
if View.gripInst and View.gripOrig then
pcall(function() View.gripInst.C0 = View.gripOrig end)
end
View.gripInst, View.gripOrig = nil, nil
end
function View.set(on)
View.on = on
local lp = LocalPlayer
if not on then
if View.conn then View.conn:Disconnect() View.conn = nil end
View.restoreGrip()
if View.minZ ~= nil then pcall(function() lp.CameraMinZoomDistance = View.minZ end) View.minZ = nil end
if View.maxZ ~= nil then pcall(function() lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance = View.maxZ end) View.maxZ = nil end
return
end
if on and Toggles.RK_Zoom and Toggles.RK_Zoom.Value then
pcall(function() Toggles.RK_Zoom:SetValue(false) end)
end
if View.minZ == nil then View.minZ = lp.CameraMinZoomDistance end
if View.maxZ == nil then View.maxZ = lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance end
if View.conn then return end
View.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not View.on then return end
if not Comfort.zoom then
pcall(function() lp.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5 lp.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 0.5 end)
end
if HandView.on then View.restoreGrip() return end
local grip = View.findGrip()
if not grip then return end
if grip ~= View.gripInst then
View.restoreGrip()
View.gripInst, View.gripOrig = grip, grip.C0
end
local off = CFrame.new(View.wx, View.wy, View.wz)
* CFrame.Angles(math.rad(View.rx), math.rad(View.ry), math.rad(View.rz))
pcall(function() grip.C0 = View.gripOrig * off end)
end)
addCleanup("viewmodel", function() View.set(false) end)
end
local function buildVisuals(page)
local perf = { on = false, took = {}, FILE = "Rake/perf.txt",
OFF = { "RK_Rtx", "RK_Skybox", "RK_FarRender", "RK_Aura", "RK_RakeAura", "RK_Orbs", "RK_JumpRings",
"RK_KillFx", "RK_RakeTrail", "RK_WepChams", "RK_FarmShowPath", "RK_BlurBg" },
ON = { "RK_NoShadows", "RK_NoDistanceBlur", "RK_NoMotionBlur" } }
function perf.write(list)
pcall(function() if typeof(writefile) == "function" then writefile(perf.FILE, table.concat(list, "\n")) end end)
end
function perf.read()
local out = {}
pcall(function()
if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(perf.FILE) then
for l in tostring(readfile(perf.FILE)):gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do out[#out + 1] = l end
end
end)
return out
end
function perf.set(on)
on = on and true or false
if on == perf.on then return end
perf.on = on
if Library.Loading then
perf.took = on and perf.read() or {}
if not on then perf.write({}) end
return
end
if on then
local took = {}
for _, idx in ipairs(perf.OFF) do
local t = Toggles[idx]
if t and t.Value then pcall(function() t:SetValue(false) end); took[#took + 1] = "-" .. idx end
end
for _, idx in ipairs(perf.ON) do
local t = Toggles[idx]
if t and not t.Value then pcall(function() t:SetValue(true) end); took[#took + 1] = "+" .. idx end
end
perf.took = took
perf.write(took)
notify("Performance Mode on - " .. #took .. " setting" .. (#took == 1 and "" or "s") .. " changed", 4)
else
local n = 0
for i = #perf.took, 1, -1 do
local rec = perf.took[i]
local t = Toggles[rec:sub(2)]
local want = rec:sub(1, 1) == "-"
if t and t.Value ~= want then pcall(function() t:SetValue(want) end); n = n + 1 end
end
perf.took = {}
perf.write({})
notify("Performance Mode off - " .. n .. " put back", 3)
end
end

gbPerf:AddToggle("RK_PerfMode", { Text = "Performance Mode", Default = false, Callback = perf.set })
:AddKeyPicker("RK_PerfModeKey", {

})

local Bypass = { on = false, marks = {}, folder = nil, mine = nil, links = 0 }
Bypass.MINE = Color3.fromRGB(90, 255, 140)
Bypass.CLEAR = 0.6
Bypass.LABEL = "anticheat doesnt work here"
Bypass.BUILTIN = {
{ pos = Vector3.new(-147.43, 20.55, 23.64) },
{ pos = Vector3.new(-137.88, 23.40, 28.72) },
{ pos = Vector3.new(-154.75, 20.77, 15.83) },
{ pos = Vector3.new(62.45, 13.76, -54.59) },
}
Bypass.LINK = 12
Bypass.MIN_WIDTH = 10
Bypass.PAD = 6
Bypass.savedFile = "Rake/bypass-spots.txt"
Bypass.SPOTS_URL = "%(SPOTS_BASE)s"
Bypass.remote = {}
function Bypass.spotsKey()
local k = (getgenv and getgenv().RakeKey) or _G.RakeKey
return (type(k) == "string" and #k >= 4) and k or nil
end
function Bypass.synced()
return type(Bypass.SPOTS_URL) == "string"
and not Bypass.SPOTS_URL:find("SPOTS_BASE", 1, true)
and Bypass.spotsKey() ~= nil
end
function Bypass.bypassHttp()
return (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or http_request or request
end
function Bypass.pull()
if not Bypass.synced() then return end
local req = Bypass.bypassHttp()
if type(req) ~= "function" then return end
task.spawn(function()
local ok, res = pcall(req, { Url = Bypass.SPOTS_URL .. "?key=" .. Bypass.spotsKey(), Method = "GET" })
if not (ok and type(res) == "table") then return end
local body = res.Body or res.body or ""
local got = {}
for x, y, z, label in body:gmatch("\"x\":(-?[%d%.]+),\"y\":(-?[%d%.]+),\"z\":(-?[%d%.]+),\"label\":\"(.-)\"") do
got[#got + 1] = { pos = Vector3.new(tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)),
label = (label ~= "" and label) or Bypass.LABEL }
end
Bypass.remote = got
Bypass.mine = nil
if Bypass.on then Bypass.set(true) end
end)
end
function Bypass.push(p)
if not Bypass.synced() then return end
local req = Bypass.bypassHttp()
if type(req) ~= "function" then return end
task.spawn(function()
pcall(req, { Url = Bypass.SPOTS_URL, Method = "POST",
Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
Body = string.format("{\"key\":%q,\"x\":%.3f,\"y\":%.3f,\"z\":%.3f,\"label\":%q}",
Bypass.spotsKey(), p.X, p.Y, p.Z, Bypass.LABEL) })
end)
end
task.defer(function() pcall(Bypass.pull) end)
function Bypass.loadMine()
if Bypass.mine then return Bypass.mine end
Bypass.mine = {}
local function add(pos, label)
if not pos then return end
for _, m in ipairs(Bypass.mine) do
if (m.pos - pos).Magnitude < Bypass.LINK then return end
end
Bypass.mine[#Bypass.mine + 1] = { pos = pos, label = label or Bypass.LABEL }
end
for _, b in ipairs(Bypass.BUILTIN) do add(b.pos, Bypass.LABEL) end
for _, r in ipairs(Bypass.remote or {}) do add(r.pos, r.label) end
if typeof(readfile) ~= "function" or typeof(isfile) ~= "function" then
return Bypass.mine
end
local ok, body = pcall(function()
if isfile(Bypass.savedFile) then return readfile(Bypass.savedFile) end
return nil
end)
if not (ok and type(body) == "string") then return Bypass.mine end
for line in body:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
local x, y, z, name = line:match("^%s*(-?%d+%.?%d*)%s+(-?%d+%.?%d*)%s+(-?%d+%.?%d*)%s*(.*)$")
if x then
add(Vector3.new(tonumber(x), tonumber(y), tonumber(z)),
(name ~= "" and name:lower() ~= "spot"
and name:lower() ~= "my spot" and name) or Bypass.LABEL)
end
end
return Bypass.mine
end
function Bypass.clear()
if Bypass.folder then pcall(function() Bypass.folder:Destroy() end) end
Bypass.folder, Bypass.marks = nil, {}
end
addCleanup("bypass", function() Bypass.clear() end)
function Bypass.clusters()
local marks = Bypass.loadMine()
local groups, taken = {}, {}
for i = 1, #marks do
if not taken[i] then
local group = { marks[i] }
taken[i] = true
local grew = true
while grew do
grew = false
for j = 1, #marks do
if not taken[j] then
for _, m in ipairs(group) do
if (m.pos - marks[j].pos).Magnitude <= Bypass.LINK then
group[#group + 1] = marks[j]
taken[j] = true
grew = true
break
end
end
end
end
end
groups[#groups + 1] = group
end
end
return groups
end
function Bypass.pillar(group)
local lo, hi
for _, m in ipairs(group) do
local p = m.pos
if not lo then lo, hi = p, p end
lo = Vector3.new(math.min(lo.X, p.X), math.min(lo.Y, p.Y), math.min(lo.Z, p.Z))
hi = Vector3.new(math.max(hi.X, p.X), math.max(hi.Y, p.Y), math.max(hi.Z, p.Z))
end
local mid = (lo + hi) * 0.5
local w = math.max(hi.X - lo.X + Bypass.PAD * 2, Bypass.MIN_WIDTH)
local d = math.max(hi.Z - lo.Z + Bypass.PAD * 2, Bypass.MIN_WIDTH)
local p = make("Part", {
Name = "RKBypass", Anchored = true, CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false,
CanTouch = false, CastShadow = false,
Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
Color = Bypass.MINE, Transparency = Bypass.CLEAR,
Size = Vector3.new(w, 400, d),
CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(mid.X, mid.Y + 190, mid.Z)),
}, Bypass.folder)
local gui = make("BillboardGui", {
Name = "RKBypassTag", AlwaysOnTop = true,
Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 34),
StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, -196, 0),
}, p)
make("TextLabel", {
BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = Bypass.MINE,
TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4,
Text = (#group == 1) and group[1].label
or (group[1].label .. " (+" .. (#group - 1) .. ")"),
}, gui)
Bypass.marks[#Bypass.marks + 1] = p
return p
end
function Bypass.saveHere()
local hrp = getHRP()
if not hrp then notify("no character", 3) return end
local p = hrp.Position
local line = string.format("%.2f %.2f %.2f %s", p.X, p.Y, p.Z, Bypass.LABEL)
local ok = pcall(function()
if typeof(makefolder) == "function" and not isfolder("Rake") then makefolder("Rake") end
local body = (typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(Bypass.savedFile)
and readfile(Bypass.savedFile)) or ""
if #body > 0 and body:sub(-1) ~= "\n" then body = body .. "\n" end
writefile(Bypass.savedFile, body .. line .. "\n")
end)
Bypass.push(p)
Bypass.remote = Bypass.remote or {}
Bypass.remote[#Bypass.remote + 1] = { pos = p, label = Bypass.LABEL }
Bypass.mine = nil
Bypass.loadMine()
if Bypass.on then Bypass.set(true) end
notify(string.format("saved bypass spot at %d, %d, %d%s", math.floor(p.X), math.floor(p.Y), math.floor(p.Z),
Bypass.synced() and " (synced across your devices)" or " (local only)"), 5)
end
function Bypass.set(on)
Bypass.on = on
Bypass.clear()
if not on then return end
Bypass.folder = make("Folder", { Name = "RKBypassSpots" }, Workspace)
local groups = Bypass.clusters()
if #groups == 0 then
notify("no spots in " .. Bypass.savedFile, 6)
return
end
local marks = 0
for _, g in ipairs(groups) do
Bypass.pillar(g)
marks = marks + #g
end
if not Bypass.fadeConn then
Bypass.fadeConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Bypass.on then return end
if os.clock() - (Bypass.fadeAt or 0) < 0.1 then return end
Bypass.fadeAt = os.clock()
for _, p in ipairs(Bypass.marks) do fadeAtEye(p, Bypass.CLEAR, 14) end
end)
addCleanup("bypassfade", function()
if Bypass.fadeConn then Bypass.fadeConn:Disconnect() Bypass.fadeConn = nil end
end)
end
notify(string.format("%d pillar%s from %d mark%s",
#groups, #groups == 1 and "" or "s", marks, marks == 1 and "" or "s"), 6)
end
local Music = {
on = false, volume = 0.5, status = "off",
station = "1LIVE", gen = 0, busy = false, seq = 0,
queue = {}, bytes = 0,
CHUNK = 262144,
dir = "Rake/radio",
}
Music.STATIONS = {
{ "1LIVE",          "https://wdr-1live-live.icecastssl.wdr.de/wdr/1live/live/mp3/128/stream.mp3" },
{ "WDR 2",          "https://wdr-wdr2-rheinland.icecastssl.wdr.de/wdr/wdr2/rheinland/mp3/128/stream.mp3" },
{ "NDR 2",          "https://icecast.ndr.de/ndr/ndr2/niedersachsen/mp3/128/stream.mp3" },
{ "SWR3",           "https://liveradio.swr.de/sw282p3/swr3/play.mp3" },
{ "Bayern 3",       "https://dispatcher.rndfnk.com/br/br3/live/mp3/mid" },
{ "Antenne Bayern", "https://s3-webradio.antenne.de/antenne" },
{ "Deutschlandfunk", "https://st01.sslstream.dlf.de/dlf/01/128/mp3/stream.mp3" },
}
Music.NAMES = {}
Music.URLS = {}
for _, row in ipairs(Music.STATIONS) do
Music.NAMES[#Music.NAMES + 1] = row[1]
Music.URLS[row[1]] = row[2]
end
Music.PROXY = "%(RADIO_BASE)s"
function Music.getURL(name)
local p = Music.PROXY
if type(p) == "string" and #p > 0 and not p:find("RADIO_BASE", 1, true) then
return p .. (name:gsub("[^%w]", function(c)
return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(c))
end))
end
return Music.URLS[name]
end
function Music.nextFile()
Music.seq = Music.seq + 1
return string.format("%s/c%d_%d.mp3", Music.dir, Music.gen, Music.seq)
end
function Music.drop(path)
if path and typeof(delfile) == "function" then pcall(delfile, path) end
end
function Music.clearFiles()
for _, path in ipairs(Music.queue) do Music.drop(path) end
Music.queue = {}
Music.drop(Music.current)
Music.current = nil
pcall(function()
if typeof(listfiles) == "function" then
for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(Music.dir)) do
if tostring(f):match("%.mp3$") then Music.drop(f) end
end
end
end)
end
function Music.canStream()
return typeof(getcustomasset) == "function" and typeof(writefile) == "function"
end
function Music.http(url)
local req = (typeof(request) == "function" and request)
or (typeof(http_request) == "function" and http_request)
or (syn and syn.request)
if req then
local hops = 0
while hops < 5 do
local ok, res = pcall(req, {
Url = url, Method = "GET",
Headers = { ["Range"] = "bytes=0-" .. Music.CHUNK,
["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0", ["Icy-MetaData"] = "0" },
})
if not (ok and type(res) == "table") then
return nil, "request failed: " .. tostring(res)
end
local code = res.StatusCode or res.status_code or 0
local body = res.Body or res.body
if code >= 300 and code < 400 then
local h = res.Headers or res.headers or {}
local loc = h.Location or h.location
if type(loc) == "string" and #loc > 0 then
url = loc
hops = hops + 1
else
return nil, "HTTP " .. tostring(code) .. " (no Location)"
end
elseif type(body) == "string" and #body > 4096 then
return body
else
return nil, "HTTP " .. tostring(code) .. " ("
.. (type(body) == "string" and #body or 0) .. "B)"
end
end
return nil, "too many redirects"
end
local ok, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
if ok and type(body) == "string" and #body > 4096 then return body end
return nil, "HttpGet failed: " .. tostring(body):sub(1, 60)
end
function Music.align(body)
local limit = math.min(#body, 8192)
for i = 1, limit - 1 do
if string.byte(body, i) == 255 then
local b2 = string.byte(body, i + 1)
if b2 and b2 >= 224 then
return (i > 1) and string.sub(body, i) or body, i - 1
end
end
end
return body, -1
end
function Music.fetch()
if Music.busy then return end
Music.busy = true
Music.fetchAt = os.clock()
local myGen = Music.gen
task.spawn(function()
local url = Music.getURL(Music.station)
local t0 = os.clock()
local body, why = Music.http(url)
if Music.gen ~= myGen or not Music.on then return end
if not body then
Music.status = Music.station .. ": " .. tostring(why)
Music.busy = false
return
end
local aligned, dropped = Music.align(body)
if dropped < 0 then
Music.status = Music.station .. ": no mp3 frame in "
.. math.floor(#body / 1024) .. " KB - not an mp3 stream?"
Music.busy = false
return
end
local path = Music.nextFile()
local wrote = pcall(function()
if not isfolder("Rake") then makefolder("Rake") end
if not isfolder(Music.dir) then makefolder(Music.dir) end
writefile(path, aligned)
end)
if not wrote then
Music.status = Music.station .. ": could not write the chunk file"
Music.busy = false
return
end
Music.bytes, Music.dropped = #aligned, dropped
Music.queue[#Music.queue + 1] = path
Music.status = string.format("%s: %d KB in %.1fs (+%dB to frame)",
Music.station, math.floor(#aligned / 1024), os.clock() - t0, dropped)
Music.busy = false
end)
end
function Music.sound()
if Music.snd and Music.snd.Parent then return Music.snd end
pcall(function()
for _, old in ipairs(game:GetService("SoundService"):GetChildren()) do
if old.Name == "RKMusic" and old ~= Music.snd then old:Destroy() end
end
end)
Music.snd = make("Sound", {
Name = "RKMusic", Looped = false, Volume = Music.volume,
}, game:GetService("SoundService"))
return Music.snd
end
function Music.playPath(path)
local snd = Music.sound()
local got, asset = pcall(getcustomasset, path)
if not (got and type(asset) == "string") then
Music.status = Music.station .. ": the executor refused the file"
Music.drop(path)
return false
end
local ok = pcall(function()
snd.SoundId = asset
snd.Volume = Music.volume
snd.TimePosition = 0
snd:Play()
end)
Music.drop(Music.current)
Music.current = path
if not ok then
Music.status = Music.station .. ": would not load the chunk"
return false
end
return true
end
function Music.pump()
if not (Music.on and Music.canStream()) then return end
if Music.busy and os.clock() - (Music.fetchAt or 0) > 12 then
Music.gen = Music.gen + 1
Music.busy = false
Music.status = Music.station .. ": fetch timed out, retrying"
end
local snd = Music.sound()
local playing, len, at = false, 0, 0
pcall(function() playing, len, at = snd.IsPlaying, snd.TimeLength, snd.TimePosition end)
if #Music.queue == 0 and not Music.busy then Music.fetch() end
local left = (len > 0) and (len - at) or 0
if playing and left > 0.2 then
Music.status = string.format("%s  %d:%02d / %d:%02d  (%d queued)", Music.station,
math.floor(at / 60), math.floor(at % 60), math.floor(len / 60), math.floor(len % 60), #Music.queue)
return
end
if #Music.queue > 0 then
local path = table.remove(Music.queue, 1)
if not Music.playPath(path) then
Music.status = Music.station .. ": " .. math.floor(Music.bytes / 1024)
.. " KB arrived but Roblox would not decode it"
end
elseif not playing then
Music.status = Music.station .. ": fetching..."
end
end
function Music.setStation(name)
Music.station = name
Music.gen = Music.gen + 1
Music.busy = false
if Music.snd then pcall(function() Music.snd:Stop() end) end
Music.clearFiles()
if Music.on then
Music.status = name .. ": switching..."
Music.fetch()
else
Music.status = "off"
end
end
function Music.set(on)
Music.on = on
if not on then
Music.gen = Music.gen + 1
Music.busy = false
if Music.pumpConn then Music.pumpConn:Disconnect() Music.pumpConn = nil end
if Music.snd then pcall(function() Music.snd:Stop() end) end
Music.clearFiles()
Music.status = "off"
return
end
if not Music.canStream() then
Music.status = "this executor has no getcustomasset"
notify(Music.status .. " - the radio needs it to play what it downloads", 8)
return
end
Music.status = Music.station .. ": starting..."
if not Music.pumpConn then
Music.pumpConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not Music.on then return end
if os.clock() - (Music.pumpAt or 0) < 0.1 then return end
Music.pumpAt = os.clock()
pcall(Music.pump)
end)
end
end
addCleanup("music", function()
Music.on = false
if Music.pumpConn then Music.pumpConn:Disconnect() Music.pumpConn = nil end
if Music.snd then
pcall(function() Music.snd:Stop() end)
pcall(function() Music.snd:Destroy() end)
Music.snd = nil
end
Music.clearFiles()
end)

function Ex.telekinesis()
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
if (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Telekinesis V5"))
or (char and char:FindFirstChild("Telekinesis V5")) then
notify("Telekinesis is already in your backpack - equip it", 4)
return
end
if typeof(loadstring) ~= "function" then
notify("this executor has no loadstring", 5)
return
end
local fn, err = loadstring(Ex.TELE_SCRIPT)
if not fn then notify("telekinesis would not load: " .. tostring(err), 6) return end
local ok, e = pcall(fn)
notify(ok and "Telekinesis added - equip it, hold click on a loose part to grab it"
or ("telekinesis error: " .. tostring(e)), 7)
end
local Mobile = { gui = nil, buttons = {}, parts = {}, shown = {}, dragActive = nil, on = false,
pos = nil, scale = 1 }
Mobile.W, Mobile.H, Mobile.GAP = 158, 44, 10
Mobile.TOP = 196
Mobile.POS_FILE = "Ruv&Rill/mobile.txt"
Mobile.ON = Color3.fromRGB(72, 199, 116)
Mobile.ALL = {
{ label = "Killaura",        index = "RK_Killaura" },
{ label = "Walkspeed",       index = "RK_SpeedOn" },
{ label = "Trap Rake",       index = "RK_AutoTrapRake" },
{ label = "Trap Player",     index = "RK_TrapPlayers" },
{ label = "Fling Drop",      index = "RK_FlingCrate" },
{ label = "Anti Block",      index = "RK_AntiBlock" },
{ label = "Silent Aim",      index = "RK_SilentAim" },
{ label = "Triggerbot",      index = "RK_Trigger" },
{ label = "Auto Dodge",      index = "RK_AutoDodge" },
{ label = "Hitbox Expander", index = "RK_Hitbox" },
{ label = "Fling Rake",      index = "RK_FlingRake" },
{ label = "Auto Farm",       index = "RK_AutoFarm" },
{ label = "Hide Under Map",  index = "RK_Hide" },
{ label = "Insta Open Drop", index = "RK_InstaCrate" },
{ label = "Instant Prompts", index = "RK_Prompts" },
{ label = "Silent Repair",   index = "RK_SilentRepair" },
{ label = "Auto Get Vest",   index = "RK_AutoGetVest" },
{ label = "Auto Get UV",     index = "RK_AutoGetUV" },
{ label = "Auto Get Stun",   index = "RK_AutoGetStun" },
{ label = "Aura",            index = "RK_Aura" },
{ label = "Rake Aura",       index = "RK_RakeAura" },
}
function Mobile.allLabels()
local out = {}
for _, e in ipairs(Mobile.ALL) do out[#out + 1] = e.label end
return out
end
function Mobile.loadPos()
Mobile.pos = {}
if typeof(isfile) ~= "function" or typeof(readfile) ~= "function" then return end
local ok, raw = pcall(function()
return isfile(Mobile.POS_FILE) and readfile(Mobile.POS_FILE) or ""
end)
if not ok or type(raw) ~= "string" then return end
for line in raw:gmatch("[^\n]+") do
local idx, x, y = line:match("^(%S+)%s+(%-?[%d%.]+)%s+(%-?[%d%.]+)")
if idx and tonumber(x) and tonumber(y) then Mobile.pos[idx] = Vector2.new(tonumber(x), tonumber(y)) end
end
end
function Mobile.savePos()
if typeof(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
local lines = {}
for idx, v in pairs(Mobile.pos or {}) do
lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("%s %d %d", idx, math.floor(v.X + 0.5), math.floor(v.Y + 0.5))
end
table.sort(lines)
pcall(ensureFolder, "Ruv&Rill")
pcall(writefile, Mobile.POS_FILE, table.concat(lines, "\n"))
end
function Mobile.clamp(x, y)
local vp = Mobile.gui and Mobile.gui.AbsoluteSize
if not vp or vp.X <= 0 then return x, y end
local hw, hh = Mobile.W * Mobile.scale / 2, Mobile.H * Mobile.scale / 2
return math.clamp(x, hw + 4, math.max(hw + 4, vp.X - hw - 4)),
math.clamp(y, hh + 4, math.max(hh + 4, vp.Y - hh - 4))
end
function Mobile.freeSlot()
local vp = Mobile.gui and Mobile.gui.AbsoluteSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
local step = (Mobile.H + Mobile.GAP) * Mobile.scale
local perCol = math.max(1, math.floor((math.max(vp.Y, 300) - Mobile.TOP - 16) / step))
for i = 0, 60 do
local col, row = math.floor(i / perCol), i % perCol
local cx = 16 + (Mobile.W * Mobile.scale) / 2 + col * (Mobile.W + Mobile.GAP) * Mobile.scale
local cy = Mobile.TOP + (Mobile.H * Mobile.scale) / 2 + row * step
local taken = false
for _, b in pairs(Mobile.buttons) do
local p = b.Position
if math.abs(p.X.Offset - cx) < Mobile.W * Mobile.scale * 0.9
and math.abs(p.Y.Offset - cy) < Mobile.H * Mobile.scale * 0.9 then
taken = true
break
end
end
if not taken then return cx, cy end
end
return 16 + Mobile.W / 2, Mobile.TOP + Mobile.H / 2
end
function Mobile.paint(index, on, instant)
local parts = Mobile.parts[index]
if not parts then return end
local function to(inst, goal)
if instant then
for k, v in pairs(goal) do pcall(function() inst[k] = v end) end
else
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(inst, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), goal):Play()
end)
end
end
to(parts.tint, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.82 or 1 })
to(parts.track, { BackgroundColor3 = on and Mobile.ON or Hud.GLASS.track })
to(parts.knob, { Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -15, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0) })
to(parts.label, { TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(22, 70, 42) or Hud.GLASS.text })
end
function Mobile.startShared()
if Mobile.moveConn then return end
Mobile.moveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
local a = Mobile.dragActive
if a and a.btn.Parent and ((a.touch and input == a.input)
or (not a.touch and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement))) then
local d = input.Position - a.start
if math.abs(d.X) + math.abs(d.Y) > 8 then a.moved = true end
if a.moved then
local x, y = Mobile.clamp(a.baseX + d.X, a.baseY + d.Y)
a.btn.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
end
end
end)
Mobile.endConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
local a = Mobile.dragActive
if a and ((a.touch and input == a.input)
or (not a.touch and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1))) then
Mobile.dragActive = nil
local parts = Mobile.parts[a.index]
if parts and not Mobile.hidden then
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(parts.scale, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
{ Scale = Mobile.scale }):Play()
end)
end
if a.moved then
local p = a.btn.Position
Mobile.pos = Mobile.pos or {}
Mobile.pos[a.index] = Vector2.new(p.X.Offset, p.Y.Offset)
Mobile.savePos()
else
local tg = Toggles[a.index]
if tg then pcall(function() tg:SetValue(not tg.Value) end) end
end
end
end)
Mobile.colorConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
Mobile.followMenu()
for index in pairs(Mobile.buttons) do
local on = (Toggles[index] and Toggles[index].Value) and true or false
if Mobile.shown[index] ~= on then
Mobile.shown[index] = on
Mobile.paint(index, on)
end
end
end)
addCleanup("mobilebtns", function()
if Mobile.moveConn then Mobile.moveConn:Disconnect() Mobile.moveConn = nil end
if Mobile.endConn then Mobile.endConn:Disconnect() Mobile.endConn = nil end
if Mobile.colorConn then Mobile.colorConn:Disconnect() Mobile.colorConn = nil end
if Mobile.gui then pcall(function() Mobile.gui:Destroy() end) Mobile.gui = nil end
Mobile.buttons, Mobile.parts, Mobile.shown = {}, {}, {}
end)
end
function Mobile.ensureGui()
if Mobile.gui and Mobile.gui.Parent then return Mobile.gui end
local parent = (typeof(gethui) == "function" and gethui())
or LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if not parent then return nil end
Mobile.hidden = Mobile.menuOpen()
Mobile.gui = make("ScreenGui", {
Name = "RKMobile", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
DisplayOrder = 9000, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
Enabled = not Mobile.hidden,
}, parent)
if not Mobile.pos then Mobile.loadPos() end
Mobile.startShared()
return Mobile.gui
end
function Mobile.add(index, label)
if Mobile.buttons[index] then return end
local gui = Mobile.ensureGui()
if not gui then return end
local x, y
local saved = Mobile.pos and Mobile.pos[index]
if saved then x, y = Mobile.clamp(saved.X, saved.Y) else x, y = Mobile.freeSlot() end
local W, H = Mobile.W, Mobile.H
local btn = make("TextButton", {
Name = "RKB_" .. index, AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = true,
AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H),
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "",
}, gui)
local scale = make("UIScale", { Scale = Mobile.hidden and 0 or Mobile.scale }, btn)
hudGlass(btn, H / 2, 0.16)
local tint = make("Frame", { Name = "Tint", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = Mobile.ON, BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 3 }, btn)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, tint)
local lbl = make("TextLabel", { Name = "Lbl", BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(17, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -17 - 50, 1, 0),
FontFace = Hud.FONT_B, TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = Hud.GLASS.text, Text = label,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex = 4 }, btn)
local trackF = make("Frame", { Name = "Track", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 17), BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = Hud.GLASS.track, ZIndex = 4 }, btn)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, trackF)
make("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Transparency = 0.9, Thickness = 1 }, trackF)
local knob = make("Frame", { Name = "Knob", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13), BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ZIndex = 5 }, trackF)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, knob)
make("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Transparency = 0.82, Thickness = 1 }, knob)
btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
Mobile.dragActive = {
input = input, touch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch,
btn = btn, index = index, moved = false, start = input.Position,
baseX = btn.Position.X.Offset, baseY = btn.Position.Y.Offset,
}
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Scale = Mobile.scale * 0.94 }):Play()
end)
end
end)
Mobile.buttons[index] = btn
Mobile.parts[index] = { scale = scale, tint = tint, label = lbl, track = trackF, knob = knob }
local on = (Toggles[index] and Toggles[index].Value) and true or false
Mobile.shown[index] = on
Mobile.paint(index, on, true)
end
function Mobile.remove(index)
local b = Mobile.buttons[index]
if b then pcall(function() b:Destroy() end) end
Mobile.buttons[index], Mobile.parts[index], Mobile.shown[index] = nil, nil, nil
end
function Mobile.setScale(v)
Mobile.scale = math.clamp(v or 1, 0.5, 2)
for index, b in pairs(Mobile.buttons) do
local parts = Mobile.parts[index]
if parts and not Mobile.hidden then parts.scale.Scale = Mobile.scale end
local x, y = Mobile.clamp(b.Position.X.Offset, b.Position.Y.Offset)
b.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
end
end
function Mobile.sync(set)
if not Mobile.on then return end
set = set or {}
for _, e in ipairs(Mobile.ALL) do
if set[e.label] then Mobile.add(e.index, e.label) else Mobile.remove(e.index) end
end
end
function Mobile.setEnabled(on)
Mobile.on = on
if not on then
for _, e in ipairs(Mobile.ALL) do Mobile.remove(e.index) end
if Mobile.gui then pcall(function() Mobile.gui.Enabled = false end) end
return
end
if Mobile.gui then pcall(function() Mobile.gui.Enabled = not Mobile.hidden end) end
Mobile.sync((Options.RK_MobList and Options.RK_MobList.Value) or {})
end
function Mobile.menuOpen()
return Library ~= nil and Library.Toggled == true
end
function Mobile.followMenu()
local open = Mobile.menuOpen()
if open ~= (Mobile.hidden == true) then Mobile.setHidden(open) end
end
function Mobile.setHidden(hide)
Mobile.hidden = hide and true or false
Mobile.hideSeq = (Mobile.hideSeq or 0) + 1
local seq, gui = Mobile.hideSeq, Mobile.gui
if not (gui and gui.Parent) then return end
if hide then
for _, parts in pairs(Mobile.parts) do
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(parts.scale, TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
{ Scale = 0 }):Play()
end)
end
task.delay(0.18, function()
if Mobile.hideSeq == seq and Mobile.hidden then pcall(function() gui.Enabled = false end) end
end)
return
end
if not Mobile.on then return end
pcall(function() gui.Enabled = true end)
local order = {}
for index, b in pairs(Mobile.buttons) do
local y = 0
pcall(function() y = b.Position.Y.Offset end)
order[#order + 1] = { index = index, y = y }
end
table.sort(order, function(a, b) return a.y < b.y end)
for i, e in ipairs(order) do
local parts = Mobile.parts[e.index]
if parts then
task.delay(math.min(i - 1, 8) * 0.035, function()
if Mobile.hideSeq ~= seq or Mobile.hidden then return end
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(parts.scale, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
{ Scale = Mobile.scale }):Play()
end)
end)
end
end
end

Dev.MINE = { RKWepCham = true, RKCham = true, RKBox = true, RKGlow = true }
Dev.GENERIC = {
LocalScript = true, Script = true, ModuleScript = true,
Server = true, Client = true, Module = true, Main = true, Handler = true,
}
function Dev.dumpHeld()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local out = { "-- every tool you are carrying, in full", "" }
local tools, held = {}, 0
local scripts = {}
local guiNames = {}
local function collect(root, where)
if not root then return end
for _, d in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Tool") then
tools[#tools + 1] = { tool = d, where = where }
if where == "EQUIPPED" then held = held + 1 end
end
end
end
collect(char, "EQUIPPED")
collect(bp, "backpack")
if #tools == 0 then
notify("no tools on you at all - buy one and hold it", 6)
return
end
for _, entry in ipairs(tools) do
local tool = entry.tool
out[#out + 1] = string.format("======== [%s] %s ========", entry.where, tool.Name)
out[#out + 1] = "path: " .. shortPath(tool)
local ok, attrs = pcall(function() return tool:GetAttributes() end)
if ok and attrs then
for k, v in pairs(attrs) do
out[#out + 1] = string.format("  @%s = %s", k, describe(v))
end
end
out[#out + 1] = "  Enabled = " .. tostring(tool.Enabled)
out[#out + 1] = "  RequiresHandle = " .. tostring(tool.RequiresHandle)
out[#out + 1] = "  ToolTip = " .. tostring(tool.ToolTip)
out[#out + 1] = ""
local base = #tool:GetFullName() + 2
for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
if not Dev.MINE[d.Name] then
local line = string.format("  [%s] %s", d.ClassName, d:GetFullName():sub(base))
local fine, val = pcall(function() return d.Value end)
if fine and val ~= nil then line = line .. "  = " .. describe(val) end
local gotText, text = pcall(function() return d.Text end)
if gotText and type(text) == "string" then
line = line .. "  TEXT=" .. string.format("%q", text)
end
local propList = (d:IsA("BaseScript") or d:IsA("ModuleScript"))
and { "Visible" } or { "Visible", "Enabled" }
for _, prop in ipairs(propList) do
local vis, on = pcall(function() return d[prop] end)
if vis and type(on) == "boolean" then
line = line .. "  " .. prop .. "=" .. tostring(on)
end
end
for _, prop in ipairs({ "SoundId", "AnimationId", "Image", "Texture" }) do
local hit, id = pcall(function() return d[prop] end)
if hit and type(id) == "string" and id ~= "" then
line = line .. "  " .. prop .. "=" .. id
end
end
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
line = line .. string.format("  pos=(%.0f,%.0f,%.0f) size=(%.1f,%.1f,%.1f)%s",
d.Position.X, d.Position.Y, d.Position.Z,
d.Size.X, d.Size.Y, d.Size.Z, d.Anchored and " anchored" or "")
end
local got, at = pcall(function() return d:GetAttributes() end)
if got and at then
for k, v in pairs(at) do line = line .. string.format("  @%s=%s", k, tostring(v)) end
end
if d:IsA("BasePart") then
for _, prop in ipairs({ "Transparency", "Material", "BrickColor" }) do
local hit, v = pcall(function() return d[prop] end)
if hit and v ~= nil then line = line .. "  " .. prop .. "=" .. tostring(v) end
end
end
if d:IsA("Light") then
local hit, b = pcall(function() return d.Brightness end)
if hit then line = line .. "  Brightness=" .. tostring(b) end
end
if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
local hit, r = pcall(function() return d.Rate end)
if hit then line = line .. "  Rate=" .. tostring(r) end
end
if d:IsA("Sound") then
local hit, p = pcall(function() return d.IsPlaying end)
if hit then line = line .. "  Playing=" .. tostring(p) end
end
if d:IsA("JointInstance") then
local j0, p0 = pcall(function() return d.Part0 end)
local j1, p1 = pcall(function() return d.Part1 end)
line = line .. "  Part0=" .. ((j0 and p0) and p0.Name or "nil")
.. "  Part1=" .. ((j1 and p1) and p1.Name or "nil")
end
if d:IsA("BaseScript") or d:IsA("ModuleScript") then
local en, enabled = pcall(function() return d.Enabled end)
if en and enabled ~= nil then line = line .. "  Enabled=" .. tostring(enabled) end
if #d.Name > 2 and not Dev.GENERIC[d.Name] then
scripts[d.Name] = tool.Name
end
end
if d:IsA("ScreenGui") or d:IsA("GuiMain") then guiNames[d.Name] = true end
out[#out + 1] = line
end
end
out[#out + 1] = ""
end
local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
if pg and next(guiNames) then
local rows = {}
for _, g in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
if guiNames[g.Name] and (g:IsA("ScreenGui") or g:IsA("GuiMain")) then
rows[#rows + 1] = string.format("  [%s] %s  Enabled=%s",
g.ClassName, g.Name, tostring(g.Enabled))
for _, d in ipairs(g:GetDescendants()) do
local row = string.format("    [%s] %s", d.ClassName,
d:GetFullName():sub(#g:GetFullName() + 2))
local got, text = pcall(function() return d.Text end)
if got and type(text) == "string" then
row = row .. "  TEXT=" .. string.format("%q", text)
end
local vis, on = pcall(function() return d.Visible end)
if vis and type(on) == "boolean" then row = row .. "  Visible=" .. tostring(on) end
rows[#rows + 1] = row
end
end
end
out[#out + 1] = "======== the live copies of those tool GUIs, in PlayerGui ========"
if #rows == 0 then
out[#out + 1] = "  (none of those GUI names is in PlayerGui right now - the tool"
out[#out + 1] = "   clones it only while it is showing something)"
else
for _, r in ipairs(rows) do out[#out + 1] = r end
end
out[#out + 1] = ""
end
if Dev.canReadConstants() and next(scripts) then
out[#out + 1] = "======== constants inside those tools' own scripts ========"
local names = {}
for name in pairs(scripts) do names[#names + 1] = name end
table.sort(names)
out[#out + 1] = "-- matching chunks named: " .. table.concat(names, ", ")
out[#out + 1] = ""
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if ok then
local seen, funcs, slice = {}, 0, os.clock()
for _, f in pairs(all) do
if type(f) == "function" and funcs < 400 then
if os.clock() - slice >= 0.008 then
task.wait()
slice = os.clock()
end
local src = Dev.info(f, "s")
if type(src) == "string" and src ~= "" and src ~= "[C]" then
local owner, best = nil, 0
for name, tool in pairs(scripts) do
if #name > best and src:find(name, 1, true) then
owner, best = name .. "  (in " .. tool .. ")", #name
end
end
if owner then
local gotK, consts = pcall(debug.getconstants, f)
if gotK and type(consts) == "table" then
local rows = {}
for _, c in pairs(consts) do
local t = type(c)
if t == "string" and #c > 0 and #c <= 200 then
rows[#rows + 1] = string.format("%q", c)
elseif t == "number" or t == "boolean" then
rows[#rows + 1] = tostring(c)
end
end
if #rows > 0 then
local line = Dev.info(f, "l")
local key = tostring(f)
if not seen[key] then
seen[key] = true
funcs = funcs + 1
out[#out + 1] = "-- " .. owner .. "  line " .. tostring(line)
out[#out + 1] = "    " .. table.concat(rows, "  ")
if typeof(debug.getupvalues) == "function" then
local gotU, ups = pcall(debug.getupvalues, f)
if gotU and type(ups) == "table" then
local urows = {}
for k, v in pairs(ups) do
local ut = type(v)
if ut == "number" or ut == "boolean" or ut == "string" then
urows[#urows + 1] = tostring(k) .. "=" .. tostring(v)
elseif typeof(v) == "Instance" then
urows[#urows + 1] = tostring(k) .. "=<" .. v.ClassName .. "> " .. v.Name
end
end
if #urows > 0 then
out[#out + 1] = "    up  " .. table.concat(urows, "  ")
end
end
end
out[#out + 1] = ""
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
out[#out + 1] = "-- " .. funcs .. " functions from those scripts"
end
out[#out + 1] = ""
elseif not Dev.canReadConstants() then
out[#out + 1] = "-- (this executor has no debug.getconstants, so the tools'"
out[#out + 1] = "--  own scripts could not be read)"
out[#out + 1] = ""
end
if typeof(getgc) == "function" then
local names = {}
for _, entry in ipairs(tools) do names[entry.tool.Name] = true end
out[#out + 1] = "======== heap tables naming one of those tools ========"
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if ok then
local hits = 0
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" and hits < 30 then
local interesting, keys = false, {}
pcall(function()
for k, val in pairs(v) do
if type(k) == "string" then
if names[k] then interesting = true end
keys[#keys + 1] = k .. " = " .. describe(val, 2)
elseif type(val) == "string" and names[val] then
interesting = true
end
end
end)
if interesting and #keys > 0 and #keys < 60 then
hits = hits + 1
out[#out + 1] = "-- table " .. hits
out[#out + 1] = "  " .. table.concat(keys, "\n  ")
out[#out + 1] = ""
end
end
end
out[#out + 1] = "-- " .. hits .. " tables"
end
end
local names = {}
for _, entry in ipairs(tools) do
names[#names + 1] = entry.tool.Name .. (entry.where == "EQUIPPED" and "*" or "")
end
local heldName = nil
for _, entry in ipairs(tools) do
if entry.where == "EQUIPPED" then heldName = entry.tool.Name break end
end
local file = heldName and ("tools-" .. heldName .. ".txt") or "tools.txt"
saveDump(file, table.concat(out, "\n"))
notify(string.format("%s\n(* = equipped)\nwritten to %s%s",
table.concat(names, ", "), file,
held == 0 and "\nNOTHING WAS EQUIPPED - hold the tool and press again" or ""), 9)
end
function Dev.testRadio()
task.spawn(function()
local out = { "-- radio pipeline, stage by stage", "" }
local function say(line)
out[#out + 1] = line
Dev.scanState = line
end
local req = (typeof(request) == "function" and "request")
or (typeof(http_request) == "function" and "http_request")
or ((syn and syn.request) and "syn.request") or nil
say("1. HTTP        : " .. (req or "NONE - falling back to game:HttpGet"))
say("   getcustomasset: " .. tostring(typeof(getcustomasset)))
say("   writefile     : " .. tostring(typeof(writefile)))
say("")
for _, row in ipairs(Music.STATIONS) do
local name = row[1]
local url = Music.getURL(name) or row[2]
say("== " .. name)
say("   " .. url)
local t0 = os.clock()
local body, why = Music.http(url)
if not body then
say("   2. fetch     : FAILED - " .. tostring(why))
else
say(string.format("   2. fetch     : %d KB in %.1fs",
math.floor(#body / 1024), os.clock() - t0))
local aligned, dropped = Music.align(body)
if dropped < 0 then
say("   3. mp3 frame : NOT FOUND in the first 8 KB")
say("      first bytes: " .. string.sub(body, 1, 60):gsub("[^%w%p ]", "."))
else
say("   3. mp3 frame : found at byte " .. (dropped + 1))
local path = Music.dir .. "/probe.mp3"
local wrote = pcall(function()
if not isfolder("Rake") then makefolder("Rake") end
if not isfolder(Music.dir) then makefolder(Music.dir) end
writefile(path, aligned)
end)
say("   4. writefile : " .. (wrote and "ok" or "FAILED"))
if wrote and typeof(getcustomasset) == "function" then
local got, asset = pcall(getcustomasset, path)
say("   5. asset     : " .. (got and tostring(asset) or "REFUSED"))
if got and type(asset) == "string" then
local snd = make("Sound", { Name = "RKProbe", Volume = 0 },
game:GetService("SoundService"))
pcall(function() snd.SoundId = asset snd:Play() end)
task.wait(2)
local ok, len = pcall(function() return snd.TimeLength end)
say("   6. decode    : " .. ((ok and type(len) == "number" and len > 0)
and string.format("%.1fs of audio", len)
or "Roblox would not decode it"))
pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end)
end
end
end
end
say("")
end
Dev.scanState = ""
saveDump("radio-test.txt", table.concat(out, "\n"))
notify("radio pipeline written to Rake/dumps/radio-test.txt", 8)
end)
end
function Dev.whatsPlaying()
local out = { "-- every Sound playing at the moment the button went down", "" }
local rows = {}
for _, root in ipairs({ Workspace, LocalPlayer, game:GetService("SoundService"),
ReplicatedStorage }) do
local ok, list = pcall(function() return root:GetDescendants() end)
if ok then
for _, d in ipairs(list) do
if d:IsA("Sound") then
local fine, playing = pcall(function() return d.IsPlaying end)
if fine and playing then
local group = d.SoundGroup and d.SoundGroup.Name or "-"
rows[#rows + 1] = string.format(
"%-26s vol %.2f  %s  group %-12s looped %-5s  %s",
d.Name, d.Volume, tostring(d.SoundId), group,
tostring(d.Looped), shortPath(d))
end
end
end
end
end
table.sort(rows)
if #rows == 0 then
out[#out + 1] = "(nothing was playing)"
else
for _, r in ipairs(rows) do out[#out + 1] = r end
end
out[#out + 1] = ""
out[#out + 1] = "-- " .. #rows .. " playing"
saveDump("playing.txt", table.concat(out, "\n"))
notify(#rows .. " sounds playing - see Rake/dumps/playing.txt", 7)
end
function Dev.dumpStunStick()
local char = LocalPlayer.Character
local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
local stick = (char and char:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName))
or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(Fight.toolName))
local out = { "-- StunStick: tool tree + malfunction/cooldown constants", "" }
if stick then
out[#out + 1] = "======== " .. stick.Name .. " ("
.. (stick.Parent == char and "EQUIPPED" or "backpack") .. ") ========"
local ok, attrs = pcall(function() return stick:GetAttributes() end)
if ok and attrs then
for k, v in pairs(attrs) do out[#out + 1] = string.format("  @%s = %s", k, describe(v)) end
end
out[#out + 1] = "  Enabled = " .. tostring(stick.Enabled)
local base = #stick:GetFullName() + 2
for _, d in ipairs(stick:GetDescendants()) do
if not Dev.MINE[d.Name] then
local line = string.format("  [%s] %s", d.ClassName, d:GetFullName():sub(base))
local fine, val = pcall(function() return d.Value end)
if fine and val ~= nil then line = line .. "  = " .. describe(val) end
local okA, at = pcall(function() return d:GetAttributes() end)
if okA and at then
for k, v in pairs(at) do line = line .. string.format("  @%s=%s", k, describe(v)) end
end
out[#out + 1] = line
end
end
else
out[#out + 1] = "-- no StunStick on you - equip/buy one and dump again; heap scan still runs"
end
out[#out + 1] = ""
out[#out + 1] = "-- heap tables with stun / malfunction / swing / cooldown / jam / break keys:"
if typeof(getgc) == "function" then
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if ok then
local hits = 0
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" and hits < 50 then
local mark, fields = false, {}
pcall(function()
for k, val in pairs(v) do
if type(k) == "string" then
local low = k:lower()
if low:find("malfunction") or low:find("stun") or low:find("swing")
or low:find("cooldown") or low:find("jam") or low:find("break")
or low:find("fail") or low:find("hitcool") then mark = true end
if type(val) == "number" or type(val) == "boolean" then
fields[#fields + 1] = k .. "=" .. tostring(val)
else
fields[#fields + 1] = k .. "(" .. type(val) .. ")"
end
end
if #fields >= 45 then break end
end
end)
if mark then
hits = hits + 1
out[#out + 1] = "  { " .. table.concat(fields, ", ") .. " }"
end
end
end
out[#out + 1] = "\n-- " .. hits .. " table(s)"
end
end
saveDump("stunstick.txt", table.concat(out, "\n"))
end
function Dev.dumpAdonis()
local out = { "-- Adonis hunt", "" }
local roots = {}
pcall(function() roots[#roots + 1] = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") end)
pcall(function() roots[#roots + 1] = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
pcall(function() roots[#roots + 1] = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end)
pcall(function() roots[#roots + 1] = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst") end)
pcall(function() if gethui then roots[#roots + 1] = gethui() end end)
out[#out + 1] = "-- instances mentioning adonis:"
for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
if root then
for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
local ok, nm = pcall(function() return tostring(d.Name) end)
if ok and nm:lower():find("adonis") then
out[#out + 1] = string.format("  [%s] %s", d.ClassName, d:GetFullName())
end
end
end
end
out[#out + 1] = ""
out[#out + 1] = "-- heap tables with Adonis-ish keys:"
if typeof(getgc) == "function" then
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if ok then
local hits = 0
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" and hits < 60 then
local keys, mark = {}, false
pcall(function()
for k in pairs(v) do
if type(k) == "string" then
keys[#keys + 1] = k
local low = k:lower()
if low:find("adonis") or k == "Anti" or k == "Variables"
or k == "Detected" or k == "Kill" then mark = true end
end
if #keys >= 40 then break end
end
end)
if mark then
hits = hits + 1
out[#out + 1] = "  { " .. table.concat(keys, ", ") .. " }"
end
end
end
out[#out + 1] = "\n-- " .. hits .. " candidate table(s)"
end
end
saveDump("adonis.txt", table.concat(out, "\n"))
end
function Dev.findNvg()
if typeof(getgc) ~= "function" then
notify("this executor has no getgc", 5)
return
end
local out = { "-- heap tables that mention night vision", "" }
local ok, all = pcall(getgc, true)
if not ok then
notify("getgc refused", 5)
return
end
local hits, scanned = 0, 0
for _, v in pairs(all) do
if type(v) == "table" then
scanned = scanned + 1
local interesting, keys = false, {}
pcall(function()
for k, val in pairs(v) do
if type(k) == "string" then
local low = k:lower()
if low:find("nv") or low:find("night") or low:find("goggle")
or low:find("vision") then
interesting = true
end
if type(val) == "number" or type(val) == "boolean" then
keys[#keys + 1] = k .. " = " .. tostring(val)
else
keys[#keys + 1] = k .. " (" .. type(val) .. ")"
end
end
end
end)
if interesting and #keys > 0 then
hits = hits + 1
out[#out + 1] = "-- table " .. hits
out[#out + 1] = "  " .. table.concat(keys, "\n  ")
out[#out + 1] = ""
if hits >= 40 then break end
end
end
end
out[#out + 1] = string.format("-- %d tables scanned, %d mentioned night vision",
scanned, hits)
saveDump("nightvision.txt", table.concat(out, "\n"))
notify(hits .. " candidate tables - see Rake/dumps/nightvision.txt", 7)
end
function Dev.tintReport()
local out = {}
local function add(line) out[#out + 1] = line end
local function rgb(c)
return string.format("(%d,%d,%d)", c.R * 255 + 0.5, c.G * 255 + 0.5, c.B * 255 + 0.5)
end
local headline = nil
local c = cam()
add("-- what is colouring the screen, taken at the moment the button was pressed")
local exe = "?"
if typeof(identifyexecutor) == "function" then
local ok, name = pcall(identifyexecutor)
if ok and name then exe = tostring(name) end
end
add("-- executor " .. exe)
add("")
add("== bright surfaces near the camera (the usual cause) ==")
local eye = c and c.CFrame.Position
local found = 0
if eye then
local ok, parts = pcall(function()
return Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(eye, 18)
end)
for _, p in ipairs((ok and parts) or {}) do
if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Transparency < 0.97 then
local ours = p.Name:sub(1, 2) == "RK" or p.Name:find("Aura")
or p.Name:find("Jump") or p.Name:find("Ring")
if p.Material == Enum.Material.Neon or ours then
found = found + 1
local d = eyeDist(p)
add(string.format("%6.1f studs  %-28s %-10s %s t=%.2f  %s",
d, p.Name, p.Material.Name, rgb(p.Color), p.Transparency,
ours and "<< OURS" or ""))
if not headline and d < 6 then
headline = p.Name .. " is on the lens - " .. rgb(p.Color)
end
end
end
end
end
if found == 0 then add("(nothing bright within eighteen studs)") end
add("")
add("== ColorCorrectionEffects ==")
for _, holder in ipairs({ Lighting, c }) do
if holder then
for _, d in ipairs(holder:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then
local flat = d.TintColor == Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
and math.abs(d.Saturation) < 0.01
and math.abs(d.Brightness) < 0.01
and math.abs(d.Contrast) < 0.01
add(string.format("%-3s %-46s tint %s sat %.2f bright %.2f contrast %.2f %s",
d.Enabled and "ON" or "off", d:GetFullName(), rgb(d.TintColor),
d.Saturation, d.Brightness, d.Contrast,
(d.Enabled and not flat) and "<<<<< COLOURED" or ""))
if d.Enabled and not flat and not headline then
headline = d.Name .. " is tinting " .. rgb(d.TintColor)
end
end
end
end
end
add("")
add("== Highlights ==")
for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Highlight") then
local adorn = d.Adornee
local away = "no adornee"
if adorn and eye then
local ok, at = pcall(function() return adorn:GetPivot().Position end)
if ok then away = string.format("%.0f studs", (at - eye).Magnitude) end
end
add(string.format("%-20s fill %s t=%.2f  %s  adornee %s (%s)",
d.Name, rgb(d.FillColor), d.FillTransparency, d.DepthMode.Name,
adorn and adorn.Name or "-", away))
end
end
add("")
add("== Lighting ==")
add(string.format("ClockTime %.2f  Ambient %s  OutdoorAmbient %s  Brightness %.2f",
Lighting.ClockTime, rgb(Lighting.Ambient), rgb(Lighting.OutdoorAmbient),
Lighting.Brightness))
add(string.format("FogColor %s  FogStart %.0f  FogEnd %.0f",
rgb(Lighting.FogColor), Lighting.FogStart, Lighting.FogEnd))
for _, d in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
if d:IsA("Atmosphere") then
add(string.format("Atmosphere %-12s colour %s  haze %.2f  density %.3f  glare %.2f",
d.Name, rgb(d.Color), d.Haze, d.Density, d.Glare))
elseif d:IsA("BloomEffect") then
add(string.format("Bloom %s  intensity %.2f  threshold %.2f  size %d",
d.Enabled and "ON" or "off", d.Intensity, d.Threshold, d.Size))
end
end
add("")
add("== round ==")
for _, name in ipairs({ "Night", "InitiateBloodHour", "Timer", "TurningToDay" }) do
local v = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(name)
add(string.format("%-20s %s", name, v and tostring(v.Value) or "(absent)"))
end
saveDump("tint-report.txt", table.concat(out, "\n"))
notify(headline or "nothing coloured found - press it WHILE the screen is red", 8)
end
local Gate = {
open = false, built = false, tries = 0, said = "",
H1 = 3596571371, H2 = 1455964860, LEN = 36,
}
function Gate.clean(text)
local out = tostring(text or ""):gsub("%D", "")
return out
end
function Gate.hash(text)
local a, b = 2166136261, 5381
for i = 1, #text do
local c = string.byte(text, i)
a = (a * 31 + c) % 4294967291
b = (b * 131 + c) % 4294967279
end
return a, b
end
function Gate.check(text)
local digits = Gate.clean(text)
if #digits ~= Gate.LEN then
return false, (#digits == 0) and "" or (#digits .. " digits, it wants " .. Gate.LEN)
end
local a, b = Gate.hash(digits)
if a == Gate.H1 and b == Gate.H2 then return true, "unlocked" end
return false, "that key is not right"
end
local function buildDevTools(page)
local gb0 = page:AddLeftGroupbox("Read The Call Shapes")
gb0:AddInput("RK_StringFilter", {
Text = "Filter", Default = "", Finished = false,
ClearTextOnFocus = false, Placeholder = "Rake",
Callback = function(v) Dev.stringFilter = v end,
})
gb0:AddButton({ Text = "Dump Loaded Scripts", Func = function()
task.spawn(function() Dev.dumpLoaded(Dev.stringFilter) end)
end })
liveLabel(gb0, function()
return Dev.scanState ~= "" and Dev.scanState or "idle"
end, 0.3)
gb0:AddButton({ Text = "Dump Client Strings", Func = function()
task.spawn(function() Dev.dumpStrings(Dev.stringFilter) end)
end })
local gb2 = page:AddLeftGroupbox("Dumps")
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "DUMP EVERYTHING", Func = Dev.dumpEverything })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Dump Remotes", Func = Dev.dumpRemotes })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Dump Your Numbers", Func = Dev.dumpNumbers })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Dump Config Tables", Func = Dev.dumpConfigs })
local gbR = page:AddLeftGroupbox("Record The Kill")
gbR:AddButton({ Text = "Start Recording", Func = Rec.arm })
gbR:AddButton({ Text = "Stop And Write", Func = Rec.disarm })
liveLabel(gbR, function()
if not Rec.on then return "not recording" end
return string.format("recording - %d events, %.0fs\nRake %s | %d traps watched",
#Rec.lines, os.clock() - Rec.t0,
Rec.rakeOn and "ATTACHED" or "not here yet", Rec.trapN or 0)
end, 0.5)
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Executor Report", Func = Dev.caps })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Re-download Obsidian", Func = function()
task.spawn(function()
local out = {}
for _, part in ipairs({ "Library.lua", "addons/ThemeManager.lua",
"addons/SaveManager.lua" }) do
local _, how = fetch(part, true)
out[#out + 1] = part .. ": " .. tostring(how)
end
notify(table.concat(out, "\n") .. "\n\nre-execute the script to use it", 10)
end)
end })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Check My Tools", Func = Items.probe })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Find Night Vision", Func = Dev.findNvg })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Save Bypass Spot Here", Func = Bypass.saveHere })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "What Is Tinting My Screen", Func = Dev.tintReport })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Dump Held Tool", Func = Dev.dumpHeld })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Dump Adonis", Func = Dev.dumpAdonis })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Dump Stun Stick", Func = Dev.dumpStunStick })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "What Is Playing", Func = Dev.whatsPlaying })
gb2:AddButton({ Text = "Test Radio", Func = Dev.testRadio })
local gb3 = page:AddRightGroupbox("What Am I Looking At")
gb3:AddButton({ Text = "Describe It", Func = function()
local c = cam()
if not c then return end
local params = RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character }
local hit = Workspace:Raycast(c.CFrame.Position, c.CFrame.LookVector * 500, params)
if not hit then notify("nothing within 500m of your crosshair", 4) return end
local inst = hit.Instance
local model = inst:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
local bits = {
"part: " .. shortPath(inst),
"distance: " .. math.floor((hit.Position - c.CFrame.Position).Magnitude) .. "m",
}
if model then
bits[#bits + 1] = "model: " .. shortPath(model)
local ok, attrs = pcall(function() return model:GetAttributes() end)
if ok then
for k, v in pairs(attrs) do bits[#bits + 1] = "  @" .. k .. " = " .. tostring(v) end
end
end
clipboard(table.concat(bits, "\n"))
notify(table.concat(bits, "\n"), 8)
end })
local gb4 = page:AddRightGroupbox("Console")
gb4:AddInput("RK_Console", {
Text = "Lua", Default = "", Finished = false, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
Placeholder = "print(game.PlaceId)",
Callback = function(v) Dev.console = v end,
})
gb4:AddButton({ Text = "Run", Func = function() Dev.run(Dev.console) end })
end
tryBuild("Controls", buildControls, Tabs.Controls)
tryBuild("Teleports", buildTeleports, Tabs.Teleports)
tryBuild("Visuals", buildVisuals, Tabs.Visuals)
