local function buildDev(page)
local gb = page:AddLeftGroupbox("Locked")
gb:AddInput("RK_DevKey", {
Text = "Key", Default = "", Finished = false,
ClearTextOnFocus = false, Placeholder = "000000-000000-000000-000000-000000-000000",
Callback = function(v) Gate.try(page, v, false) end,
})
gb:AddButton({ Text = "Unlock", Func = function()
Gate.try(page, Options.RK_DevKey and Options.RK_DevKey.Value or "", true)
end })
end
function Gate.try(page, text, loud)
if Gate.open then return end
local ok, why = Gate.check(text)
Gate.said = why or ""
if not ok then
local digits = Gate.clean(text)
if loud or #digits == Gate.LEN then
Gate.tries = Gate.tries + 1
notify(why or "that key is not right", 4)
end
return
end
Gate.open = true
if not Gate.built then
Gate.built = true
tryBuild("Dev Tools", buildDevTools, page)
end
notify("dev tab unlocked", 4)
end
tryBuild("Dev", buildDev, Tabs.Dev)
local function stage(name, fn)
Device.Boot.step(Device.Boot.LABEL[name] or name)
local ok, err = pcall(fn)
if not ok then
Device.Boot.fail(Device.Boot.LABEL[name] or name, err)
warn("[Ruv&Rill] boot stage '" .. name .. "' failed: " .. tostring(err))
UIReport[#UIReport + 1] = { name = name, ok = false, err = tostring(err) }
end
return ok
end
stage("config manager", function()
if not (ThemeManager and SaveManager) then return end
ThemeManager:SetLibrary(Library)
SaveManager:SetLibrary(Library)
SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
SaveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes({ "RK_MenuKey", "RK_WalkMacro", "RK_WalkMacroKey", "RK_Device", "RK_LoaderOpts",
"RK_SafeMode" })
SaveManager:SetNoShare({ "RK_TrapWho", "RK_FlareFrom", "RK_DevKey", "RK_Console", "RK_StringFilter",
"RK_UiScale", "RK_CornerRadius", "RK_MenuOrb", "RK_MobileOn", "RK_MobList", "RK_MobScale", "RK_BlurBg" })
ThemeManager:SetFolder("Rake")
SaveManager:SetFolder("Rake/configs")
SaveManager:BuildConfigSection(Tabs.Settings)
ThemeManager:ApplyToTab(Tabs.Settings)
end)
Library._pop = { depth = 0 }
do
function Library._popAside(away)
local st = Library._pop
local guis = { Library.ScreenGui, Hud.gui, Mobile.gui }
if away then
st.depth = st.depth + 1
if st.depth == 1 then
st.saved = {}
for i = 1, 3 do
local g = guis[i]
if g and g.Parent then
st.saved[i] = { gui = g, on = g.Enabled }
pcall(function() g.Enabled = false end)
end
end
end
Library:Blur("popup", true)
return
end
st.depth = math.max(0, st.depth - 1)
if st.depth > 0 then return end
for _, rec in pairs(st.saved or {}) do
if rec.gui and rec.gui.Parent then pcall(function() rec.gui.Enabled = rec.on end) end
end
st.saved = nil
Library:Blur("popup", false)
end
function Library.Popup(opts, cb)
local TEXT, SUB = Color3.fromRGB(15, 17, 24), Color3.fromRGB(62, 68, 82)
local ACCENT, EDGE, WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(36, 40, 52), Color3.fromRGB(206, 211, 221), Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
local FONT = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.Regular)
local BOLD = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/Nunito.json", Enum.FontWeight.SemiBold)
local W, H = 380, 206
local st = Library._pop
local function tw(o, t, props, style, dir)
local x = TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint,
dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
x:Play()
return x
end
local stepAside = Library._popAside
opts = opts or {}
if st.current then st.current.close(st.current.other) end
local parent
pcall(function() if typeof(gethui) == "function" then parent = gethui() end end)
if not parent then parent = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") end
if not parent then
if cb then pcall(cb, nil) end
return nil
end
local ui = { conns = {} }
st.current = ui
local function hook(signal, fn) ui.conns[#ui.conns + 1] = signal:Connect(fn) end
local gui = make("ScreenGui", { Name = "Ruv&Rill_Popup", IgnoreGuiInset = true, ResetOnSpawn = false,
DisplayOrder = 1000001, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling }, parent)
ui.gui = gui
local scrim = make("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Modal = true, BorderSizePixel = 0,
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(6, 7, 9), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
ZIndex = 1 }, gui)
local holder = make("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H), ZIndex = 2 }, gui)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, holder)
local rim = make("UIStroke", { Color = WHITE, Thickness = 1.2, Transparency = 1 }, holder)
local scale = make("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, holder)
local fit = 1
pcall(function()
local vp = Workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
if vp.X > 0 and vp.Y > 0 then fit = math.min(1, (vp.X - 24) / W, (vp.Y - 24) / H) end
end)
scale.Scale = fit * 0.94
local shadow = make("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Controls/DropShadow.png", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
SliceCenter = Rect.new(10, 10, 118, 118), ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 12, 24), ImageTransparency = 1,
Size = UDim2.new(1, 44, 1, 44), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-22, -14), ZIndex = 2 }, holder)
local okCg, card = pcall(function() return make("CanvasGroup", { GroupTransparency = 1 }, holder) end)
if not okCg then card = make("Frame", {}, holder) end
card.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(250, 251, 255)
card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
card.BorderSizePixel = 0
card.ZIndex = 3
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, card)
make("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(WHITE, Color3.fromRGB(226, 234, 250)) }, card)
local mark = make("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 186, 38), BorderSizePixel = 0,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 22), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28), ZIndex = 5 }, card)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, mark)
make("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "!", FontFace = BOLD, TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = WHITE,
Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 6 }, mark)
make("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = tostring(opts.title or "Ruv&Rill"), FontFace = BOLD,
TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = TEXT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(62, 24), Size = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0, 24), ZIndex = 5 }, card)
make("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = tostring(opts.text or ""), FontFace = FONT,
TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = SUB, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextWrapped = true,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 64), Size = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 74), ZIndex = 5 }, card)
local buttons = opts.buttons or { { id = "ok", text = "OK", primary = true } }
local n = #buttons
local bw = (W - 48 - (n - 1) * 12) / n
for i, b in ipairs(buttons) do
local primary = b.primary and true or false
if primary then ui.primary = b.id elseif ui.other == nil then ui.other = b.id end
local btn = make("TextButton", { Name = tostring(b.id), Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false,
BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = primary and ACCENT or WHITE,
BackgroundTransparency = primary and 0 or 0.25,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24 + (i - 1) * (bw + 12), H - 24 - 38), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(bw, 38),
ZIndex = 5 }, card)
make("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, btn)
if not primary then make("UIStroke", { Color = EDGE, Thickness = 1 }, btn) end
make("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = tostring(b.text or b.id), FontFace = BOLD,
TextSize = 14, TextColor3 = primary and WHITE or TEXT, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 6 }, btn)
local sc = make("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, btn)
local over = false
hook(btn.MouseEnter, function() over = true; tw(sc, 0.15, { Scale = 1.03 }) end)
hook(btn.MouseLeave, function() over = false; tw(sc, 0.15, { Scale = 1 }) end)
hook(btn.MouseButton1Down, function() tw(sc, 0.08, { Scale = 0.97 }) end)
hook(btn.MouseButton1Up, function() tw(sc, 0.12, { Scale = over and 1.03 or 1 }) end)
hook(btn.Activated, function() ui.close(b.id) end)
end
ui.primary = ui.primary or buttons[1].id
ui.other = ui.other or buttons[#buttons].id
function ui.close(id)
if ui.done then return end
ui.done = true
if st.current == ui then st.current = nil end
for _, c in ipairs(ui.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
ui.conns = {}
tw(scrim, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
tw(holder, 0.25, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -10) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
tw(scale, 0.25, { Scale = fit * 0.96 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
tw(shadow, 0.2, { ImageTransparency = 1 })
tw(rim, 0.2, { Transparency = 1 })
if okCg then tw(card, 0.22, { GroupTransparency = 1 }) else card.Visible = false end
task.delay(0.26, function()
pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
if cb then pcall(cb, id) end
stepAside(false)
end)
end
hook(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input)
if ui.done then return end
local k = input.KeyCode
if k == Enum.KeyCode.Return or k == Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter then ui.close(ui.primary)
elseif k == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then ui.close(ui.other) end
end)
hook(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default end)
pcall(function() UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true end)
end)
stepAside(true)
tw(scrim, 0.35, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 })
tw(holder, 0.45, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) })
tw(scale, 0.45, { Scale = fit }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
tw(shadow, 0.4, { ImageTransparency = 0.5 })
tw(rim, 0.4, { Transparency = 0.2 })
if okCg then tw(card, 0.3, { GroupTransparency = 0 }) end
return ui
end
addCleanup("popup", function() if Library._pop.current then Library._pop.current.close(nil) end end)
end
do
local menuGroup = Tabs.Settings:AddLeftGroupbox("Menu")
menuGroup:AddLabel("Menu bind"):AddKeyPicker("RK_MenuKey", {
Default = "RightShift", NoUI = true, Text = "Menu keybind",
})
menuGroup:AddSlider("RK_CornerRadius", { Text = "Corner Radius", Suffix = "px", Default = 4, Min = 0, Max = 16,
Rounding = 0, Standalone = true, Callback = function(v) Library:SetCornerRadius(v) end })
local scalePending, scaleRunning = nil, false
local function applyScale(v)
scalePending = v
Library._scaleQuiet = Library.Loading and true or false
if scaleRunning then return end
scaleRunning = true
task.spawn(function()
local last
repeat
last = scalePending
task.wait(0.15)
until scalePending == last
local want = scalePending
local now = math.floor((Library.DPIScale or 1) * 100 + 0.5)
if want > 100 and now <= 100 and not Library._scaleQuiet and Library.Popup then
scaleRunning = false
Library.Popup({
title = "UI Scale above 100%",
text = string.format("At %d%% the menu may not fit on your screen, and parts of it can get "
.. "cut off. Keep %d%%, or go back to 100%%?", want, want),
buttons = { { id = "back", text = "Back to 100%" }, { id = "keep", text = "Keep " .. want .. "%", primary = true } },
}, function(id)
if id == "keep" then
pcall(function() Library:SetDPIScale(want) end)
elseif Options.RK_UiScale then
pcall(function() Options.RK_UiScale:SetValue(100) end)
end
end)
return
end
pcall(function() Library:SetDPIScale(want) end)
scaleRunning = false
end)
end
menuGroup:AddSlider("RK_UiScale", {
Text = "UI Scale", Suffix = "%", Default = math.floor((Library.DPIScale or 1) * 100 + 0.5), Min = 50, Max = 200, Rounding = 0,
Callback = applyScale,
})
menuGroup:AddDropdown("RK_Device", {
Text = "Device", Values = { "Ask Every Time", "PC", "Mobile" }, Standalone = true,
Default = Device.remembered and Device.name or "Ask Every Time",
Callback = function(v)
if Library.Loading then return end
Device.remember(v ~= "Ask Every Time" and v or nil)
Device.remembered = v ~= "Ask Every Time"
notify(v == "Ask Every Time" and "it will ask PC or Mobile each time the script runs"
or ("from the next run it sets up for " .. v .. " without asking"), 4)
end,
})
menuGroup:AddToggle("RK_BlurBg", { Text = "Blur Background", Default = true,
Callback = function(v)
Library.BlurBackground = v and true or false
Library:Blur("menu", Library.Toggled)
end })
menuGroup:AddToggle("RK_LoaderOpts", { Text = "Loading Options", Default = not Device.Boot.opt.fast,
Callback = function(v)
if Library.Loading then return end
Device.Boot.opt.fast = not v
Device.Boot.saveOpts()
end })
menuGroup:AddToggle("RK_SafeMode", { Text = "Safe Mode", Default = Device.Boot.opt.safe,
Tooltip = "Turns off and locks every blatant feature: movement, combat, traps, teleports, remote actions, the farm",
Callback = function(v)
if Library.Loading then return end
Device.Boot.opt.safe = v and true or false
Device.Boot.saveOpts()
if Device.Safe and Device.Safe.set then Device.Safe.set(v) end

RK_AlertSounds = true, RK_FarmStats = true,
RK_SafeMode = true,
RK_LoaderOpts = true,
}
menuGroup:AddButton({ Text = "Turn Everything Off", Func = function()
local n = 0
for idx, t in pairs(Toggles) do
if not PANIC_KEEP[idx] and t and t.Value == true and t.Type ~= "SubToggle"
and typeof(t.SetValue) == "function" then
pcall(function() t:SetValue(false) end)
n = n + 1
end
end
notify(n .. " switched off", 3)
end }):AddKeyPicker("RK_PanicKey", { Mode = "Press", Text = "Panic", Default = "None" })

menuGroup:AddButton({ Text = "Check Keybinds", Func = function() pcall(function() Library:CheckKeys(false) end) end })

local reportGroup = Tabs.Settings:AddRightGroupbox("Build Report")

local bits = {}
for _, r in ipairs(UIReport) do
bits[#bits + 1] = (r.ok and "ok   " or "FAIL ") .. r.name .. (r.ok and "" or "  " .. tostring(r.err))
end

bits[#bits + 1] = typeof(getgc) == "function" and "getgc: yes" or "getgc: NO - infinite stamina unavailable"
bits[#bits + 1] = "namecall hook: never installed - this game kicks for those"
return table.concat(bits, "\n")
end, 2)
end

if Ex.hidden or Ex.hideActive then

Device.Safe = { on = false, count = 0,
WHY = "Safe Mode is on - blatant features are locked (Settings > Menu > Safe Mode)" }
Device.Safe.TOGGLES = {
"RK_InfStamina", "RK_NoFall", "RK_AntiRagdoll", "RK_AntiTrap", "RK_Noclip", "RK_SpeedOn",
"RK_Fly", "RK_InfJump", "RK_AntiVoid", "RK_Spin", "RK_WalkFling",
"RK_Killaura", "RK_SilentAim", "RK_Trigger", "RK_NoCooldown", "RK_NoMalfunction", "RK_AntiBlock",
"RK_AutoDodge", "RK_InstaKillRake", "RK_InstaKillV2", "RK_FaceRake", "RK_Hitbox",
"RK_AutoTrapRake", "RK_TrapPlayers",
"RK_AutoFarm",
"RK_FakeLag", "RK_InstaCrate", "RK_SilentRepair", "RK_BypassShock", "RK_AutoFlare", "RK_AutoScrap",
"RK_Hide", "RK_Float", "RK_FlingCrate", "RK_FlingRake", "RK_TrapArea",
"RK_Prompts", "RK_DoorNoclip", "RK_Moonwalk", "RK_AutoGetVest", "RK_AutoGetUV", "RK_AutoGetStun",
}
Device.Safe.BUTTONS = {
["Player/Blink Forward"] = true, ["Player/Teleport To My Mouse"] = true,
["Exploits/Bring Scrap"] = true, ["Exploits/Fire Flare"] = true,
["Exploits/Restore Power"] = true, ["Exploits/Repair Instantly"] = true,
["Controls/Door"] = true, ["Controls/Light"] = true, ["Controls/Open Door From Anywhere"] = true,
["Controls/Lights"] = true, ["Controls/Hatch"] = true, ["Controls/Radar"] = true,
["Controls/Remove Invisible Walls"] = true,
["Misc/Telekinesis"] = true,
["Teleports/*"] = true,
}
function Device.Safe.covers(button)
local key = tostring(button.Key or "")
return Device.Safe.BUTTONS[key] or Device.Safe.BUTTONS[(key:match("^(.-)/") or "") .. "/*"] or false
end
function Device.Safe.set(on, quiet)
Device.Safe.on = on and true or false
local why = Device.Safe.on and Device.Safe.WHY or nil
local n = 0
for _, idx in ipairs(Device.Safe.TOGGLES) do
local t = Toggles[idx]
if t then
pcall(function() Library:SetLocked(t, why) end)
n = n + 1
end
end
for _, b in ipairs(Library.Buttons or {}) do
if Device.Safe.covers(b) then
pcall(function() Library:SetLocked(b, why) end)
n = n + 1
end
end
Device.Safe.count = n
if not quiet then
notify(Device.Safe.on and ("Safe Mode on - " .. n .. " blatant features off and locked")
or "Safe Mode off - everything is unlocked", 5)
end
return n
end
stage("safe mode", function()
if not Device.Boot.opt.safe then return end
Device.Safe.set(true, true)
notify("Safe Mode: " .. Device.Safe.count .. " blatant features are off and locked", 6)
end)
stage("autoload", function()
if SaveManager then pcall(function() SaveManager:SnapDefaults() end) end
if SaveManager and Device.Boot.opt.config then SaveManager:LoadAutoloadConfig() end
pcall(function() Library:CheckKeys(true) end)
if Device.Boot.opt.perf and Toggles.RK_PerfMode and not Toggles.RK_PerfMode.Value then
pcall(function() Toggles.RK_PerfMode:SetValue(true) end)
end
end)
stage("hit ping", function()
if Hit.ping then Hit.setPing(true) end
end)
Device.Boot.finish()
function Device.Boot.sayMissing() return nil end function Device.Boot.sayMissing_disabled()
local list = {}
for _, c in ipairs(Device.Boot.missing or {}) do
list[#list + 1] = c.label .. " (" .. c.what .. ")"
end
if #list == 0 then return nil end
local text = Device.Boot.executor() .. " is missing " .. #list .. " of " .. #Device.Boot.CAPS
.. ": " .. table.concat(list, ", ") .. " - the rest works"
notify(text, 10)
return text
end
Device.Boot.sayMissing()
do
local failed = 0
for _, r in ipairs(UIReport) do
if not r.ok then failed = failed + 1 end
end
local how = IS_MOBILE and "the round menu button opens the menu"
or (tostring(Options.RK_MenuKey and Options.RK_MenuKey.Value or "RightShift") .. " opens the menu")
notify(failed == 0
and ("loaded for " .. tostring(Device.name) .. " - " .. how
.. (Device.remembered and " (Settings > Menu > Device to change)" or ""))
or (failed .. " part(s) failed - see Settings > Build Report"), 7)
print("[Ruv&Rill] ready - " .. #UIReport .. " sections, " .. failed .. " failed")
end
